## CONTEXT

A refactoring block was applied to a Java project, but the configured Maven build failed.

The goal of this step is to repair the current workspace so that the applied refactoring block compiles and passes the configured build command.

This is a repair step, not a new refactoring step.

## TASK

Fix only the errors reported in the build log.

Use the current code state, the staged block, the files already changed by the executor, and the files mentioned by the build log to produce a minimal repair.

Do not redo the refactoring from scratch.

Do not change the refactoring strategy.

Do not undo the refactoring unless that is the smallest safe repair.

## CONSTRAINTS

- Modify only files listed in allowed_files.
- Return the full new content for every modified file.
- Do not return partial patches.
- Do not edit unrelated files.
- Do not edit unrelated tests.
- Do not remove tests.
- Do not weaken assertions.
- Do not change expected behavior to match a broken implementation.
- Do not introduce compatibility wrappers unless they are necessary to make the current refactoring compile.
- Do not undo previous successful blocks.
- If the error cannot be safely repaired using only allowed_files, return an empty files_to_write list and explain why in notes.
- Return only files that actually changed.
- Do not include unchanged files in files_to_write.

      "path": "src/main/java/com/gmail/nossr50/chat/ChatManager.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package com.gmail.nossr50.chat;\n\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.chat.author.Author;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.chat.author.ConsoleAuthor;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.chat.mailer.AdminChatMailer;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.chat.mailer.PartyChatMailer;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.config.ChatConfig;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.chat.ChatChannel;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.party.Party;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.player.McMMOPlayer;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.locale.LocaleLoader;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.mcMMO;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.Misc;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.Permissions;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.text.StringUtils;\nimport net.kyori.adventure.audience.Audience;\nimport net.kyori.adventure.text.TextComponent;\nimport org.bukkit.command.ConsoleCommandSender;\nimport org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;\n\n//TODO: Micro optimization - Cache audiences and update cache when needed\npublic class ChatManager {\n\n    private final @NotNull AdminChatMailer adminChatMailer;\n    private final @NotNull PartyChatMailer partyChatMailer;\n\n    private final @NotNull ConsoleAuthor consoleAuthor;\n    private final @NotNull Audience consoleAudience;\n\n    private final boolean isChatEnabled;\n\n    public ChatManager(@NotNull mcMMO pluginRef) {\n        adminChatMailer = new AdminChatMailer(pluginRef);\n        partyChatMailer = new PartyChatMailer(pluginRef);\n\n        this.consoleAuthor = new ConsoleAuthor(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Chat.Identity.Console\"));\n        this.consoleAudience = mcMMO.getAudiences().filter((cs) -> cs instanceof ConsoleCommandSender);\n        this.isChatEnabled = ChatConfig.getInstance().isChatEnabled();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Handles player messaging when they are either in party chat or admin chat modes\n     *\n     * @param mmoPlayer target player\n     * @param rawMessage the raw message from the player as it was typed\n     * @param isAsync whether this is getting processed via async\n     */\n    public void processPlayerMessage(@NotNull McMMOPlayer mmoPlayer, @NotNull String rawMessage, boolean isAsync) {\n        processPlayerMessage(mmoPlayer, mmoPlayer.getChatChannel(), rawMessage, isAsync);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Handles player messaging for a specific chat channel\n     *\n     * @param mmoPlayer target player\n     * @param args the raw command arguments from the player\n     * @param chatChannel target channel\n     */\n    public void processPlayerMessage(@NotNull McMMOPlayer mmoPlayer, @NotNull String[] args, @NotNull ChatChannel chatChannel) {\n        String chatMessageWithoutCommand = buildChatMessage(args);\n\n        //Commands are never async\n        processPlayerMessage(mmoPlayer, chatChannel, chatMessageWithoutCommand, false);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Handles player messaging for a specific chat channel\n     *\n     * @param mmoPlayer target player\n     * @param chatChannel target chat channel\n     * @param rawMessage raw chat message as it was typed\n     * @param isAsync whether this is getting processed via async\n     */\n    private void processPlayerMessage(@NotNull McMMOPlayer mmoPlayer, @NotNull ChatChannel chatChannel, @NotNull String rawMessage, boolean isAsync) {\n        switch (chatChannel) {\n            case ADMIN:\n                adminChatMailer.processChatMessage(mmoPlayer.getPlayerAuthor(), rawMessage, isAsync, Permissions.colorChat(mmoPlayer.getPlayer()));\n                break;\n            case PARTY:\n                partyChatMailer.processChatMessage(mmoPlayer.getPlayerAuthor(), rawMessage, mmoPlayer.getParty(), isAsync, Permissions.colorChat(mmoPlayer.getPlayer()), Misc.isPartyLeader(mmoPlayer));\n                break;\n            case PARTY_OFFICER:\n            case NONE:\n                break;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Handles console messaging to admins\n     * @param rawMessage raw message from the console\n     */\n    public void processConsoleMessage(@NotNull String rawMessage) {\n        adminChatMailer.processChatMessage(getConsoleAuthor(), rawMessage, false, true);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Handles console messaging to admins\n     * @param args raw command args from the console\n     */\n    public void processConsoleMessage(@NotNull String[] args) {\n        processConsoleMessage(buildChatMessage(args));\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Handles console messaging to a specific party\n     * @param rawMessage raw message from the console\n     * @param party target party\n     */\n    public void processConsoleMessage(@NotNull String rawMessage, @NotNull Party party) {\n        partyChatMailer.processChatMessage(getConsoleAuthor(), rawMessage, party, false, true, false);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Gets a console author\n     * @return a {@link ConsoleAuthor}\n     */\n    private @NotNull Author getConsoleAuthor() {\n        return consoleAuthor;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Change the chat channel of a {@link McMMOPlayer}\n     *  Targeting the channel a player is already in will remove that player from the chat channel\n     * @param mmoPlayer target player\n     * @param targetChatChannel target chat channel\n     */\n    public void setOrToggleChatChannel(@NotNull McMMOPlayer mmoPlayer, @NotNull ChatChannel targetChatChannel) {\n        if (targetChatChannel == mmoPlayer.getChatChannel()) {\n            //Disabled message\n            mmoPlayer.getPlayer().sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Chat.Channel.Off\", StringUtils.getCapitalized(targetChatChannel.toString())));\n            mmoPlayer.setChatMode(ChatChannel.NONE);\n        } else {\n            mmoPlayer.setChatMode(targetChatChannel);\n            mmoPlayer.getPlayer().sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Chat.Channel.On\", StringUtils.getCapitalized(targetChatChannel.toString())));\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Create a chat message from an array of {@link String}\n     * @param args array of {@link String}\n     * @return a String built from the array\n     */\n    private @NotNull String buildChatMessage(@NotNull String[] args) {\n        StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();\n\n        for(int i = 0; i < args.length; i++) {\n            if (i + 1 >= args.length) {\n                stringBuilder.append(args[i]);\n            } else {\n                stringBuilder.append(args[i]).append(\" \");\n            }\n        }\n\n        return stringBuilder.toString();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Whether the player is allowed to send a message to the chat channel they are targeting\n     * @param mmoPlayer target player\n     * @return true if the player can send messages to that chat channel\n     */\n    public boolean isMessageAllowed(@NotNull McMMOPlayer mmoPlayer) {\n        switch (mmoPlayer.getChatChannel()) {\n            case ADMIN:\n                if (mmoPlayer.getPlayer().isOp() || Permissions.adminChat(mmoPlayer.getPlayer())) {\n                    return true;\n                }\n                break;\n            case PARTY:\n                if (mmoPlayer.getParty() != null && Permissions.partyChat(mmoPlayer.getPlayer())) {\n                    return true;\n                }\n                break;\n            case PARTY_OFFICER:\n            case NONE:\n                return false;\n        }\n\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Sends just the console a message\n     * @param author author of the message\n     * @param message message contents in component form\n     */\n    public void sendConsoleMessage(@NotNull Author author, @NotNull TextComponent message) {\n        consoleAudience.sendMessage(author, message);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Whether the mcMMO chat system which handles party and admin chat is enabled or disabled\n     * @return true if mcMMO chat processing (for party/admin chat) is enabled\n     */\n    public boolean isChatEnabled() {\n        return isChatEnabled;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Whether a specific chat channel is enabled\n     * ChatChannels are enabled/disabled via user config\n     *\n     * If chat is disabled, this always returns false\n     * If NONE is passed as a {@link ChatChannel} it will return true\n     * @param chatChannel target chat channel\n     * @return true if the chat channel is enabled\n     */\n    public boolean isChatChannelEnabled(@NotNull ChatChannel chatChannel) {\n        if (!isChatEnabled) {\n            return false;\n        } else {\n            switch(chatChannel) {\n                case ADMIN:\n                case PARTY:\n                case PARTY_OFFICER:\n                    return ChatConfig.getInstance().isChatChannelEnabled(chatChannel);\n                case NONE:\n                    return true;\n                default:\n                    return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n}"
    },
    {
      "path": "src/main/java/com/gmail/nossr50/commands/McImportCommand.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package com.gmail.nossr50.commands;\n\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.ModConfigType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.mcMMO;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.Misc;\nimport org.bukkit.Material;\nimport org.bukkit.command.Command;\nimport org.bukkit.command.CommandExecutor;\nimport org.bukkit.command.CommandSender;\nimport org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;\n\nimport java.io.*;\nimport java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.HashMap;\nimport java.util.Locale;\n\npublic class McImportCommand implements CommandExecutor {\n    int fileAmount;\n\n    @Override\n    public boolean onCommand(@NotNull CommandSender sender, @NotNull Command command, @NotNull String label, String[] args) {\n        if (args.length == 0) {\n            importModConfig();\n            return true;\n        }\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    public boolean importModConfig() {\n        String importFilePath = mcMMO.getModDirectory() + File.separator + \"import\";\n        File importFile = new File(importFilePath, \"import.log\");\n        mcMMO.p.getLogger().info(\"Starting import of mod materials...\");\n        fileAmount = 0;\n\n        HashMap<ModConfigType, ArrayList<String>> materialNames = new HashMap<>();\n\n        BufferedReader in = null;\n\n        try {\n            // Open the file\n            in = new BufferedReader(new FileReader(importFile));\n\n            String line;\n            String materialName;\n            String modName;\n\n            // While not at the end of the file\n            while ((line = in.readLine()) != null) {\n                String[] split1 = line.split(\"material \");\n\n                if (split1.length != 2) {\n                    continue;\n                }\n\n                String[] split2 = split1[1].split(\" with\");\n\n                if (split2.length != 2) {\n                    continue;\n                }\n\n                materialName = split2[0];\n\n                // Categorise each material under a mod config type\n                ModConfigType type = ModConfigType.getModConfigType(materialName);\n\n                if (!materialNames.containsKey(type)) {\n                    materialNames.put(type, new ArrayList<>());\n                }\n\n                materialNames.get(type).add(materialName);\n            }\n        }\n        catch (FileNotFoundException e) {\n            mcMMO.p.getLogger().warning(\"Could not find \" + importFile.getAbsolutePath() + \" ! (No such file or directory)\");\n            mcMMO.p.getLogger().warning(\"Copy and paste latest.log to \" + importFile.getParentFile().getAbsolutePath() + \" and rename it to import.log\");\n            return false;\n        }\n        catch (Exception e) {\n            e.printStackTrace();\n            return false;\n        }\n        finally {\n            tryClose(in);\n        }\n\n        createOutput(materialNames);\n\n        mcMMO.p.getLogger().info(\"Import finished! Created \" + fileAmount + \" files!\");\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    private void createOutput(HashMap<ModConfigType, ArrayList<String>> materialNames) {\n        for (ModConfigType modConfigType : materialNames.keySet()) {\n            HashMap<String, ArrayList<String>> materialNamesType = new HashMap<>();\n\n            for (String materialName : materialNames.get(modConfigType)) {\n                String modName = Misc.getModName(materialName);\n\n                if (!materialNamesType.containsKey(modName)) {\n                    materialNamesType.put(modName, new ArrayList<>());\n                }\n\n                materialNamesType.get(modName).add(materialName);\n            }\n\n            createOutput(modConfigType, materialNamesType);\n        }\n\n    }\n\n    private void tryClose(Closeable c) {\n        if (c == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        try {\n            c.close();\n        }\n        catch (IOException e) {\n            e.printStackTrace();\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void createOutput(ModConfigType modConfigType, HashMap<String, ArrayList<String>> materialNames) {\n        File outputFilePath = new File(mcMMO.getModDirectory() + File.separator + \"output\");\n        if (!outputFilePath.exists() && !outputFilePath.mkdirs()) {\n            mcMMO.p.getLogger().severe(\"Could not create output directory! \" + outputFilePath.getAbsolutePath());\n        }\n\n        FileWriter out = null;\n        String type = modConfigType.name().toLowerCase(Locale.ENGLISH);\n\n        for (String modName : materialNames.keySet()) {\n            File outputFile = new File(outputFilePath, modName + \".\" + type + \".yml\");\n            mcMMO.p.getLogger().info(\"Creating \" + outputFile.getName());\n            try {\n                if (outputFile.exists() && !outputFile.delete()) {\n                    mcMMO.p.getLogger().severe(\"Not able to delete old output file! \" + outputFile.getAbsolutePath());\n                }\n\n                if (!outputFile.createNewFile()) {\n                    mcMMO.p.getLogger().severe(\"Could not create output file! \" + outputFile.getAbsolutePath());\n                    continue;\n                }\n\n                StringBuilder writer = new StringBuilder();\n                HashMap<String, ArrayList<String>> configSections = getConfigSections(modConfigType, modName, materialNames);\n\n                if (configSections == null) {\n                    mcMMO.p.getLogger().severe(\"Something went wrong!! type is \" + type);\n                    return;\n                }\n\n                // Write the file, go through each skill and write all the materials\n                for (String configSection : configSections.keySet()) {\n                    if (configSection.equals(\"UNIDENTIFIED\")) {\n                        writer.append(\"# This isn't a valid config section and all materials in this category need to be\").append(\"\\r\\n\");\n                        writer.append(\"# copy and pasted to a valid section of this config file.\").append(\"\\r\\n\");\n                    }\n                    writer.append(configSection).append(\":\").append(\"\\r\\n\");\n\n                    for (String line : configSections.get(configSection)) {\n                        writer.append(line).append(\"\\r\\n\");\n                    }\n\n                    writer.append(\"\\r\\n\");\n                }\n\n                out = new FileWriter(outputFile);\n                out.write(writer.toString());\n            } catch (Exception e) {\n                e.printStackTrace();\n                return;\n            } finally {\n                tryClose(out);\n                fileAmount++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    private HashMap<String, ArrayList<String>> getConfigSections(ModConfigType type, String modName, HashMap<String, ArrayList<String>> materialNames) {\n        switch (type) {\n            case BLOCKS:\n                return getConfigSectionsBlocks(modName, materialNames);\n            case TOOLS:\n                return getConfigSectionsTools(modName, materialNames);\n            case ARMOR:\n                return getConfigSectionsArmor(modName, materialNames);\n            case UNKNOWN:\n                return getConfigSectionsUnknown(modName, materialNames);\n        }\n\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    private HashMap<String, ArrayList<String>> getConfigSectionsBlocks(String modName, HashMap<String, ArrayList<String>> materialNames) {\n        HashMap<String, ArrayList<String>> configSections = new HashMap<>();\n\n        // Go through all the materials and categorise them under a skill\n        for (String materialName : materialNames.get(modName)) {\n            String skillName = \"UNIDENTIFIED\";\n            if (materialName.contains(\"ORE\")) {\n                skillName = \"Mining\";\n            } else if (materialName.contains(\"LOG\") || materialName.contains(\"LEAVES\")) {\n                skillName = \"Woodcutting\";\n            } else if (materialName.contains(\"GRASS\") || materialName.contains(\"SHORT_GRASS\") || materialName.contains(\"FLOWER\") || materialName.contains(\"CROP\")) {\n                skillName = \"Herbalism\";\n            } else if (materialName.contains(\"DIRT\") || materialName.contains(\"SAND\")) {\n                skillName = \"Excavation\";\n            }\n\n            if (!configSections.containsKey(skillName)) {\n                configSections.put(skillName, new ArrayList<>());\n            }\n\n            ArrayList<String> skillContents = configSections.get(skillName);\n            skillContents.add(\"    \" + materialName + \"|0:\");\n            skillContents.add(\"    \" + \"    \" + \"XP_Gain: 99\");\n            skillContents.add(\"    \" + \"    \" + \"Double_Drops_Enabled: true\");\n\n            if (skillName.equals(\"Mining\")) {\n                skillContents.add(\"    \" + \"    \" + \"Smelting_XP_Gain: 9\");\n            } else if (skillName.equals(\"Woodcutting\")) {\n                skillContents.add(\"    \" + \"    \" + \"Is_Log: \" + materialName.contains(\"LOG\"));\n            }\n        }\n\n        return configSections;\n    }\n\n    private HashMap<String, ArrayList<String>> getConfigSectionsTools(String modName, HashMap<String, ArrayList<String>> materialNames) {\n        HashMap<String, ArrayList<String>> configSections = new HashMap<>();\n\n        // Go through all the materials and categorise them under a tool type\n        for (String materialName : materialNames.get(modName)) {\n            String toolType = \"UNIDENTIFIED\";\n            if (materialName.contains(\"PICKAXE\")) {\n                toolType = \"Pickaxes\";\n            } else if (materialName.contains(\"AXE\")) {\n                toolType = \"Axes\";\n            } else if (materialName.contains(\"BOW\")) {\n                toolType = \"Bows\";\n            } else if (materialName.contains(\"HOE\")) {\n                toolType = \"Hoes\";\n            } else if (materialName.contains(\"SHOVEL\") || materialName.contains(\"SPADE\")) {\n                toolType = \"Shovels\";\n            } else if (materialName.contains(\"SWORD\")) {\n                toolType = \"Swords\";\n            }\n\n            if (!configSections.containsKey(toolType)) {\n                configSections.put(toolType, new ArrayList<>());\n            }\n\n            ArrayList<String> skillContents = configSections.get(toolType);\n            skillContents.add(\"    \" + materialName + \":\");\n            skillContents.add(\"    \" + \"    \" + \"XP_Modifier: 1.0\");\n            skillContents.add(\"    \" + \"    \" + \"Tier: 1\");\n            skillContents.add(\"    \" + \"    \" + \"Ability_Enabled: true\");\n            addRepairableLines(materialName, skillContents);\n        }\n\n        return configSections;\n    }\n\n    private HashMap<String, ArrayList<String>> getConfigSectionsArmor(String modName, HashMap<String, ArrayList<String>> materialNames) {\n        HashMap<String, ArrayList<String>> configSections = new HashMap<>();\n\n        // Go through all the materials and categorise them under an armor type\n        for (String materialName : materialNames.get(modName)) {\n            String toolType = \"UNIDENTIFIED\";\n            if (materialName.contains(\"BOOT\") || materialName.contains(\"SHOE\")) {\n                toolType = \"Boots\";\n            } else if (materialName.contains(\"CHESTPLATE\") || materialName.contains(\"CHEST\")) {\n                toolType = \"Chestplates\";\n            } else if (materialName.contains(\"HELM\") || materialName.contains(\"HAT\")) {\n                toolType = \"Helmets\";\n            } else if (materialName.contains(\"LEGGINGS\") || materialName.contains(\"LEGS\") || materialName.contains(\"PANTS\")) {\n                toolType = \"Leggings\";\n            }\n\n            if (!configSections.containsKey(toolType)) {\n                configSections.put(toolType, new ArrayList<>());\n            }\n\n            ArrayList<String> skillContents = configSections.get(toolType);\n            skillContents.add(\"    \" + materialName + \":\");\n            addRepairableLines(materialName, skillContents);\n        }\n\n        return configSections;\n    }\n\n    private void addRepairableLines(String materialName, ArrayList<String> skillContents) {\n        skillContents.add(\"    \" + \"    \" + \"Repairable: true\");\n        skillContents.add(\"    \" + \"    \" + \"Repair_Material: REPAIR_MATERIAL_NAME\");\n        skillContents.add(\"    \" + \"    \" + \"Repair_Material_Data_Value: 0\");\n        skillContents.add(\"    \" + \"    \" + \"Repair_Material_Quantity: 9\");\n        skillContents.add(\"    \" + \"    \" + \"Repair_Material_Pretty_Name: Repair Item Name\");\n        skillContents.add(\"    \" + \"    \" + \"Repair_MinimumLevel: 0\");\n        skillContents.add(\"    \" + \"    \" + \"Repair_XpMultiplier: 1.0\");\n\n        Material material = Material.matchMaterial(materialName);\n        short durability = (material == null) ? (short) 9999 : material.getMaxDurability();\n        skillContents.add(\"    \" + \"    \" + \"Durability: \" + ((durability > 0) ? durability : (short) 9999));\n    }\n\n    private HashMap<String, ArrayList<String>> getConfigSectionsUnknown(String modName, HashMap<String, ArrayList<String>> materialNames) {\n        HashMap<String, ArrayList<String>> configSections = new HashMap<>();\n\n        // Go through all the materials and print them\n        for (String materialName : materialNames.get(modName)) {\n            String configKey = \"UNIDENTIFIED\";\n\n            if (!configSections.containsKey(configKey)) {\n                configSections.put(configKey, new ArrayList<>());\n            }\n\n            ArrayList<String> skillContents = configSections.get(configKey);\n            skillContents.add(\"    \" + materialName);\n        }\n\n        return configSections;\n    }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "src/main/java/com/gmail/nossr50/commands/skills/SkillCommand.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package com.gmail.nossr50.commands.skills;\n\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.player.McMMOPlayer;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.PrimarySkillType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.SubSkillType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.locale.LocaleLoader;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.mcMMO;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.Permissions;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.commands.CommandUtils;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.player.NotificationManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.player.UserManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.scoreboards.ScoreboardManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.skills.PerksUtils;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.skills.SkillTools;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.text.StringUtils;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.text.TextComponentFactory;\nimport com.google.common.collect.ImmutableList;\nimport net.kyori.adventure.text.Component;\nimport net.md_5.bungee.api.ChatColor;\nimport org.bukkit.command.Command;\nimport org.bukkit.command.CommandExecutor;\nimport org.bukkit.command.CommandSender;\nimport org.bukkit.command.TabExecutor;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.Player;\nimport org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;\n\nimport java.text.DecimalFormat;\nimport java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.Locale;\n\npublic abstract class SkillCommand implements TabExecutor {\n    protected PrimarySkillType skill;\n\n    protected DecimalFormat percent = new DecimalFormat(\"##0.00%\");\n    protected DecimalFormat decimal = new DecimalFormat(\"##0.00\");\n    protected McMMOPlayer mmoPlayer;\n\n    private final CommandExecutor skillGuideCommand;\n\n    public SkillCommand(PrimarySkillType skill) {\n        this.skill = skill;\n        skillGuideCommand = new SkillGuideCommand(skill);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public boolean onCommand(@NotNull CommandSender sender, @NotNull Command command, @NotNull String label, String[] args) {\n        if (CommandUtils.noConsoleUsage(sender)) {\n            return true;\n        }\n\n        if (!CommandUtils.hasPlayerDataKey(sender)) {\n            return true;\n        }\n\n        Player player = (Player) sender;\n        mmoPlayer = UserManager.getPlayer(player);\n\n        if (mmoPlayer == null) {\n            sender.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Profile.PendingLoad\"));\n            return true;\n        }\n\n        if (args.length == 0) {\n            boolean isLucky = Permissions.lucky(player, skill);\n            boolean hasEndurance = PerksUtils.handleActivationPerks(player, 0, 0) != 0;\n            float skillValue = mmoPlayer.getSkillLevel(skill);\n\n            //Send the players a few blank lines to make finding the top of the skill command easier\n            if (mcMMO.p.getAdvancedConfig().doesSkillCommandSendBlankLines())\n                for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++) {\n                    player.sendMessage(\"\");\n                }\n\n            permissionsCheck(player);\n            dataCalculations(player, skillValue);\n\n            sendSkillCommandHeader(mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().getLocalizedSkillName(skill),\n                    player, mmoPlayer, (int) skillValue);\n\n            //Make JSON text components\n            List<Component> subskillTextComponents = getTextComponents(player);\n\n            //Subskills Header\n            player.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Skills.Overhaul.Header\", LocaleLoader.getString(\"Effects.SubSkills.Overhaul\")));\n\n            //Send JSON text components\n\n            TextComponentFactory.sendPlayerSubSkillList(player, subskillTextComponents);\n\n                /*for(TextComponent tc : subskillTextComponents) {\n                    player.spigot().sendMessage(new TextComponent[]{tc, new TextComponent(\": TESTING\")});\n                }*/\n\n            //Stats\n            getStatMessages(player, isLucky, hasEndurance, skillValue);\n\n            //Header\n\n\n            //Link Header\n            if (mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getUrlLinksEnabled()) {\n                player.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Overhaul.mcMMO.Header\"));\n                TextComponentFactory.sendPlayerUrlHeader(player);\n            }\n\n\n            if (mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getScoreboardsEnabled() && mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getSkillUseBoard()) {\n                ScoreboardManager.enablePlayerSkillScoreboard(player, skill);\n            }\n\n            return true;\n        } else if (\"keep\".equals(args[0].toLowerCase())) {\n            if (!mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getAllowKeepBoard()\n                    || !mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getScoreboardsEnabled()\n                    || !mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getSkillUseBoard()) {\n                sender.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Commands.Disabled\"));\n                return true;\n            }\n\n            ScoreboardManager.enablePlayerSkillScoreboard(player, skill);\n            ScoreboardManager.keepBoard(sender.getName());\n            sender.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Commands.Scoreboard.Keep\"));\n            return true;\n        }\n\n        return skillGuideCommand.onCommand(sender, command, label, args);\n    }\n\n    private void getStatMessages(Player player, boolean isLucky, boolean hasEndurance, float skillValue) {\n        List<String> statsMessages = statsDisplay(player, skillValue, hasEndurance, isLucky);\n\n        if (!statsMessages.isEmpty()) {\n            player.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Skills.Overhaul.Header\", LocaleLoader.getString(\"Commands.Stats.Self.Overhaul\")));\n\n            for (String message : statsMessages) {\n                player.sendMessage(message);\n            }\n        }\n\n        final String skillName = mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().getLocalizedSkillName(skill);\n        player.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Guides.Available\",\n                skillName,\n                skillName.toLowerCase(Locale.ENGLISH)));\n    }\n\n    private void sendSkillCommandHeader(String skillName, Player player, McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer, int skillValue) {\n        // send header\n        player.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Skills.Overhaul.Header\", skillName));\n\n        if (!SkillTools.isChildSkill(skill)) {\n            /*\n             * NON-CHILD SKILLS\n             */\n\n            //XP GAIN METHOD\n            player.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Commands.XPGain.Overhaul\", LocaleLoader.getString(\"Commands.XPGain.\" + StringUtils.getCapitalized(skill.toString()))));\n\n            //LEVEL\n            player.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Effects.Level.Overhaul\", skillValue, mcMMOPlayer.getSkillXpLevel(skill), mcMMOPlayer.getXpToLevel(skill)));\n\n        } else {\n            /*\n             * CHILD SKILLS\n             */\n\n\n            var parents = mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().getChildSkillParents(skill);\n\n            //TODO: Add JSON here\n            /*player.sendMessage(parent.getName() + \" - \" + LocaleLoader.getString(\"Effects.Level.Overhaul\", mcMMOPlayer.getSkillLevel(parent), mcMMOPlayer.getSkillXpLevel(parent), mcMMOPlayer.getXpToLevel(parent)))*/\n            ArrayList<PrimarySkillType> parentList = new ArrayList<>(parents);\n\n            StringBuilder parentMessage = new StringBuilder();\n\n            for(int i = 0; i < parentList.size(); i++) {\n                if (i+1 < parentList.size()) {\n                    parentMessage.append(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Effects.Child.ParentList\", mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().getLocalizedSkillName(parentList.get(i)), mcMMOPlayer.getSkillLevel(parentList.get(i))));\n                    parentMessage.append(ChatColor.GRAY).append(\", \");\n                } else {\n                    parentMessage.append(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Effects.Child.ParentList\", mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().getLocalizedSkillName(parentList.get(i)), mcMMOPlayer.getSkillLevel(parentList.get(i))));\n                }\n            }\n\n            //XP GAIN METHOD\n            player.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Commands.XPGain.Overhaul\", LocaleLoader.getString(\"Commands.XPGain.Child\")));\n\n            player.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Effects.Child.Overhaul\", skillValue, parentMessage.toString()));\n            //LEVEL\n            //player.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Effects.Child.Overhaul\", skillValue, skillValue));\n\n        }\n        /*\n        if (!SkillTools.isChildSkill(skill)) {\n            player.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Skills.Header\", skillName));\n            player.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Commands.XPGain\", LocaleLoader.getString(\"Commands.XPGain.\" + StringUtils.getCapitalized(skill.toString()))));\n            player.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Effects.Level\", skillValue, mcMMOPlayer.getSkillXpLevel(skill), mcMMOPlayer.getXpToLevel(skill)));\n        } else {\n            player.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Skills.Header\", skillName + \" \" + LocaleLoader.getString(\"Skills.Child\")));\n            player.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Commands.XPGain\", LocaleLoader.getString(\"Commands.XPGain.Child\")));\n            player.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Effects.Child\", skillValue));\n\n            player.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Skills.Header\", LocaleLoader.getString(\"Skills.Parents\")));\n            Set<PrimarySkillType> parents = FamilyTree.getParents(skill);\n\n            for (PrimarySkillType parent : parents) {\n                player.sendMessage(parent.getName() + \" - \" + LocaleLoader.getString(\"Effects.Level\", mcMMOPlayer.getSkillLevel(parent), mcMMOPlayer.getSkillXpLevel(parent), mcMMOPlayer.getXpToLevel(parent)));\n            }\n        }\n        */\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public List<String> onTabComplete(@NotNull CommandSender sender, @NotNull Command command, @NotNull String alias, String[] args) {\n        if (args.length == 1) {\n            return ImmutableList.of(\"?\", \"keep\");\n        }\n        return ImmutableList.of();\n    }\n\n    protected int calculateRank(float skillValue, int maxLevel, int rankChangeLevel) {\n        return Math.min((int) skillValue, maxLevel) / rankChangeLevel;\n    }\n\n//    protected String[] getAbilityDisplayValues(SkillActivationType skillActivationType, Player player, SubSkillType subSkill) {\n//        return RandomChanceUtil.calculateAbilityDisplayValues(skillActivationType, player, subSkill);\n//    }\n\n    protected String[] calculateLengthDisplayValues(Player player, float skillValue) {\n        int maxLength = mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().getSuperAbilityMaxLength(mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().getSuperAbility(skill));\n        int abilityLengthVar = mcMMO.p.getAdvancedConfig().getAbilityLength();\n        int abilityLengthCap = mcMMO.p.getAdvancedConfig().getAbilityLengthCap();\n\n        int length;\n\n        if (abilityLengthCap <= 0) {\n            length = 2 + (int) (skillValue / abilityLengthVar);\n        } else {\n            length = 2 + (int) (Math.min(abilityLengthCap, skillValue) / abilityLengthVar);\n        }\n\n        int enduranceLength = PerksUtils.handleActivationPerks(player, length, maxLength);\n\n        if (maxLength != 0) {\n            length = Math.min(length, maxLength);\n        }\n\n        return new String[] { String.valueOf(length), String.valueOf(enduranceLength) };\n    }\n\n    protected String getStatMessage(SubSkillType subSkillType, String... vars) {\n        return getStatMessage(false, false, subSkillType, vars);\n    }\n\n    protected String getStatMessage(boolean isExtra, boolean isCustom, SubSkillType subSkillType, String... vars) {\n        String templateKey = isCustom ? \"Ability.Generic.Template.Custom\" : \"Ability.Generic.Template\";\n        String statDescriptionKey = !isExtra ? subSkillType.getLocaleKeyStatDescription() : subSkillType.getLocaleKeyStatExtraDescription();\n\n        if (isCustom)\n            return LocaleLoader.getString(templateKey, LocaleLoader.getString(statDescriptionKey, vars));\n        else {\n            String[] mergedList = NotificationManager.addItemToFirstPositionOfArray(LocaleLoader.getString(statDescriptionKey), vars);\n            return LocaleLoader.getString(templateKey, mergedList);\n        }\n    }\n\n    protected String getLimitBreakDescriptionParameter() {\n        if (mcMMO.p.getAdvancedConfig().canApplyLimitBreakPVE()) {\n            return \"(PVP/PVE)\";\n        } else {\n            return \"(PVP)\";\n        }\n    }\n\n    protected abstract void dataCalculations(Player player, float skillValue);\n\n    protected abstract void permissionsCheck(Player player);\n\n    //protected abstract List<String> effectsDisplay();\n\n    protected abstract List<String> statsDisplay(Player player, float skillValue, boolean hasEndurance, boolean isLucky);\n\n    protected abstract List<Component> getTextComponents(Player player);\n\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "src/main/java/com/gmail/nossr50/datatypes/LevelUpBroadcastPredicate.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes;\n\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.party.Party;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.player.McMMOPlayer;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.mcMMO;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.Misc;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.player.UserManager;\nimport org.bukkit.command.CommandSender;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.Player;\nimport org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;\n\nimport java.util.function.Predicate;\n\n//TODO: Allow for offline players to broadcast\npublic class LevelUpBroadcastPredicate<T extends CommandSender> implements Predicate<T> {\n\n    private final @NotNull T broadcaster;\n\n    public LevelUpBroadcastPredicate(@NotNull T broadcaster) {\n        this.broadcaster = broadcaster;\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public boolean test(@NotNull T t) {\n        Player broadcastingPlayer = (Player) broadcaster; //Always a player no need to check cast\n\n        //Broadcaster should be online\n        if (!broadcastingPlayer.isOnline()) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        McMMOPlayer mmoBroadcastingPlayer = UserManager.getPlayer(broadcastingPlayer);\n\n        if (mmoBroadcastingPlayer == null) {\n            //This should never be null, but just in case...\n            mcMMO.p.getLogger().severe(\"McMMOPlayer was null for broadcaster in LevelUpBroadcastPredicate when it should never be null!\");\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        if (t instanceof Player listeningPlayer) {\n\n            //Party Member Check\n            if (mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().isLevelUpBroadcastsPartyMembersOnly()) {\n                McMMOPlayer mmoListeningPlayer = UserManager.getPlayer(listeningPlayer);\n\n                if (mmoListeningPlayer == null) {\n                    return false; //No profile so therefor no party\n                }\n\n                Party playerWhoLeveledParty = mmoBroadcastingPlayer.getParty();\n                Party broadcastRecipientParty = mmoListeningPlayer.getParty();\n\n                if (playerWhoLeveledParty == null || broadcastRecipientParty == null) {\n                    return false; //No party on either player when being in the same party is required\n                }\n\n                if (!playerWhoLeveledParty.equals(broadcastRecipientParty)) {\n                    return false; //Not in the same party when it is required\n                }\n            }\n\n            //Same world check\n            if (isLevelUpBroadcastsSameWorldOnly()) {\n                if (!mmoBroadcastingPlayer.getPlayer().getWorld().equals(listeningPlayer.getWorld())) {\n                    return false; //Not in the same world when its required\n                }\n\n                //Distance checks\n                if (mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().shouldLevelUpBroadcastsRestrictDistance()) {\n                    if (!Misc.isNear(mmoBroadcastingPlayer.getPlayer().getLocation(), listeningPlayer.getLocation(), mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getLevelUpBroadcastRadius())) {\n                        return false;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n\n            //Visibility checks\n            if (!listeningPlayer.canSee(mmoBroadcastingPlayer.getPlayer()) && listeningPlayer != mmoBroadcastingPlayer.getPlayer()) {\n                return false; //Player who leveled should be invisible to this player so don't send the message\n            }\n\n            return true;\n        } else {\n            //Send out to console\n            return mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().shouldLevelUpBroadcastToConsole();\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static boolean isLevelUpBroadcastsSameWorldOnly() {\n        return mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().isLevelUpBroadcastsSameWorldOnly();\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public String toString() {\n        return \"LevelUpBroadcastPredicate{\" +\n                \"broadcaster=\" + broadcaster +\n                '}';\n    }\n}\n"
    },
    {

      "content": "package com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes;\n\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.party.Party;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.player.McMMOPlayer;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.mcMMO;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.Misc;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.player.UserManager;\nimport org.bukkit.command.CommandSender;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.Player;\nimport org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;\n\nimport java.util.function.Predicate;\n\n//TODO: Allow for offline players to broadcast\npublic class PowerLevelUpBroadcastPredicate<T extends CommandSender> implements Predicate<T> {\n\n    private final @NotNull T broadcaster;\n\n    public PowerLevelUpBroadcastPredicate(@NotNull T broadcaster) {\n        this.broadcaster = broadcaster;\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public boolean test(@NotNull T t) {\n        Player broadcastingPlayer = (Player) broadcaster; //Always a player no need to check cast\n\n        //Broadcaster should be online\n        if (!broadcastingPlayer.isOnline()) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        McMMOPlayer mmoBroadcastingPlayer = UserManager.getPlayer(broadcastingPlayer);\n\n        if (mmoBroadcastingPlayer == null) {\n            //This should never be null, but just in case...\n            mcMMO.p.getLogger().severe(\"McMMOPlayer was null for broadcaster in LevelUpBroadcastPredicate when it should never be null!\");\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        if (t instanceof Player listeningPlayer) {\n\n            //Party Member Check\n            if (mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().isPowerLevelUpBroadcastsPartyMembersOnly()) {\n                McMMOPlayer mmoListeningPlayer = UserManager.getPlayer(listeningPlayer);\n\n                if (mmoListeningPlayer == null) {\n                    return false; //No profile so therefor no party\n                }\n\n                Party playerWhoLeveledParty = mmoBroadcastingPlayer.getParty();\n                Party broadcastRecipientParty = mmoListeningPlayer.getParty();\n\n                if (playerWhoLeveledParty == null || broadcastRecipientParty == null) {\n                    return false; //No party on either player when being in the same party is required\n                }\n\n                if (!playerWhoLeveledParty.equals(broadcastRecipientParty)) {\n                    return false; //Not in the same party when it is required\n                }\n            }\n\n            //Same world check\n            if (isPowerLevelUpBroadcastsSameWorldOnly()) {\n                if (!mmoBroadcastingPlayer.getPlayer().getWorld().equals(listeningPlayer.getWorld())) {\n                    return false; //Not in the same world when its required\n                }\n\n                //Distance checks\n                if (mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().shouldPowerLevelUpBroadcastsRestrictDistance()) {\n                    if (!Misc.isNear(mmoBroadcastingPlayer.getPlayer().getLocation(), listeningPlayer.getLocation(), mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getPowerLevelUpBroadcastRadius())) {\n                        return false;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n\n            //Visibility checks\n            if (!listeningPlayer.canSee(mmoBroadcastingPlayer.getPlayer()) && listeningPlayer != mmoBroadcastingPlayer.getPlayer()) {\n                return false; //Player who leveled should be invisible to this player so don't send the message\n            }\n\n            return true;\n        } else {\n            //Send out to console\n            return mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().shouldPowerLevelUpBroadcastToConsole();\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static boolean isPowerLevelUpBroadcastsSameWorldOnly() {\n        return mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().isPowerLevelUpBroadcastsSameWorldOnly();\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public String toString() {\n        return \"PowerLevelUpBroadcastPredicate{\" +\n                \"broadcaster=\" + broadcaster +\n                '}';\n    }\n}\n"
    },
