      "path": "src/main/java/com/gmail/nossr50/datatypes/party/Party.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.party;\n\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.chat.SamePartyPredicate;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.config.experience.ExperienceConfig;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.experience.FormulaType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.player.McMMOPlayer;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.locale.LocaleLoader;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.mcMMO;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.EventUtils;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.Misc;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.sounds.SoundManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.sounds.SoundType;\nimport org.bukkit.Bukkit;\nimport org.bukkit.ChatColor;\nimport org.bukkit.OfflinePlayer;\nimport org.bukkit.command.CommandSender;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.Player;\nimport org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;\n\nimport java.text.DecimalFormat;\nimport java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.LinkedHashMap;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.UUID;\nimport java.util.function.Predicate;\n\npublic class Party {\n    private final @NotNull Predicate<CommandSender> samePartyPredicate;\n    private final LinkedHashMap<UUID, String> members = new LinkedHashMap<>();\n    private final List<Player> onlineMembers = new ArrayList<>();\n\n    private PartyLeader leader;\n    private String name;\n    private String password;\n    private boolean locked;\n    private Party ally;\n    private int level;\n    private float xp;\n\n    private ShareMode xpShareMode   = ShareMode.NONE;\n    private ShareMode itemShareMode = ShareMode.NONE;\n\n    private boolean shareLootDrops        = true;\n    private boolean shareMiningDrops      = true;\n    private boolean shareHerbalismDrops   = true;\n    private boolean shareWoodcuttingDrops = true;\n    private boolean shareMiscDrops        = true;\n\n    public Party(String name) {\n        this.name = name;\n        samePartyPredicate = new SamePartyPredicate<>(this);\n    }\n\n    public Party(PartyLeader leader, String name) {\n        this.leader = leader;\n        this.name = name;\n        this.locked = true;\n        this.level = 0;\n        samePartyPredicate = new SamePartyPredicate<>(this);\n    }\n\n    public Party(PartyLeader leader, String name, String password) {\n        this.leader = leader;\n        this.name = name;\n        this.password = password;\n        this.locked = true;\n        this.level = 0;\n        samePartyPredicate = new SamePartyPredicate<>(this);\n    }\n\n    public Party(PartyLeader leader, String name, String password, boolean locked) {\n        this.leader = leader;\n        this.name = name;\n        this.password = password;\n        this.locked = locked;\n        this.level = 0;\n        samePartyPredicate = new SamePartyPredicate<>(this);\n    }\n\n    public LinkedHashMap<UUID, String> getMembers() {\n        return members;\n    }\n\n    public List<Player> getOnlineMembers() {\n        return onlineMembers;\n    }\n\n    public List<Player> getVisibleMembers(Player player) {\n        ArrayList<Player> visibleMembers = new ArrayList<>();\n\n        for(Player p : onlineMembers) {\n            if (player.canSee(p))\n                visibleMembers.add(p);\n        }\n\n        return visibleMembers;\n    }\n\n    public List<String> getOnlinePlayerNames(CommandSender sender) {\n        Player player = sender instanceof Player ? (Player) sender : null;\n        List<String> onlinePlayerNames = new ArrayList<>();\n\n        for (Player onlinePlayer : getOnlineMembers()) {\n            if (player != null && player.canSee(onlinePlayer)) {\n                onlinePlayerNames.add(onlinePlayer.getName());\n            }\n        }\n\n        return onlinePlayerNames;\n    }\n\n    public boolean addOnlineMember(Player player) {\n        return onlineMembers.add(player);\n    }\n\n    public boolean removeOnlineMember(Player player) {\n        return onlineMembers.remove(player);\n    }\n\n    public String getName() {\n        return name;\n    }\n\n    public PartyLeader getLeader() {\n        return leader;\n    }\n\n    public String getPassword() {\n        return password;\n    }\n\n    public boolean isLocked() {\n        return locked;\n    }\n\n    public Party getAlly() {\n        return ally;\n    }\n\n    public List<String> getItemShareCategories() {\n        List<String> shareCategories = new ArrayList<>();\n\n        for (ItemShareType shareType : ItemShareType.values()) {\n            if (sharingDrops(shareType)) {\n                shareCategories.add(shareType.getLocaleString());\n            }\n        }\n\n        return shareCategories;\n    }\n\n    public void setName(String name) {\n        this.name = name;\n    }\n\n    public void setLeader(PartyLeader leader) {\n        this.leader = leader;\n    }\n\n    public void setPassword(String password) {\n        this.password = password;\n    }\n\n    public void setLocked(boolean locked) {\n        this.locked = locked;\n    }\n\n    public void setAlly(Party ally) {\n        this.ally = ally;\n    }\n\n    public int getLevel() {\n        return level;\n    }\n\n    public void setLevel(int level) {\n        this.level = level;\n    }\n\n    public float getXp() {\n        return xp;\n    }\n\n    public void setXp(float xp) {\n        this.xp = xp;\n    }\n\n    public void addXp(float xp) {\n        setXp(getXp() + xp);\n    }\n\n    protected float levelUp() {\n        float xpRemoved = getXpToLevel();\n\n        setLevel(getLevel() + 1);\n        setXp(getXp() - xpRemoved);\n\n        return xpRemoved;\n    }\n\n    public int getXpToLevel() {\n        FormulaType formulaType = ExperienceConfig.getInstance().getFormulaType();\n        return (mcMMO.getFormulaManager().getXPtoNextLevel(level, formulaType)) * (getOnlineMembers().size() + mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getPartyXpCurveMultiplier());\n    }\n\n    public String getXpToLevelPercentage() {\n        DecimalFormat percent = new DecimalFormat(\"##0.00%\");\n        return percent.format(this.getXp() / getXpToLevel());\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Applies an experience gain\n     *\n     * @param xp Experience amount to add\n     */\n    public void applyXpGain(float xp) {\n        if (!EventUtils.handlePartyXpGainEvent(this, xp)) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        if (getXp() < getXpToLevel()) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        int levelsGained = 0;\n        float xpRemoved = 0;\n\n        while (getXp() >= getXpToLevel()) {\n            if (hasReachedLevelCap()) {\n                setXp(0);\n                return;\n            }\n\n            xpRemoved += levelUp();\n            levelsGained++;\n        }\n\n        if (!EventUtils.handlePartyLevelChangeEvent(this, levelsGained, xpRemoved)) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        if (!mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getPartyInformAllMembers()) {\n            Player leader = mcMMO.p.getServer().getPlayer(this.leader.getUniqueId());\n\n            if (leader != null) {\n                leader.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Party.LevelUp\", levelsGained, getLevel()));\n\n                if (mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getLevelUpSoundsEnabled()) {\n                    SoundManager.sendSound(leader, leader.getLocation(), SoundType.LEVEL_UP);\n                }\n            }\n        } else {\n            mcMMO.p.getPartyManager().informPartyMembersLevelUp(this, levelsGained, getLevel());\n        }\n\n    }\n\n    public boolean hasReachedLevelCap() {\n        return mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getPartyLevelCap() < getLevel() + 1;\n    }\n\n    public void setXpShareMode(ShareMode xpShareMode) {\n        this.xpShareMode = xpShareMode;\n    }\n\n    public ShareMode getXpShareMode() {\n        return xpShareMode;\n    }\n\n    public void setItemShareMode(ShareMode itemShareMode) {\n        this.itemShareMode = itemShareMode;\n    }\n\n    public ShareMode getItemShareMode() {\n        return itemShareMode;\n    }\n\n    public boolean sharingDrops(ItemShareType shareType) {\n        switch (shareType) {\n            case HERBALISM:\n                return shareHerbalismDrops;\n\n            case LOOT:\n                return shareLootDrops;\n\n            case MINING:\n                return shareMiningDrops;\n\n            case MISC:\n                return shareMiscDrops;\n\n            case WOODCUTTING:\n                return shareWoodcuttingDrops;\n\n            default:\n                return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    public void setSharingDrops(ItemShareType shareType, boolean enabled) {\n        switch (shareType) {\n            case HERBALISM:\n                shareHerbalismDrops = enabled;\n                break;\n\n            case LOOT:\n                shareLootDrops = enabled;\n                break;\n\n            case MINING:\n                shareMiningDrops = enabled;\n                break;\n\n            case MISC:\n                shareMiscDrops = enabled;\n                break;\n\n            case WOODCUTTING:\n                shareWoodcuttingDrops = enabled;\n                break;\n\n            default:\n        }\n    }\n\n    public boolean hasMember(String memberName) {\n        return this.getMembers().values().stream().anyMatch(memberName::equalsIgnoreCase);\n    }\n\n    public boolean hasMember(UUID uuid) {\n        return this.getMembers().containsKey(uuid);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Makes a formatted list of party members based on the perspective of a target player\n     * Players that are hidden will be shown as offline (formatted in the same way)\n     * Party leader will be formatted a specific way as well\n     * @param player target player to use as POV\n     * @return formatted list of party members from the POV of a player\n     */\n    public String createMembersList(Player player) {\n        StringBuilder memberList = new StringBuilder();\n        List<String> coloredNames = new ArrayList<>();\n\n        for(UUID playerUUID : members.keySet()) {\n            OfflinePlayer offlinePlayer = Bukkit.getOfflinePlayer(playerUUID);\n\n            if (offlinePlayer.isOnline() && player.canSee((Player) offlinePlayer)) {\n                ChatColor onlineColor = leader.getUniqueId().equals(playerUUID) ? ChatColor.GOLD : ChatColor.GREEN;\n                coloredNames.add(onlineColor + offlinePlayer.getName());\n            } else {\n                coloredNames.add(ChatColor.DARK_GRAY + members.get(playerUUID));\n            }\n        }\n\n        buildChatMessage(memberList, coloredNames.toArray(new String[0]));\n        return memberList.toString();\n    }\n\n    private void buildChatMessage(@NotNull StringBuilder stringBuilder, String @NotNull [] names) {\n        for(int i = 0; i < names.length; i++) {\n            if (i + 1 >= names.length) {\n                stringBuilder\n                        .append(names[i]);\n            } else {\n                stringBuilder\n                        .append(names[i])\n                        .append(\" \");\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Get the near party members.\n     *\n     * @param mcMMOPlayer The player to check\n     * @return the near party members\n     */\n    public List<Player> getNearMembers(McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer) {\n        List<Player> nearMembers = new ArrayList<>();\n        Party party = mcMMOPlayer.getParty();\n\n        if (party != null) {\n            Player player = mcMMOPlayer.getPlayer();\n            double range = mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getPartyShareRange();\n\n            for (Player member : party.getOnlineMembers()) {\n                if (!player.equals(member) && member.isValid() && Misc.isNear(player.getLocation(), member.getLocation(), range)) {\n                    nearMembers.add(member);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        return nearMembers;\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public boolean equals(Object obj) {\n        if (obj == null) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        if (!(obj instanceof Party other)) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        if ((this.getName() == null) || (other.getName() == null)) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        return this.getName().equals(other.getName());\n    }\n\n    public @NotNull Predicate<CommandSender> getSamePartyPredicate() {\n        return samePartyPredicate;\n    }\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "src/main/java/com/gmail/nossr50/datatypes/player/PlayerProfile.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.player;\n\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.config.experience.ExperienceConfig;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.experience.FormulaType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.experience.SkillXpGain;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.PrimarySkillType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.SuperAbilityType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.mcMMO;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.runnables.player.PlayerProfileSaveTask;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.player.UserManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.skills.SkillTools;\nimport com.google.common.collect.ImmutableList;\nimport com.google.common.collect.ImmutableMap;\nimport org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;\nimport org.jetbrains.annotations.Nullable;\n\nimport java.util.EnumMap;\nimport java.util.Map;\nimport java.util.Objects;\nimport java.util.UUID;\nimport java.util.concurrent.DelayQueue;\n\npublic class PlayerProfile {\n    private final String playerName;\n    private @Nullable UUID uuid;\n    private boolean loaded;\n    private volatile boolean changed;\n\n    /* HUDs */\n    private int scoreboardTipsShown;\n    private int saveAttempts = 0;\n\n    private @Nullable Long lastLogin;\n\n    /* Skill Data */\n    private final Map<PrimarySkillType, Integer>   skills     = new EnumMap<>(PrimarySkillType.class);   // Skill & Level\n    private final Map<PrimarySkillType, Float>     skillsXp   = new EnumMap<>(PrimarySkillType.class);     // Skill & XP\n    private final Map<SuperAbilityType, Integer> abilityDATS = new EnumMap<>(SuperAbilityType.class); // Ability & Cooldown\n    private final Map<UniqueDataType, Integer> uniquePlayerData = new EnumMap<>(UniqueDataType.class); //Misc data that doesn't fit into other categories (chimaera wing, etc..)\n\n    // Store previous XP gains for diminished returns\n    private final DelayQueue<SkillXpGain> gainedSkillsXp = new DelayQueue<>();\n    private final Map<PrimarySkillType, Float> rollingSkillsXp = new EnumMap<>(PrimarySkillType.class);\n\n    @Deprecated\n    public PlayerProfile(String playerName) {\n        this(playerName, null, 0);\n    }\n\n    @Deprecated\n    public PlayerProfile(String playerName, UUID uuid) {\n        this(playerName, uuid, 0);\n    }\n\n    @Deprecated\n    public PlayerProfile(String playerName, int startingLevel) {\n        this(playerName, null, startingLevel);\n    }\n\n    public PlayerProfile(String playerName, @Nullable UUID uuid, int startingLevel) {\n        this.uuid = uuid;\n        this.playerName = playerName;\n\n        scoreboardTipsShown = 0;\n\n        for (SuperAbilityType superAbilityType : SuperAbilityType.values()) {\n            abilityDATS.put(superAbilityType, 0);\n        }\n\n        for (PrimarySkillType primarySkillType : SkillTools.NON_CHILD_SKILLS) {\n            skills.put(primarySkillType, startingLevel);\n            skillsXp.put(primarySkillType, 0F);\n        }\n\n        //Misc Cooldowns\n        uniquePlayerData.put(UniqueDataType.CHIMAERA_WING_DATS, 0); //Chimaera wing\n        lastLogin = System.currentTimeMillis();\n    }\n\n    @Deprecated\n    public PlayerProfile(@NotNull String playerName, boolean isLoaded, int startingLvl) {\n        this(playerName, startingLvl);\n        this.loaded = isLoaded;\n    }\n\n    public PlayerProfile(@NotNull String playerName, UUID uuid, boolean isLoaded, int startingLvl) {\n        this(playerName, uuid, startingLvl);\n        this.loaded = isLoaded;\n    }\n\n    public PlayerProfile(@NotNull String playerName, @Nullable UUID uuid, Map<PrimarySkillType, Integer> levelData, Map<PrimarySkillType, Float> xpData, Map<SuperAbilityType, Integer> cooldownData, int scoreboardTipsShown, Map<UniqueDataType, Integer> uniqueProfileData, @Nullable Long lastLogin) {\n        this.playerName = playerName;\n        this.uuid = uuid;\n        this.scoreboardTipsShown = scoreboardTipsShown;\n\n        skills.putAll(levelData);\n        skillsXp.putAll(xpData);\n        abilityDATS.putAll(cooldownData);\n        uniquePlayerData.putAll(uniqueProfileData);\n\n        loaded = true;\n\n        if (lastLogin != null)\n            this.lastLogin = lastLogin;\n    }\n\n    public void scheduleAsyncSave() {\n        mcMMO.p.getFoliaLib().getImpl().runAsync(new PlayerProfileSaveTask(this, false));\n    }\n\n    public void scheduleAsyncSaveDelay() {\n        mcMMO.p.getFoliaLib().getImpl().runLaterAsync(new PlayerProfileSaveTask(this, false), 20);\n    }\n\n    @Deprecated\n    public void scheduleSyncSaveDelay() {\n        mcMMO.p.getFoliaLib().getImpl().runLater(new PlayerProfileSaveTask(this, true), 20);\n    }\n\n    public void save(boolean useSync) {\n        if (!changed || !loaded) {\n            saveAttempts = 0;\n            return;\n        }\n\n        // TODO should this part be synchronized?\n        PlayerProfile profileCopy = new PlayerProfile(playerName, uuid, ImmutableMap.copyOf(skills), ImmutableMap.copyOf(skillsXp), ImmutableMap.copyOf(abilityDATS), scoreboardTipsShown, ImmutableMap.copyOf(uniquePlayerData), lastLogin);\n        changed = !mcMMO.getDatabaseManager().saveUser(profileCopy);\n\n        if (changed) {\n            mcMMO.p.getLogger().severe(\"PlayerProfile saving failed for player: \" + playerName + \" \" + uuid);\n\n            if (saveAttempts > 0) {\n                mcMMO.p.getLogger().severe(\"Attempted to save profile for player \"+getPlayerName()\n                        + \" resulted in failure. \"+saveAttempts+\" have been made so far.\");\n            }\n\n            if (saveAttempts < 10) {\n                saveAttempts++;\n\n                //Back out of async saving if we detect a server shutdown, this is not always going to be caught\n                if (mcMMO.isServerShutdownExecuted() || useSync)\n                    mcMMO.p.getFoliaLib().getImpl().runNextTick(new PlayerProfileSaveTask(this, true));\n                else\n                    scheduleAsyncSave();\n\n            } else {\n                mcMMO.p.getLogger().severe(\"mcMMO has failed to save the profile for \"\n                        +getPlayerName()+\" numerous times.\" +\n                        \" mcMMO will now stop attempting to save this profile.\" +\n                        \" Check your console for errors and inspect your DB for issues.\");\n            }\n\n        } else {\n            saveAttempts = 0;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Get this users last login, will return current java.lang.System#currentTimeMillis() if it doesn't exist\n     * @return the last login\n     * @deprecated This is only function for FlatFileDB atm, and it's only here for unit testing right now\n     */\n    public @NotNull Long getLastLogin() {\n        return Objects.requireNonNullElse(lastLogin, -1L);\n    }\n\n    public void updateLastLogin() {\n        this.lastLogin = System.currentTimeMillis();\n    }\n\n    public String getPlayerName() {\n        return playerName;\n    }\n\n    public UUID getUniqueId() {\n        return uuid;\n    }\n\n    public void setUniqueId(UUID uuid) {\n        markProfileDirty();\n\n        this.uuid = uuid;\n    }\n\n    public boolean isLoaded() {\n        return loaded;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Marks the profile as \"dirty\" which flags a profile to be saved in the next save operation\n     */\n    public void markProfileDirty() {\n        changed = true;\n    }\n\n    public int getScoreboardTipsShown() {\n        return scoreboardTipsShown;\n    }\n\n    public void setScoreboardTipsShown(int scoreboardTipsShown) {\n        markProfileDirty();\n\n        this.scoreboardTipsShown = scoreboardTipsShown;\n    }\n\n    public void increaseTipsShown() {\n        setScoreboardTipsShown(getScoreboardTipsShown() + 1);\n    }\n\n    /*\n     * Cooldowns\n     */\n\n    public int getChimaerWingDATS() { return uniquePlayerData.get(UniqueDataType.CHIMAERA_WING_DATS);}\n\n    protected void setChimaeraWingDATS(int DATS) {\n        markProfileDirty();\n        uniquePlayerData.put(UniqueDataType.CHIMAERA_WING_DATS, DATS);\n    }\n\n    public void setUniqueData(UniqueDataType uniqueDataType, int newData) {\n        markProfileDirty();\n        uniquePlayerData.put(uniqueDataType, newData);\n    }\n\n    public long getUniqueData(UniqueDataType uniqueDataType) { return uniquePlayerData.get(uniqueDataType); }\n\n    /**\n     * Get the current deactivation timestamp of an ability.\n     *\n     * @param ability The {@link SuperAbilityType} to get the DATS for\n     * @return the deactivation timestamp for the ability\n     */\n    public long getAbilityDATS(SuperAbilityType ability) {\n        return abilityDATS.get(ability);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Set the current deactivation timestamp of an ability.\n     *\n     * @param ability The {@link SuperAbilityType} to set the DATS for\n     * @param DATS the DATS of the ability\n     */\n    protected void setAbilityDATS(SuperAbilityType ability, long DATS) {\n        markProfileDirty();\n\n        abilityDATS.put(ability, (int) (DATS * .001D));\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Reset all ability cooldowns.\n     */\n    protected void resetCooldowns() {\n        markProfileDirty();\n\n        abilityDATS.replaceAll((a, v) -> 0);\n    }\n\n    /*\n     * Xp Functions\n     */\n\n    public int getSkillLevel(PrimarySkillType skill) {\n        return SkillTools.isChildSkill(skill) ? getChildSkillLevel(skill) : skills.get(skill);\n    }\n\n    public float getSkillXpLevelRaw(PrimarySkillType skill) {\n        return skillsXp.get(skill);\n    }\n\n    public int getSkillXpLevel(PrimarySkillType skill) {\n        if (SkillTools.isChildSkill(skill)) {\n            return 0;\n        }\n\n        return (int) Math.floor(getSkillXpLevelRaw(skill));\n    }\n\n    public void setSkillXpLevel(PrimarySkillType skill, float xpLevel) {\n        if (SkillTools.isChildSkill(skill)) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        markProfileDirty();\n\n        skillsXp.put(skill, xpLevel);\n    }\n\n    protected float levelUp(PrimarySkillType skill) {\n        float xpRemoved = getXpToLevel(skill);\n\n        markProfileDirty();\n\n        skills.put(skill, skills.get(skill) + 1);\n        skillsXp.put(skill, skillsXp.get(skill) - xpRemoved);\n\n        return xpRemoved;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Remove Xp from a skill.\n     *\n     * @param skill Type of skill to modify\n     * @param xp Amount of xp to remove\n     */\n    public void removeXp(PrimarySkillType skill, int xp) {\n        if (SkillTools.isChildSkill(skill)) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        markProfileDirty();\n\n        skillsXp.put(skill, skillsXp.get(skill) - xp);\n    }\n\n    public void removeXp(PrimarySkillType skill, float xp) {\n        if (SkillTools.isChildSkill(skill)) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        markProfileDirty();\n\n        skillsXp.put(skill, skillsXp.get(skill) - xp);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Modify a skill level.\n     *\n     * @param skill Type of skill to modify\n     * @param level New level value for the skill\n     */\n    public void modifySkill(PrimarySkillType skill, int level) {\n        if (SkillTools.isChildSkill(skill)) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        markProfileDirty();\n\n        //Don't allow levels to be negative\n        if (level < 0)\n            level = 0;\n\n        skills.put(skill, level);\n        skillsXp.put(skill, 0F);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Add levels to a skill.\n     *\n     * @param skill Type of skill to add levels to\n     * @param levels Number of levels to add\n     */\n    public void addLevels(PrimarySkillType skill, int levels) {\n        modifySkill(skill, skills.get(skill) + levels);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Add Experience to a skill.\n     *\n     * @param skill Type of skill to add experience to\n     * @param xp Number of experience to add\n     */\n    public void addXp(PrimarySkillType skill, float xp) {\n        markProfileDirty();\n\n        if (SkillTools.isChildSkill(skill)) {\n            var parentSkills = mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().getChildSkillParents(skill);\n            float dividedXP = (xp / parentSkills.size());\n\n            for (PrimarySkillType parentSkill : parentSkills) {\n                skillsXp.put(parentSkill, skillsXp.get(parentSkill) + dividedXP);\n            }\n        } else {\n            skillsXp.put(skill, skillsXp.get(skill) + xp);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Get the registered amount of experience gained\n     * This is used for diminished XP returns\n     *\n     * @return xp Experience amount registered\n     */\n    public float getRegisteredXpGain(PrimarySkillType primarySkillType) {\n        float xp = 0F;\n\n        if (rollingSkillsXp.get(primarySkillType) != null) {\n            xp = rollingSkillsXp.get(primarySkillType);\n        }\n\n        return xp;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Register an experience gain\n     * This is used for diminished XP returns\n     *\n     * @param primarySkillType Skill being used\n     * @param xp Experience amount to add\n     */\n    public void registerXpGain(PrimarySkillType primarySkillType, float xp) {\n        gainedSkillsXp.add(new SkillXpGain(primarySkillType, xp));\n        rollingSkillsXp.put(primarySkillType, getRegisteredXpGain(primarySkillType) + xp);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Remove experience gains older than a given time\n     * This is used for diminished XP returns\n     */\n    public void purgeExpiredXpGains() {\n        SkillXpGain gain;\n        while ((gain = gainedSkillsXp.poll()) != null) {\n            rollingSkillsXp.put(gain.getSkill(), getRegisteredXpGain(gain.getSkill()) - gain.getXp());\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Get the amount of Xp remaining before the next level.\n     *\n     * @param primarySkillType Type of skill to check\n     * @return the total amount of Xp until next level\n     */\n    public int getXpToLevel(PrimarySkillType primarySkillType) {\n        if (SkillTools.isChildSkill(primarySkillType)) {\n            return 0;\n        }\n\n        int level = (ExperienceConfig.getInstance().getCumulativeCurveEnabled()) ? UserManager.getPlayer(playerName).getPowerLevel() : skills.get(primarySkillType);\n        FormulaType formulaType = ExperienceConfig.getInstance().getFormulaType();\n\n        return mcMMO.getFormulaManager().getXPtoNextLevel(level, formulaType);\n    }\n\n    private int getChildSkillLevel(@NotNull PrimarySkillType primarySkillType) throws IllegalArgumentException {\n        if (!SkillTools.isChildSkill(primarySkillType)) {\n            throw new IllegalArgumentException(primarySkillType + \" is not a child skill!\");\n        }\n\n        ImmutableList<PrimarySkillType> parents = mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().getChildSkillParents(primarySkillType);\n        int sum = 0;\n\n        for (PrimarySkillType parent : parents) {\n            sum += Math.min(getSkillLevel(parent), mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().getLevelCap(parent));\n        }\n\n        return sum / parents.size();\n    }\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "src/main/java/com/gmail/nossr50/datatypes/skills/subskills/acrobatics/Roll.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.subskills.acrobatics;\n\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.config.experience.ExperienceConfig;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.experience.XPGainReason;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.interactions.NotificationType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.player.McMMOPlayer;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.PrimarySkillType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.SubSkillType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.locale.LocaleLoader;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.mcMMO;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.EventUtils;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.ItemUtils;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.Permissions;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.player.NotificationManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.random.Probability;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.random.ProbabilityUtil;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.skills.PerksUtils;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.skills.RankUtils;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.skills.SkillUtils;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.sounds.SoundManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.sounds.SoundType;\nimport net.kyori.adventure.text.Component;\nimport net.kyori.adventure.text.TextComponent;\nimport org.bukkit.Location;\nimport org.bukkit.Material;\nimport org.bukkit.SoundCategory;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.Player;\nimport org.bukkit.event.Event;\nimport org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;\nimport org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityDamageEvent;\nimport org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack;\nimport org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;\nimport org.jetbrains.annotations.VisibleForTesting;\n\nimport java.util.Locale;\n\npublic class Roll extends AcrobaticsSubSkill {\n\n\n    public Roll() {\n        super(\"Roll\", EventPriority.HIGHEST, SubSkillType.ACROBATICS_ROLL);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Executes the interaction between this subskill and Minecraft\n     *\n     * @param event the vector of interaction\n     * @return true if interaction wasn't cancelled\n     */\n    @Override\n    public boolean doInteraction(Event event, mcMMO plugin) {\n        //TODO: Go through and API this up\n\n        /*\n         * Roll is a SubSkill which allows players to negate fall damage from certain heights with sufficient Acrobatics skill and luck\n         * Roll is activated when a player takes damage from a fall\n         * If a player holds shift, they double their odds at a successful roll and upon success are told they did a graceful roll.\n         */\n\n        //Casting\n        EntityDamageEvent entityDamageEvent = (EntityDamageEvent) event;\n\n        //Make sure a real player was damaged in this event\n        if (!EventUtils.isRealPlayerDamaged(entityDamageEvent))\n            return false;\n\n        if (entityDamageEvent.getCause() == EntityDamageEvent.DamageCause.FALL) {//Grab the player\n            McMMOPlayer mmoPlayer = EventUtils.getMcMMOPlayer(entityDamageEvent.getEntity());\n\n            if (mmoPlayer == null)\n                return false;\n\n            /*\n             * Check for success\n             */\n            \n            if (canRoll(mmoPlayer)) {\n                entityDamageEvent.setDamage(rollCheck(mmoPlayer, entityDamageEvent.getFinalDamage()));\n\n                if (entityDamageEvent.getFinalDamage() == 0) {\n                    entityDamageEvent.setCancelled(true);\n                    return true;\n                }\n            } else if (mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().doesPlayerHaveSkillPermission(mmoPlayer.getPlayer(), PrimarySkillType.ACROBATICS)) {\n                //Give XP Anyways\n                SkillUtils.applyXpGain(mmoPlayer, getPrimarySkill(), calculateRollXP(mmoPlayer, ((EntityDamageEvent) event).getFinalDamage(), false), XPGainReason.PVE);\n            }\n        }\n\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Grabs the permission node for this skill\n     *\n     * @return permission node address\n     */\n    @Override\n    public String getPermissionNode() {\n        return (\"mcmmo.ability.\"+getPrimaryKeyName()+\".\"+getConfigKeyName()).toLowerCase(Locale.ENGLISH);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Checks if a player has permission to use this skill\n     *\n     * @param player target player\n     * @return true if player has permission\n     */\n    @Override\n    public boolean hasPermission(Player player) {\n        return Permissions.isSubSkillEnabled(player, getSubSkillType());\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Adds detailed stats specific to this skill\n     *\n     * @param componentBuilder target component builder\n     * @param mmoPlayer target player\n     */\n    @Override\n    public void addStats(TextComponent.Builder componentBuilder, McMMOPlayer mmoPlayer) {\n        String rollChance, rollChanceLucky, gracefulRollChance, gracefulRollChanceLucky;\n\n        /* Values related to the player */\n        float skillValue = mmoPlayer.getSkillLevel(getPrimarySkill());\n        boolean isLucky = Permissions.lucky(mmoPlayer.getPlayer(), getPrimarySkill());\n\n        String[] rollStrings = ProbabilityUtil.getRNGDisplayValues(mmoPlayer, SubSkillType.ACROBATICS_ROLL);\n        rollChance = rollStrings[0];\n        rollChanceLucky = rollStrings[1];\n\n        /*\n         * Graceful is double the odds of a normal roll\n         */\n        Probability probability = getRollProbability(mmoPlayer);\n        Probability gracefulProbability = Probability.ofValue(probability.getValue() * 2);\n        String[] gracefulRollStrings = ProbabilityUtil.getRNGDisplayValues(gracefulProbability);\n        gracefulRollChance = gracefulRollStrings[0];\n        gracefulRollChanceLucky = gracefulRollStrings[1];\n\n        /*\n         *   Append the messages\n         */\n\n        /*componentBuilder.append(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Effects.Template\", LocaleLoader.getString(\"Acrobatics.Effect.2\"), LocaleLoader.getString(\"Acrobatics.Effect.3\")));\n        componentBuilder.append(\"\\n\");*/\n\n        //Acrobatics.SubSkill.Roll.Chance\n        componentBuilder.append(Component.text(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Acrobatics.SubSkill.Roll.Chance\", rollChance) + (isLucky ? LocaleLoader.getString(\"Perks.Lucky.Bonus\", rollChanceLucky) : \"\")));\n        componentBuilder.append(Component.newline());\n        componentBuilder.append(Component.text(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Acrobatics.SubSkill.Roll.GraceChance\", gracefulRollChance) + (isLucky ? LocaleLoader.getString(\"Perks.Lucky.Bonus\", gracefulRollChanceLucky) : \"\")));\n        //Activation Tips\n        componentBuilder.append(Component.newline()).append(Component.text(LocaleLoader.getString(\"JSON.Hover.Tips\"))).append(Component.newline());\n        componentBuilder.append(Component.text(getTips()));\n        componentBuilder.append(Component.newline());\n        //Advanced\n\n        //Lucky Notice\n        if (isLucky) {\n            componentBuilder.append(Component.text(LocaleLoader.getString(\"JSON.JWrapper.Perks.Header\")));\n            componentBuilder.append(Component.newline());\n            componentBuilder.append(Component.text(LocaleLoader.getString(\"JSON.JWrapper.Perks.Lucky\", \"33\")));\n        }\n\n    }\n\n    @NotNull\n    private Probability getRollProbability(McMMOPlayer mmoPlayer) {\n        return ProbabilityUtil.getSubSkillProbability(SubSkillType.ACROBATICS_ROLL, mmoPlayer);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public boolean isSuperAbility() {\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public boolean isActiveUse() {\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public boolean isPassive() {\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    private boolean canRoll(McMMOPlayer mmoPlayer) {\n        return RankUtils.hasUnlockedSubskill(mmoPlayer.getPlayer(), SubSkillType.ACROBATICS_ROLL)\n                && Permissions.isSubSkillEnabled(mmoPlayer.getPlayer(), SubSkillType.ACROBATICS_ROLL);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Handle the damage reduction and XP gain from the Roll ability\n     *\n     * @param damage The amount of damage initially dealt by the event\n     * @return the modified event damage if the ability was successful, the original event damage otherwise\n     */\n    @VisibleForTesting\n    public double rollCheck(McMMOPlayer mmoPlayer, double damage) {\n        int skillLevel = mmoPlayer.getSkillLevel(getPrimarySkill());\n\n        if (mmoPlayer.getPlayer().isSneaking()) {\n            return gracefulRollCheck(mmoPlayer, damage, skillLevel);\n        }\n\n        double modifiedDamage = calculateModifiedRollDamage(damage, mcMMO.p.getAdvancedConfig().getRollDamageThreshold());\n\n        if (!isFatal(mmoPlayer, modifiedDamage)\n                && ProbabilityUtil.isSkillRNGSuccessful(SubSkillType.ACROBATICS_ROLL, mmoPlayer)) {\n            NotificationManager.sendPlayerInformation(mmoPlayer.getPlayer(), NotificationType.SUBSKILL_MESSAGE, \"Acrobatics.Roll.Text\");\n            SoundManager.sendCategorizedSound(mmoPlayer.getPlayer(), mmoPlayer.getPlayer().getLocation(), SoundType.ROLL_ACTIVATED, SoundCategory.PLAYERS);\n            //mmoPlayer.getPlayer().sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Acrobatics.Roll.Text\"));\n\n            //if (!SkillUtils.cooldownExpired((long) mcMMOmmoPlayer.getPlayer().getTeleportATS(), Config.getInstance().getXPAfterTeleportCooldown())) {\n            if (!isExploiting(mmoPlayer) && mmoPlayer.getAcrobaticsManager().canGainRollXP())\n                SkillUtils.applyXpGain(mmoPlayer, getPrimarySkill(), calculateRollXP(mmoPlayer, damage, true), XPGainReason.PVE);\n            //}\n\n            addFallLocation(mmoPlayer);\n            return modifiedDamage;\n        } else if (!isFatal(mmoPlayer, damage)) {\n            //if (!SkillUtils.cooldownExpired((long) mmoPlayer.getTeleportATS(), Config.getInstance().getXPAfterTeleportCooldown())) {\n            if (!isExploiting(mmoPlayer) && mmoPlayer.getAcrobaticsManager().canGainRollXP())\n                SkillUtils.applyXpGain(mmoPlayer, getPrimarySkill(), calculateRollXP(mmoPlayer, damage, false), XPGainReason.PVE);\n            //}\n        }\n\n        addFallLocation(mmoPlayer);\n        return damage;\n    }\n\n    private int getActivationChance(McMMOPlayer mmoPlayer) {\n        return PerksUtils.handleLuckyPerks(mmoPlayer, getPrimarySkill());\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Handle the damage reduction and XP gain from the Graceful Roll ability\n     *\n     * @param damage The amount of damage initially dealt by the event\n     * @return the modified event damage if the ability was successful, the original event damage otherwise\n     */\n    private double gracefulRollCheck(McMMOPlayer mmoPlayer, double damage, int skillLevel) {\n        double modifiedDamage = calculateModifiedRollDamage(damage, mcMMO.p.getAdvancedConfig().getRollDamageThreshold() * 2);\n\n        final Probability gracefulProbability = getGracefulProbability(mmoPlayer);\n\n        if (!isFatal(mmoPlayer, modifiedDamage)\n                //TODO: Graceful isn't sending out an event\n                && ProbabilityUtil.isStaticSkillRNGSuccessful(PrimarySkillType.ACROBATICS, mmoPlayer, gracefulProbability)) {\n            NotificationManager.sendPlayerInformation(mmoPlayer.getPlayer(), NotificationType.SUBSKILL_MESSAGE, \"Acrobatics.Ability.Proc\");\n            SoundManager.sendCategorizedSound(mmoPlayer.getPlayer(), mmoPlayer.getPlayer().getLocation(), SoundType.ROLL_ACTIVATED, SoundCategory.PLAYERS,0.5F);\n            if (!isExploiting(mmoPlayer) && mmoPlayer.getAcrobaticsManager().canGainRollXP())\n                SkillUtils.applyXpGain(mmoPlayer, getPrimarySkill(), calculateRollXP(mmoPlayer, damage, true), XPGainReason.PVE);\n\n            addFallLocation(mmoPlayer);\n            return modifiedDamage;\n        } else if (!isFatal(mmoPlayer, damage)) {\n            if (!isExploiting(mmoPlayer) && mmoPlayer.getAcrobaticsManager().canGainRollXP())\n                SkillUtils.applyXpGain(mmoPlayer, getPrimarySkill(), calculateRollXP(mmoPlayer, damage, false), XPGainReason.PVE);\n            \n            addFallLocation(mmoPlayer);\n        }\n\n        return damage;\n    }\n\n    @NotNull\n    public static Probability getGracefulProbability(McMMOPlayer mmoPlayer) {\n        double gracefulOdds = ProbabilityUtil.getSubSkillProbability(SubSkillType.ACROBATICS_ROLL, mmoPlayer).getValue() * 2;\n        return Probability.ofValue(gracefulOdds);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Check if the player is \"farming\" Acrobatics XP using\n     * exploits in the game.\n     *\n     * @return true if exploits are detected, false otherwise\n     */\n    private boolean isExploiting(McMMOPlayer mmoPlayer) {\n        if (!ExperienceConfig.getInstance().isAcrobaticsExploitingPrevented()) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        if (ItemUtils.hasItemInEitherHand(mmoPlayer.getPlayer(), Material.ENDER_PEARL) || mmoPlayer.getPlayer().isInsideVehicle()) {\n            if (mmoPlayer.isDebugMode()) {\n                mmoPlayer.getPlayer().sendMessage(\"Acrobatics XP Prevented: Ender Pearl or Inside Vehicle\");\n            }\n            return true;\n        }\n\n        if (mmoPlayer.getAcrobaticsManager().hasFallenInLocationBefore(getBlockLocation(mmoPlayer))) {\n            if (mmoPlayer.isDebugMode()) {\n                mmoPlayer.getPlayer().sendMessage(\"Acrobatics XP Prevented: Fallen in location before\");\n            }\n\n            return true;\n        }\n\n        return false; //NOT EXPLOITING\n    }\n\n    private float calculateRollXP(McMMOPlayer mmoPlayer, double damage, boolean isRoll) {\n        //Clamp Damage to account for insane DRs\n        damage = Math.min(20, damage);\n\n        ItemStack boots = mmoPlayer.getPlayer().getInventory().getBoots();\n        float xp = (float) (damage * (isRoll ? ExperienceConfig.getInstance().getRollXPModifier() : ExperienceConfig.getInstance().getFallXPModifier()));\n\n        if (boots != null && boots.containsEnchantment(mcMMO.p.getEnchantmentMapper().getFeatherFalling())) {\n            xp *= ExperienceConfig.getInstance().getFeatherFallXPModifier();\n        }\n\n        return xp;\n    }\n\n    protected static double calculateModifiedRollDamage(double damage, double damageThreshold) {\n        return Math.max(damage - damageThreshold, 0.0);\n    }\n\n    private boolean isFatal(McMMOPlayer mmoPlayer, double damage) {\n        return mmoPlayer.getPlayer().getHealth() - damage <= 0;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Gets the number of ranks for this subskill, 0 for no ranks\n     *\n     * @return the number of ranks for this subskill, 0 for no ranks\n     */\n    @Override\n    public int getNumRanks() {\n        return 0;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Prints detailed info about this subskill to the player\n     *\n     * @param mmoPlayer the target player\n     */\n    @Override\n    public void printInfo(McMMOPlayer mmoPlayer) {\n        //Header\n        super.printInfo(mmoPlayer);\n\n        //Start the description string.\n        //player.sendMessage(getDescription());\n        //Player stats\n        mmoPlayer.getPlayer().sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Commands.MmoInfo.Stats\",\n                            LocaleLoader.getString(\"Acrobatics.SubSkill.Roll.Stats\", getStats(mmoPlayer))));\n\n        //Mechanics\n        mmoPlayer.getPlayer().sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Commands.MmoInfo.Mechanics\"));\n        mmoPlayer.getPlayer().sendMessage(getMechanics());\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public String getMechanics() {\n        return \"Under Construction: This will work in a future update.\";\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Get an array of various stats for a player\n     *\n     * @param mmoPlayer target player\n     * @return stat array for target player for this skill\n     */\n    @Override\n    public Double[] getStats(McMMOPlayer mmoPlayer) {\n        double playerChanceRoll = ProbabilityUtil.getSubSkillProbability(subSkillType, mmoPlayer).getValue();\n        double playerChanceGrace = playerChanceRoll * 2;\n\n        double gracefulOdds = ProbabilityUtil.getSubSkillProbability(subSkillType, mmoPlayer).getValue() * 2;\n\n        return new Double[]{ playerChanceRoll, playerChanceGrace };\n    }\n\n    public void addFallLocation(@NotNull McMMOPlayer mmoPlayer) {\n        mmoPlayer.getAcrobaticsManager().addLocationToFallMap(getBlockLocation(mmoPlayer));\n    }\n\n    public @NotNull Location getBlockLocation(@NotNull McMMOPlayer mmoPlayer) {\n        return mmoPlayer.getPlayer().getLocation().getBlock().getLocation();\n    }\n}\n"
    },

      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package com.gmail.nossr50.events.skills.repair;\n\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.PrimarySkillType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.events.skills.McMMOPlayerSkillEvent;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.Player;\nimport org.bukkit.event.Cancellable;\nimport org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack;\n\n/**\n * Called just before a player repairs an object with mcMMO.\n */\npublic class McMMOPlayerRepairCheckEvent extends McMMOPlayerSkillEvent implements Cancellable {\n    private final short repairAmount;\n    private final ItemStack repairMaterial;\n    private final ItemStack repairedObject;\n    private boolean cancelled;\n\n    public McMMOPlayerRepairCheckEvent(Player player, short repairAmount, ItemStack repairMaterial, ItemStack repairedObject) {\n        super(player, PrimarySkillType.REPAIR);\n        this.repairAmount = repairAmount;\n        this.repairMaterial = repairMaterial;\n        this.repairedObject = repairedObject;\n        this.cancelled = false;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @return The amount this item will be repaired.\n     */\n    public short getRepairAmount() {\n        return repairAmount;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @return The material used to repair this item\n     */\n    public ItemStack getRepairMaterial() {\n        return repairMaterial;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @return The item that was repaired\n     */\n    public ItemStack getRepairedObject() {\n        return repairedObject;\n    }\n\n    /** Following are required for Cancellable **/\n    @Override\n    public boolean isCancelled() {\n        return cancelled;\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public void setCancelled(boolean cancelled) {\n        this.cancelled = cancelled;\n    }\n}\n"
    },

      "content": "package com.gmail.nossr50.events.skills.salvage;\n\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.PrimarySkillType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.events.skills.McMMOPlayerSkillEvent;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.Player;\nimport org.bukkit.event.Cancellable;\nimport org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack;\n\n/**\n * Called just before a player salvages an item with mcMMO.\n */\npublic class McMMOPlayerSalvageCheckEvent extends McMMOPlayerSkillEvent implements Cancellable {\n    private final ItemStack salvageItem;\n    private final ItemStack salvageResults;\n    private final ItemStack enchantedBook;\n    private boolean cancelled;\n\n    public McMMOPlayerSalvageCheckEvent(Player player, ItemStack salvageItem, ItemStack salvageResults, ItemStack enchantedBook) {\n        super(player, PrimarySkillType.SALVAGE);\n        this.salvageItem = salvageItem;\n        this.salvageResults = salvageResults;\n        this.enchantedBook = enchantedBook;\n        this.cancelled = false;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @return The item that should get salvaged.\n     */\n    public ItemStack getSalvageItem() {\n        return salvageItem;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @return The results that should be dropped after salvaging.\n     */\n    public ItemStack getSalvageResults() {\n        return salvageResults;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @return The enchanted book that should drop after salvaging or null if no book should be dropped.\n     */\n    public ItemStack getEnchantedBook() {\n        return enchantedBook;\n    }\n\n    /** Following are required for Cancellable **/\n    @Override\n    public boolean isCancelled() {\n        return cancelled;\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public void setCancelled(boolean cancel) {\n        this.cancelled = cancel;\n    }\n}\n"
