com.gmail.nossr50.util.player.NotificationManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.player.UserManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.random.ProbabilityUtil;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.skills.CombatUtils;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.worldguard.WorldGuardManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.worldguard.WorldGuardUtils;\nimport org.bukkit.ChatColor;\nimport org.bukkit.Material;\nimport org.bukkit.NamespacedKey;\nimport org.bukkit.OfflinePlayer;\nimport org.bukkit.attribute.Attribute;\nimport org.bukkit.block.Block;\nimport org.bukkit.enchantments.Enchantment;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.*;\nimport org.bukkit.event.Cancellable;\nimport org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;\nimport org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;\nimport org.bukkit.event.Listener;\nimport org.bukkit.event.entity.*;\nimport org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityDamageEvent.DamageCause;\nimport org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack;\nimport org.bukkit.inventory.meta.ItemMeta;\nimport org.bukkit.inventory.meta.PotionMeta;\nimport org.bukkit.metadata.FixedMetadataValue;\nimport org.bukkit.potion.PotionEffect;\nimport org.bukkit.potion.PotionEffectType;\nimport org.bukkit.projectiles.ProjectileSource;\n\nimport static com.gmail.nossr50.util.MobMetadataUtils.*;\n\npublic class EntityListener implements Listener {\n    private final mcMMO pluginRef;\n\n    /**\n     * We can use this {@link NamespacedKey} for {@link Enchantment} comparisons to\n     * check if a {@link Player} has a {@link Trident} enchanted with \"Piercing\".\n     */\n    private final NamespacedKey piercingEnchantment = NamespacedKey.minecraft(\"piercing\");\n\n    public EntityListener(final mcMMO pluginRef) {\n        this.pluginRef = pluginRef;\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)\n    public void onEntityTransform(EntityTransformEvent event) {\n        if (event.getEntity() instanceof LivingEntity livingEntity) {\n\n            //Transfer metadata keys from mob-spawned mobs to new mobs\n            if (hasMobFlags(livingEntity)) {\n                for(Entity entity : event.getTransformedEntities()) {\n                    if (entity instanceof LivingEntity transformedEntity) {\n                        addMobFlags(livingEntity, transformedEntity);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onEntityTargetEntity(EntityTargetLivingEntityEvent event) {\n        if (!ExperienceConfig.getInstance().isEndermanEndermiteFarmingPrevented())\n            return;\n\n        //It's rare but targets can be null sometimes\n        if (event.getTarget() == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        //Prevent entities from giving XP if they target endermite\n        if (event.getTarget() instanceof Endermite) {\n            if (event.getEntity() instanceof Enderman enderman) {\n\n                if (!hasMobFlag(MobMetaFlagType.EXPLOITED_ENDERMEN, enderman)) {\n                    flagMetadata(MobMetaFlagType.EXPLOITED_ENDERMEN, enderman);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = false)\n    public void onEntityShootBow(EntityShootBowEvent event) {\n        /* WORLD BLACKLIST CHECK */\n        if (WorldBlacklist.isWorldBlacklisted(event.getEntity().getWorld()))\n            return;\n\n        if (event.getEntity() instanceof Player player) {\n            Entity projectile = event.getProjectile();\n\n            //Should be noted that there are API changes regarding Arrow from 1.13.2 to current versions of the game\n            if (!(projectile instanceof Arrow arrow)) {\n                return;\n            }\n\n            ItemStack bow = event.getBow();\n\n            if (bow == null)\n                return;\n\n            if (bow.containsEnchantment(mcMMO.p.getEnchantmentMapper().getInfinity())) {\n                projectile.setMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_INF_ARROW, MetadataConstants.MCMMO_METADATA_VALUE);\n            }\n\n            // Set BowType, Force, and Distance metadata\n            projectile.setMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_BOW_FORCE, new FixedMetadataValue(pluginRef, Math.min(event.getForce() * mcMMO.p.getAdvancedConfig().getForceMultiplier(), 1.0)));\n            projectile.setMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_ARROW_DISTANCE, new FixedMetadataValue(pluginRef, arrow.getLocation()));\n\n            //Cleanup metadata in 1 minute in case normal collection falls through\n            CombatUtils.delayArrowMetaCleanup(arrow);\n        }\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onProjectileLaunch(ProjectileLaunchEvent event) {\n        /* WORLD BLACKLIST CHECK */\n        if (WorldBlacklist.isWorldBlacklisted(event.getEntity().getWorld()))\n            return;\n\n        if (event.getEntity().getShooter() instanceof Player player) {\n\n            /* WORLD GUARD MAIN FLAG CHECK */\n            if (WorldGuardUtils.isWorldGuardLoaded()) {\n                if (!WorldGuardManager.getInstance().hasMainFlag(player))\n                    return;\n            }\n\n            if (event.getEntity() instanceof Arrow arrow) {\n                // Delayed metadata cleanup in case other cleanup hooks fail\n                CombatUtils.delayArrowMetaCleanup(arrow);\n\n                // If fired from an item with multi-shot, we need to track\n                if (ItemUtils.doesPlayerHaveEnchantmentInHands(player, \"multishot\")) {\n                    arrow.setMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_MULTI_SHOT_ARROW, MetadataConstants.MCMMO_METADATA_VALUE);\n                }\n\n                if (!arrow.hasMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_BOW_FORCE))\n                    arrow.setMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_BOW_FORCE, new FixedMetadataValue(pluginRef, 1.0));\n\n                if (!arrow.hasMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_ARROW_DISTANCE))\n                    arrow.setMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_ARROW_DISTANCE, new FixedMetadataValue(pluginRef, arrow.getLocation()));\n\n                //Check both hands\n                if (ItemUtils.doesPlayerHaveEnchantmentInHands(player, \"piercing\")) {\n                    return;\n                }\n\n                if (ProbabilityUtil.isSkillRNGSuccessful(SubSkillType.ARCHERY_ARROW_RETRIEVAL, UserManager.getPlayer(player))) {\n                    arrow.setMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_TRACKED_ARROW, MetadataConstants.MCMMO_METADATA_VALUE);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Monitor EntityChangeBlock events.\n     *\n     * @param event\n     *            The event to watch\n     */\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onEntityChangeBlock(EntityChangeBlockEvent event) {\n        /* WORLD BLACKLIST CHECK */\n        if (WorldBlacklist.isWorldBlacklisted(event.getEntity().getWorld()))\n            return;\n\n        Block block = event.getBlock();\n        Entity entity = event.getEntity();\n        Material notYetReplacedType = block.getState().getType(); //because its from getState() this is the block that hasn't been changed yet, which is likely air/lava/water etc\n\n        // When the event is fired for the falling block that changes back to a\n        // normal block\n        // event.getBlock().getType() returns AIR\n        if (!BlockUtils.shouldBeWatched(block.getState())\n                && notYetReplacedType != Material.WATER && notYetReplacedType != Material.LAVA\n                && block.getType() != Material.AIR && block.getType() != Material.CAVE_AIR) {\n            return;\n        }\n        //I could just have it mark all blocks after this but it would potentially cause some really edge case consistency issues that no one would notice\n\n        /*\n         * This mess of code tries to avoid marking the moved block as true in our place store\n         * It's a headache to read but it works, I'm tempted to just remove it\n         */\n        if (entity instanceof FallingBlock || entity instanceof Enderman) {\n            boolean isTracked = entity.hasMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_TRAVELING_BLOCK);\n\n            if (mcMMO.getUserBlockTracker().isIneligible(block) && !isTracked) {\n                mcMMO.getUserBlockTracker().setEligible(block);\n\n                entity.setMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_TRAVELING_BLOCK, MetadataConstants.MCMMO_METADATA_VALUE);\n                TravelingBlockMetaCleanup metaCleanupTask = new TravelingBlockMetaCleanup(entity, pluginRef);\n                mcMMO.p.getFoliaLib().getImpl().runAtEntityTimer(entity, metaCleanupTask, 20, 20*60); //6000 ticks is 5 minutes\n            } else if (isTracked) {\n                BlockUtils.setUnnaturalBlock(block);\n                entity.removeMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_TRAVELING_BLOCK, pluginRef);\n            }\n        } else if ((block.getType() == Material.REDSTONE_ORE || block.getType().getKey().getKey().equalsIgnoreCase(\"deepslate_redstone_ore\"))) {\n            //Redstone ore fire this event and should be ignored\n        } else {\n            if (mcMMO.getUserBlockTracker().isIneligible(block)) {\n                mcMMO.getUserBlockTracker().setEligible(block);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onEntityCombustByEntityEvent(EntityCombustByEntityEvent event) {\n        //Prevent players from setting fire to each other if they are in the same party\n        if (event.getEntity() instanceof Player defender) {\n\n            if (event.getCombuster() instanceof Projectile projectile) {\n                if (projectile.getShooter() instanceof Player attacker) {\n                    if (checkIfInPartyOrSamePlayer(event, defender, attacker)) {\n                        event.setCancelled(true);\n                    }\n                }\n            } else if (event.getCombuster() instanceof Player attacker) {\n                if (checkIfInPartyOrSamePlayer(event, defender, attacker)) {\n                    event.setCancelled(true);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Handle EntityDamageByEntity events that involve modifying the event.\n     *\n     * @param event\n     *            The event to watch\n     */\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onEntityDamageByEntity(EntityDamageByEntityEvent event) {\n        if (event.getEntity() instanceof LivingEntity livingEntity) {\n            if (CombatUtils.hasIgnoreDamageMetadata(livingEntity)) {\n                return;\n            }\n        }\n\n        double damage = event.getFinalDamage();\n        Entity defender = event.getEntity();\n        Entity attacker = event.getDamager();\n\n        if (WorldGuardUtils.isWorldGuardLoaded()) {\n            if (attacker instanceof Player) {\n\n                if (!WorldGuardManager.getInstance().hasMainFlag((Player) attacker)) {\n                    return;\n                }\n\n            } else if (attacker instanceof Projectile projectile) {\n\n                if (projectile.getShooter() instanceof Player) {\n                    if (!WorldGuardManager.getInstance().hasMainFlag((Player) projectile.getShooter())) {\n                        return;\n                    }\n                }\n\n            }\n        }\n\n        /* WORLD BLACKLIST CHECK */\n        if (WorldBlacklist.isWorldBlacklisted(event.getEntity().getWorld())) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        // Don't process this event for marked entities, for players this is handled above,\n        // However, for entities, we do not wanna cancel this event to allow plugins to observe changes\n        // properly\n\n        if (CombatUtils.isProcessingNoInvulnDamage()) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        if (event.getEntity() instanceof ArmorStand) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n\n        if ((ExperienceConfig.getInstance().isNPCInteractionPrevented() && Misc.isNPCEntityExcludingVillagers(defender)) || !defender.isValid() || !(defender instanceof LivingEntity target)) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        if (CombatUtils.isInvincible(target, damage)) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        if (ExperienceConfig.getInstance().isNPCInteractionPrevented() && Misc.isNPCEntityExcludingVillagers(attacker)) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        if (CombatUtils.hasIgnoreDamageMetadata(target)) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        if (attacker instanceof Tameable) {\n            AnimalTamer animalTamer = ((Tameable) attacker).getOwner();\n\n            if (animalTamer != null && ((OfflinePlayer) animalTamer).isOnline()) {\n                attacker = (Entity) animalTamer;\n            }\n        } else if (attacker instanceof TNTPrimed && defender instanceof Player) {\n            if (BlastMining.processBlastMiningExplosion(event, (TNTPrimed) attacker, (Player) defender)) {\n                return;\n            }\n        }\n\n        //Friendly fire checks\n        if (defender instanceof Player defendingPlayer) {\n            //If the attacker is a Player or a projectile belonging to a player\n            if (attacker instanceof Projectile projectile) {\n                if (projectile.getShooter() instanceof Player attackingPlayer && !attackingPlayer.equals(defendingPlayer)) {\n                    //Check for friendly fire and cancel the event\n                    if (checkIfInPartyOrSamePlayer(event, defendingPlayer, attackingPlayer)) {\n                        return;\n                    }\n                }\n\n                //Deflect checks\n                final McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer = UserManager.getPlayer(defendingPlayer);\n                if (mcMMOPlayer != null) {\n                    UnarmedManager unarmedManager = mcMMOPlayer.getUnarmedManager();\n\n                    if (unarmedManager.canDeflect()) {\n                        if (projectile instanceof Arrow && unarmedManager.deflectCheck()) {\n                            event.setCancelled(true);\n                            return;\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            } else if (attacker instanceof Player attackingPlayer){\n                if (checkIfInPartyOrSamePlayer(event, defendingPlayer, attackingPlayer))\n                    return;\n            }\n        }\n\n        //Required setup for processCombatAttack\n        if (attacker instanceof Projectile) {\n            ProjectileSource shooter = ((Projectile) attacker).getShooter();\n            if (shooter instanceof LivingEntity) {\n                attacker = (LivingEntity) shooter;\n            }\n        }\n\n        /*\n         * This was put here to solve a plugin conflict with a mod called Project Korra\n         * Project Korra sends out a damage event with exactly 0 damage\n         * mcMMO does some calculations for the damage in an event and it ends up dividing by zero,\n         *  as a result of the modifiers for the event being 0 and the damage set for this event being 0.\n         *\n         * Surprising this kind of thing\n         *\n         */\n        if (mcMMO.isProjectKorraEnabled()) {\n            if (event.getFinalDamage() == 0) {\n                return;\n            }\n        }\n\n        CombatUtils.processCombatAttack(event, attacker, target);\n        CombatUtils.handleHealthbars(attacker, target, event.getFinalDamage(), pluginRef);\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler(priority =  EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = false)\n    public void onEntityDamageMonitor(EntityDamageByEntityEvent entityDamageEvent) {\n        if (entityDamageEvent.getEntity() instanceof LivingEntity livingEntity) {\n\n            if (entityDamageEvent.getFinalDamage() >= livingEntity.getHealth()) {\n                //This sets entity names back to whatever they are supposed to be\n                CombatUtils.fixNames(livingEntity);\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (entityDamageEvent.getDamager() instanceof Arrow arrow) {\n            CombatUtils.delayArrowMetaCleanup(arrow);\n        }\n\n        if (entityDamageEvent.getEntity() instanceof Player player && entityDamageEvent.getDamager() instanceof Player) {\n            McMMOPlayer mmoPlayer = UserManager.getPlayer(player);\n            if (mmoPlayer != null) {\n                if (mmoPlayer.isDebugMode()) {\n                    player.sendMessage(ChatColor.GOLD + \"(mmodebug start of combat report) EntityDamageByEntityEvent DEBUG Info:\");\n                    player.sendMessage(\"You are being damaged by another player in this event\");\n                    player.sendMessage(\"Raw Damage: \" + entityDamageEvent.getDamage());\n                    player.sendMessage(\"Your max health: \"+player.getAttribute(Attribute.GENERIC_MAX_HEALTH).getValue());\n                    player.sendMessage(\"Your current health: \"+player.getHealth());\n\n                    player.sendMessage(ChatColor.GREEN + \"Damage Modifiers (final damage)\");\n                    for (EntityDamageEvent.DamageModifier modifier : EntityDamageEvent.DamageModifier.values()) {\n                        player.sendMessage(\"Modifier \"+modifier.name()+\": \" + entityDamageEvent.getDamage(modifier));\n                    }\n\n                    player.sendMessage(\"Final damage: \" + entityDamageEvent.getFinalDamage());\n\n                    if (entityDamageEvent.isCancelled()) {\n                        player.sendMessage(\"Event was cancelled, which means no damage should be done.\");\n                    }\n\n                    player.sendMessage(ChatColor.RED + \"(mmodebug end of combat report)\");\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (entityDamageEvent.getDamager() instanceof Player player && entityDamageEvent.getEntity() instanceof Player otherPlayer) {\n            McMMOPlayer mmoPlayer = UserManager.getPlayer(player);\n            if (mmoPlayer != null) {\n                if (mmoPlayer.isDebugMode()) {\n                    player.sendMessage(ChatColor.GOLD + \"(mmodebug start of combat report) EntityDamageByEntityEvent DEBUG Info:\");\n                    player.sendMessage(\"You are dealing damage to another player in this event\");\n                    player.sendMessage(\"Raw Damage: \" + entityDamageEvent.getDamage());\n\n                    player.sendMessage(ChatColor.GREEN + \"Damage Modifiers (final damage)\");\n                    for (EntityDamageEvent.DamageModifier modifier : EntityDamageEvent.DamageModifier.values()) {\n                        player.sendMessage(\"Modifier \"+modifier.name()+\": \" + entityDamageEvent.getDamage(modifier));\n                    }\n\n                    player.sendMessage(\"Final damage: \" + entityDamageEvent.getFinalDamage());\n                    player.sendMessage(\"Target players max health: \"+otherPlayer.getAttribute(Attribute.GENERIC_MAX_HEALTH).getValue());\n                    player.sendMessage(\"Target players current health: \"+otherPlayer.getHealth());\n\n                    if (entityDamageEvent.isCancelled()) {\n                        player.sendMessage(\"Event was cancelled, which means no damage should be done.\");\n                    }\n\n                    player.sendMessage(ChatColor.RED + \"(mmodebug end of combat report)\");\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    public boolean checkIfInPartyOrSamePlayer(Cancellable event, Player defendingPlayer, Player attackingPlayer) {\n        // This check is probably necessary outside of the party system\n        if (defendingPlayer.equals(attackingPlayer)) {\n            return true;\n        }\n\n        if (!pluginRef.isPartySystemEnabled()) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        if (!UserManager.hasPlayerDataKey(defendingPlayer) || !UserManager.hasPlayerDataKey(attackingPlayer)) {\n            return true;\n        }\n\n        //Party Friendly Fire\n        if (!mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getPartyFriendlyFire())\n            if ((mcMMO.p.getPartyManager().inSameParty(defendingPlayer, attackingPlayer)\n                    || mcMMO.p.getPartyManager().areAllies(defendingPlayer, attackingPlayer))\n                    && !(Permissions.friendlyFire(attackingPlayer)\n                    && Permissions.friendlyFire(defendingPlayer))) {\n                event.setCancelled(true);\n                return true;\n            }\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Handle EntityDamage events that involve modifying the event.\n     *\n     * @param event\n     *            The event to modify\n     */\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onEntityDamage(EntityDamageEvent event) {\n        /* WORLD BLACKLIST CHECK */\n        if (WorldBlacklist.isWorldBlacklisted(event.getEntity().getWorld()))\n            return;\n\n        if (event.getEntity().hasMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_EXPLOSION_FROM_RUPTURE)) {\n            event.getEntity().removeMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_EXPLOSION_FROM_RUPTURE, mcMMO.p);\n        }\n\n        if (event.getEntity() instanceof Player player) {\n            /* WORLD GUARD MAIN FLAG CHECK */\n            if (WorldGuardUtils.isWorldGuardLoaded()) {\n                if (!WorldGuardManager.getInstance().hasMainFlag(player))\n                    return;\n            }\n        }\n\n        /*\n         * Process Registered Interactions\n         */\n\n        InteractionManager.processEvent(event, pluginRef, InteractType.ON_ENTITY_DAMAGE);\n\n        /*\n         * Old code\n         */\n\n        if (event.getEntity() instanceof LivingEntity livingEntity) {\n            if (CombatUtils.hasIgnoreDamageMetadata(livingEntity)) {\n                return;\n            }\n        }\n\n        double damage = event.getFinalDamage();\n\n        if (damage <= 0) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        Entity entity = event.getEntity();\n\n        /*\n        As far as I can tell at one point we registered meta-data about custom damage and we no longer do that.\n        if (entity.hasMetadata(mcMMO.customDamageKey)) {\n            entity.removeMetadata(mcMMO.customDamageKey, plugin);\n            return;\n        }\n        */\n\n        if ((ExperienceConfig.getInstance().isNPCInteractionPrevented() && Misc.isNPCEntityExcludingVillagers(entity)) || !entity.isValid() || !(entity instanceof LivingEntity livingEntity)) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        if (CombatUtils.isInvincible(livingEntity, damage)) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        DamageCause cause = event.getCause();\n\n        if (livingEntity instanceof Player) {\n            Player player = (Player) entity;\n\n            if (!UserManager.hasPlayerDataKey(player)) {\n                return;\n            }\n\n            McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer = UserManager.getPlayer(player);\n\n            //Profile not loaded\n            if (mcMMOPlayer == null)\n                return;\n\n            /* Check for invincibility */\n            if (mcMMOPlayer.getGodMode()) {\n                event.setCancelled(true);\n                return;\n            }\n\n            if (event.getFinalDamage() >= 1) {\n                mcMMOPlayer.actualizeRecentlyHurt();\n            }\n\n        }\n\n        else if (livingEntity instanceof Tameable pet) {\n            AnimalTamer owner = pet.getOwner();\n\n            if (owner instanceof Player player) {\n                /* WORLD GUARD MAIN FLAG CHECK */\n                if (WorldGuardUtils.isWorldGuardLoaded()) {\n                    if (!WorldGuardManager.getInstance().hasMainFlag(player))\n                        return;\n                }\n            }\n\n            if (Taming.canPreventDamage(pet, owner)) {\n                Player player = (Player) owner;\n                Wolf wolf = (Wolf) pet;\n\n                //Profile not loaded\n                if (UserManager.getPlayer(player) == null) {\n                    return;\n                }\n\n                TamingManager tamingManager = UserManager.getPlayer(player).getTamingManager();\n\n                switch (cause) {\n                    case CONTACT:\n                    case FIRE:\n                    case HOT_FLOOR:\n                    case LAVA:\n                        if (tamingManager.canUseEnvironmentallyAware()) {\n                            tamingManager.processEnvironmentallyAware(wolf, event.getDamage());\n                        }\n                        return;\n\n                    case FALL:\n                        if (tamingManager.canUseEnvironmentallyAware()) {\n                            event.setCancelled(true);\n                        }\n                        return;\n\n                    case ENTITY_ATTACK:\n                    case PROJECTILE:\n                        if (tamingManager.canUseThickFur()) {\n                            event.setDamage(Taming.processThickFur(wolf, event.getDamage()));\n\n                            if (event.getFinalDamage() == 0) {\n                                event.setCancelled(true);\n                            }\n                        }\n                        return;\n\n                    case FIRE_TICK:\n                        if (tamingManager.canUseThickFur()) {\n                            Taming.processThickFurFire(wolf);\n                        }\n                        return;\n\n                    case MAGIC:\n                    case POISON:\n                    case WITHER:\n                        if (tamingManager.canUseHolyHound()) {\n                            Taming.processHolyHound(wolf, event.getDamage());\n                        }\n                        return;\n\n                    case BLOCK_EXPLOSION:\n                    case ENTITY_EXPLOSION:\n                    case LIGHTNING:\n                        if (tamingManager.canUseShockProof()) {\n                            event.setDamage(Taming.processShockProof(wolf, event.getDamage()));\n\n                            if (event.getFinalDamage() == 0) {\n                                event.setCancelled(true);\n                            }\n                        }\n                        return;\n\n                    default:\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Monitor EntityDeath events.\n     *\n     * @param event\n     *            The event to watch\n     */\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.LOWEST)\n    public void onEntityDeathLowest(EntityDeathEvent event) {\n        mcMMO.getTransientMetadataTools().cleanLivingEntityMetadata(event.getEntity());\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Monitor EntityDeath events.\n     *\n     * @param event\n     *            The event to watch\n     */\n    @EventHandler(ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onEntityDeath(EntityDeathEvent event) {\n        LivingEntity entity = event.getEntity();\n\n        if (mcMMO.getTransientEntityTracker().isTransientSummon(entity)) {\n            mcMMO.getTransientEntityTracker().removeSummon(entity, null, false);\n        }\n\n        /* WORLD BLACKLIST CHECK */\n        if (WorldBlacklist.isWorldBlacklisted(event.getEntity().getWorld())) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        if (ExperienceConfig.getInstance().isNPCInteractionPrevented() && Misc.isNPCEntityExcludingVillagers(entity)) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        Archery.arrowRetrievalCheck(entity);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Monitor CreatureSpawn events.\n     *\n     * @param event\n     *            The event to watch\n     */\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)\n    public void onCreatureSpawn(CreatureSpawnEvent event) {\n        /* WORLD BLACKLIST CHECK */\n        if (WorldBlacklist.isWorldBlacklisted(event.getEntity().getWorld()))\n            return;\n\n        LivingEntity livingEntity = event.getEntity();\n\n        switch (event.getSpawnReason()) {\n            case NETHER_PORTAL:\n                trackSpawnedAndPassengers(livingEntity, MobMetaFlagType.NETHER_PORTAL_MOB);\n                break;\n            case SPAWNER:\n            case SPAWNER_EGG:\n                trackSpawnedAndPassengers(livingEntity, MobMetaFlagType.MOB_SPAWNER_MOB);\n                break;\n            case DISPENSE_EGG:\n            case EGG:\n                trackSpawnedAndPassengers(livingEntity, MobMetaFlagType.EGG_MOB);\n                break;\n            case BREEDING:\n                trackSpawnedAndPassengers(livingEntity, MobMetaFlagType.PLAYER_BRED_MOB);\n                break;\n            default:\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void trackSpawnedAndPassengers(LivingEntity livingEntity, MobMetaFlagType mobMetaFlagType) {\n        flagMetadata(mobMetaFlagType, livingEntity);\n\n        for(Entity passenger : livingEntity.getPassengers()) {\n            if (passenger != null) {\n                flagMetadata(mobMetaFlagType, livingEntity);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler(ignoreCancelled = true, priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST)\n    public void onEntityBreed(EntityBreedEvent event) {\n        if (ExperienceConfig.getInstance().isCOTWBreedingPrevented()) {\n            if (hasMobFlag(MobMetaFlagType.COTW_SUMMONED_MOB, event.getFather()) || hasMobFlag(MobMetaFlagType.COTW_SUMMONED_MOB, event.getMother())) {\n                event.setCancelled(true);\n                Animals mom = (Animals) event.getMother();\n                Animals father = (Animals) event.getFather();\n\n                //Prevent love mode spam\n                mom.setLoveModeTicks(0);\n                father.setLoveModeTicks(0);\n\n                //Inform the player\n                if (event.getBreeder() instanceof Player player) {\n                    NotificationManager.sendPlayerInformationChatOnly(player, \"Taming.Summon.COTW.BreedingDisallowed\");\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Handle ExplosionPrime events that involve modifying the event.\n     *\n     * @param event\n     *            The event to modify\n     */\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onExplosionPrime(ExplosionPrimeEvent event) {\n        /* WORLD BLACKLIST CHECK */\n        if (WorldBlacklist.isWorldBlacklisted(event.getEntity().getWorld()))\n            return;\n\n        Entity entity = event.getEntity();\n\n        if (!(entity instanceof TNTPrimed) || !entity.hasMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_TRACKED_TNT)) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        // We can make this assumption because we (should) be the only ones\n        // using this exact metadata\n        Player player = pluginRef.getServer().getPlayerExact(entity.getMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_TRACKED_TNT).get(0).asString());\n\n        if (!UserManager.hasPlayerDataKey(player)) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        //Profile not loaded\n        if (UserManager.getPlayer(player) == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        /* WORLD GUARD MAIN FLAG CHECK */\n        if (WorldGuardUtils.isWorldGuardLoaded()) {\n            if (!WorldGuardManager.getInstance().hasMainFlag(player))\n                return;\n        }\n\n        MiningManager miningManager = UserManager.getPlayer(player).getMiningManager();\n\n        if (miningManager.canUseBiggerBombs()) {\n            event.setRadius(miningManager.biggerBombs(event.getRadius()));\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Handle EntityExplode events that involve modifying the event.\n     *\n     * @param event\n     *            The event to modify\n     */\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onEnitityExplode(EntityExplodeEvent event) {\n        /* WORLD BLACKLIST CHECK */\n        if (WorldBlacklist.isWorldBlacklisted(event.getEntity().getWorld()))\n            return;\n\n        Entity entity = event.getEntity();\n\n        if (!(entity instanceof TNTPrimed) || !entity.hasMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_TRACKED_TNT)) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        // We can make this assumption because we (should) be the only ones\n        // using this exact metadata\n        Player player = pluginRef.getServer().getPlayerExact(entity.getMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_TRACKED_TNT).get(0).asString());\n\n        if (!UserManager.hasPlayerDataKey(player)) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        /* WORLD GUARD MAIN FLAG CHECK */\n        if (WorldGuardUtils.isWorldGuardLoaded()) {\n            if (!WorldGuardManager.getInstance().hasMainFlag(player))\n                return;\n        }\n\n        //Profile not loaded\n        if (UserManager.getPlayer(player) == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        MiningManager miningManager = UserManager.getPlayer(player).getMiningManager();\n\n        if (miningManager.canUseBlastMining()) {\n            miningManager.blastMiningDropProcessing(event.getYield(), event);\n//            event.setYield(0);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Handle FoodLevelChange events that involve modifying the event.\n     *\n     * @param event\n     *            The event to modify\n     */\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.LOW, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onFoodLevelChange(FoodLevelChangeEvent event) {\n        /* WORLD BLACKLIST CHECK */\n        if (WorldBlacklist.isWorldBlacklisted(event.getEntity().getWorld()))\n            return;\n\n        Entity entity = event.getEntity();\n\n        if (!(entity instanceof Player player)) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        //Profile not loaded\n        if (UserManager.getPlayer(player) == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        /* WORLD GUARD MAIN FLAG CHECK */\n        if (WorldGuardUtils.isWorldGuardLoaded()) {\n            if (!WorldGuardManager.getInstance().hasMainFlag(player))\n                return;\n        }\n\n        if (!UserManager.hasPlayerDataKey(player)) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        int currentFoodLevel = player.getFoodLevel();\n        int newFoodLevel = event.getFoodLevel();\n        int foodChange = newFoodLevel - currentFoodLevel;\n\n        if (foodChange <= 0) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        //Determine which hand is eating food\n        //The main hand is used over the off hand if they both have food, so check the main hand first\n        Material foodInHand;\n\n        if (mcMMO.getMaterialMapStore().isFood(player.getInventory().getItemInMainHand().getType())) {\n            foodInHand = player.getInventory().getItemInMainHand().getType();\n        } else if (mcMMO.getMaterialMapStore().isFood(player.getInventory().getItemInOffHand().getType())) {\n            foodInHand = player.getInventory().getItemInOffHand().getType();\n        } else {\n            return; //Not Food\n        }\n\n        /*\n         * Some foods have 3 ranks Some foods have 5 ranks The number of ranks\n         * is based on how 'common' the item is We can adjust this quite easily\n         * if we find something is giving too much of a bonus\n         */\n\n        //Hacky 1.17 support\n        if (foodInHand.getKey().getKey().equalsIgnoreCase(\"glow_berries\")) {\n            if (Permissions.isSubSkillEnabled(player, SubSkillType.HERBALISM_FARMERS_DIET)) {\n                event.setFoodLevel(UserManager.getPlayer(player).getHerbalismManager().farmersDiet(newFoodLevel));\n            }\n\n            return;\n        }\n\n        switch (foodInHand) {\n            case BAKED_POTATO: /*\n                                * RESTORES 3 HUNGER - RESTORES 5 1/2 HUNGER @\n                                * 1000\n                                */\n            case BEETROOT:\n            case BREAD: /* RESTORES 2 1/2 HUNGER - RESTORES 5 HUNGER @ 1000 */\n            case CARROT: /*\n                               * RESTORES 2 HUNGER - RESTORES 4 1/2 HUNGER @\n                               * 1000\n                               */\n            case GOLDEN_CARROT: /*\n                                 * RESTORES 3 HUNGER - RESTORES 5 1/2 HUNGER @\n                                 * 1000\n                                 */\n            case MUSHROOM_STEW: /*\n                                 * RESTORES 4 HUNGER - RESTORES 6 1/2 HUNGER @\n                                 * 1000\n                                 */\n            case PUMPKIN_PIE: /*\n                               * RESTORES 4 HUNGER - RESTORES 6 1/2 HUNGER @\n                               * 1000\n                               */\n                if (Permissions.isSubSkillEnabled(player, SubSkillType.HERBALISM_FARMERS_DIET)) {\n                    event.setFoodLevel(UserManager.getPlayer(player).getHerbalismManager().farmersDiet(newFoodLevel));\n                }\n                return;\n\n            case COOKIE: /* RESTORES 1/2 HUNGER - RESTORES 2 HUNGER @ 1000 */\n            case MELON_SLICE: /* RESTORES 1 HUNGER - RESTORES 2 1/2 HUNGER @ 1000 */\n            case POISONOUS_POTATO: /*\n                                    * RESTORES 1 HUNGER - RESTORES 2 1/2 HUNGER\n                                    * @ 1000\n                                    */\n            case POTATO: /* RESTORES 1/2 HUNGER - RESTORES 2 HUNGER @ 1000 */\n                if (Permissions.isSubSkillEnabled(player, SubSkillType.HERBALISM_FARMERS_DIET)) {\n                    event.setFoodLevel(UserManager.getPlayer(player).getHerbalismManager().farmersDiet(newFoodLevel));\n                }\n                return;\n            case COD:\n            case SALMON:\n            case TROPICAL_FISH:\n            case COOKED_COD:\n            case COOKED_SALMON:\n\n                if (Permissions.isSubSkillEnabled(player, SubSkillType.FISHING_FISHERMANS_DIET)) {\n                    event.setFoodLevel(UserManager.getPlayer(player).getFishingManager().handleFishermanDiet(newFoodLevel));\n                }\n                return;\n\n            default:\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Monitor EntityTame events.\n     *\n     * @param event\n     *            The event to watch\n     */\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onEntityTame(EntityTameEvent event) {\n        /* WORLD BLACKLIST CHECK */\n        if (WorldBlacklist.isWorldBlacklisted(event.getEntity().getWorld()))\n            return;\n\n        if (event instanceof FakeEntityTameEvent) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        Player player = (Player) event.getOwner();\n\n        /* WORLD GUARD MAIN FLAG CHECK */\n        if (WorldGuardUtils.isWorldGuardLoaded()) {\n            if (!WorldGuardManager.getInstance().hasMainFlag(player))\n                return;\n        }\n\n        LivingEntity livingEntity = event.getEntity();\n\n        if (!UserManager.hasPlayerDataKey(player)\n                || (ExperienceConfig.getInstance().isNPCInteractionPrevented() && Misc.isNPCEntityExcludingVillagers(livingEntity))\n                || hasMobFlag(MobMetaFlagType.EGG_MOB, livingEntity)\n                || hasMobFlag(MobMetaFlagType.MOB_SPAWNER_MOB, livingEntity)) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        flagMetadata(MobMetaFlagType.PLAYER_TAMED_MOB, livingEntity);\n\n        //Profile not loaded\n        if (UserManager.getPlayer(player) == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        UserManager.getPlayer(player).getTamingManager().awardTamingXP(livingEntity);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Handle EntityTarget events.\n     *\n     * @param event\n     *            The event to process\n     */\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onEntityTarget(EntityTargetEvent event) {\n        /* WORLD BLACKLIST CHECK */\n        if (WorldBlacklist.isWorldBlacklisted(event.getEntity().getWorld()))\n            return;\n\n        Entity entity = event.getEntity();\n        Entity target = event.getTarget();\n\n        if (!(entity instanceof Tameable tameable) || !(target instanceof Player player)) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        /* WORLD GUARD MAIN FLAG CHECK */\n        if (WorldGuardUtils.isWorldGuardLoaded()) {\n            if (!WorldGuardManager.getInstance().hasMainFlag(player))\n                return;\n        }\n\n        if (!UserManager.hasPlayerDataKey(player) || !CombatUtils.isFriendlyPet(player, tameable)) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        // isFriendlyPet ensures that the Tameable is: Tamed, owned by a player,\n        // and the owner is in the same party\n        // So we can make some assumptions here, about our casting and our check\n        if (!(Permissions.friendlyFire(player) && Permissions.friendlyFire((Player) tameable.getOwner()))) {\n            event.setCancelled(true);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Handle PotionSplash events in order to fix broken Splash Potion of\n     * Saturation.\n     *\n     * @param event\n     *            The event to process\n     */\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onPotionSplash(PotionSplashEvent event) {\n        /* WORLD BLACKLIST CHECK */\n        if (WorldBlacklist.isWorldBlacklisted(event.getEntity().getWorld()))\n            return;\n\n        ItemMeta meta = event.getPotion().getItem().getItemMeta();\n\n        if (meta == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        for (PotionEffect effect : ((PotionMeta) meta).getCustomEffects()) {\n            if (!effect.getType().equals(PotionEffectType.SATURATION)) {\n                return;\n            }\n\n            for (LivingEntity entity : event.getAffectedEntities()) {\n                int duration = (int) (effect.getDuration() * event.getIntensity(entity));\n                entity.addPotionEffect(new PotionEffect(effect.getType(), duration, effect.getAmplifier(), effect.isAmbient()));\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onProjectileHitEvent(ProjectileHitEvent event) {\n        /* WORLD BLACKLIST CHECK */\n        if (WorldBlacklist.isWorldBlacklisted(event.getEntity().getWorld()))\n            return;\n\n        if (event.getEntity() instanceof Arrow arrow) {\n            if (arrow.isShotFromCrossbow()) {\n                Crossbows.processCrossbows(event, pluginRef, arrow);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "src/main/java/com/gmail/nossr50/listeners/PlayerListener.java",

      "content": "package com.gmail.nossr50.listeners;\n\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.config.WorldBlacklist;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.config.experience.ExperienceConfig;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.chat.ChatChannel;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.player.McMMOPlayer;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.PrimarySkillType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.SubSkillType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.subskills.taming.CallOfTheWildType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.events.McMMOReplaceVanillaTreasureEvent;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.locale.LocaleLoader;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.mcMMO;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.party.ShareHandler;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.runnables.MobHealthDisplayUpdaterTask;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.runnables.player.PlayerProfileLoadingTask;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.skills.fishing.FishingManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.skills.herbalism.HerbalismManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.skills.mining.MiningManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.skills.repair.Repair;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.skills.repair.RepairManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.skills.salvage.Salvage;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.skills.salvage.SalvageManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.skills.taming.TamingManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.*;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.player.UserManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.scoreboards.ScoreboardManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.skills.RankUtils;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.skills.SkillUtils;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.sounds.SoundManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.sounds.SoundType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.worldguard.WorldGuardManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.worldguard.WorldGuardUtils;\nimport org.bukkit.Bukkit;\nimport org.bukkit.GameMode;\nimport org.bukkit.Material;\nimport org.bukkit.block.Block;\nimport org.bukkit.block.BlockState;\nimport org.bukkit.enchantments.Enchantment;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.*;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.minecart.PoweredMinecart;\nimport org.bukkit.event.Event;\nimport org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;\nimport org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;\nimport org.bukkit.event.Listener;\nimport org.bukkit.event.block.Action;\nimport org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityDamageByEntityEvent;\nimport org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityPickupItemEvent;\nimport org.bukkit.event.entity.PlayerDeathEvent;\nimport org.bukkit.event.player.*;\nimport org.bukkit.inventory.EquipmentSlot;\nimport org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack;\n\nimport java.util.Locale;\n\npublic class PlayerListener implements Listener {\n    private final mcMMO plugin;\n\n    public PlayerListener(final mcMMO plugin) {\n        this.plugin = plugin;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Monitor PlayerTeleportEvents.\n     * <p>\n     * These events are monitored for the purpose of setting the\n     * player's last teleportation timestamp, in order to prevent\n     * possible Acrobatics exploitation.\n     *\n     * @param event The event to monitor\n     */\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onPlayerTeleport(PlayerTeleportEvent event) {\n        /* WORLD BLACKLIST CHECK */\n        if (WorldBlacklist.isWorldBlacklisted(event.getPlayer().getWorld())) {\n            //Remove scoreboards\n            if (mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getScoreboardsEnabled()) {\n                ScoreboardManager.teardownPlayer(event.getPlayer());\n            }\n            return;\n        } else if (WorldBlacklist.isWorldBlacklisted(event.getFrom().getWorld()) && mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getScoreboardsEnabled()) {\n            //This only fires if they are travelling to a non-blacklisted world from a blacklisted world\n\n            //Setup scoreboards\n            ScoreboardManager.setupPlayer(event.getPlayer());\n        }\n\n        Player player = event.getPlayer();\n\n        /* WORLD GUARD MAIN FLAG CHECK */\n        if (WorldGuardUtils.isWorldGuardLoaded()) {\n            if (!WorldGuardManager.getInstance().hasMainFlag(player))\n                return;\n        }\n\n        if (!UserManager.hasPlayerDataKey(player) || mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getXPAfterTeleportCooldown() <= 0 || event.getFrom().equals(event.getTo())) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        //Profile not loaded\n        if (UserManager.getPlayer(player) == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        UserManager.getPlayer(player).actualizeTeleportATS();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Handle {@link EntityDamageByEntityEvent} at the highest priority.\n     * <p>\n     * This handler is used to clear the names of mobs with health bars to\n     * fix death messages showing mob health bars on death.\n     *\n     * @param event the event to listen to\n     */\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onEntityDamageByEntityHighest(EntityDamageByEntityEvent event) {\n        // we only care about players as this is for fixing player death messages\n        if (!(event.getEntity() instanceof Player player))\n            return;\n\n        // get the attacker\n        LivingEntity attacker;\n        if (event.getDamager() instanceof LivingEntity)\n            attacker = (LivingEntity) event.getDamager();\n        // attempt to find creator of a projectile\n        else if (event.getDamager() instanceof Projectile && ((Projectile) event.getDamager()).getShooter() instanceof LivingEntity)\n            attacker = (LivingEntity) ((Projectile) event.getDamager()).getShooter();\n        else\n            return;\n\n        if (attacker instanceof HumanEntity)\n            return;\n\n        // world blacklist check\n        if (WorldBlacklist.isWorldBlacklisted(event.getEntity().getWorld()))\n            return;\n        // world guard main flag check\n        if (WorldGuardUtils.isWorldGuardLoaded() && !WorldGuardManager.getInstance().hasMainFlag((Player) event.getEntity()))\n            return;\n\n        // we only want to handle player deaths\n        if ((player.getHealth() - event.getFinalDamage()) > 0)\n            return;\n\n        // temporarily clear the mob's name\n        new MobHealthDisplayUpdaterTask(attacker).run();\n\n        // set the name back\n        mcMMO.p.getFoliaLib().getImpl().runAtEntityLater(attacker, () -> MobHealthbarUtils.handleMobHealthbars(attacker, 0, mcMMO.p), 1);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Monitor PlayerDeathEvents.\n     * <p>\n     * These events are monitored for the purpose of dealing the penalties\n     * associated with hardcore and vampirism modes. If neither of these\n     * modes are enabled, or if the player who died has hardcore bypass\n     * permissions, this handler does nothing.\n     *\n     * @param event The event to monitor\n     */\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onPlayerDeathMonitor(PlayerDeathEvent event) {\n        /* WORLD BLACKLIST CHECK */\n        if (WorldBlacklist.isWorldBlacklisted(event.getEntity().getWorld()))\n            return;\n\n        boolean statLossEnabled = HardcoreManager.isStatLossEnabled();\n        boolean vampirismEnabled = HardcoreManager.isVampirismEnabled();\n\n        if (!statLossEnabled && !vampirismEnabled) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        Player killedPlayer = event.getEntity();\n\n        if (!killedPlayer.hasMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_PLAYER_DATA) || Permissions.hardcoreBypass(killedPlayer)) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        Player killer = killedPlayer.getKiller();\n\n        /* WORLD GUARD MAIN FLAG CHECK */\n        if (WorldGuardUtils.isWorldGuardLoaded()) {\n            if (!WorldGuardManager.getInstance().hasMainFlag(killedPlayer))\n                return;\n        }\n\n        if (statLossEnabled || (killer != null && vampirismEnabled)) {\n            if (EventUtils.callPreDeathPenaltyEvent(killedPlayer).isCancelled()) {\n                return;\n            }\n\n            if (killer != null && vampirismEnabled) {\n                HardcoreManager.invokeVampirism(killer, killedPlayer);\n            }\n\n            if (statLossEnabled) {\n                HardcoreManager.invokeStatPenalty(killedPlayer);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.NORMAL, ignoreCancelled = false)\n    public void onPlayerDeathNormal(PlayerDeathEvent playerDeathEvent) {\n        SkillUtils.removeAbilityBoostsFromInventory(playerDeathEvent.getEntity());\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Monitor PlayerChangedWorldEvents.\n     * <p>\n     * These events are monitored for the purpose of removing god mode or\n     * player parties if they are not allowed on the world the player has\n     * changed to.\n     *\n     * @param event The event to monitor\n     */\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onPlayerWorldChange(PlayerChangedWorldEvent event) {\n        Player player = event.getPlayer();\n\n        if (!UserManager.hasPlayerDataKey(player)) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        //Profile not loaded\n        if (UserManager.getPlayer(player) == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer = UserManager.getPlayer(player);\n\n        mcMMOPlayer.checkGodMode();\n        mcMMOPlayer.checkParty();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Monitor PlayerDropItemEvents.\n     * <p>\n     * These events are monitored for the purpose of flagging sharable\n     * dropped items, as well as removing ability buffs from pickaxes\n     * and shovels.\n     *\n     * @param event The event to monitor\n     */\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onPlayerDropItem(PlayerDropItemEvent event) {\n        /* WORLD BLACKLIST CHECK */\n        if (WorldBlacklist.isWorldBlacklisted(event.getPlayer().getWorld()))\n            return;\n\n        /* WORLD GUARD MAIN FLAG CHECK */\n        if (WorldGuardUtils.isWorldGuardLoaded()) {\n            if (!WorldGuardManager.getInstance().hasMainFlag(event.getPlayer()))\n                return;\n        }\n\n        Item drop = event.getItemDrop();\n        ItemStack dropStack = drop.getItemStack();\n\n        if (ItemUtils.isSharable(dropStack)) {\n            drop.setMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_TRACKED_ITEM, MetadataConstants.MCMMO_METADATA_VALUE);\n        }\n\n        SkillUtils.removeAbilityBuff(dropStack);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Handle PlayerFishEvents at the highest priority.\n     * <p>\n     * These events are used for the purpose of handling our anti-exploit\n     * code, as well as dealing with ice fishing.\n     *\n     * @param event The event to modify\n     */\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGH, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onPlayerFishHighest(PlayerFishEvent event) {\n        /* WORLD BLACKLIST CHECK */\n        if (WorldBlacklist.isWorldBlacklisted(event.getPlayer().getWorld()))\n            return;\n\n        Player player = event.getPlayer();\n\n        /* WORLD GUARD MAIN FLAG CHECK */\n        if (WorldGuardUtils.isWorldGuardLoaded()) {\n            if (!WorldGuardManager.getInstance().hasMainFlag(player))\n                return;\n        }\n\n        if (!UserManager.hasPlayerDataKey(player) || !mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().doesPlayerHaveSkillPermission(player, PrimarySkillType.FISHING)) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        //Profile not loaded\n        if (UserManager.getPlayer(player) == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        FishingManager fishingManager = UserManager.getPlayer(player).getFishingManager();\n\n        switch (event.getState()) {\n            case CAUGHT_FISH:\n                //TODO Update to new API once available! Waiting for case CAUGHT_TREASURE\n                if (event.getCaught() != null) {\n                    Item fishingCatch = (Item) event.getCaught();\n\n                    if (mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getFishingOverrideTreasures() &&\n                            fishingCatch.getItemStack().getType() != Material.SALMON &&\n                            fishingCatch.getItemStack().getType() != Material.COD &&\n                            fishingCatch.getItemStack().getType() != Material.TROPICAL_FISH &&\n                            fishingCatch.getItemStack().getType() != Material.PUFFERFISH) {\n\n                        ItemStack replacementCatch = new ItemStack(Material.SALMON, 1);\n\n                        McMMOReplaceVanillaTreasureEvent replaceVanillaTreasureEvent = new McMMOReplaceVanillaTreasureEvent(fishingCatch, replacementCatch);\n                        Bukkit.getPluginManager().callEvent(replaceVanillaTreasureEvent);\n\n                        //Replace\n                        replacementCatch = replaceVanillaTreasureEvent.getReplacementItemStack();\n                        fishingCatch.setItemStack(replacementCatch);\n                    }\n\n                    if (Permissions.vanillaXpBoost(player, PrimarySkillType.FISHING)) {\n                        //Don't modify XP below vanilla values\n                        if (fishingManager.handleVanillaXpBoost(event.getExpToDrop()) > 1)\n                            event.setExpToDrop(fishingManager.handleVanillaXpBoost(event.getExpToDrop()));\n                    }\n                }\n                return;\n\n            case IN_GROUND:\n                Block block = player.getTargetBlock(null, 100);\n\n                if (fishingManager.canIceFish(block)) {\n\n                    cancelFishingEventAndDropXp(event, player);\n\n                    fishingManager.iceFishing(event.getHook(), block);\n                }\n                return;\n\n            default:\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void cancelFishingEventAndDropXp(PlayerFishEvent event, Player player) {\n        event.setCancelled(true);\n        ExperienceOrb experienceOrb = (ExperienceOrb) player.getWorld().spawnEntity(player.getEyeLocation(), EntityType.EXPERIENCE_ORB);\n        experienceOrb.setExperience(event.getExpToDrop());\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Monitor PlayerFishEvents.\n     * <p>\n     * These events are monitored for the purpose of handling the various\n     * Fishing skills and abilities.\n     *\n     * @param event The event to monitor\n     */\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onPlayerFishMonitor(PlayerFishEvent event) {\n        /* WORLD BLACKLIST CHECK */\n        if (WorldBlacklist.isWorldBlacklisted(event.getPlayer().getWorld()))\n            return;\n\n        Player player = event.getPlayer();\n\n        /* WORLD GUARD MAIN FLAG CHECK */\n        if (WorldGuardUtils.isWorldGuardLoaded()) {\n            if (!WorldGuardManager.getInstance().hasMainFlag(player))\n                return;\n        }\n\n        if (!UserManager.hasPlayerDataKey(player) || !mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().doesPlayerHaveSkillPermission(player, PrimarySkillType.FISHING)) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        //Profile not loaded\n        if (UserManager.getPlayer(player) == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        Entity caught = event.getCaught();\n        FishingManager fishingManager = UserManager.getPlayer(player).getFishingManager();\n\n        //Track the hook\n        if (ExperienceConfig.getInstance().isFishingExploitingPrevented()) {\n            if (event.getHook().getMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_FISH_HOOK_REF).size() == 0) {\n                fishingManager.setFishHookReference(event.getHook());\n            }\n\n            //Spam Fishing\n            if (event.getState() == PlayerFishEvent.State.CAUGHT_FISH && fishingManager.isFishingTooOften()) {\n                event.setExpToDrop(0);\n\n                if (caught instanceof Item caughtItem) {\n                    caughtItem.remove();\n                }\n\n                return;\n            }\n        }\n\n        switch (event.getState()) {\n            case FISHING:\n                if (fishingManager.canMasterAngler()) {\n                    int lureLevel = 0;\n                    ItemStack inHand = player.getInventory().getItemInMainHand();\n\n                    //Grab lure level\n                    if (inHand != null\n                            && inHand.getItemMeta() != null\n                            && inHand.getType().getKey().getKey().equalsIgnoreCase(\"fishing_rod\")) {\n                        if (inHand.getItemMeta().hasEnchants()) {\n                            for(Enchantment enchantment : inHand.getItemMeta().getEnchants().keySet()) {\n                                if (enchantment.toString().toLowerCase().contains(\"lure\")) {\n                                    lureLevel = inHand.getEnchantmentLevel(enchantment);\n                                }\n                            }\n                        }\n\n                    // Prevent any potential odd behavior by only processing if no offhand fishing rod is present\n                    if (!player.getInventory().getItemInOffHand().getType().getKey().getKey().equalsIgnoreCase(\"fishing_rod\")) {\n                        // In case of offhand fishing rod, don't process anything\n                        fishingManager.masterAngler(event.getHook(), lureLevel);\n                        fishingManager.setFishingTarget();\n                    }\n                }\n                }\n                return;\n            case CAUGHT_FISH:\n                if (caught instanceof Item) {\n                    if (ExperienceConfig.getInstance().isFishingExploitingPrevented()) {\n\n                        fishingManager.processExploiting(event.getHook().getLocation().toVector());\n\n                        if (fishingManager.isExploitingFishing(event.getHook().getLocation().toVector())) {\n                            player.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Fishing.ScarcityTip\", ExperienceConfig.getInstance().getFishingExploitingOptionMoveRange()));\n                            event.setExpToDrop(0);\n                            Item caughtItem = (Item) caught;\n                            caughtItem.remove();\n\n                            return;\n                        }\n                    }\n\n                    fishingManager.processFishing((Item) caught);\n                    fishingManager.setFishingTarget();\n                }\n                return;\n            case CAUGHT_ENTITY:\n                if (fishingManager.canShake(caught)) {\n                    fishingManager.shakeCheck((LivingEntity) caught);\n                    fishingManager.setFishingTarget();\n                }\n                return;\n            default:\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Handle PlayerPickupItemEvents at the highest priority.\n     * <p>\n     * These events are used to handle item sharing between party members and\n     * are also used to handle item pickup for the Unarmed skill.\n     *\n     * @param event The event to modify\n     */\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onPlayerPickupItem(EntityPickupItemEvent event) {\n        /* WORLD BLACKLIST CHECK */\n        if (WorldBlacklist.isWorldBlacklisted(event.getEntity().getWorld()))\n            return;\n\n        if (Misc.isNPCEntityExcludingVillagers(event.getEntity())) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        if (event.getEntity() instanceof Player player) {\n\n            /* WORLD GUARD MAIN FLAG CHECK */\n            if (WorldGuardUtils.isWorldGuardLoaded()) {\n                if (!WorldGuardManager.getInstance().hasMainFlag(player))\n                    return;\n            }\n\n            if (!UserManager.hasPlayerDataKey(player)) {\n                return;\n            }\n\n            //Profile not loaded\n            McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer = UserManager.getPlayer(player);\n            if (mcMMOPlayer == null) {\n                return;\n            }\n\n            Item drop = event.getItem();\n            ItemStack dropStack = drop.getItemStack();\n\n            //Remove tracking\n            if (drop.hasMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_TRACKED_ARROW)) {\n                drop.removeMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_TRACKED_ARROW, mcMMO.p);\n            }\n\n            if (drop.hasMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_DISARMED_ITEM)) {\n                if (!player.getName().equals(drop.getMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_DISARMED_ITEM).get(0).asString())) {\n                    event.setCancelled(true);\n                }\n\n                return;\n            }\n\n            if (!drop.hasMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_TRACKED_ITEM) && mcMMOPlayer.inParty() && ItemUtils.isSharable(dropStack)) {\n                event.setCancelled(ShareHandler.handleItemShare(drop, mcMMOPlayer));\n\n                if (event.isCancelled()) {\n                    SoundManager.sendSound(player, player.getLocation(), SoundType.POP);\n                }\n            }\n\n            /*if (player.getInventory().getItemInMainHand().getType() == Material.AIR) {\n                Unarmed.handleItemPickup(player, event);\n                *//*boolean cancel = Config.getInstance().getUnarmedItemPickupDisabled() || pickupSuccess;\n                event.setCancelled(cancel);\n\n                if (pickupSuccess) {\n\n                    return;\n                }*//*\n            }*/\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Monitor PlayerQuitEvents.\n     * <p>\n     * These events are monitored for the purpose of resetting player\n     * variables and other garbage collection tasks that must take place when\n     * a player exits the server.\n     *\n     * @param event The event to monitor\n     */\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onPlayerQuit(PlayerQuitEvent event) {\n        Player player = event.getPlayer();\n\n        if (!UserManager.hasPlayerDataKey(player)) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer = UserManager.getPlayer(player);\n\n        //Profile not loaded\n        if (mcMMOPlayer == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        //Use a sync save if the server is shutting down to avoid race conditions\n        mcMMOPlayer.logout(mcMMO.isServerShutdownExecuted());\n        mcMMO.getTransientMetadataTools().cleanLivingEntityMetadata(event.getPlayer());\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Monitor PlayerJoinEvents.\n     * <p>\n     * These events are monitored for the purpose of initializing player\n     * variables, as well as handling the MOTD display and other important\n     * join messages.\n     *\n     * @param event The event to monitor\n     */\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onPlayerJoin(PlayerJoinEvent event) {\n        Player player = event.getPlayer();\n\n        //Delay loading for 3 seconds in case the player has a save task running, its hacky but it should do the trick\n        mcMMO.p.getFoliaLib().getImpl().runLaterAsync(new PlayerProfileLoadingTask(player), 60);\n\n        if (mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getMOTDEnabled() && Permissions.motd(player)) {\n            Motd.displayAll(player);\n        }\n\n        if (plugin.isXPEventEnabled() && mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().playerJoinEventInfo()) {\n            player.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"XPRate.Event\", ExperienceConfig.getInstance().getExperienceGainsGlobalMultiplier()));\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Monitor PlayerRespawnEvents.\n     * <p>\n     * These events are monitored for the purpose of setting the\n     * player's last respawn timestamp, in order to prevent\n     * possible exploitation.\n     *\n     * @param event The event to monitor\n     */\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onPlayerRespawn(PlayerRespawnEvent event) {\n        Player player = event.getPlayer();\n\n        if (!UserManager.hasPlayerDataKey(player)) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        //Profile not loaded\n        if (UserManager.getPlayer(player) == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        UserManager.getPlayer(player).actualizeRespawnATS();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Handle PlayerInteractEvents at the lowest priority.\n     *\n     * @param event The event to modify\n     */\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.LOWEST, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onPlayerInteractLowest(PlayerInteractEvent event) {\n        /* WORLD BLACKLIST CHECK */\n        if (WorldBlacklist.isWorldBlacklisted(event.getPlayer().getWorld()))\n            return;\n\n        Player player = event.getPlayer();\n\n        /* WORLD GUARD MAIN FLAG CHECK */\n        if (WorldGuardUtils.isWorldGuardLoaded()) {\n            if (!WorldGuardManager.getInstance().hasMainFlag(player))\n                return;\n        }\n\n        if (event.getClickedBlock() == null)\n            return;\n\n        Block clickedBlock = event.getClickedBlock();\n        Material clickedBlockType = clickedBlock.getType();\n        //The blacklist contains interactable blocks so its a convenient filter\n        if (clickedBlockType == Repair.anvilMaterial || clickedBlockType == Salvage.anvilMaterial) {\n            event.setUseItemInHand(Event.Result.ALLOW);\n\n            if (!event.getPlayer().isSneaking() && mcMMO.getMaterialMapStore().isToolActivationBlackListed(clickedBlockType)) {\n                event.setUseInteractedBlock(Event.Result.DENY);\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (event.getHand() != EquipmentSlot.HAND || !UserManager.hasPlayerDataKey(player) || player.getGameMode() == GameMode.CREATIVE) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        //Profile not loaded\n        if (UserManager.getPlayer(player) == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer = UserManager.getPlayer(player);\n        MiningManager miningManager = mcMMOPlayer.getMiningManager();\n        ItemStack heldItem = player.getInventory().getItemInMainHand();\n\n        switch (event.getAction()) {\n            case RIGHT_CLICK_BLOCK:\n                Material type = clickedBlock.getType();\n\n                if (!mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getAbilitiesOnlyActivateWhenSneaking() || player.isSneaking()) {\n                    /* REPAIR CHECKS */\n                    if (type == Repair.anvilMaterial\n                            && mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().doesPlayerHaveSkillPermission(player, PrimarySkillType.REPAIR)\n                            && mcMMO.getRepairableManager().isRepairable(heldItem)\n                            && heldItem.getAmount() <= 1) {\n                        RepairManager repairManager = mcMMOPlayer.getRepairManager();\n                        event.setCancelled(true);\n\n                        // Make sure the player knows what he's doing when trying to repair an enchanted item\n                        if (repairManager.checkConfirmation(true)) {\n                            repairManager.handleRepair(heldItem);\n                            player.updateInventory();\n                        }\n                    }\n                    /* SALVAGE CHECKS */\n                    else if (type == Salvage.anvilMaterial\n                            && mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().doesPlayerHaveSkillPermission(player, PrimarySkillType.SALVAGE)\n                            && RankUtils.hasUnlockedSubskill(player, SubSkillType.SALVAGE_SCRAP_COLLECTOR)\n                            && mcMMO.getSalvageableManager().isSalvageable(heldItem)\n                            && heldItem.getAmount() <= 1) {\n                                SalvageManager salvageManager = UserManager.getPlayer(player).getSalvageManager();\n                                event.setCancelled(true);\n\n                                // Make sure the player knows what he's doing when trying to salvage an enchanted item\n                                if (salvageManager.checkConfirmation(true)) {\n                                    SkillUtils.removeAbilityBoostsFromInventory(player);\n                                    salvageManager.handleSalvage(clickedBlock.getLocation(), heldItem);\n                                    player.updateInventory();\n                                }\n                    }\n\n                }\n                /* BLAST MINING CHECK */\n                else if (miningManager.canDetonate()) {\n                    if (type == Material.TNT) {\n                        event.setCancelled(true); // Don't detonate the TNT if they're too close\n                    } else {\n                        miningManager.remoteDetonation();\n                    }\n                }\n\n                break;\n\n            case LEFT_CLICK_BLOCK:\n                type = clickedBlock.getType();\n\n                if (!mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getAbilitiesOnlyActivateWhenSneaking() || player.isSneaking()) {\n                    /* REPAIR CHECKS */\n                    if (type == Repair.anvilMaterial && mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().doesPlayerHaveSkillPermission(player, PrimarySkillType.REPAIR) && mcMMO.getRepairableManager().isRepairable(heldItem)) {\n                        RepairManager repairManager = mcMMOPlayer.getRepairManager();\n\n                        // Cancel repairing an enchanted item\n                        if (repairManager.checkConfirmation(false)) {\n                            repairManager.setLastAnvilUse(0);\n                            player.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Skills.Cancelled\", LocaleLoader.getString(\"Repair.Pretty.Name\")));\n                        }\n                    }\n                    /* SALVAGE CHECKS */\n                    else if (type == Salvage.anvilMaterial && mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().doesPlayerHaveSkillPermission(player, PrimarySkillType.SALVAGE) && mcMMO.getSalvageableManager().isSalvageable(heldItem)) {\n                        SalvageManager salvageManager = mcMMOPlayer.getSalvageManager();\n\n                        // Cancel salvaging an enchanted item\n                        if (salvageManager.checkConfirmation(false)) {\n                            salvageManager.setLastAnvilUse(0);\n                            player.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Skills.Cancelled\", LocaleLoader.getString(\"Salvage.Pretty.Name\")));\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n\n                break;\n\n            default:\n                break;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Monitor PlayerInteractEvents.\n     *\n     * @param event The event to monitor\n     */\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)\n    public void onPlayerInteractMonitor(PlayerInteractEvent event) {\n        /* WORLD BLACKLIST CHECK */\n        if (WorldBlacklist.isWorldBlacklisted(event.getPlayer().getWorld()))\n            return;\n\n        Player player = event.getPlayer();\n\n        /* WORLD GUARD MAIN FLAG CHECK */\n        if (WorldGuardUtils.isWorldGuardLoaded()) {\n            if (!WorldGuardManager.getInstance().hasMainFlag(player))\n                return;\n        }\n\n        if (event.getHand() != EquipmentSlot.HAND\n                || !UserManager.hasPlayerDataKey(player)\n                || player.getGameMode() == GameMode.CREATIVE) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        //Profile not loaded\n        if (UserManager.getPlayer(player) == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer = UserManager.getPlayer(player);\n        if (mcMMOPlayer == null)\n            return;\n\n        ItemStack heldItem = player.getInventory().getItemInMainHand();\n\n        //Spam Fishing Detection\n        if (event.getAction() == Action.RIGHT_CLICK_BLOCK || event.getAction() == Action.RIGHT_CLICK_AIR) {\n            if (ExperienceConfig.getInstance().isFishingExploitingPrevented()\n                       && (heldItem.getType() == Material.FISHING_ROD || player.getInventory().getItemInOffHand().getType() == Material.FISHING_ROD)) {\n                if (player.isInsideVehicle() && (player.getVehicle() instanceof Minecart || player.getVehicle() instanceof PoweredMinecart)) {\n                    player.getVehicle().eject();\n                }\n\n                //mcMMOPlayer.getFishingManager().setFishingRodCastTimestamp();\n            }\n        }\n\n        switch (event.getAction()) {\n            case RIGHT_CLICK_BLOCK:\n                if (player.getInventory().getItemInOffHand().getType() != Material.AIR && !player.isInsideVehicle() && !player.isSneaking()) {\n                    break;\n                }\n\n                //Hmm\n                if (event.getClickedBlock() == null)\n                    return;\n\n                Block block = event.getClickedBlock();\n                BlockState blockState = block.getState();\n\n                /* ACTIVATION & ITEM CHECKS */\n                if (BlockUtils.canActivateTools(blockState)) {\n                    if (mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getAbilitiesEnabled()) {\n                        if (BlockUtils.canActivateHerbalism(blockState)) {\n                            mcMMOPlayer.processAbilityActivation(PrimarySkillType.HERBALISM);\n                        }\n\n                        mcMMOPlayer.processAbilityActivation(PrimarySkillType.AXES);\n                        mcMMOPlayer.processAbilityActivation(PrimarySkillType.EXCAVATION);\n                        mcMMOPlayer.processAbilityActivation(PrimarySkillType.MINING);\n                        mcMMOPlayer.processAbilityActivation(PrimarySkillType.SWORDS);\n                        mcMMOPlayer.processAbilityActivation(PrimarySkillType.UNARMED);\n                        mcMMOPlayer.processAbilityActivation(PrimarySkillType.WOODCUTTING);\n                    }\n\n                    ChimaeraWing.activationCheck(player);\n                }\n\n                HerbalismManager herbalismManager = mcMMOPlayer.getHerbalismManager();\n\n                // FakePlayerAnimationEvent fakeSwing = new FakePlayerAnimationEvent(event.getPlayer(), PlayerAnimationType.ARM_SWING); //PlayerAnimationEvent compat\n                if (!event.isCancelled() || event.useInteractedBlock() != Event.Result.DENY) {\n                    //TODO: Is this code to set false from bone meal even needed? I'll have to double check later.\n                    if (heldItem.getType() == Material.BONE_MEAL) {\n                        switch (blockState.getType().toString()) {\n                            case \"BEETROOTS\":\n                            case \"CARROT\":\n                            case \"COCOA\":\n                            case \"WHEAT\":\n                            case \"NETHER_WART_BLOCK\":\n                            case \"POTATO\":\n                            case \"MANGROVE_PROPAGULE\":\n                                mcMMO.getUserBlockTracker().setEligible(blockState);\n                                break;\n                        }\n                    }\n\n                    if (herbalismManager.canGreenThumbBlock(blockState)) {\n                        //call event for Green Thumb Block\n                        if (!EventUtils.callSubSkillBlockEvent(player, SubSkillType.HERBALISM_GREEN_THUMB, block).isCancelled()) {\n                            // Bukkit.getPluginManager().callEvent(fakeSwing);\n                            player.getInventory().getItemInMainHand().setAmount(heldItem.getAmount() - 1);\n                            player.updateInventory();\n                            if (herbalismManager.processGreenThumbBlocks(blockState) && EventUtils.simulateBlockBreak(block, player)) {\n                                blockState.update(true);\n                            }\n                        }\n                    }\n                    /* SHROOM THUMB CHECK */\n                    else if (herbalismManager.canUseShroomThumb(blockState)) {\n                        if (!EventUtils.callSubSkillBlockEvent(player, SubSkillType.HERBALISM_SHROOM_THUMB, block).isCancelled()) {\n                            // Bukkit.getPluginManager().callEvent(fakeSwing);\n                            event.setCancelled(true);\n                            if (herbalismManager.processShroomThumb(blockState)\n                                    && EventUtils.simulateBlockBreak(block, player)) {\n                                blockState.update(true);\n                            }\n                        }\n                    } else {\n                        herbalismManager.processBerryBushHarvesting(blockState);\n                    }\n                }\n                break;\n\n            case RIGHT_CLICK_AIR:\n                if (player.getInventory().getItemInOffHand().getType() != Material.AIR && !player.isInsideVehicle() && !player.isSneaking()) {\n                    break;\n                }\n\n                /* ACTIVATION CHECKS */\n                if (mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getAbilitiesEnabled()) {\n                    mcMMOPlayer.processAbilityActivation(PrimarySkillType.AXES);\n                    mcMMOPlayer.processAbilityActivation(PrimarySkillType.EXCAVATION);\n                    mcMMOPlayer.processAbilityActivation(PrimarySkillType.HERBALISM);\n                    mcMMOPlayer.processAbilityActivation(PrimarySkillType.MINING);\n                    mcMMOPlayer.processAbilityActivation(PrimarySkillType.SWORDS);\n
