mcMMOPlayer.processAbilityActivation(PrimarySkillType.UNARMED);\n                    mcMMOPlayer.processAbilityActivation(PrimarySkillType.WOODCUTTING);\n                }\n\n                /* ITEM CHECKS */\n                ChimaeraWing.activationCheck(player);\n\n                /* BLAST MINING CHECK */\n                MiningManager miningManager = mcMMOPlayer.getMiningManager();\n                if (miningManager.canDetonate()) {\n                    miningManager.remoteDetonation();\n                }\n\n                break;\n\n            case LEFT_CLICK_AIR:\n            case LEFT_CLICK_BLOCK:\n\n                if (!player.isSneaking()) {\n                    break;\n                }\n\n                /* CALL OF THE WILD CHECKS */\n                Material type = heldItem.getType();\n                TamingManager tamingManager = mcMMOPlayer.getTamingManager();\n\n                if (type == mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getTamingCOTWMaterial(CallOfTheWildType.WOLF.getConfigEntityTypeEntry())) {\n                    tamingManager.summonWolf();\n                } else if (type == mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getTamingCOTWMaterial(CallOfTheWildType.CAT.getConfigEntityTypeEntry())) {\n                    tamingManager.summonOcelot();\n                } else if (type == mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getTamingCOTWMaterial(CallOfTheWildType.HORSE.getConfigEntityTypeEntry())) {\n                    tamingManager.summonHorse();\n                }\n\n                break;\n\n            default:\n                break;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Handle PlayerChatEvents at high priority.\n     *\n     * @param event The event to watch\n     */\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGH, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onPlayerChat(AsyncPlayerChatEvent event) {\n        Player player = event.getPlayer();\n\n        if ((ExperienceConfig.getInstance().isNPCInteractionPrevented() && Misc.isNPCEntityExcludingVillagers(player)) || !UserManager.hasPlayerDataKey(player)) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer = UserManager.getOfflinePlayer(player);\n\n        if (mcMMOPlayer == null) {\n            LogUtils.debug(mcMMO.p.getLogger(), player.getName() + \"is chatting, but is currently not logged in to the server.\");\n            LogUtils.debug(mcMMO.p.getLogger(), \"Party & Admin chat will not work properly for this player.\");\n            return;\n        }\n\n        if (plugin.getChatManager().isChatChannelEnabled(mcMMOPlayer.getChatChannel())) {\n            if (mcMMOPlayer.getChatChannel() != ChatChannel.NONE) {\n                if (plugin.getChatManager().isMessageAllowed(mcMMOPlayer)) {\n                    //If the message is allowed we cancel this event to avoid double sending messages\n                    plugin.getChatManager().processPlayerMessage(mcMMOPlayer, event.getMessage(), event.isAsynchronous());\n                    event.setCancelled(true);\n                } else {\n                    //Message wasn't allowed, remove the player from their channel\n                    plugin.getChatManager().setOrToggleChatChannel(mcMMOPlayer, mcMMOPlayer.getChatChannel());\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Handle \"ugly\" aliasing /skillname commands, since setAliases doesn't work.\n     *\n     * @param event The event to watch\n     */\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.LOWEST)\n    public void onPlayerCommandPreprocess(PlayerCommandPreprocessEvent event) {\n        if (!mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getLocale().equalsIgnoreCase(\"en_US\")) {\n            String message = event.getMessage();\n            String command = message.substring(1).split(\" \")[0];\n            String lowerCaseCommand = command.toLowerCase(Locale.ENGLISH);\n\n            // Do these ACTUALLY have to be lower case to work properly?\n            for (PrimarySkillType skill : PrimarySkillType.values()) {\n                String skillName = skill.toString().toLowerCase(Locale.ENGLISH);\n                String localizedName = mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().getLocalizedSkillName(skill).toLowerCase(Locale.ENGLISH);\n\n                if (lowerCaseCommand.equals(localizedName)) {\n                    event.setMessage(message.replace(command, skillName));\n                    break;\n                }\n\n                if (lowerCaseCommand.equals(skillName)) {\n                    break;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * When a {@link Player} attempts to place an {@link ItemStack}\n     * into an {@link ItemFrame}, we want to make sure to remove any\n     * Ability buffs from that item.\n     *\n     * @param event The {@link PlayerInteractEntityEvent} to handle\n     */\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGH, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onPlayerInteractEntity(PlayerInteractEntityEvent event) {\n        /*\n         *  We can check for an instance instead of EntityType here, so we are\n         *  ready for the infamous \"Glow Item Frame\" in 1.17 too!\n         */\n        if (event.getRightClicked() instanceof ItemFrame frame) {\n\n            // Check for existing items (ignore rotations)\n            if (frame.getItem().getType() != Material.AIR) {\n                return;\n            }\n\n            // Get the item the Player is about to place\n            ItemStack itemInHand;\n\n            if (event.getHand() == EquipmentSlot.OFF_HAND) {\n                itemInHand = event.getPlayer().getInventory().getItemInOffHand();\n            } else {\n                itemInHand = event.getPlayer().getInventory().getItemInMainHand();\n            }\n\n            // and remove any skill ability buffs!\n            SkillUtils.removeAbilityBuff(itemInHand);\n        }\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler(ignoreCancelled = true, priority = EventPriority.NORMAL)\n    public void onPlayerSwapHandItems(PlayerSwapHandItemsEvent event) {\n        SkillUtils.removeAbilityBuff(event.getMainHandItem());\n        SkillUtils.removeAbilityBuff(event.getOffHandItem());\n    }\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "src/main/java/com/gmail/nossr50/party/PartyManager.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package com.gmail.nossr50.party;\n\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.chat.ChatChannel;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.interactions.NotificationType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.party.ItemShareType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.party.Party;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.party.PartyLeader;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.party.ShareMode;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.player.McMMOPlayer;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.events.party.McMMOPartyAllianceChangeEvent;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.events.party.McMMOPartyChangeEvent;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.events.party.McMMOPartyChangeEvent.EventReason;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.locale.LocaleLoader;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.mcMMO;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.LogUtils;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.Misc;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.Permissions;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.player.NotificationManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.player.UserManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.sounds.SoundManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.sounds.SoundType;\nimport org.bukkit.OfflinePlayer;\nimport org.bukkit.configuration.file.YamlConfiguration;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.Player;\nimport org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;\nimport org.jetbrains.annotations.Nullable;\n\nimport java.io.File;\nimport java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.LinkedHashMap;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.Map.Entry;\nimport java.util.UUID;\nimport java.util.logging.Level;\n\nimport static java.util.Objects.requireNonNull;\n\npublic final class PartyManager {\n    private final @NotNull List<Party> parties;\n    private final @NotNull File partyFile;\n    private final @NotNull mcMMO pluginRef;\n\n    public PartyManager(@NotNull mcMMO pluginRef) {\n        this.pluginRef = pluginRef;\n        final String partiesFilePath = mcMMO.getFlatFileDirectory() + \"parties.yml\";\n        this.partyFile = new File(partiesFilePath);\n        this.parties = new ArrayList<>();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Checks if the player can join a party, parties can have a size limit, although there is a permission to bypass this\n     *\n     * @param player      player who is attempting to join the party\n     * @param targetParty the target party\n     * @return true if party is full and cannot be joined\n     */\n    public boolean isPartyFull(@NotNull Player player, @NotNull Party targetParty) {\n        requireNonNull(player, \"player cannot be null!\");\n        requireNonNull(targetParty, \"targetParty cannot be null!\");\n        return !Permissions.partySizeBypass(player) && pluginRef.getGeneralConfig().getPartyMaxSize() >= 1 && targetParty.getOnlineMembers().size() >= pluginRef.getGeneralConfig().getPartyMaxSize();\n    }\n\n    public boolean areAllies(@NotNull Player firstPlayer, @NotNull Player secondPlayer) {\n        requireNonNull(firstPlayer, \"firstPlayer cannot be null!\");\n        requireNonNull(secondPlayer, \"secondPlayer cannot be null!\");\n\n        //Profile not loaded\n        if (UserManager.getPlayer(firstPlayer) == null) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        //Profile not loaded\n        if (UserManager.getPlayer(secondPlayer) == null) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        Party firstParty = UserManager.getPlayer(firstPlayer).getParty();\n        Party secondParty = UserManager.getPlayer(secondPlayer).getParty();\n\n        if (firstParty == null || secondParty == null || firstParty.getAlly() == null || secondParty.getAlly() == null) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        return firstParty.equals(secondParty.getAlly()) && secondParty.equals(firstParty.getAlly());\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Get the near party members.\n     *\n     * @param mmoPlayer The player to check\n     * @return the near party members\n     */\n    public @NotNull List<Player> getNearMembers(@NotNull McMMOPlayer mmoPlayer) {\n        requireNonNull(mmoPlayer, \"mmoPlayer cannot be null!\");\n        List<Player> nearMembers = new ArrayList<>();\n        Party party = mmoPlayer.getParty();\n\n        if (party != null) {\n            Player player = mmoPlayer.getPlayer();\n            double range = pluginRef.getGeneralConfig().getPartyShareRange();\n\n            for (Player member : party.getOnlineMembers()) {\n                if (!player.equals(member) && member.isValid() && Misc.isNear(player.getLocation(), member.getLocation(), range)) {\n                    nearMembers.add(member);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        return nearMembers;\n    }\n\n    public @NotNull List<Player> getNearVisibleMembers(@NotNull McMMOPlayer mmoPlayer) {\n        requireNonNull(mmoPlayer, \"mmoPlayer cannot be null!\");\n        List<Player> nearMembers = new ArrayList<>();\n        Party party = mmoPlayer.getParty();\n\n        if (party != null) {\n            Player player = mmoPlayer.getPlayer();\n            double range = pluginRef.getGeneralConfig().getPartyShareRange();\n\n            for (Player member : party.getVisibleMembers(player)) {\n                if (!player.equals(member)\n                        && member.isValid()\n                        && Misc.isNear(player.getLocation(), member.getLocation(), range)) {\n                    nearMembers.add(member);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        return nearMembers;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Get a list of all players in this player's party.\n     *\n     * @param player The player to check\n     * @return all the players in the player's party\n     */\n    public @NotNull LinkedHashMap<UUID, String> getAllMembers(@NotNull Player player) {\n        requireNonNull(player, \"player cannot be null!\");\n        Party party = getParty(player);\n\n        return party == null ? new LinkedHashMap<>() : party.getMembers();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Get a list of all online players in this party.\n     *\n     * @param partyName The party to check\n     * @return all online players in this party\n     */\n    public @NotNull List<Player> getOnlineMembers(@NotNull String partyName) {\n        requireNonNull(partyName, \"partyName cannot be null!\");\n        return getOnlineMembers(getParty(partyName));\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Get a list of all online players in this party.\n     *\n     * @param player The player to check\n     * @return all online players in this party\n     */\n    public @NotNull List<Player> getOnlineMembers(@NotNull Player player) {\n        requireNonNull(player, \"player cannot be null!\");\n        return getOnlineMembers(getParty(player));\n    }\n\n    private List<Player> getOnlineMembers(@Nullable Party party) {\n        return party == null ? new ArrayList<>() : party.getOnlineMembers();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Retrieve a party by its name\n     *\n     * @param partyName The party name\n     * @return the existing party, null otherwise\n     */\n    public @Nullable Party getParty(@NotNull String partyName) {\n        requireNonNull(partyName, \"partyName cannot be null!\");\n        for (Party party : parties) {\n            if (party.getName().equalsIgnoreCase(partyName)) {\n                return party;\n            }\n        }\n\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Retrieve a party by a members name\n     *\n     * @param playerName The members name\n     * @return the existing party, null otherwise\n     */\n    @Deprecated\n    public @Nullable Party getPlayerParty(@NotNull String playerName) {\n        requireNonNull(playerName, \"playerName cannot be null!\");\n        for (Party party : parties) {\n            if (party.getMembers().containsValue(playerName)) {\n                return party;\n            }\n        }\n\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Retrieve a party by a members uuid\n     *\n     * @param uuid The members uuid\n     * @return the existing party, null otherwise\n     */\n    public @Nullable Party getPlayerParty(@NotNull String playerName, @NotNull UUID uuid) {\n        requireNonNull(playerName, \"playerName cannot be null!\");\n        requireNonNull(uuid, \"uuid cannot be null!\");\n        for (Party party : parties) {\n            LinkedHashMap<UUID, String> members = party.getMembers();\n            if (members.containsKey(uuid) || members.containsValue(playerName)) {\n\n                // Name changes\n                if (members.get(uuid) == null || !members.get(uuid).equals(playerName)) {\n                    members.put(uuid, playerName);\n                }\n\n                return party;\n            }\n        }\n\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Retrieve a party by member\n     *\n     * @param player The member\n     * @return the existing party, null otherwise\n     */\n    public @Nullable Party getParty(@NotNull Player player) {\n        requireNonNull(player, \"player cannot be null!\");\n        //Profile not loaded\n        if (UserManager.getPlayer(player) == null) {\n            return null;\n        }\n\n        final McMMOPlayer mmoPlayer = UserManager.getPlayer(player);\n        if (mmoPlayer == null)\n            return null;\n\n        return mmoPlayer.getParty();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Get a list of all current parties.\n     *\n     * @return the list of parties.\n     */\n    public @NotNull List<Party> getParties() {\n        return parties;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Remove a player from a party.\n     *\n     * @param player The player to remove\n     * @param party  The party\n     */\n    public void removeFromParty(@NotNull OfflinePlayer player, @NotNull Party party) {\n        requireNonNull(player, \"player cannot be null!\");\n        requireNonNull(party, \"party cannot be null!\");\n\n        LinkedHashMap<UUID, String> members = party.getMembers();\n        String playerName = player.getName();\n\n        members.remove(player.getUniqueId());\n\n        if (player.isOnline()) {\n            party.getOnlineMembers().remove(player.getPlayer());\n        }\n\n        if (members.isEmpty()) {\n            parties.remove(party);\n        } else {\n            // If the leaving player was the party leader, appoint a new leader from the party members\n            if (party.getLeader().getUniqueId().equals(player.getUniqueId())) {\n                setPartyLeader(members.keySet().iterator().next(), party);\n            }\n\n            informPartyMembersQuit(party, playerName);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Remove a player from a party.\n     *\n     * @param mcMMOPlayer The player to remove\n     */\n    public void removeFromParty(@NotNull McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer) {\n        requireNonNull(mcMMOPlayer, \"mcMMOPlayer cannot be null!\");\n        if (mcMMOPlayer.getParty() == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        removeFromParty(mcMMOPlayer.getPlayer(), mcMMOPlayer.getParty());\n        processPartyLeaving(mcMMOPlayer);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Disband a party. Kicks out all members and removes the party.\n     *\n     * @param party The party to remove\n     * @deprecated Use {@link #disbandParty(McMMOPlayer, Party)}\n     */\n    public void disbandParty(@NotNull Party party) {\n        requireNonNull(party, \"party cannot be null!\");\n        disbandParty(null, party);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Disband a party. Kicks out all members and removes the party.\n     *\n     * @param mcMMOPlayer The player to remove (can be null? lol)\n     * @param party The party to remove\n     */\n    public void disbandParty(@Nullable McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer, @NotNull Party party) {\n        requireNonNull(party, \"party cannot be null!\");\n        //TODO: Potential issues with unloaded profile?\n        for (final Player member : party.getOnlineMembers()) {\n            //Profile not loaded\n            if (UserManager.getPlayer(member) == null) {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            processPartyLeaving(UserManager.getPlayer(member));\n        }\n\n        // Disband the alliance between the disbanded party and it's ally\n        if (party.getAlly() != null) {\n            party.getAlly().setAlly(null);\n        }\n\n        parties.remove(party);\n        if (mcMMOPlayer != null) {\n            handlePartyChangeEvent(mcMMOPlayer.getPlayer(), party.getName(), null, EventReason.DISBANDED_PARTY);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Create a new party\n     *\n     * @param mcMMOPlayer The player to add to the party\n     * @param partyName   The party to add the player to\n     * @param password    The password for this party, null if there was no password\n     */\n    public void createParty(@NotNull McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer, @NotNull String partyName, @Nullable String password) {\n        Player player = mcMMOPlayer.getPlayer();\n\n        Party party = new Party(new PartyLeader(player.getUniqueId(), player.getName()), partyName.replace(\".\", \"\"), password);\n\n        if (password != null) {\n            player.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Party.Password.Set\", password));\n        }\n\n        parties.add(party);\n\n        player.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Commands.Party.Create\", party.getName()));\n        addToParty(mcMMOPlayer, party);\n        handlePartyChangeEvent(player, null, partyName, EventReason.CREATED_PARTY);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Check if a player can join a party\n     *\n     * @param player   The player trying to join a party\n     * @param party    The party\n     * @param password The password provided by the player\n     * @return true if the player can join the party\n     */\n    public boolean checkPartyPassword(@NotNull Player player, @NotNull Party party, @Nullable String password) {\n        if (party.isLocked()) {\n            String partyPassword = party.getPassword();\n\n            if (partyPassword == null) {\n                player.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Party.Locked\"));\n                return false;\n            }\n\n            if (password == null) {\n                player.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Party.Password.None\"));\n                return false;\n            }\n\n            if (!password.equals(partyPassword)) {\n                player.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Party.Password.Incorrect\"));\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Accept a party invitation\n     *\n     * @param mcMMOPlayer The player to add to the party\n     */\n    public void joinInvitedParty(@NotNull McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer) {\n        requireNonNull(mcMMOPlayer, \"mcMMOPlayer cannot be null!\");\n        Party invite = mcMMOPlayer.getPartyInvite();\n\n        // Check if the party still exists, it might have been disbanded\n        if (!parties.contains(invite)) {\n            NotificationManager.sendPlayerInformation(mcMMOPlayer.getPlayer(), NotificationType.PARTY_MESSAGE, \"Party.Disband\");\n            return;\n        }\n\n        /*\n         * Don't let players join a full party\n         */\n        if (pluginRef.getGeneralConfig().getPartyMaxSize() > 0 && invite.getMembers().size() >= pluginRef.getGeneralConfig().getPartyMaxSize()) {\n            NotificationManager.sendPlayerInformation(mcMMOPlayer.getPlayer(), NotificationType.PARTY_MESSAGE, \"Commands.Party.PartyFull.InviteAccept\", invite.getName(), String.valueOf(pluginRef.getGeneralConfig().getPartyMaxSize()));\n            return;\n        }\n\n        NotificationManager.sendPlayerInformation(mcMMOPlayer.getPlayer(), NotificationType.PARTY_MESSAGE, \"Commands.Party.Invite.Accepted\", invite.getName());\n        mcMMOPlayer.removePartyInvite();\n        addToParty(mcMMOPlayer, invite);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Accept a party alliance invitation\n     *\n     * @param mcMMOPlayer The player who accepts the alliance invite\n     */\n    public void acceptAllianceInvite(@NotNull McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer) {\n        requireNonNull(mcMMOPlayer, \"mcMMOPlayer cannot be null!\");\n        Party invite = mcMMOPlayer.getPartyAllianceInvite();\n        Player player = mcMMOPlayer.getPlayer();\n\n        // Check if the party still exists, it might have been disbanded\n        if (!parties.contains(invite)) {\n            player.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Party.Disband\"));\n            return;\n        }\n\n        if (!handlePartyChangeAllianceEvent(player, mcMMOPlayer.getParty().getName(), invite.getName(), McMMOPartyAllianceChangeEvent.EventReason.FORMED_ALLIANCE)) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        player.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Commands.Party.Alliance.Invite.Accepted\", invite.getName()));\n        mcMMOPlayer.removePartyAllianceInvite();\n\n        createAlliance(mcMMOPlayer.getParty(), invite);\n    }\n\n    public void createAlliance(@NotNull Party firstParty, @NotNull Party secondParty) {\n        requireNonNull(firstParty, \"firstParty cannot be null!\");\n        requireNonNull(secondParty, \"secondParty cannot be null!\");\n\n        firstParty.setAlly(secondParty);\n        secondParty.setAlly(firstParty);\n\n        for (Player member : firstParty.getOnlineMembers()) {\n            member.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Party.Alliance.Formed\", secondParty.getName()));\n        }\n\n        for (Player member : secondParty.getOnlineMembers()) {\n            member.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Party.Alliance.Formed\", firstParty.getName()));\n        }\n    }\n\n    public boolean disbandAlliance(@NotNull Player player, @NotNull Party firstParty, @NotNull Party secondParty) {\n        requireNonNull(player, \"player cannot be null!\");\n        requireNonNull(firstParty, \"firstParty cannot be null!\");\n        requireNonNull(secondParty, \"secondParty cannot be null!\");\n\n        if (!handlePartyChangeAllianceEvent(player, firstParty.getName(), secondParty.getName(), McMMOPartyAllianceChangeEvent.EventReason.DISBAND_ALLIANCE)) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        disbandAlliance(firstParty, secondParty);\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    private void disbandAlliance(@NotNull Party firstParty, @NotNull Party secondParty) {\n        requireNonNull(firstParty, \"firstParty cannot be null!\");\n        requireNonNull(secondParty, \"secondParty cannot be null!\");\n        firstParty.setAlly(null);\n        secondParty.setAlly(null);\n\n        for (Player member : firstParty.getOnlineMembers()) {\n            member.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Party.Alliance.Disband\", secondParty.getName()));\n        }\n\n        for (Player member : secondParty.getOnlineMembers()) {\n            member.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Party.Alliance.Disband\", firstParty.getName()));\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Add a player to a party\n     *\n     * @param mcMMOPlayer The player to add to the party\n     * @param party       The party\n     */\n    public void addToParty(@NotNull McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer, @NotNull Party party) {\n        requireNonNull(mcMMOPlayer, \"mcMMOPlayer cannot be null!\");\n        requireNonNull(party, \"party cannot be null!\");\n\n        Player player = mcMMOPlayer.getPlayer();\n        String playerName = player.getName();\n\n        informPartyMembersJoin(party, playerName);\n        mcMMOPlayer.setParty(party);\n        party.getMembers().put(player.getUniqueId(), player.getName());\n        party.getOnlineMembers().add(player);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Get the leader of a party.\n     *\n     * @param partyName The party name\n     * @return the leader of the party\n     */\n    public @Nullable String getPartyLeaderName(@NotNull String partyName) {\n        requireNonNull(partyName, \"partyName cannot be null!\");\n        Party party = getParty(partyName);\n\n        return party == null ? null : party.getLeader().getPlayerName();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Set the leader of a party.\n     *\n     * @param uuid  The uuid of the player to set as leader\n     * @param party The party\n     */\n    public void setPartyLeader(@NotNull UUID uuid, @NotNull Party party) {\n        requireNonNull(uuid, \"uuid cannot be null!\");\n        requireNonNull(party, \"party cannot be null!\");\n        OfflinePlayer player = pluginRef.getServer().getOfflinePlayer(uuid);\n        UUID leaderUniqueId = party.getLeader().getUniqueId();\n\n        for (Player member : party.getOnlineMembers()) {\n            UUID memberUniqueId = member.getUniqueId();\n\n            if (memberUniqueId.equals(player.getUniqueId())) {\n                member.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Party.Owner.Player\"));\n            } else if (memberUniqueId.equals(leaderUniqueId)) {\n                member.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Party.Owner.NotLeader\"));\n            } else {\n                member.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Party.Owner.New\", player.getName()));\n            }\n        }\n\n        party.setLeader(new PartyLeader(player.getUniqueId(), player.getName()));\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Check if a player can invite others to his party.\n     *\n     * @return true if the player can invite\n     */\n    public boolean canInvite(@NotNull McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer) {\n        requireNonNull(mcMMOPlayer, \"mcMMOPlayer cannot be null!\");\n        Party party = mcMMOPlayer.getParty();\n\n        return !party.isLocked() || party.getLeader().getUniqueId().equals(mcMMOPlayer.getPlayer().getUniqueId());\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Check if a party with a given name already exists.\n     *\n     * @param player    The player to notify\n     * @param partyName The name of the party to check\n     * @return true if a party with that name exists, false otherwise\n     */\n    public boolean checkPartyExistence(@NotNull Player player, @NotNull String partyName) {\n        requireNonNull(player, \"player cannot be null!\");\n        requireNonNull(partyName, \"partyName cannot be null!\");\n\n        if (getParty(partyName) == null) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        player.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Commands.Party.AlreadyExists\", partyName));\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Attempt to change parties or join a new party.\n     *\n     * @param mmoPlayer    The player changing or joining parties\n     * @param newPartyName The name of the party being joined\n     * @return true if the party was joined successfully, false otherwise\n     */\n    public boolean changeOrJoinParty(@NotNull McMMOPlayer mmoPlayer, @NotNull String newPartyName) {\n        requireNonNull(mmoPlayer, \"mmoPlayer cannot be null!\");\n        requireNonNull(newPartyName, \"newPartyName cannot be null!\");\n\n        final Player player = mmoPlayer.getPlayer();\n\n        if (mmoPlayer.inParty()) {\n            final Party oldParty = mmoPlayer.getParty();\n\n            if (!handlePartyChangeEvent(player, oldParty.getName(), newPartyName, EventReason.CHANGED_PARTIES)) {\n                return false;\n            }\n\n            removeFromParty(mmoPlayer);\n        } else return handlePartyChangeEvent(player, null, newPartyName, EventReason.JOINED_PARTY);\n\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Check if two online players are in the same party.\n     *\n     * @param firstPlayer  The first player\n     * @param secondPlayer The second player\n     * @return true if they are in the same party, false otherwise\n     */\n    public boolean inSameParty(@NotNull Player firstPlayer, @NotNull Player secondPlayer) {\n        requireNonNull(firstPlayer, \"firstPlayer cannot be null!\");\n        requireNonNull(secondPlayer, \"secondPlayer cannot be null!\");\n\n        //Profile not loaded\n        if (UserManager.getPlayer(firstPlayer) == null) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        //Profile not loaded\n        if (UserManager.getPlayer(secondPlayer) == null) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        Party firstParty = UserManager.getPlayer(firstPlayer).getParty();\n        Party secondParty = UserManager.getPlayer(secondPlayer).getParty();\n\n        if (firstParty == null || secondParty == null) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        return firstParty.equals(secondParty);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Load party file.\n     */\n    public void loadParties() {\n        if (!pluginRef.getPartyConfig().isPartyEnabled() || !partyFile.exists()) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        try {\n            YamlConfiguration partiesFile;\n            partiesFile = YamlConfiguration.loadConfiguration(partyFile);\n\n            ArrayList<Party> hasAlly = new ArrayList<>();\n\n            for (String partyName : partiesFile.getConfigurationSection(\"\").getKeys(false)) {\n                try {\n                    Party party = new Party(partyName);\n\n                    String[] leaderSplit = partiesFile.getString(partyName + \".Leader\").split(\"[|]\");\n                    party.setLeader(new PartyLeader(UUID.fromString(leaderSplit[0]), leaderSplit[1]));\n                    party.setPassword(partiesFile.getString(partyName + \".Password\"));\n                    party.setLocked(partiesFile.getBoolean(partyName + \".Locked\"));\n                    party.setLevel(partiesFile.getInt(partyName + \".Level\"));\n                    party.setXp(partiesFile.getInt(partyName + \".Xp\"));\n\n                    if (partiesFile.getString(partyName + \".Ally\") != null) {\n                        hasAlly.add(party);\n                    }\n\n                    party.setXpShareMode(ShareMode.getShareMode(partiesFile.getString(partyName + \".ExpShareMode\", \"NONE\")));\n                    party.setItemShareMode(ShareMode.getShareMode(partiesFile.getString(partyName + \".ItemShareMode\", \"NONE\")));\n\n                    for (ItemShareType itemShareType : ItemShareType.values()) {\n                        party.setSharingDrops(itemShareType, partiesFile.getBoolean(partyName + \".ItemShareType.\" + itemShareType.toString(), true));\n                    }\n\n                    LinkedHashMap<UUID, String> members = party.getMembers();\n\n                    for (String memberEntry : partiesFile.getStringList(partyName + \".Members\")) {\n                        String[] memberSplit = memberEntry.split(\"[|]\");\n                        members.put(UUID.fromString(memberSplit[0]), memberSplit[1]);\n                    }\n\n                    parties.add(party);\n                } catch (Exception e) {\n                    pluginRef.getLogger().log(Level.WARNING, \"An exception occurred while loading a party with name '\" + partyName + \"'. Skipped loading party.\", e);\n                }\n            }\n\n            LogUtils.debug(pluginRef.getLogger(), \"Loaded (\" + parties.size() + \") Parties...\");\n\n            for (Party party : hasAlly) {\n                party.setAlly(getParty(partiesFile.getString(party.getName() + \".Ally\")));\n            }\n\n        } catch (Exception e) {\n            e.printStackTrace();\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Save party file.\n     */\n    public void saveParties() {\n        LogUtils.debug(pluginRef.getLogger(), \"[Party Data] Saving...\");\n\n        if (partyFile.exists()) {\n            if (!partyFile.delete()) {\n                pluginRef.getLogger().warning(\"Could not delete party file. Party saving failed!\");\n                return;\n            }\n        }\n\n        YamlConfiguration partiesFile = new YamlConfiguration();\n\n        for (Party party : parties) {\n            String partyName = party.getName();\n            PartyLeader leader = party.getLeader();\n\n            partiesFile.set(partyName + \".Leader\", leader.getUniqueId().toString() + \"|\" + leader.getPlayerName());\n            partiesFile.set(partyName + \".Password\", party.getPassword());\n            partiesFile.set(partyName + \".Locked\", party.isLocked());\n            partiesFile.set(partyName + \".Level\", party.getLevel());\n            partiesFile.set(partyName + \".Xp\", (int) party.getXp());\n            partiesFile.set(partyName + \".Ally\", (party.getAlly() != null) ? party.getAlly().getName() : \"\");\n            partiesFile.set(partyName + \".ExpShareMode\", party.getXpShareMode().toString());\n            partiesFile.set(partyName + \".ItemShareMode\", party.getItemShareMode().toString());\n\n            for (ItemShareType itemShareType : ItemShareType.values()) {\n                partiesFile.set(partyName + \".ItemShareType.\" + itemShareType.toString(), party.sharingDrops(itemShareType));\n            }\n\n            List<String> members = new ArrayList<>();\n\n            for (Entry<UUID, String> memberEntry : party.getMembers().entrySet()) {\n                String memberUniqueId = memberEntry.getKey() == null ? \"\" : memberEntry.getKey().toString();\n                String memberName = memberEntry.getValue();\n\n                if (!members.contains(memberName)) {\n                    members.add(memberUniqueId + \"|\" + memberName);\n                }\n            }\n\n            partiesFile.set(partyName + \".Members\", members);\n        }\n\n        try {\n            partiesFile.save(partyFile);\n        } catch (Exception e) {\n            e.printStackTrace();\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Handle party change event.\n     *\n     * @param player       The player changing parties\n     * @param oldPartyName The name of the old party\n     * @param newPartyName The name of the new party\n     * @param reason       The reason for changing parties\n     * @return true if the change event was successful, false otherwise\n     */\n    public boolean handlePartyChangeEvent(Player player, String oldPartyName, String newPartyName, EventReason reason) {\n        McMMOPartyChangeEvent event = new McMMOPartyChangeEvent(player, oldPartyName, newPartyName, reason);\n        pluginRef.getServer().getPluginManager().callEvent(event);\n\n        return !event.isCancelled();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Handle party alliance change event.\n     *\n     * @param player      The player changing party alliances\n     * @param oldAllyName The name of the old ally\n     * @param newAllyName The name of the new ally\n     * @param reason      The reason for changing allies\n     * @return true if the change event was successful, false otherwise\n     */\n    public boolean handlePartyChangeAllianceEvent(Player player, String oldAllyName, String newAllyName, McMMOPartyAllianceChangeEvent.EventReason reason) {\n        McMMOPartyAllianceChangeEvent event = new McMMOPartyAllianceChangeEvent(player, oldAllyName, newAllyName, reason);\n        pluginRef.getServer().getPluginManager().callEvent(event);\n\n        return !event.isCancelled();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Remove party data from the mcMMOPlayer.\n     *\n     * @param mcMMOPlayer The player to remove party data from.\n     */\n    public void processPartyLeaving(@NotNull McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer) {\n        mcMMOPlayer.removeParty();\n        mcMMOPlayer.setChatMode(ChatChannel.NONE);\n        mcMMOPlayer.setItemShareModifier(10);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Notify party members when the party levels up.\n     *\n     * @param party        The concerned party\n     * @param levelsGained The amount of levels gained\n     * @param level        The current party level\n     */\n    public void informPartyMembersLevelUp(Party party, int levelsGained, int level) {\n        boolean levelUpSoundsEnabled = pluginRef.getGeneralConfig().getLevelUpSoundsEnabled();\n        for (Player member : party.getOnlineMembers()) {\n            member.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Party.LevelUp\", levelsGained, level));\n\n            if (levelUpSoundsEnabled) {\n                SoundManager.sendSound(member, member.getLocation(), SoundType.LEVEL_UP);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Notify party members when a player joins.\n     *\n     * @param party      The concerned party\n     * @param playerName The name of the player that joined\n     */\n    private void informPartyMembersJoin(Party party, String playerName) {\n        for (Player member : party.getOnlineMembers()) {\n            member.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Party.InformedOnJoin\", playerName));\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Notify party members when a party member quits.\n     *\n     * @param party      The concerned party\n     * @param playerName The name of the player that left\n     */\n    private void informPartyMembersQuit(Party party, String playerName) {\n        for (Player member : party.getOnlineMembers()) {\n            member.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Party.InformedOnQuit\", playerName));\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "src/main/java/com/gmail/nossr50/party/ShareHandler.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package com.gmail.nossr50.party;\n\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.config.party.ItemWeightConfig;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.experience.XPGainReason;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.experience.XPGainSource;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.party.ItemShareType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.party.Party;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.party.ShareMode;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.player.McMMOPlayer;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.PrimarySkillType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.mcMMO;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.Misc;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.player.UserManager;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.Item;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.Player;\nimport org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack;\n\nimport java.util.List;\n\npublic final class ShareHandler {\n    private ShareHandler() {}\n\n    /**\n     * Distribute Xp amongst party members.\n     *\n     * @param xp Xp without party sharing\n     * @param mcMMOPlayer Player initiating the Xp gain\n     * @param primarySkillType Skill being used\n     * @return True is the xp has been shared\n     */\n    public static boolean handleXpShare(float xp, McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer, PrimarySkillType primarySkillType, XPGainReason xpGainReason) {\n        Party party = mcMMOPlayer.getParty();\n\n        if (party.getXpShareMode() != ShareMode.EQUAL) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        List<Player> nearMembers = mcMMO.p.getPartyManager().getNearVisibleMembers(mcMMOPlayer);\n\n        if (nearMembers.isEmpty()) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        nearMembers.add(mcMMOPlayer.getPlayer());\n\n        int partySize = nearMembers.size();\n        double shareBonus = Math.min(mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getPartyShareBonusBase()\n                + (partySize * mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getPartyShareBonusIncrease()),\n                mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getPartyShareBonusCap());\n        float splitXp = (float) (xp / partySize * shareBonus);\n\n        for (Player member : nearMembers) {\n            //Profile not loaded\n            if (UserManager.getPlayer(member) == null) {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            UserManager.getPlayer(member).beginUnsharedXpGain(primarySkillType, splitXp, xpGainReason, XPGainSource.PARTY_MEMBERS);\n        }\n\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Distribute Items amongst party members.\n     *\n     * @param drop Item that will get shared\n     * @param mcMMOPlayer Player who picked up the item\n     * @return True if the item has been shared\n     */\n    public static boolean handleItemShare(Item drop, McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer) {\n        ItemStack itemStack = drop.getItemStack();\n        ItemShareType dropType = ItemShareType.getShareType(itemStack);\n\n        if (dropType == null) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        Party party = mcMMOPlayer.getParty();\n\n        if (!party.sharingDrops(dropType)) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        ShareMode shareMode = party.getItemShareMode();\n\n        if (shareMode == ShareMode.NONE) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        List<Player> nearMembers = mcMMO.p.getPartyManager().getNearMembers(mcMMOPlayer);\n\n        if (nearMembers.isEmpty()) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        Player winningPlayer = null;\n        ItemStack newStack = itemStack.clone();\n\n        nearMembers.add(mcMMOPlayer.getPlayer());\n        int partySize = nearMembers.size();\n\n        drop.remove();\n        newStack.setAmount(1);\n\n        switch (shareMode) {\n            case EQUAL:\n                int itemWeight = ItemWeightConfig.getInstance().getItemWeight(itemStack.getType());\n\n                for (int i = 0; i < itemStack.getAmount(); i++) {\n                    int highestRoll = 0;\n\n                    for (Player member : nearMembers) {\n                        McMMOPlayer mcMMOMember = UserManager.getPlayer(member);\n\n                        //Profile not loaded\n                        if (UserManager.getPlayer(member) == null) {\n                            continue;\n                        }\n\n                        int itemShareModifier = mcMMOMember.getItemShareModifier();\n                        int diceRoll = Misc.getRandom().nextInt(itemShareModifier);\n\n                        if (diceRoll <= highestRoll) {\n                            mcMMOMember.setItemShareModifier(itemShareModifier + itemWeight);\n                            continue;\n                        }\n\n                        highestRoll = diceRoll;\n\n                        if (winningPlayer != null) {\n                            McMMOPlayer mcMMOWinning = UserManager.getPlayer(winningPlayer);\n                            mcMMOWinning.setItemShareModifier(mcMMOWinning.getItemShareModifier() + itemWeight);\n                        }\n\n                        winningPlayer = member;\n                    }\n\n                    McMMOPlayer mcMMOTarget = UserManager.getPlayer(winningPlayer);\n                    mcMMOTarget.setItemShareModifier(mcMMOTarget.getItemShareModifier() - itemWeight);\n                    awardDrop(winningPlayer, newStack);\n                }\n\n                return true;\n\n            case RANDOM:\n                for (int i = 0; i < itemStack.getAmount(); i++) {\n                    winningPlayer = nearMembers.get(Misc.getRandom().nextInt(partySize));\n                    awardDrop(winningPlayer, newStack);\n                }\n\n                return true;\n\n            default:\n                return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    public static XPGainReason getSharedXpGainReason(XPGainReason xpGainReason) {\n        if (xpGainReason == XPGainReason.PVE) {\n            return XPGainReason.SHARED_PVE;\n        } else if (xpGainReason == XPGainReason.PVP) {\n            return XPGainReason.SHARED_PVP;\n        } else {\n            return xpGainReason;\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static void awardDrop(Player winningPlayer, ItemStack drop) {\n        if (winningPlayer.getInventory().addItem(drop).size() != 0) {\n            winningPlayer.getWorld().dropItem(winningPlayer.getLocation(), drop);\n        }\n\n        winningPlayer.updateInventory();\n    }\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "src/main/java/com/gmail/nossr50/runnables/player/PlayerProfileLoadingTask.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package com.gmail.nossr50.runnables.player;\n\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.player.McMMOPlayer;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.player.PlayerProfile;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.locale.LocaleLoader;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.mcMMO;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.runnables.commands.McScoreboardKeepTask;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.CancellableRunnable;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.EventUtils;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.LogUtils;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.Misc;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.player.UserManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.scoreboards.ScoreboardManager;\nimport org.bukkit.Server;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.Player;\n\npublic class PlayerProfileLoadingTask extends CancellableRunnable {\n    private final Player player;\n    private int attempt = 0;\n\n    public PlayerProfileLoadingTask(Player player) {\n        this.player = player;\n    }\n\n    private PlayerProfileLoadingTask(Player player, int attempt) {\n        this.player = player;\n        this.attempt = attempt;\n    }\n\n    // WARNING: ASYNC TASK\n    // DO NOT MODIFY THE McMMOPLAYER FROM THIS CODE\n    @Override\n    public void run() {\n\n        if (Misc.isNPCIncludingVillagers(player)) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        // Quit if they logged out\n        if (!player.isOnline()) {\n            LogUtils.debug(mcMMO.p.getLogger(), \"Aborting profile loading recovery for \" + player.getName() + \" - player logged out\");\n            return;\n        }\n\n        PlayerProfile profile = mcMMO.getDatabaseManager().loadPlayerProfile(player);\n\n        if (!profile.isLoaded()) {\n            LogUtils.debug(mcMMO.p.getLogger(), \"Creating new data for player: \"+player.getName());\n            //Profile isn't loaded so add as new user\n            profile = mcMMO.getDatabaseManager().newUser(player);\n        }\n\n        // If successful, schedule the apply\n        if (profile.isLoaded()) {\n            mcMMO.p.getFoliaLib().getImpl().runAtEntity(player, new ApplySuccessfulProfile(new McMMOPlayer(player, profile)));\n            EventUtils.callPlayerProfileLoadEvent(player, profile);\n            return;\n        }\n\n        // Print errors to console/logs if we're failing at least 2 times in a row to load the profile\n        if (attempt >= 3) {\n            //Log the error\n            mcMMO.p.getLogger().severe(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Profile.Loading.FailureNotice\",\n                    player.getName(), String.valueOf(attempt)));\n\n            //Notify the admins\n            mcMMO.p.getServer().broadcast(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Profile.Loading.FailureNotice\", player.getName()), Server.BROADCAST_CHANNEL_ADMINISTRATIVE);\n\n            //Notify the player\n            player.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Profile.Loading.FailurePlayer\", String.valueOf(attempt)).split(\"\\n\"));\n        }\n\n        // Increment attempt counter and try\n        attempt++;\n\n        mcMMO.p.getFoliaLib().getImpl().runLaterAsync(new PlayerProfileLoadingTask(player, attempt), (100 + (attempt * 100L)));\n    }\n\n    private class ApplySuccessfulProfile extends CancellableRunnable {\n        private final McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer;\n\n        private ApplySuccessfulProfile(McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer) {\n            this.mcMMOPlayer = mcMMOPlayer;\n        }\n\n        // Synchronized task\n        // No database access permitted\n        @Override\n        public void run() {\n            if (!player.isOnline()) {\n                mcMMO.p.getLogger().info(\"Aborting profile loading recovery for \" + player.getName() + \" - player logged out\");\n                return;\n            }\n\n            mcMMOPlayer.getProfile().updateLastLogin();\n\n            mcMMOPlayer.setupPartyData();\n            UserManager.track(mcMMOPlayer);\n            mcMMOPlayer.actualizeRespawnATS();\n\n            if (mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getScoreboardsEnabled()) {\n                ScoreboardManager.setupPlayer(player);\n\n                if (mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getShowStatsAfterLogin()) {\n                    ScoreboardManager.enablePlayerStatsScoreboard(player);\n                    mcMMO.p.getFoliaLib().getImpl().runAtEntityLater(player, new McScoreboardKeepTask(player), Misc.TICK_CONVERSION_FACTOR);\n                }\n            }\n\n            if (mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getShowProfileLoadedMessage()) {\n                player.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Profile.Loading.Success\"));\n            }\n\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "src/main/java/com/gmail/nossr50/runnables/skills/AbilityDisableTask.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package com.gmail.nossr50.runnables.skills;\n\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.interactions.NotificationType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.player.McMMOPlayer;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.SuperAbilityType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.mcMMO;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.CancellableRunnable;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.EventUtils;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.Misc;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.player.NotificationManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.skills.PerksUtils;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.skills.SkillUtils;\nimport org.bukkit.Chunk;\nimport org.bukkit.World;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.Player;\n\npublic class AbilityDisableTask extends CancellableRunnable {\n    private final McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer;\n    private final SuperAbilityType ability;\n\n    public AbilityDisableTask(McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer, SuperAbilityType ability) {\n        this.mcMMOPlayer = mcMMOPlayer;\n        this.ability = ability;\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public void run() {\n        if (!mcMMOPlayer.getAbilityMode(ability)) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        Player player = mcMMOPlayer.getPlayer();\n\n        switch (ability) {\n            case SUPER_BREAKER:\n            case GIGA_DRILL_BREAKER:\n                SkillUtils.removeAbilityBoostsFromInventory(player);\n                // Fallthrough\n\n            case BERSERK:\n                if (mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getRefreshChunksEnabled()) {\n                    resendChunkRadiusAt(player);\n                }\n                // Fallthrough\n\n            default:\n                break;\n        }\n\n        EventUtils.callAbilityDeactivateEvent(player, ability);\n\n        mcMMOPlayer.setAbilityMode(ability, false);\n        mcMMOPlayer.setAbilityInformed(ability, false);\n\n//        ParticleEffectUtils.playAbilityDisabledEffect(player);\n\n        if (mcMMOPlayer.useChatNotifications()) {\n            //player.sendMessage(ability.getAbilityOff());\n            NotificationManager.sendPlayerInformation(player, NotificationType.ABILITY_OFF, ability.getAbilityOff());\n        }\n\n        if (mcMMO.p.getAdvancedConfig().sendAbilityNotificationToOtherPlayers()) {\n            SkillUtils.sendSkillMessage(player, NotificationType.SUPER_ABILITY_ALERT_OTHERS, ability.getAbilityPlayerOff());\n        }\n        if (!mcMMO.isServerShutdownExecuted()) {\n            mcMMO.p.getFoliaLib().getImpl().runAtEntityLater(player, new AbilityCooldownTask(mcMMOPlayer, ability), (long) PerksUtils.handleCooldownPerks(player, ability.getCooldown()) * Misc.TICK_CONVERSION_FACTOR);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void resendChunkRadiusAt(Player player) {\n        Chunk chunk = player.getLocation().getChunk();\n        World world = player.getWorld();\n\n        int chunkX = chunk.getX();\n        int chunkZ = chunk.getZ();\n\n        int radius = 1;\n\n        for (int x = chunkX - radius; x <= chunkX + radius; x++) {\n            for (int z = chunkZ - radius; z <= chunkZ + radius; z++) {\n                world.refreshChunk(x, z);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "src/main/java/com/gmail/nossr50/runnables/skills/AlchemyBrewTask.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package com.gmail.nossr50.runnables.skills;\n\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.player.McMMOPlayer;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.PrimarySkillType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.SubSkillType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.events.skills.alchemy.McMMOPlayerBrewEvent;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.events.skills.alchemy.McMMOPlayerCatalysisEvent;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.mcMMO;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.skills.alchemy.Alchemy;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.skills.alchemy.AlchemyPotionBrewer;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.CancellableRunnable;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.ContainerMetadataUtils;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.Misc;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.Permissions;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.player.UserManager;\nimport org.bukkit.Material;\nimport org.bukkit.OfflinePlayer;\nimport org.bukkit.block.BlockState;\nimport org.bukkit.block.BrewingStand;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.Player;\nimport org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;\n\npublic class AlchemyBrewTask extends CancellableRunnable {\n    private static final double DEFAULT_BREW_SPEED = 1.0;\n    private static final int    DEFAULT_BREW_TICKS = 400;\n\n    private final BlockState brewingStand;\n    private final OfflinePlayer offlinePlayer;\n    private McMMOPlayer mmoPlayer;\n    private double brewSpeed;\n    private double brewTimer;\n    private int fuel;\n    private boolean firstRun = true;\n    private int ingredientLevel = 1;\n\n    @Deprecated(forRemoval = true, since = \"2.2.010\")\n    public AlchemyBrewTask(@NotNull BlockState brewingStand, Player ignored) {\n        this(brewingStand);\n    }\n\n    public AlchemyBrewTask(@NotNull BlockState brewingStand) {\n        offlinePlayer = ContainerMetadataUtils.getContainerOwner(brewingStand);\n        McMMOPlayer mmoPlayer = null;\n        if (offlinePlayer != null && offlinePlayer.isOnline()) {\n            mmoPlayer = UserManager.getPlayer(offlinePlayer.getPlayer());\n        }\n\n        this.brewingStand = brewingStand;\n\n        brewSpeed = DEFAULT_BREW_SPEED;\n        brewTimer = DEFAULT_BREW_TICKS;\n\n        if (mmoPlayer != null\n                && !Misc.isNPCEntityExcludingVillagers(mmoPlayer.getPlayer())\n                && Permissions.isSubSkillEnabled(mmoPlayer.getPlayer(), SubSkillType.ALCHEMY_CATALYSIS)) {\n            ingredientLevel = mmoPlayer.getAlchemyManager().getTier();\n\n            double catalysis = mmoPlayer.getAlchemyManager().calculateBrewSpeed(Permissions.lucky(mmoPlayer.getPlayer(),\n                    PrimarySkillType.ALCHEMY));\n\n            McMMOPlayerCatalysisEvent event = new McMMOPlayerCatalysisEvent(mmoPlayer, catalysis);\n            mcMMO.p.getServer().getPluginManager().callEvent(event);\n\n            if (!event.isCancelled()) {\n                brewSpeed = catalysis;\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (Alchemy.brewingStandMap.containsKey(brewingStand.getLocation())) {\n            Alchemy.brewingStandMap.get(brewingStand.getLocation()).cancel();\n        }\n\n        fuel = ((BrewingStand) brewingStand).getFuelLevel();\n\n        if (((BrewingStand) brewingStand).getBrewingTime() == -1) // Only decrement on our end if it isn't a vanilla ingredient.\n            fuel--;\n\n        Alchemy.brewingStandMap.put(brewingStand.getLocation(), this);\n        mcMMO.p.getFoliaLib().getImpl().runAtLocationTimer(brewingStand.getLocation(), this, 1, 1);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public void run() {\n        // Check if preconditions for brewing are not met\n        if (shouldCancelBrewing()) {\n            if (Alchemy.brewingStandMap.containsKey(brewingStand.getLocation())) {\n                Alchemy.brewingStandMap.remove(brewingStand.getLocation());\n            }\n            this.cancel();\n            return;\n        }\n\n        // Initialize the brewing stand on the first run\n        initializeBrewing();\n\n        // Update the brewing process timer\n        brewTimer -= brewSpeed;\n\n        // Check if the brewing process should finish\n        if (isBrewingComplete()) {\n            this.cancel();\n            finish();\n        } else {\n            updateBrewingTime();\n        }\n    }\n\n    private boolean shouldCancelBrewing() {\n        if (offlinePlayer == null) {\n            return true;\n        }\n        if (brewingStand == null) {\n            return true;\n        }\n        if (brewingStand.getType() != Material.BREWING_STAND) {\n            return true;\n        }\n        return !AlchemyPotionBrewer.isValidIngredientByLevel(\n                getIngredientLevelUpdated(), ((BrewingStand) brewingStand).getInventory().getContents()[Alchemy.INGREDIENT_SLOT]);\n    }\n\n    private int getIngredientLevelUpdated() {\n        if (mmoPlayer != null) {\n            ingredientLevel = mmoPlayer.getAlchemyManager().getTier();\n            return ingredientLevel;\n        } else if (offlinePlayer.isOnline() && mmoPlayer == null) {\n            final McMMOPlayer fetchedMMOPlayer = UserManager.getPlayer(offlinePlayer.getPlayer());\n            if (fetchedMMOPlayer != null) {\n                this.mmoPlayer = fetchedMMOPlayer;\n                ingredientLevel = mmoPlayer.getAlchemyManager().getTier();\n            }\n            return ingredientLevel;\n        } else {\n            return ingredientLevel;\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void initializeBrewing() {\n        if (firstRun) {\n            firstRun = false;\n            ((BrewingStand) brewingStand).setFuelLevel(fuel);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private boolean isBrewingComplete() {\n        return brewTimer < Math.max(brewSpeed, 2);\n    }\n\n    private void updateBrewingTime() {\n        ((BrewingStand) brewingStand).setBrewingTime((int) brewTimer);\n    }\n\n\n    private void finish() {\n        final McMMOPlayerBrewEvent event = new McMMOPlayerBrewEvent(mmoPlayer, brewingStand);\n        mcMMO.p.getServer().getPluginManager().callEvent(event);\n\n        if (!event.isCancelled()) {\n            AlchemyPotionBrewer.finishBrewing(brewingStand, mmoPlayer.getPlayer(), false);\n        }\n\n        Alchemy.brewingStandMap.remove(brewingStand.getLocation());\n    }\n\n    public void finishImmediately() {\n        this.cancel();\n\n        AlchemyPotionBrewer.finishBrewing(brewingStand, mmoPlayer.getPlayer(), true);\n        Alchemy.brewingStandMap.remove(brewingStand.getLocation());\n    }\n\n    public void cancelBrew() {\n        this.cancel();\n\n        ((BrewingStand) brewingStand).setBrewingTime(-1);\n        Alchemy.brewingStandMap.remove(brewingStand.getLocation());\n    }\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "src/main/java/com/gmail/nossr50/skills/acrobatics/AcrobaticsManager.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package com.gmail.nossr50.skills.acrobatics;\n\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.config.experience.ExperienceConfig;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.BlockLocationHistory;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.experience.XPGainReason;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.interactions.NotificationType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.player.McMMOPlayer;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.PrimarySkillType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.SubSkillType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.mcMMO;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.runnables.MobDodgeMetaCleanup;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.skills.SkillManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.MetadataConstants;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.Misc;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.Permissions;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.player.NotificationManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.player.UserManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.random.ProbabilityUtil;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.skills.ParticleEffectUtils;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.skills.RankUtils;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.skills.SkillUtils;\nimport org.bukkit.Location;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.Entity;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.LightningStrike;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.Mob;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.Player;\nimport org.bukkit.metadata.FixedMetadataValue;\nimport org.bukkit.metadata.MetadataValue;\n\npublic class AcrobaticsManager extends SkillManager {\n\n    public AcrobaticsManager(McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer) {\n        super(mcMMOPlayer, PrimarySkillType.ACROBATICS);\n        fallLocationMap = new BlockLocationHistory(50);\n    }\n\n    private long rollXPCooldown = 0;\n    private final long rollXPInterval = (1000 * 3); //1 Minute\n    private long rollXPIntervalLengthen = (1000 * 10); //10 Seconds\n    private final BlockLocationHistory fallLocationMap;\n\n    public boolean hasFallenInLocationBefore(Location location) {\n        return fallLocationMap.contains(location);\n    }\n\n    public void addLocationToFallMap(Location location) {\n        fallLocationMap.add(location);\n    }\n\n    public boolean canGainRollXP() {\n        if (!ExperienceConfig.getInstance().isAcrobaticsExploitingPrevented())\n            return true;\n\n        if (System.currentTimeMillis() >= rollXPCooldown) {\n            rollXPCooldown = System.currentTimeMillis() + rollXPInterval;\n            rollXPIntervalLengthen = (1000 * 10); //5 Seconds\n            return true;\n        } else {\n            rollXPCooldown += rollXPIntervalLengthen;\n            rollXPIntervalLengthen += 1000; //Add another second to the next penalty\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    public boolean canDodge(Entity damager) {\n        if (getPlayer().isBlocking())\n            return false;\n\n        if (!RankUtils.hasUnlockedSubskill(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.ACROBATICS_DODGE))\n            return false;\n\n        if (Permissions.isSubSkillEnabled(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.ACROBATICS_DODGE)) {\n            if (damager instanceof LightningStrike && Acrobatics.dodgeLightningDisabled) {\n                return false;\n            }\n\n            return mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().canCombatSkillsTrigger(skill, damager);\n        }\n\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Handle the damage reduction and XP gain from the Dodge ability\n     *\n     * @param damage The amount of damage initially dealt by the event\n     * @return the modified event damage if the ability was successful, the original event damage otherwise\n     */\n    public double dodgeCheck(Entity attacker, double damage) {\n        double modifiedDamage = Acrobatics.calculateModifiedDodgeDamage(damage, Acrobatics.dodgeDamageModifier);\n        Player player = getPlayer();\n\n        if (!isFatal(modifiedDamage)\n                && ProbabilityUtil.isSkillRNGSuccessful(SubSkillType.ACROBATICS_DODGE, UserManager.getPlayer(player))) {\n            ParticleEffectUtils.playDodgeEffect(player);\n\n            if (mmoPlayer.useChatNotifications()) {\n                NotificationManager.sendPlayerInformation(player, NotificationType.SUBSKILL_MESSAGE, \"Acrobatics.Combat.Proc\");\n            }\n\n            if (SkillUtils.cooldownExpired(mmoPlayer.getRespawnATS(), Misc.PLAYER_RESPAWN_COOLDOWN_SECONDS)) {\n                if (attacker instanceof Mob mob) {\n                    //Check to see how many dodge XP rewards this mob has handed out\n                    if (mob.hasMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_DODGE_TRACKER) && ExperienceConfig.getInstance().isAcrobaticsExploitingPrevented()) {\n                        //If Dodge XP has been handed out 5 times then consider it being exploited\n                        MetadataValue metadataValue = mob.getMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_DODGE_TRACKER).get(0);\n                        int count = metadataValue.asInt();\n\n                        if (count <= 5) {\n                            applyXpGain((float) (damage * Acrobatics.dodgeXpModifier), XPGainReason.PVE);\n                            mob.setMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_DODGE_TRACKER, new FixedMetadataValue(mcMMO.p, count + 1));\n                            MobDodgeMetaCleanup metaCleanupTask = new MobDodgeMetaCleanup(mob, mcMMO.p);\n                            mcMMO.p.getFoliaLib().getImpl().runAtEntityTimer(mob, metaCleanupTask, 20, 20*60); //one minute\n                        }\n                    } else {\n                        applyXpGain((float) (damage * Acrobatics.dodgeXpModifier), XPGainReason.PVE);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n\n            //Check respawn to prevent abuse\n            return modifiedDamage;\n        }\n\n        return damage;\n    }\n\n    private boolean isFatal(double damage) {\n        return getPlayer().getHealth() - damage <= 0;\n    }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "src/main/java/com/gmail/nossr50/skills/archery/ArcheryManager.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package com.gmail.nossr50.skills.archery;\n\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.interactions.NotificationType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.player.McMMOPlayer;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.PrimarySkillType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.SubSkillType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.mcMMO;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.skills.SkillManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.MetadataConstants;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.Misc;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.Permissions;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.player.NotificationManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.random.ProbabilityUtil;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.skills.RankUtils;\nimport org.bukkit.Location;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.Entity;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.LivingEntity;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.Player;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.Projectile;\nimport org.bukkit.potion.PotionEffect;\n\nimport static com.gmail.nossr50.util.PotionEffectUtil.getNauseaPotionEffectType;\n\npublic class ArcheryManager extends SkillManager {\n    public ArcheryManager(McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer) {\n        super(mcMMOPlayer, PrimarySkillType.ARCHERY);\n    }\n\n    public boolean canDaze(LivingEntity target) {\n        if (!RankUtils.hasUnlockedSubskill(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.ARCHERY_DAZE))\n            return false;\n\n        return target instanceof Player && Permissions.isSubSkillEnabled(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.ARCHERY_DAZE);\n    }\n\n    public boolean canSkillShot() {\n        if (!RankUtils.hasUnlockedSubskill(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.ARCHERY_SKILL_SHOT))\n            return false;\n\n        return Permissions.isSubSkillEnabled(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.ARCHERY_SKILL_SHOT);\n    }\n\n    public boolean canRetrieveArrows() {\n        if (!RankUtils.hasUnlockedSubskill(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.ARCHERY_ARROW_RETRIEVAL))\n            return false;\n\n        return Permissions.isSubSkillEnabled(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.ARCHERY_ARROW_RETRIEVAL);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Calculate bonus XP awarded for Archery when hitting a far-away target.\n     *\n     * @param target The {@link LivingEntity} damaged by the arrow\n     * @param arrow The {@link Entity} who shot the arrow\n     */\n    public static double distanceXpBonusMultiplier(LivingEntity target, Entity arrow) {\n        //Hacky Fix - some plugins spawn arrows and assign them to players after the ProjectileLaunchEvent fires\n        if (!arrow.hasMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_ARROW_DISTANCE))\n            return 1;\n\n        Location firedLocation = (Location) arrow.getMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_ARROW_DISTANCE).get(0).value();\n        Location targetLocation = target.getLocation();\n\n        if (firedLocation == null || firedLocation.getWorld() == null)\n            return 1;\n\n        if (firedLocation.getWorld() != targetLocation.getWorld()) {\n            return 1;\n        }\n\n        return 1 + Math.min(firedLocation.distance(targetLocation), 50) * Archery.DISTANCE_XP_MULTIPLIER;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Track arrows fired for later retrieval.\n     *\n     * @param target The {@link LivingEntity} damaged by the arrow\n     */\n    public void retrieveArrows(LivingEntity target, Projectile projectile) {\n        if (projectile.hasMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_TRACKED_ARROW)) {\n            Archery.incrementTrackerValue(target);\n            projectile.removeMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_TRACKED_ARROW, mcMMO.p); //Only 1 entity per projectile\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Handle the effects of the Daze ability\n     *\n     * @param defender The {@link Player} being affected by the ability\n     */\n    public double daze(Player defender) {\n        if (!ProbabilityUtil.isSkillRNGSuccessful(SubSkillType.ARCHERY_DAZE, mmoPlayer)) {\n            return 0;\n        }\n\n        Location dazedLocation = defender.getLocation();\n        dazedLocation.setPitch(90 - Misc.getRandom().nextInt(181));\n\n        mcMMO.p.getFoliaLib().getImpl().teleportAsync(defender, dazedLocation);\n        defender.addPotionEffect(new PotionEffect(getNauseaPotionEffectType(), 20 * 10, 10));\n\n        if (NotificationManager.doesPlayerUseNotifications(defender)) {\n            NotificationManager.sendPlayerInformation(defender, NotificationType.SUBSKILL_MESSAGE, \"Combat.TouchedFuzzy\");\n        }\n\n        if (mmoPlayer.useChatNotifications()) {\n            NotificationManager.sendPlayerInformation(getPlayer(), NotificationType.SUBSKILL_MESSAGE, \"Combat.TargetDazed\");\n        }\n\n        return Archery.dazeBonusDamage;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Calculates the damage to deal after Skill Shot has been applied\n     *\n     * @param oldDamage The raw damage value of this arrow before we modify it\n     */\n    public double skillShot(double oldDamage) {\n        if (ProbabilityUtil.isNonRNGSkillActivationSuccessful(SubSkillType.ARCHERY_SKILL_SHOT, mmoPlayer)) {\n            return Archery.getSkillShotBonusDamage(getPlayer(), oldDamage);\n        } else {\n            return oldDamage;\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "src/main/java/com/gmail/nossr50/skills/excavation/ExcavationManager.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package com.gmail.nossr50.skills.excavation;\n\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.api.ItemSpawnReason;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.experience.XPGainReason;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.experience.XPGainSource;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.player.McMMOPlayer;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.PrimarySkillType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.SubSkillType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.treasure.ExcavationTreasure;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.mcMMO;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.skills.SkillManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.Misc;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.Permissions;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.random.ProbabilityUtil;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.skills.RankUtils;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.skills.SkillUtils;\nimport org.bukkit.Location;\nimport org.bukkit.block.BlockState;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.Player;\nimport org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;\nimport org.jetbrains.annotations.VisibleForTesting;\n\nimport java.util.List;\n\nimport static java.util.Objects.requireNonNull;\n\npublic class ExcavationManager extends SkillManager {\n    public ExcavationManager(McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer) {\n        super(mcMMOPlayer, PrimarySkillType.EXCAVATION);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Process treasure drops & XP gain for Excavation.\n     *\n     * @param blockState The {@link BlockState} to check ability activation for\n     */\n    public void excavationBlockCheck(BlockState blockState) {\n        int xp = Excavation.getBlockXP(blockState);\n        requireNonNull(blockState, \"excavationBlockCheck: blockState cannot be null\");\n        if (Permissions.isSubSkillEnabled(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.EXCAVATION_ARCHAEOLOGY)) {\n            List<ExcavationTreasure> treasures = getTreasures(blockState);\n\n            if (!treasures.isEmpty()) {\n                int skillLevel = getSkillLevel();\n                Location location = Misc.getBlockCenter(blockState);\n\n                for (ExcavationTreasure treasure : treasures) {\n                    if (skillLevel >= treasure.getDropLevel()\n                            && ProbabilityUtil.isStaticSkillRNGSuccessful(\n                                    PrimarySkillType.EXCAVATION, mmoPlayer, treasure.getDropProbability())) {\n                        processExcavationBonusesOnBlock(blockState, treasure, location);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        applyXpGain(xp, XPGainReason.PVE, XPGainSource.SELF);\n    }\n\n    @VisibleForTesting\n    public List<ExcavationTreasure> getTreasures(@NotNull BlockState blockState) {\n        requireNonNull(blockState, \"blockState cannot be null\");\n        return Excavation.getTreasures(blockState);\n    }\n\n    @VisibleForTesting\n    public void processExcavationBonusesOnBlock(BlockState blockState, ExcavationTreasure treasure, Location location) {\n        //Spawn Vanilla XP orbs if a dice roll succeeds\n        if (ProbabilityUtil.isStaticSkillRNGSuccessful(\n                PrimarySkillType.EXCAVATION, mmoPlayer, getArchaelogyExperienceOrbChance())) {\n            Misc.spawnExperienceOrb(location, getExperienceOrbsReward());\n        }\n\n        int xp = 0;\n        xp += treasure.getXp();\n        Misc.spawnItem(getPlayer(), location, treasure.getDrop(), ItemSpawnReason.EXCAVATION_TREASURE);\n        if (xp > 0) {\n            applyXpGain(xp, XPGainReason.PVE, XPGainSource.SELF);\n        }\n    }\n\n    public int getExperienceOrbsReward() {\n        return getArchaeologyRank();\n    }\n\n    public double getArchaelogyExperienceOrbChance() {\n        return getArchaeologyRank() * 2;\n    }\n\n    public int getArchaeologyRank() {\n        return RankUtils.getRank(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.EXCAVATION_ARCHAEOLOGY);\n    }\n\n    public void printExcavationDebug(Player player, BlockState blockState) {\n        if (Permissions.isSubSkillEnabled(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.EXCAVATION_ARCHAEOLOGY)) {\n            List<ExcavationTreasure> treasures = Excavation.getTreasures(blockState);\n\n            if (!treasures.isEmpty()) {\n                for (ExcavationTreasure treasure : treasures) {\n                    player.sendMessage(\"|||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||\");\n                    player.sendMessage(\"[mcMMO DEBUG] Treasure found: (\"+treasure.getDrop().getType().toString()+\")\");\n                    player.sendMessage(\"[mcMMO DEBUG] Drop Chance for Treasure: \"+treasure.getDropChance());\n                    player.sendMessage(\"[mcMMO DEBUG] Skill Level Required: \"+treasure.getDropLevel());\n                    player.sendMessage(\"[mcMMO DEBUG] XP for Treasure: \"+treasure.getXp());\n                }\n            } else {\n                player.sendMessage(\"[mcMMO DEBUG] No treasures found for this block.\");\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Process the Giga Drill Breaker ability.\n     *\n     * @param blockState The {@link BlockState} to check ability activation for\n     */\n    public void gigaDrillBreaker(BlockState blockState) {\n        excavationBlockCheck(blockState);\n        excavationBlockCheck(blockState);\n\n        SkillUtils.handleDurabilityChange(getPlayer().getInventory().getItemInMainHand(), mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getAbilityToolDamage());\n    }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "src/main/java/com/gmail/nossr50/skills/fishing/Fishing.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package com.gmail.nossr50.skills.fishing;\n\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.config.treasure.FishingTreasureConfig;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.treasure.ShakeTreasure;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.Misc;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.adapter.BiomeAdapter;\nimport org.bukkit.Material;\nimport org.bukkit.block.Biome;\nimport org.bukkit.enchantments.Enchantment;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.LivingEntity;\nimport org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack;\n\nimport java.util.HashMap;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.Set;\n\npublic final class Fishing {\n\n    protected static final HashMap<Material, List<Enchantment>> ENCHANTABLE_CACHE = new HashMap<>();\n\n    public static Set<Biome> masterAnglerBiomes = BiomeAdapter.WATER_BIOMES;\n    public static Set<Biome> iceFishingBiomes   = BiomeAdapter.ICE_BIOMES;\n\n    private Fishing() {}\n\n    /**\n     * Finds the possible drops of an entity\n     *\n     * @param target\n     *            Targeted entity\n     * @return possibleDrops List of ItemStack that can be dropped\n     */\n    protected static List<ShakeTreasure> findPossibleDrops(LivingEntity target) {\n        if (FishingTreasureConfig.getInstance().shakeMap.containsKey(target.getType()))\n            return FishingTreasureConfig.getInstance().shakeMap.get(target.getType());\n\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Randomly chooses a drop among the list\n     *\n     * @param possibleDrops\n     *            List of ItemStack that can be dropped\n     * @return Chosen ItemStack\n     */\n    protected static ItemStack chooseDrop(List<ShakeTreasure> possibleDrops) {\n        int dropProbability = Misc.getRandom().nextInt(100);\n        double cumulatedProbability = 0;\n\n        for (ShakeTreasure treasure : possibleDrops) {\n            cumulatedProbability += treasure.getDropChance();\n\n            if (dropProbability < cumulatedProbability) {\n                return treasure.getDrop().clone();\n            }\n        }\n\n        return null;\n    }\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "src/main/java/com/gmail/nossr50/skills/fishing/FishingManager.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package com.gmail.nossr50.skills.fishing;\n\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.api.ItemSpawnReason;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.config.experience.ExperienceConfig;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.config.treasure.FishingTreasureConfig;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.experience.XPGainReason;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.interactions.NotificationType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.player.McMMOPlayer;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.PrimarySkillType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.SubSkillType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.treasure.*;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.events.skills.fishing.McMMOPlayerFishingTreasureEvent;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.events.skills.fishing.McMMOPlayerShakeEvent;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.locale.LocaleLoader;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.mcMMO;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.runnables.skills.MasterAnglerTask;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.skills.SkillManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.*;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.compat.layers.skills.MasterAnglerCompatibilityLayer;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.player.NotificationManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.random.ProbabilityUtil;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.skills.CombatUtils;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.skills.RankUtils;\nimport
