com.gmail.nossr50.util.skills.SkillUtils;\nimport org.bukkit.ChatColor;\nimport org.bukkit.Location;\nimport org.bukkit.Material;\nimport org.bukkit.block.Block;\nimport org.bukkit.block.BlockFace;\nimport org.bukkit.enchantments.Enchantment;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.*;\nimport org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityDamageEvent;\nimport org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack;\nimport org.bukkit.inventory.PlayerInventory;\nimport org.bukkit.inventory.meta.SkullMeta;\nimport org.bukkit.util.BoundingBox;\nimport org.bukkit.util.Vector;\nimport org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;\nimport org.jetbrains.annotations.Nullable;\n\nimport java.util.*;\n\npublic class FishingManager extends SkillManager {\n    public static final int FISHING_ROD_CAST_CD_MILLISECONDS = 100;\n    private final long FISHING_COOLDOWN_SECONDS = 1000L;\n\n    private long fishingRodCastTimestamp = 0L;\n    private long fishHookSpawnTimestamp = 0L;\n    private long lastWarned = 0L;\n    private long lastWarnedExhaust = 0L;\n    private FishHook fishHookReference;\n    private BoundingBox lastFishingBoundingBox;\n    private boolean sameTarget;\n    private Item fishingCatch;\n    private Location hookLocation;\n    private int fishCaughtCounter = 1;\n\n    public FishingManager(McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer) {\n        super(mcMMOPlayer, PrimarySkillType.FISHING);\n    }\n\n    public boolean canShake(Entity target) {\n        return target instanceof LivingEntity && RankUtils.hasUnlockedSubskill(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.FISHING_SHAKE)\n                && Permissions.isSubSkillEnabled(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.FISHING_SHAKE);\n    }\n\n    public boolean canMasterAngler() {\n        return mcMMO.getCompatibilityManager().getMasterAnglerCompatibilityLayer() != null\n                && getSkillLevel() >= RankUtils.getUnlockLevel(SubSkillType.FISHING_MASTER_ANGLER)\n                && Permissions.isSubSkillEnabled(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.FISHING_MASTER_ANGLER);\n    }\n\n//    public void setFishingRodCastTimestamp()\n//    {\n//        long currentTime = System.currentTimeMillis();\n//        //Only track spam casting if the fishing hook is fresh\n//        if (currentTime > fishHookSpawnTimestamp + 1000)\n//            return;\n//\n//        if (currentTime < fishingRodCastTimestamp + FISHING_ROD_CAST_CD_MILLISECONDS)\n//        {\n//            ItemStack fishingRod = getPlayer().getInventory().getItemInMainHand();\n//\n//            //Ensure correct hand item is damaged\n//            if (fishingRod.getType() != Material.FISHING_ROD) {\n//                fishingRod = getPlayer().getInventory().getItemInOffHand();\n//            }\n//\n//            getPlayer().setFoodLevel(Math.max(getPlayer().getFoodLevel() - 1, 0));\n//            fishingRod.setDurability((short) (fishingRod.getDurability() + 5));\n//            getPlayer().updateInventory();\n//\n//            if (lastWarnedExhaust + (1000) < currentTime)\n//            {\n//                getPlayer().sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Fishing.Exhausting\"));\n//                lastWarnedExhaust = currentTime;\n//                SoundManager.sendSound(getPlayer(), getPlayer().getLocation(), SoundType.TIRED);\n//            }\n//        }\n//\n//        fishingRodCastTimestamp = System.currentTimeMillis();\n//    }\n\n    public void setFishHookReference(FishHook fishHook) {\n        if (fishHook.getMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_FISH_HOOK_REF).size() > 0)\n            return;\n\n        fishHook.setMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_FISH_HOOK_REF, MetadataConstants.MCMMO_METADATA_VALUE);\n        this.fishHookReference = fishHook;\n        fishHookSpawnTimestamp = System.currentTimeMillis();\n        fishingRodCastTimestamp = System.currentTimeMillis();\n\n    }\n\n    public boolean isFishingTooOften() {\n        long currentTime = System.currentTimeMillis();\n        long fishHookSpawnCD = fishHookSpawnTimestamp + 1000;\n        boolean hasFished = (currentTime < fishHookSpawnCD);\n\n        if (hasFished && (lastWarned + (1000) < currentTime)) {\n            getPlayer().sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Fishing.Scared\"));\n            lastWarned = System.currentTimeMillis();\n        }\n\n        return hasFished;\n    }\n\n    public void processExploiting(Vector centerOfCastVector) {\n        BoundingBox newCastBoundingBox = makeBoundingBox(centerOfCastVector);\n        this.sameTarget = lastFishingBoundingBox != null && lastFishingBoundingBox.overlaps(newCastBoundingBox);\n\n        if (this.sameTarget) {\n            fishCaughtCounter++;\n        } else {\n            fishCaughtCounter = 1;\n        }\n\n        //If the new bounding box does not intersect with the old one, then update our bounding box reference\n        if (!this.sameTarget) lastFishingBoundingBox = newCastBoundingBox;\n\n        if (fishCaughtCounter + 1 == ExperienceConfig.getInstance().getFishingExploitingOptionOverFishLimit()) {\n            getPlayer().sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Fishing.LowResourcesTip\", ExperienceConfig.getInstance().getFishingExploitingOptionMoveRange()));\n        }\n    }\n\n    public boolean isExploitingFishing(Vector centerOfCastVector) {\n\n        /*Block targetBlock = getPlayer().getTargetBlock(BlockUtils.getTransparentBlocks(), 100);\n\n        if (!targetBlock.isLiquid()) {\n            return false;\n        }*/\n\n        return this.sameTarget && fishCaughtCounter >= ExperienceConfig.getInstance().getFishingExploitingOptionOverFishLimit();\n    }\n\n    public static BoundingBox makeBoundingBox(Vector centerOfCastVector) {\n        int exploitingRange = ExperienceConfig.getInstance().getFishingExploitingOptionMoveRange();\n        return BoundingBox.of(centerOfCastVector, exploitingRange / 2, 1, exploitingRange / 2);\n    }\n\n    public void setFishingTarget() {\n        getPlayer().getTargetBlock(BlockUtils.getTransparentBlocks(), 100);\n    }\n\n    public boolean canIceFish(Block block) {\n        if (getSkillLevel() < RankUtils.getUnlockLevel(SubSkillType.FISHING_ICE_FISHING)) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        if (block.getType() != Material.ICE) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        // Make sure this is a body of water, not just a block of ice.\n        if (!Fishing.iceFishingBiomes.contains(block.getBiome()) && (block.getRelative(BlockFace.DOWN, 3).getType() != Material.WATER)) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        Player player = getPlayer();\n\n        if (!Permissions.isSubSkillEnabled(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.FISHING_ICE_FISHING)) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        return EventUtils.simulateBlockBreak(block, player);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Gets the loot tier\n     *\n     * @return the loot tier\n     */\n    public int getLootTier() {\n        return RankUtils.getRank(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.FISHING_TREASURE_HUNTER);\n    }\n\n    public double getShakeChance() {\n        return mcMMO.p.getAdvancedConfig().getShakeChance(RankUtils.getRank(mmoPlayer.getPlayer(), SubSkillType.FISHING_SHAKE));\n    }\n\n    protected int getVanillaXPBoostModifier() {\n        return mcMMO.p.getAdvancedConfig().getFishingVanillaXPModifier(getLootTier());\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Gets the Shake Mob probability\n     *\n     * @return Shake Mob probability\n     */\n    public double getShakeProbability() {\n        return getShakeChance();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Handle the Fisherman's Diet ability\n     *\n     * @param eventFoodLevel The initial change in hunger from the event\n     *\n     * @return the modified change in hunger for the event\n     */\n    public int handleFishermanDiet(int eventFoodLevel) {\n        return SkillUtils.handleFoodSkills(getPlayer(), eventFoodLevel, SubSkillType.FISHING_FISHERMANS_DIET);\n    }\n\n    public void iceFishing(FishHook hook, Block block) {\n        // Make a hole\n        block.setType(Material.WATER);\n\n        for (int x = -1; x <= 1; x++) {\n            for (int z = -1; z <= 1; z++) {\n                Block relative = block.getRelative(x, 0, z);\n\n                if (relative.getType() == Material.ICE) {\n                    relative.setType(Material.WATER);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Recast in the new spot\n        EventUtils.callFakeFishEvent(getPlayer(), hook);\n    }\n\n    public void masterAngler(@NotNull FishHook hook, int lureLevel) {\n        mcMMO.p.getFoliaLib().getImpl().runAtEntityLater(hook, new MasterAnglerTask(hook, this, lureLevel), 1); //We run later to get the lure bonus applied\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Processes master angler\n     * Reduced tick time on fish hook, etc\n     * @param fishHook target fish hook\n     */\n    public void processMasterAngler(@NotNull FishHook fishHook, int lureLevel) {\n        MasterAnglerCompatibilityLayer masterAnglerCompatibilityLayer = (MasterAnglerCompatibilityLayer) mcMMO.getCompatibilityManager().getMasterAnglerCompatibilityLayer();\n\n        if (masterAnglerCompatibilityLayer != null) {\n            int maxWaitTicks = masterAnglerCompatibilityLayer.getMaxWaitTime(fishHook);\n            int minWaitTicks = masterAnglerCompatibilityLayer.getMinWaitTime(fishHook);\n\n            int masterAnglerRank = RankUtils.getRank(mmoPlayer, SubSkillType.FISHING_MASTER_ANGLER);\n            int convertedLureBonus = 0;\n\n            //This avoids a Minecraft bug where lure levels above 3 break fishing\n            if (lureLevel > 0) {\n                masterAnglerCompatibilityLayer.setApplyLure(fishHook, false);\n                convertedLureBonus = lureLevel * 100;\n            }\n\n            boolean boatBonus = isInBoat();\n            int minWaitReduction = getMasterAnglerTickMinWaitReduction(masterAnglerRank, boatBonus);\n            int maxWaitReduction = getMasterAnglerTickMaxWaitReduction(masterAnglerRank, boatBonus, convertedLureBonus);\n\n            //Ticks for minWait and maxWait never go below this value\n            int bonusCapMin = mcMMO.p.getAdvancedConfig().getFishingReductionMinWaitCap();\n            int bonusCapMax = mcMMO.p.getAdvancedConfig().getFishingReductionMaxWaitCap();\n\n            int reducedMinWaitTime = getReducedTicks(minWaitTicks, minWaitReduction, bonusCapMin);\n            int reducedMaxWaitTime = getReducedTicks(maxWaitTicks, maxWaitReduction, bonusCapMax);\n\n            boolean badValuesFix = false;\n\n            //If we find bad values correct it\n            if (reducedMaxWaitTime < reducedMinWaitTime) {\n                reducedMaxWaitTime = reducedMinWaitTime + 100;\n                badValuesFix = true;\n            }\n\n            if (mmoPlayer.isDebugMode()) {\n                mmoPlayer.getPlayer().sendMessage(ChatColor.GOLD + \"Master Angler Debug\");\n\n                if (badValuesFix) {\n                    mmoPlayer.getPlayer().sendMessage(ChatColor.RED + \"Bad values were applied and corrected, check your configs, max wait should never be lower than min wait.\");\n                }\n\n                mmoPlayer.getPlayer().sendMessage(\"ALLOW STACK WITH LURE: \" + masterAnglerCompatibilityLayer.getApplyLure(fishHook));\n                mmoPlayer.getPlayer().sendMessage(\"MIN TICK REDUCTION: \" + minWaitReduction);\n                mmoPlayer.getPlayer().sendMessage(\"MAX TICK REDUCTION: \" + maxWaitReduction);\n                mmoPlayer.getPlayer().sendMessage(\"BOAT BONUS: \" + boatBonus);\n\n                if (boatBonus) {\n                    mmoPlayer.getPlayer().sendMessage(\"BOAT MAX TICK REDUCTION: \" + maxWaitReduction);\n                    mmoPlayer.getPlayer().sendMessage(\"BOAT MIN TICK REDUCTION: \" + maxWaitReduction);\n                }\n\n                mmoPlayer.getPlayer().sendMessage(\"\");\n\n                mmoPlayer.getPlayer().sendMessage(ChatColor.DARK_AQUA + \"BEFORE MASTER ANGLER WAS APPLIED\");\n                mmoPlayer.getPlayer().sendMessage(\"Original Max Wait Ticks: \" + maxWaitTicks);\n                mmoPlayer.getPlayer().sendMessage(\"Original Min Wait Ticks: \" + minWaitTicks);\n                mmoPlayer.getPlayer().sendMessage(\"\");\n\n                mmoPlayer.getPlayer().sendMessage(ChatColor.DARK_AQUA + \"AFTER MASTER ANGLER WAS APPLIED\");\n                mmoPlayer.getPlayer().sendMessage(\"Current Max Wait Ticks: \" + reducedMaxWaitTime);\n                mmoPlayer.getPlayer().sendMessage(\"Current Min Wait Ticks: \" + reducedMinWaitTime);\n\n                mmoPlayer.getPlayer().sendMessage(\"\");\n\n                mmoPlayer.getPlayer().sendMessage(ChatColor.DARK_AQUA + \"Caps / Limits (edit in advanced.yml)\");\n                mmoPlayer.getPlayer().sendMessage(\"Lowest possible max wait ticks \" + bonusCapMax);\n                mmoPlayer.getPlayer().sendMessage(\"Lowest possible min wait ticks \" + bonusCapMin);\n            }\n\n            masterAnglerCompatibilityLayer.setMaxWaitTime(fishHook, reducedMaxWaitTime);\n            masterAnglerCompatibilityLayer.setMinWaitTime(fishHook, reducedMinWaitTime);\n        }\n\n    }\n\n    public int getReducedTicks(int ticks, int totalBonus, int tickBounds) {\n        return Math.max(tickBounds, ticks - totalBonus);\n    }\n\n    public boolean isInBoat() {\n        return mmoPlayer.getPlayer().isInsideVehicle() && mmoPlayer.getPlayer().getVehicle() instanceof Boat;\n    }\n\n    public int getMasterAnglerTickMaxWaitReduction(int masterAnglerRank, boolean boatBonus, int emulatedLureBonus) {\n        int totalBonus = mcMMO.p.getAdvancedConfig().getFishingReductionMaxWaitTicks() * masterAnglerRank;\n\n        if (boatBonus) {\n            totalBonus += getFishingBoatMaxWaitReduction();\n        }\n\n        totalBonus += emulatedLureBonus;\n\n        return totalBonus;\n    }\n\n    public int getMasterAnglerTickMinWaitReduction(int masterAnglerRank, boolean boatBonus) {\n        int totalBonus = mcMMO.p.getAdvancedConfig().getFishingReductionMinWaitTicks() * masterAnglerRank;\n\n        if (boatBonus) {\n            totalBonus += getFishingBoatMinWaitReduction();\n        }\n\n        return totalBonus;\n    }\n\n    public int getFishingBoatMinWaitReduction() {\n        return mcMMO.p.getAdvancedConfig().getFishingBoatReductionMinWaitTicks();\n    }\n\n    public int getFishingBoatMaxWaitReduction() {\n        return mcMMO.p.getAdvancedConfig().getFishingBoatReductionMaxWaitTicks();\n    }\n\n    public boolean isMagicHunterEnabled() {\n        return RankUtils.hasUnlockedSubskill(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.FISHING_MAGIC_HUNTER)\n                && RankUtils.hasUnlockedSubskill(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.FISHING_TREASURE_HUNTER)\n                && Permissions.isSubSkillEnabled(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.FISHING_TREASURE_HUNTER);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Process the results from a successful fishing trip\n     *\n     * @param fishingCatch The {@link Item} initially caught\n     */\n    public void processFishing(@NotNull Item fishingCatch) {\n        this.fishingCatch = fishingCatch;\n        int fishXp = ExperienceConfig.getInstance().getXp(PrimarySkillType.FISHING, fishingCatch.getItemStack().getType());\n        int treasureXp = 0;\n        ItemStack treasureDrop = null;\n        Player player = getPlayer();\n        FishingTreasure treasure = null;\n        boolean fishingSucceeds = false;\n\n        if (mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getFishingDropsEnabled() && Permissions.isSubSkillEnabled(player, SubSkillType.FISHING_TREASURE_HUNTER)) {\n            treasure = getFishingTreasure();\n            this.fishingCatch = null;\n        }\n\n        if (treasure != null) {\n            if (treasure instanceof FishingTreasureBook) {\n                treasureDrop = ItemUtils.createEnchantBook((FishingTreasureBook) treasure);\n            } else {\n                treasureDrop = treasure.getDrop().clone(); // Not cloning is bad, m'kay?\n\n            }\n            Map<Enchantment, Integer> enchants = new HashMap<>();\n            McMMOPlayerFishingTreasureEvent event;\n\n            /*\n             * Books get some special treatment\n             */\n            if (treasure instanceof FishingTreasureBook) {\n                //Skip the magic hunter stuff\n                if (treasureDrop.getItemMeta() != null) {\n                    enchants.putAll(treasureDrop.getItemMeta().getEnchants());\n                }\n\n                event = EventUtils.callFishingTreasureEvent(mmoPlayer, treasureDrop, treasure.getXp(), enchants);\n            } else {\n                if (isMagicHunterEnabled() && ItemUtils.isEnchantable(treasureDrop)) {\n                    enchants = processMagicHunter(treasureDrop);\n                }\n\n                event = EventUtils.callFishingTreasureEvent(mmoPlayer, treasureDrop, treasure.getXp(), enchants);\n            }\n\n            if (!event.isCancelled()) {\n                treasureDrop = event.getTreasure();\n                treasureXp = event.getXp();\n\n                // Drop the original catch at the feet of the player and set the treasure as the real catch\n                if (treasureDrop != null) {\n                    fishingSucceeds = true;\n                    boolean enchanted = false;\n\n                    if (treasure instanceof FishingTreasureBook) {\n                        enchanted = true;\n                    } else if (!enchants.isEmpty()) {\n                        treasureDrop.addUnsafeEnchantments(enchants);\n                        enchanted = true;\n                    }\n\n                    if (enchanted) {\n                        NotificationManager.sendPlayerInformation(player, NotificationType.SUBSKILL_MESSAGE, \"Fishing.Ability.TH.MagicFound\");\n                    }\n\n                }\n            } else {\n                treasureDrop = null;\n                treasureXp = 0;\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (fishingSucceeds) {\n            if (mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getFishingExtraFish()) {\n                Misc.spawnItem(getPlayer(), player.getEyeLocation(), fishingCatch.getItemStack(), ItemSpawnReason.FISHING_EXTRA_FISH);\n            }\n\n            fishingCatch.setItemStack(treasureDrop);\n        }\n\n        applyXpGain(fishXp + treasureXp, XPGainReason.PVE);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Handle the vanilla XP boost for Fishing\n     *\n     * @param experience The amount of experience initially awarded by the event\n     *\n     * @return the modified event damage\n     */\n    public int handleVanillaXpBoost(int experience) {\n        return experience * getVanillaXpMultiplier();\n    }\n\n    public Location getHookLocation() {\n        return hookLocation;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Handle the Shake ability\n     *\n     * @param target The {@link LivingEntity} affected by the ability\n     */\n    public void shakeCheck(@NotNull LivingEntity target) {\n        if (ProbabilityUtil.isStaticSkillRNGSuccessful(PrimarySkillType.FISHING, mmoPlayer, getShakeChance())) {\n            List<ShakeTreasure> possibleDrops = Fishing.findPossibleDrops(target);\n\n            if (possibleDrops == null || possibleDrops.isEmpty()) {\n                return;\n            }\n\n            ItemStack drop = Fishing.chooseDrop(possibleDrops);\n\n            // It's possible that chooseDrop returns null if the sum of probability in possibleDrops is inferior than 100\n            if (drop == null) {\n                return;\n            }\n\n            // Extra processing depending on the mob and drop type\n            switch (target.getType()) {\n                case PLAYER:\n                    Player targetPlayer = (Player) target;\n\n                    switch (drop.getType()) {\n                        case PLAYER_HEAD:\n                            drop.setDurability((short) 3);\n                            SkullMeta skullMeta = (SkullMeta) drop.getItemMeta();\n                            skullMeta.setOwningPlayer(targetPlayer);\n                            drop.setItemMeta(skullMeta);\n                            break;\n\n                        case BEDROCK:\n                            if (FishingTreasureConfig.getInstance().getInventoryStealEnabled()) {\n                                PlayerInventory inventory = targetPlayer.getInventory();\n                                int length = inventory.getContents().length;\n                                int slot = Misc.getRandom().nextInt(length);\n                                drop = inventory.getItem(slot);\n\n                                if (drop == null) {\n                                    break;\n                                }\n\n                                if (FishingTreasureConfig.getInstance().getInventoryStealStacks()) {\n                                    inventory.setItem(slot, null);\n                                } else {\n                                    inventory.setItem(slot, (drop.getAmount() > 1) ? new ItemStack(drop.getType(), drop.getAmount() - 1) : null);\n                                    drop.setAmount(1);\n                                }\n\n                                targetPlayer.updateInventory();\n                            }\n                            break;\n\n                        default:\n                            break;\n                    }\n                    break;\n\n                case SHEEP:\n                    Sheep sheep = (Sheep) target;\n\n                    if (drop.getType().name().endsWith(\"WOOL\")) {\n                        if (sheep.isSheared()) {\n                            return;\n                        }\n                        sheep.setSheared(true);\n                    }\n                    break;\n                default:\n                    break;\n            }\n\n            McMMOPlayerShakeEvent shakeEvent = new McMMOPlayerShakeEvent(getPlayer(), drop);\n\n            drop = shakeEvent.getDrop();\n\n            if (shakeEvent.isCancelled() || drop == null) {\n                return;\n            }\n\n            Misc.spawnItem(getPlayer(), target.getLocation(), drop, ItemSpawnReason.FISHING_SHAKE_TREASURE);\n            CombatUtils.dealDamage(target, Math.min(Math.max(target.getMaxHealth() / 4, 1), 10), EntityDamageEvent.DamageCause.CUSTOM, getPlayer()); // Make it so you can shake a mob no more than 4 times.\n            applyXpGain(ExperienceConfig.getInstance().getFishingShakeXP(), XPGainReason.PVE);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Process the Treasure Hunter ability for Fishing\n     *\n     * @return The {@link FishingTreasure} found, or null if no treasure was found.\n     */\n    private @Nullable FishingTreasure getFishingTreasure() {\n        double diceRoll = Misc.getRandom().nextDouble() * 100;\n        int luck;\n\n        if (getPlayer().getInventory().getItemInMainHand().getType() == Material.FISHING_ROD) {\n            luck = getPlayer().getInventory().getItemInMainHand().getEnchantmentLevel(\n                    mcMMO.p.getEnchantmentMapper().getLuckOfTheSea());\n        } else {\n            // We know something was caught, so if the rod wasn't in the main hand it must be in the offhand\n            luck = getPlayer().getInventory().getItemInOffHand().getEnchantmentLevel(\n                    mcMMO.p.getEnchantmentMapper().getLuckOfTheSea());\n        }\n\n        // Rather than subtracting luck (and causing a minimum 3% chance for every drop), scale by luck.\n        diceRoll *= (1.0 - luck * mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getFishingLureModifier() / 100);\n\n        FishingTreasure treasure = null;\n\n        for (Rarity rarity : Rarity.values()) {\n            double dropRate = FishingTreasureConfig.getInstance().getItemDropRate(getLootTier(), rarity);\n\n            if (diceRoll <= dropRate) {\n\n                List<FishingTreasure> fishingTreasures = FishingTreasureConfig.getInstance().fishingRewards.get(rarity);\n\n                if (fishingTreasures.isEmpty()) {\n                    return null;\n                }\n\n                treasure = fishingTreasures.get(Misc.getRandom().nextInt(fishingTreasures.size()));\n                break;\n            }\n\n            diceRoll -= dropRate;\n        }\n\n        if (treasure == null) {\n            return null;\n        }\n\n        ItemStack treasureDrop = treasure.getDrop().clone();\n        short maxDurability = treasureDrop.getType().getMaxDurability();\n\n        if (maxDurability > 0) {\n            treasureDrop.setDurability((short) (Misc.getRandom().nextInt(maxDurability)));\n        }\n\n        //TODO: Add option to randomize the amount rewarded\n        /*if (treasureDrop.getAmount() > 1) {\n            treasureDrop.setAmount(Misc.getRandom().nextInt(treasureDrop.getAmount()) + 1);\n        }*/\n\n        treasure.setDrop(treasureDrop);\n\n        return treasure;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Process the Magic Hunter ability\n     *\n     * @param treasureDrop The {@link ItemStack} to enchant\n     */\n    private Map<Enchantment, Integer> processMagicHunter(@NotNull ItemStack treasureDrop) {\n        Map<Enchantment, Integer> enchants = new HashMap<>();\n        List<EnchantmentTreasure> fishingEnchantments = null;\n\n        double diceRoll = Misc.getRandom().nextDouble() * 100;\n\n        for (Rarity rarity : Rarity.values()) {\n\n            double dropRate = FishingTreasureConfig.getInstance().getEnchantmentDropRate(getLootTier(), rarity);\n\n            if (diceRoll <= dropRate) {\n                // Make sure enchanted books always get some kind of enchantment.  --hoorigan\n                if (treasureDrop.getType() == Material.ENCHANTED_BOOK) {\n                    diceRoll = dropRate + 1;\n                    continue;\n                }\n\n                fishingEnchantments = FishingTreasureConfig.getInstance().fishingEnchantments.get(rarity);\n                break;\n            }\n\n            diceRoll -= dropRate;\n        }\n\n        if (fishingEnchantments == null) {\n            return enchants;\n        }\n\n        List<Enchantment> validEnchantments = getPossibleEnchantments(treasureDrop);\n        List<EnchantmentTreasure> possibleEnchants = new ArrayList<>();\n\n        for (EnchantmentTreasure enchantmentTreasure : fishingEnchantments) {\n            if (validEnchantments.contains(enchantmentTreasure.getEnchantment())) {\n                possibleEnchants.add(enchantmentTreasure);\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (possibleEnchants.isEmpty()) {\n            return enchants;\n        }\n\n        // This make sure that the order isn't always the same, for example previously Unbreaking had a lot more chance to be used than any other enchant\n        Collections.shuffle(possibleEnchants, Misc.getRandom());\n\n        int specificChance = 1;\n\n        for (EnchantmentTreasure enchantmentTreasure : possibleEnchants) {\n            Enchantment possibleEnchantment = enchantmentTreasure.getEnchantment();\n\n            if (treasureDrop.getItemMeta().hasConflictingEnchant(possibleEnchantment) || Misc.getRandom().nextInt(specificChance) != 0) {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            enchants.put(possibleEnchantment, enchantmentTreasure.getLevel());\n\n            specificChance *= 2;\n        }\n\n        return enchants;\n    }\n\n    private List<Enchantment> getPossibleEnchantments(ItemStack treasureDrop) {\n        Material dropType = treasureDrop.getType();\n\n        if (Fishing.ENCHANTABLE_CACHE.containsKey(dropType)) {\n            return Fishing.ENCHANTABLE_CACHE.get(dropType);\n        }\n\n        List<Enchantment> possibleEnchantments = new ArrayList<>();\n\n        for (Enchantment enchantment : Enchantment.values()) {\n            if (enchantment.canEnchantItem(treasureDrop)) {\n                possibleEnchantments.add(enchantment);\n            }\n        }\n\n        Fishing.ENCHANTABLE_CACHE.put(dropType, possibleEnchantments);\n        return possibleEnchantments;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Gets the vanilla XP multiplier\n     *\n     * @return the vanilla XP multiplier\n     */\n    private int getVanillaXpMultiplier() {\n        return getVanillaXPBoostModifier();\n    }\n}"
    },
    {
      "path": "src/main/java/com/gmail/nossr50/skills/herbalism/HerbalismManager.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package com.gmail.nossr50.skills.herbalism;\n\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.api.ItemSpawnReason;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.config.experience.ExperienceConfig;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.config.treasure.TreasureConfig;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.BlockSnapshot;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.experience.XPGainReason;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.experience.XPGainSource;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.interactions.NotificationType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.meta.RecentlyReplantedCropMeta;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.player.McMMOPlayer;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.PrimarySkillType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.SubSkillType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.SuperAbilityType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.ToolType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.treasure.HylianTreasure;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.mcMMO;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.runnables.skills.DelayedCropReplant;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.runnables.skills.DelayedHerbalismXPCheckTask;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.skills.SkillManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.*;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.player.NotificationManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.random.ProbabilityUtil;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.skills.RankUtils;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.skills.SkillUtils;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.sounds.SoundManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.sounds.SoundType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.text.StringUtils;\nimport org.bukkit.Location;\nimport org.bukkit.Material;\nimport org.bukkit.block.Block;\nimport org.bukkit.block.BlockFace;\nimport org.bukkit.block.BlockState;\nimport org.bukkit.block.data.Ageable;\nimport org.bukkit.block.data.BlockData;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.Player;\nimport org.bukkit.event.block.BlockBreakEvent;\nimport org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack;\nimport org.bukkit.inventory.PlayerInventory;\nimport org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;\n\nimport java.util.*;\n\nimport static com.gmail.nossr50.util.ItemUtils.hasItemIncludingOffHand;\nimport static com.gmail.nossr50.util.ItemUtils.removeItemIncludingOffHand;\nimport static java.util.Objects.requireNonNull;\n\npublic class HerbalismManager extends SkillManager {\n    public HerbalismManager(McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer) {\n        super(mcMMOPlayer, PrimarySkillType.HERBALISM);\n    }\n\n    public boolean canGreenThumbBlock(BlockState blockState) {\n        if (!RankUtils.hasUnlockedSubskill(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.HERBALISM_GREEN_THUMB))\n            return false;\n\n        Player player = getPlayer();\n        ItemStack item = player.getInventory().getItemInMainHand();\n\n        return item.getAmount() > 0 && item.getType() == Material.WHEAT_SEEDS && BlockUtils.canMakeMossy(blockState) && Permissions.greenThumbBlock(player, blockState.getType());\n    }\n\n    public boolean canUseShroomThumb(BlockState blockState) {\n        if (!BlockUtils.canMakeShroomy(blockState)) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        if (!RankUtils.hasUnlockedSubskill(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.HERBALISM_SHROOM_THUMB))\n            return false;\n\n        Player player = getPlayer();\n        PlayerInventory inventory = player.getInventory();\n        Material itemType = inventory.getItemInMainHand().getType();\n\n        return (itemType == Material.BROWN_MUSHROOM\n                || itemType == Material.RED_MUSHROOM)\n                && inventory.contains(Material.BROWN_MUSHROOM, 1)\n                && inventory.contains(Material.RED_MUSHROOM, 1)\n                && Permissions.isSubSkillEnabled(player, SubSkillType.HERBALISM_SHROOM_THUMB);\n    }\n\n    public void processBerryBushHarvesting(@NotNull BlockState blockState) {\n        /* Check if the player is harvesting a berry bush */\n        if (blockState.getType().toString().equalsIgnoreCase(\"sweet_berry_bush\")) {\n            if (mmoPlayer.isDebugMode()) {\n                mmoPlayer.getPlayer().sendMessage(\"Processing sweet berry bush rewards\");\n            }\n            //Check the age\n            if (blockState.getBlockData() instanceof Ageable ageable) {\n                int rewardByAge = 0;\n\n                if (ageable.getAge() == 2) {\n                    rewardByAge = 1; //Normal XP\n                } else if (ageable.getAge() == 3) {\n                    rewardByAge = 2; //Double XP\n                } else {\n                    return; //Not old enough, back out of processing\n                }\n\n                if (mmoPlayer.isDebugMode()) {\n                    mmoPlayer.getPlayer().sendMessage(\"Bush Reward Multiplier: \" + rewardByAge);\n                }\n\n                int xpReward = ExperienceConfig.getInstance().getXp(PrimarySkillType.HERBALISM, blockState) * rewardByAge;\n\n                if (mmoPlayer.isDebugMode()) {\n                    mmoPlayer.getPlayer().sendMessage(\"Bush XP: \" + xpReward);\n                }\n\n                CheckBushAge checkBushAge = new CheckBushAge(blockState.getBlock(), mmoPlayer, xpReward);\n                mcMMO.p.getFoliaLib().getImpl().runAtLocationLater(blockState.getLocation(), checkBushAge, 1);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    private class CheckBushAge extends CancellableRunnable {\n\n        @NotNull Block block;\n        @NotNull McMMOPlayer mmoPlayer;\n        int xpReward;\n\n        public CheckBushAge(@NotNull Block block, @NotNull McMMOPlayer mmoPlayer, int xpReward) {\n            this.block = block;\n            this.mmoPlayer = mmoPlayer;\n            this.xpReward = xpReward;\n        }\n\n        @Override\n        public void run() {\n            BlockState blockState = block.getState();\n\n            if (blockState.getType().toString().equalsIgnoreCase(\"sweet_berry_bush\")) {\n                if (blockState.getBlockData() instanceof Ageable ageable) {\n\n                    if (ageable.getAge() <= 1) {\n                        applyXpGain(xpReward, XPGainReason.PVE, XPGainSource.SELF);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n\n    public boolean canUseHylianLuck() {\n        if (!RankUtils.hasUnlockedSubskill(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.HERBALISM_HYLIAN_LUCK))\n            return false;\n\n        return Permissions.isSubSkillEnabled(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.HERBALISM_HYLIAN_LUCK);\n    }\n\n    public boolean canGreenTerraBlock(BlockState blockState) {\n        return mmoPlayer.getAbilityMode(SuperAbilityType.GREEN_TERRA) && BlockUtils.canMakeMossy(blockState);\n    }\n\n    public boolean canActivateAbility() {\n        return mmoPlayer.getToolPreparationMode(ToolType.HOE) && Permissions.greenTerra(getPlayer());\n    }\n\n    public boolean isGreenTerraActive() {\n        return mmoPlayer.getAbilityMode(SuperAbilityType.GREEN_TERRA);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Handle the Farmer's Diet ability\n     *\n     * @param eventFoodLevel The initial change in hunger from the event\n     * @return the modified change in hunger for the event\n     */\n    public int farmersDiet(int eventFoodLevel) {\n        return SkillUtils.handleFoodSkills(getPlayer(), eventFoodLevel, SubSkillType.HERBALISM_FARMERS_DIET);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Process the Green Terra ability.\n     *\n     * @param blockState The {@link BlockState} to check ability activation for\n     * @return true if the ability was successful, false otherwise\n     */\n    public boolean processGreenTerraBlockConversion(BlockState blockState) {\n        Player player = getPlayer();\n\n        if (!Permissions.greenThumbBlock(player, blockState.getType())) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        PlayerInventory playerInventory = player.getInventory();\n        ItemStack seed = new ItemStack(Material.WHEAT_SEEDS);\n\n        if (!playerInventory.containsAtLeast(seed, 1)) {\n            NotificationManager.sendPlayerInformation(player, NotificationType.REQUIREMENTS_NOT_MET, \"Herbalism.Ability.GTe.NeedMore\");\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        playerInventory.removeItem(seed);\n        player.updateInventory(); // Needed until replacement available\n\n        return Herbalism.convertGreenTerraBlocks(blockState);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Handles herbalism abilities and XP rewards from a BlockBreakEvent\n     * @param blockBreakEvent The Block Break Event to process\n     */\n    public void processHerbalismBlockBreakEvent(BlockBreakEvent blockBreakEvent) {\n        Player player = getPlayer();\n\n        Block block = blockBreakEvent.getBlock();\n\n        if (mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getHerbalismPreventAFK() && player.isInsideVehicle()) {\n            if (block.hasMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_REPLANT)) {\n                block.removeMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_REPLANT, mcMMO.p);\n            }\n            return;\n        }\n\n        //Check if the plant was recently replanted\n        if (block.getBlockData() instanceof Ageable ageableCrop) {\n            if (block.getMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_REPLANT).size() >= 1) {\n                if (block.getMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_REPLANT).get(0).asBoolean()) {\n                    if (isAgeableMature(ageableCrop)) {\n                        block.removeMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_REPLANT, mcMMO.p);\n                    } else {\n                        //Crop is recently replanted to back out of destroying it\n                        blockBreakEvent.setCancelled(true);\n                        return;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        /*\n         * There are single-block plants and multi-block plants in Minecraft\n         * In order to give out proper rewards, we need to collect all blocks that would be broken from this event\n         */\n\n        //Grab all broken blocks\n        HashSet<Block> brokenBlocks = getBrokenHerbalismBlocks(blockBreakEvent);\n\n        if (brokenBlocks.size() == 0)\n            return;\n\n        //Handle rewards, xp, ability interactions, etc\n        processHerbalismOnBlocksBroken(blockBreakEvent, brokenBlocks);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Process rewards for a set of plant blocks for Herbalism\n     * @param blockBreakEvent the block break event\n     * @param brokenPlants plant blocks to process\n     */\n    private void processHerbalismOnBlocksBroken(BlockBreakEvent blockBreakEvent, HashSet<Block> brokenPlants) {\n        if (blockBreakEvent.isCancelled())\n            return;\n\n        BlockState originalBreak = blockBreakEvent.getBlock().getState();\n        boolean greenThumbActivated = false;\n\n        //TODO: The design of Green Terra needs to change, this is a mess\n        if (Permissions.greenThumbPlant(getPlayer(), originalBreak.getType())) {\n            if (mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().isGreenThumbReplantableCrop(originalBreak.getType())) {\n                if (!getPlayer().isSneaking()) {\n                    greenThumbActivated = processGreenThumbPlants(originalBreak, blockBreakEvent, isGreenTerraActive());\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        /*\n         * Mark blocks for double drops\n         * Be aware of the hacky interactions we are doing with Chorus Plants\n         */\n        checkDoubleDropsOnBrokenPlants(blockBreakEvent.getPlayer(), brokenPlants);\n\n        //It would take an expensive algorithm to predict which parts of a Chorus Tree will break as a result of root break\n        //So this hacky method is used instead\n        ArrayList<BlockSnapshot> delayedChorusBlocks = new ArrayList<>(); //Blocks that will be checked in future ticks\n        HashSet<Block> noDelayPlantBlocks = new HashSet<>(); //Blocks that will be checked immediately\n\n        for(Block brokenPlant : brokenPlants) {\n            /*\n             * This check is to make XP bars appear to work properly with Chorus Trees by giving XP for the originalBreak immediately instead of later\n             */\n            if (brokenPlant.getLocation().equals(originalBreak.getBlock().getLocation())) {\n                //If its the same block as the original, we are going to directly check it for being a valid XP gain and add it to the nonChorusBlocks list even if its a chorus block\n                //This stops a delay from happening when bringing up the XP bar for chorus trees\n                if (!mcMMO.getUserBlockTracker().isIneligible(originalBreak)) {\n                    //Even if its a chorus block, the original break will be moved to nonChorusBlocks for immediate XP rewards\n                    noDelayPlantBlocks.add(brokenPlant);\n                } else {\n                    if (isChorusTree(brokenPlant.getType())) {\n                        //If its a chorus tree AND it was marked as true in the placestore then we add this block to the list of chorus blocks\n                        delayedChorusBlocks.add(new BlockSnapshot(brokenPlant.getType(), brokenPlant));\n                    } else {\n                        noDelayPlantBlocks.add(brokenPlant); //If its not a chorus plant that was marked as unnatural but it was marked unnatural, put it in the nodelay list to be handled\n                    }\n                }\n            } else if (isChorusTree(brokenPlant.getType())) {\n                //Chorus Blocks get checked for XP several ticks later to avoid expensive calculations\n                delayedChorusBlocks.add(new BlockSnapshot(brokenPlant.getType(), brokenPlant));\n            } else {\n                noDelayPlantBlocks.add(brokenPlant);\n            }\n        }\n\n        //Give out XP to the non-chorus blocks\n        if (noDelayPlantBlocks.size() > 0) {\n            //Note: Will contain 1 chorus block if the original block was a chorus block, this is to prevent delays for the XP bar\n            awardXPForPlantBlocks(noDelayPlantBlocks);\n        }\n\n        if (delayedChorusBlocks.size() > 0) {\n            //Check XP for chorus blocks\n            DelayedHerbalismXPCheckTask delayedHerbalismXPCheckTask = new DelayedHerbalismXPCheckTask(mmoPlayer, delayedChorusBlocks);\n\n            //Large delay because the tree takes a while to break\n            mcMMO.p.getFoliaLib().getImpl().runAtEntity(mmoPlayer.getPlayer(), delayedHerbalismXPCheckTask); //Calculate Chorus XP + Bonus Drops 1 tick later\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Check for double drops on a collection of broken blocks\n     * If a double drop has occurred, it will be marked here for bonus drops\n     * @param player player who broke the blocks\n     * @param brokenPlants the collection of broken plants\n     */\n    public void checkDoubleDropsOnBrokenPlants(Player player, Collection<Block> brokenPlants) {\n\n        //Only proceed if skill unlocked and permission enabled\n        if (!RankUtils.hasUnlockedSubskill(player, SubSkillType.HERBALISM_DOUBLE_DROPS)\n                || !Permissions.isSubSkillEnabled(player, SubSkillType.HERBALISM_DOUBLE_DROPS)) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        for(Block brokenPlant : brokenPlants) {\n            BlockState brokenPlantState = brokenPlant.getState();\n            BlockData plantData = brokenPlantState.getBlockData();\n\n            //Check for double drops\n            if (!mcMMO.getUserBlockTracker().isIneligible(brokenPlant)) {\n\n                /*\n                 *\n                 * Natural Blocks\n                 *\n                 *\n                 *\n                 */\n\n                //Not all things that are natural should give double drops, make sure its fully mature as well\n                if (plantData instanceof Ageable ageable) {\n\n                    if (isAgeableMature(ageable) || isBizarreAgeable(plantData)) {\n                        if (checkDoubleDrop(brokenPlantState)) {\n                            markForBonusDrops(brokenPlantState);\n                        }\n                    }\n                } else if (checkDoubleDrop(brokenPlantState)) {\n                    //Add metadata to mark this block for double or triple drops\n                    markForBonusDrops(brokenPlantState);\n                }\n            } else {\n\n                /*\n                 *\n                 * Unnatural Blocks\n                 *\n                 */\n\n                //If it's a Crop we need to reward XP when its fully grown\n                if (isAgeableAndFullyMature(plantData) && !isBizarreAgeable(plantData)) {\n                    //Add metadata to mark this block for double or triple drops\n                    markForBonusDrops(brokenPlantState);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Checks if BlockData is bizarre ageable, and we cannot trust that age for Herbalism rewards/XP reasons\n     * @param blockData target BlockData\n     * @return returns true if the BlockData is a bizarre ageable for Herbalism XP / Rewards\n     */\n    public boolean isBizarreAgeable(BlockData blockData) {\n        if (blockData instanceof Ageable) {\n            //Catcus and Sugar Canes cannot be trusted\n            return switch (blockData.getMaterial()) {\n                case CACTUS, KELP, SUGAR_CANE, BAMBOO -> true;\n                default -> false;\n            };\n        }\n\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    public void markForBonusDrops(BlockState brokenPlantState) {\n        //Add metadata to mark this block for double or triple drops\n        boolean awardTriple = mmoPlayer.getAbilityMode(SuperAbilityType.GREEN_TERRA);\n        BlockUtils.markDropsAsBonus(brokenPlantState, awardTriple);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Checks if a block is an ageable and if that ageable is fully mature\n     * @param plantData target plant\n     * @return returns true if the block is both an ageable and fully mature\n     */\n    public boolean isAgeableAndFullyMature(BlockData plantData) {\n        return plantData instanceof Ageable && isAgeableMature((Ageable) plantData);\n    }\n\n    public void awardXPForPlantBlocks(HashSet<Block> brokenPlants) {\n        int xpToReward = 0;\n\n        for(Block brokenPlantBlock : brokenPlants) {\n            BlockState brokenBlockNewState = brokenPlantBlock.getState();\n            BlockData plantData = brokenBlockNewState.getBlockData();\n\n            if (mcMMO.getUserBlockTracker().isIneligible(brokenBlockNewState)) {\n                /*\n                 *\n                 * Unnatural Blocks\n                 *\n                 *\n                 */\n\n                //If its a Crop we need to reward XP when its fully grown\n                if (isAgeableAndFullyMature(plantData) && !isBizarreAgeable(plantData)) {\n                    xpToReward += ExperienceConfig.getInstance().getXp(PrimarySkillType.HERBALISM, brokenBlockNewState.getType());\n                }\n\n                //Mark it as natural again as it is being broken\n                mcMMO.getUserBlockTracker().setEligible(brokenBlockNewState);\n            } else {\n                /*\n                 *\n                 * Natural Blocks\n                 *\n                 *\n                 */\n\n                //Calculate XP\n                if (plantData instanceof Ageable plantAgeable) {\n\n                    if (isAgeableMature(plantAgeable) || isBizarreAgeable(plantData)) {\n                        xpToReward += ExperienceConfig.getInstance().getXp(PrimarySkillType.HERBALISM, brokenBlockNewState.getType());\n                    }\n\n                } else {\n                    xpToReward += ExperienceConfig.getInstance().getXp(PrimarySkillType.HERBALISM, brokenPlantBlock.getType());\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (mmoPlayer.isDebugMode()) {\n            mmoPlayer.getPlayer().sendMessage(\"Plants processed: \"+brokenPlants.size());\n        }\n\n        //Reward XP\n        if (xpToReward > 0) {\n            applyXpGain(xpToReward, XPGainReason.PVE, XPGainSource.SELF);\n        }\n    }\n\n    public boolean isAgeableMature(Ageable ageable) {\n        return ageable.getAge() == ageable.getMaximumAge()\n                && ageable.getAge() != 0;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Award XP for any blocks that used to be something else but are now AIR\n     * @param brokenPlants snapshot of broken blocks\n     */\n    public void awardXPForBlockSnapshots(ArrayList<BlockSnapshot> brokenPlants) {\n        /*\n         * This handles XP for blocks that we need to check are broken after the fact\n         * This only applies to chorus trees right now\n         */\n        int xpToReward = 0;\n        int blocksGivingXP = 0;\n\n        for(BlockSnapshot blockSnapshot : brokenPlants) {\n            BlockState brokenBlockNewState = blockSnapshot.getBlockRef().getState();\n\n            //Remove metadata from the snapshot of blocks\n            if (brokenBlockNewState.hasMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_BONUS_DROPS)) {\n                brokenBlockNewState.removeMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_BONUS_DROPS, mcMMO.p);\n            }\n\n            //If the block is not AIR that means it wasn't broken\n            if (brokenBlockNewState.getType() != Material.AIR) {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            if (mcMMO.getUserBlockTracker().isIneligible(brokenBlockNewState)) {\n                //Mark it as natural again as it is being broken\n                mcMMO.getUserBlockTracker().setEligible(brokenBlockNewState);\n            } else {\n                //TODO: Do we care about chorus flower age?\n                //Calculate XP for the old type\n                xpToReward += ExperienceConfig.getInstance().getXp(PrimarySkillType.HERBALISM, blockSnapshot.getOldType());\n                blocksGivingXP++;\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (mmoPlayer.isDebugMode()) {\n            mmoPlayer.getPlayer().sendMessage(\"Chorus Plants checked for XP: \"+brokenPlants.size());\n            mmoPlayer.getPlayer().sendMessage(\"Valid Chorus Plant XP Gains: \"+blocksGivingXP);\n        }\n\n        //Reward XP\n        if (xpToReward > 0) {\n            applyXpGain(xpToReward, XPGainReason.PVE, XPGainSource.SELF);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Process and return plant blocks from a BlockBreakEvent\n     * @param blockBreakEvent target event\n     * @return a set of plant-blocks that were broken as a result of this event\n     */\n    private HashSet<Block> getBrokenHerbalismBlocks(@NotNull BlockBreakEvent blockBreakEvent) {\n        //Get an updated capture of this block\n        BlockState originBlockState = blockBreakEvent.getBlock().getState();\n        Material originBlockMaterial = originBlockState.getType();\n        HashSet<Block> blocksBroken = new HashSet<>(); //Blocks broken\n\n        //Add the initial block\n        blocksBroken.add(originBlockState.getBlock());\n\n        if (!isOneBlockPlant(originBlockMaterial)) {\n            //If the block is a multi-block structure, capture a set of all blocks broken and return that\n            blocksBroken = getBrokenBlocksMultiBlockPlants(originBlockState);\n        }\n\n        //Return all broken plant-blocks\n        return blocksBroken;\n    }\n\n    private HashSet<Block> getBrokenChorusBlocks(BlockState originalBreak) {\n        return grabChorusTreeBrokenBlocksRecursive(originalBreak.getBlock(), new HashSet<>());\n    }\n\n    private HashSet<Block> grabChorusTreeBrokenBlocksRecursive(Block currentBlock, HashSet<Block> traversed) {\n        if (!isChorusTree(currentBlock.getType()))\n            return traversed;\n\n        // Prevent any infinite loops, who needs more than 256 chorus anyways\n        if (traversed.size() > 256)\n            return traversed;\n\n        if (!traversed.add(currentBlock))\n            return traversed;\n\n        //Grab all Blocks in the Tree\n        for (BlockFace blockFace : new BlockFace[] { BlockFace.UP, BlockFace.NORTH, BlockFace.SOUTH, BlockFace.EAST ,BlockFace.WEST})\n            grabChorusTreeBrokenBlocksRecursive(currentBlock.getRelative(blockFace, 1), traversed);\n\n        traversed.add(currentBlock);\n\n        return traversed;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Grab a set of all plant blocks that are broken as a result of this event\n     * The method to grab these blocks is a bit hacky and does not hook into the API\n     * Basically we expect the blocks to be broken if this event is not cancelled and we determine which block are broken on our end rather than any event state captures\n     *\n     * @return a set of plant-blocks broken from this event\n     */\n    protected HashSet<Block> getBrokenBlocksMultiBlockPlants(BlockState brokenBlock) {\n        //Track the broken blocks\n        HashSet<Block> brokenBlocks;\n\n        if (isChorusBranch(brokenBlock.getType())) {\n            brokenBlocks = getBrokenChorusBlocks(brokenBlock);\n        } else {\n            brokenBlocks = getBlocksBrokenAboveOrBelow(brokenBlock, false, mcMMO.getMaterialMapStore().isMultiBlockHangingPlant(brokenBlock.getType()));\n        }\n\n        return brokenBlocks;\n    }\n\n    private boolean isChorusBranch(Material blockType) {\n        return blockType == Material.CHORUS_PLANT;\n    }\n\n    private boolean isChorusTree(Material blockType) {\n        return blockType == Material.CHORUS_PLANT || blockType == Material.CHORUS_FLOWER;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Grabs blocks upwards from a target block\n     * A lot of Plants/Crops in Herbalism only break vertically from a broken block\n     * The vertical search returns early if it runs into anything that is not a multi-block plant\n     * Multi-block plants are hard-coded and kept in {@link MaterialMapStore}\n     *\n     * @param originBlock The point of the \"break\"\n     * @param inclusive Whether to include the origin block\n     * @param below Whether to search down instead of up.\n     * @return A set of blocks above the target block which can be assumed to be broken\n     */\n    private HashSet<Block> getBlocksBrokenAboveOrBelow(BlockState originBlock, boolean inclusive, boolean below) {\n        HashSet<Block> brokenBlocks = new HashSet<>();\n        Block block = originBlock.getBlock();\n\n        //Add the initial block to the set\n        if (inclusive)\n            brokenBlocks.add(block);\n\n        //Limit our search\n        int maxHeight = 512;\n\n        final BlockFace relativeFace = below ? BlockFace.DOWN : BlockFace.UP;\n\n        // Search vertically for multi-block plants, exit early if any non-multi block plants\n        for (int y = 0; y < maxHeight; y++) {\n            //TODO: Should this grab state? It would be more expensive..\n            Block relativeBlock = block.getRelative(relativeFace, y);\n\n            //Abandon our search if the block isn't multi\n            if (isOneBlockPlant(relativeBlock.getType()))\n                break;\n\n            brokenBlocks.add(relativeBlock);\n        }\n\n        return brokenBlocks;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * If the plant is considered a one block plant\n     * This is determined by seeing if it exists in a hard-coded collection of Multi-Block plants\n     * @param material target plant material\n     * @return true if the block is not contained in the collection of multi-block plants\n     */\n    private boolean isOneBlockPlant(Material material) {\n        return !mcMMO.getMaterialMapStore().isMultiBlockPlant(material) && !mcMMO.getMaterialMapStore().isMultiBlockHangingPlant(material);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Check for success on herbalism double drops\n     *\n     * @param blockState target block state\n     * @return true if the double drop succeeds\n     */\n    private boolean checkDoubleDrop(@NotNull BlockState blockState) {\n        requireNonNull(blockState, \"BlockState cannot be null\");\n        return BlockUtils.checkDoubleDrops(mmoPlayer, blockState, SubSkillType.HERBALISM_DOUBLE_DROPS);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Process the Green Thumb ability for blocks.\n     *\n     * @param blockState The {@link BlockState} to check ability activation for\n     * @return true if the ability was successful, false otherwise\n     */\n    public boolean processGreenThumbBlocks(BlockState blockState) {\n        if (!ProbabilityUtil.isSkillRNGSuccessful(SubSkillType.HERBALISM_GREEN_THUMB, mmoPlayer)) {\n            NotificationManager.sendPlayerInformation(getPlayer(), NotificationType.SUBSKILL_MESSAGE_FAILED, \"Herbalism.Ability.GTh.Fail\");\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        return Herbalism.convertGreenTerraBlocks(blockState);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Process the Hylian Luck ability.\n     *\n     * @param blockState The {@link BlockState} to check ability activation for\n     * @return true if the ability was successful, false otherwise\n     */\n    public boolean processHylianLuck(BlockState blockState) {\n        if (!ProbabilityUtil.isSkillRNGSuccessful(SubSkillType.HERBALISM_HYLIAN_LUCK, mmoPlayer)) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        String friendly = StringUtils.getFriendlyConfigBlockDataString(blockState.getBlockData());\n        if (!TreasureConfig.getInstance().hylianMap.containsKey(friendly))\n            return false;\n        List<HylianTreasure> treasures = TreasureConfig.getInstance().hylianMap.get(friendly);\n\n\n        if (treasures.isEmpty()) {\n            return false;\n        }\n        int skillLevel = getSkillLevel();\n        Location location = Misc.getBlockCenter(blockState);\n\n        for (HylianTreasure treasure : treasures) {\n            if (skillLevel >= treasure.getDropLevel()\n                    && ProbabilityUtil.isStaticSkillRNGSuccessful(PrimarySkillType.HERBALISM, mmoPlayer, treasure.getDropChance())) {\n                if (!EventUtils.simulateBlockBreak(blockState.getBlock(), mmoPlayer.getPlayer())) {\n                    return false;\n                }\n                blockState.setType(Material.AIR);\n                Misc.spawnItem(getPlayer(), location, treasure.getDrop(), ItemSpawnReason.HYLIAN_LUCK_TREASURE);\n                NotificationManager.sendPlayerInformation(mmoPlayer.getPlayer(), NotificationType.SUBSKILL_MESSAGE, \"Herbalism.HylianLuck\");\n                return true;\n            }\n        }\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Process the Shroom Thumb ability.\n     *\n     * @param blockState The {@link BlockState} to check ability activation for\n     * @return true if the ability was successful, false otherwise\n     */\n    public boolean processShroomThumb(BlockState blockState) {\n        PlayerInventory playerInventory = getPlayer().getInventory();\n        \n        if (!playerInventory.contains(Material.BROWN_MUSHROOM, 1)) {\n            NotificationManager.sendPlayerInformation(getPlayer(), NotificationType.REQUIREMENTS_NOT_MET, \"Skills.NeedMore\", StringUtils.getPrettyItemString(Material.BROWN_MUSHROOM));\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        if (!playerInventory.contains(Material.RED_MUSHROOM, 1)) {\n            NotificationManager.sendPlayerInformation(getPlayer(), NotificationType.REQUIREMENTS_NOT_MET, \"Skills.NeedMore\", StringUtils.getPrettyItemString(Material.RED_MUSHROOM));\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        playerInventory.removeItem(new ItemStack(Material.BROWN_MUSHROOM));\n        playerInventory.removeItem(new ItemStack(Material.RED_MUSHROOM));\n        getPlayer().updateInventory();\n\n        if (!ProbabilityUtil.isSkillRNGSuccessful(SubSkillType.HERBALISM_SHROOM_THUMB, mmoPlayer)) {\n            NotificationManager.sendPlayerInformation(getPlayer(), NotificationType.SUBSKILL_MESSAGE_FAILED, \"Herbalism.Ability.ShroomThumb.Fail\");\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        return Herbalism.convertShroomThumb(blockState);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Starts the delayed replant task and turns\n     * @param desiredCropAge the desired age of the crop\n     * @param blockBreakEvent the {@link BlockBreakEvent} this crop was involved in\n     * @param cropState the {@link BlockState} of the crop\n     */\n    private void startReplantTask(int desiredCropAge, BlockBreakEvent blockBreakEvent, BlockState cropState, boolean isImmature) {\n        //Mark the plant as recently replanted to avoid accidental breakage\n        mcMMO.p.getFoliaLib().getImpl().runAtLocationLater(blockBreakEvent.getBlock().getLocation(), new DelayedCropReplant(blockBreakEvent, cropState, desiredCropAge, isImmature), 2 * Misc.TICK_CONVERSION_FACTOR);\n        blockBreakEvent.getBlock().setMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_REPLANT, new RecentlyReplantedCropMeta(mcMMO.p, true));\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Process the Green Thumb ability for plants.\n     *\n     * @param blockState The {@link BlockState} to check ability activation for\n     * @param greenTerra boolean to determine if greenTerra is active or not\n     */\n    private boolean processGreenThumbPlants(@NotNull BlockState blockState, @NotNull BlockBreakEvent blockBreakEvent,\n                                            boolean greenTerra) {\n        if (!ItemUtils.isHoe(blockBreakEvent.getPlayer().getInventory().getItemInMainHand())\n            && !ItemUtils.isAxe(blockBreakEvent.getPlayer().getInventory().getItemInMainHand())) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        final BlockData blockData = blockState.getBlockData();\n\n        if (!(blockData instanceof Ageable ageable)) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        //If the ageable is NOT mature and the player is NOT using a hoe, abort\n\n        final Player player = getPlayer();\n        final Material replantMaterial;\n\n        switch (blockState.getType().getKey().getKey().toLowerCase(Locale.ENGLISH)) {\n            case \"carrots\" -> replantMaterial = Material.matchMaterial(\"CARROT\");\n            case \"wheat\" -> replantMaterial = Material.matchMaterial(\"WHEAT_SEEDS\");\n            case \"nether_wart\" -> replantMaterial = Material.getMaterial(\"NETHER_WART\");\n            case \"potatoes\" -> replantMaterial = Material.matchMaterial(\"POTATO\");\n            case \"beetroots\" -> replantMaterial = Material.matchMaterial(\"BEETROOT_SEEDS\");\n            case \"cocoa\" -> replantMaterial = Material.matchMaterial(\"COCOA_BEANS\");\n            case \"torchflower\" -> replantMaterial = Material.matchMaterial(\"TORCHFLOWER_SEEDS\");\n            default -> {\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (replantMaterial == null) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        if (ItemUtils.isAxe(blockBreakEvent.getPlayer().getInventory().getItemInMainHand())\n                && blockState.getType() != Material.COCOA) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        if (!greenTerra && !ProbabilityUtil.isSkillRNGSuccessful(SubSkillType.HERBALISM_GREEN_THUMB, mmoPlayer)) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        if (!hasItemIncludingOffHand(player, replantMaterial)) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        if (EventUtils.callSubSkillBlockEvent(player, SubSkillType.HERBALISM_GREEN_THUMB, blockState.getBlock())\n                .isCancelled()) {\n            return false;\n        } else {\n            if (!processGrowingPlants(blockState, ageable, blockBreakEvent, greenTerra)) {\n                return false;\n            }\n            // remove the item from the player's inventory\n            removeItemIncludingOffHand(player, replantMaterial, 1);\n            // player.updateInventory(); // Needed until replacement available\n\n            //Play sound\n            SoundManager.sendSound(player, player.getLocation(), SoundType.ITEM_CONSUMED);\n            return true;\n        }\n    }\n\n    private boolean processGrowingPlants(BlockState blockState, Ageable ageable, BlockBreakEvent blockBreakEvent, boolean greenTerra) {\n        //This check is needed\n        if (isBizarreAgeable(ageable)) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        int finalAge;\n        int greenThumbStage = getGreenThumbStage(greenTerra);\n\n        //Immature plants will start over at 0\n        if (!isAgeableMature(ageable)) {\n//            blockBreakEvent.setCancelled(true);\n            startReplantTask(0, blockBreakEvent, blockState, true);\n//            blockState.setType(Material.AIR);\n            blockBreakEvent.setDropItems(false);\n            return true;\n        }\n\n        switch (blockState.getType().getKey().getKey()) {\n\n            case \"potatoes\":\n            case \"carrots\":\n            case \"wheat\":\n\n                    finalAge = getGreenThumbStage(greenTerra);\n                break;\n\n            case \"beetroots\":\n            case \"nether_wart\":\n\n                if (greenTerra || greenThumbStage > 2) {\n                    finalAge = 2;\n                } else if (greenThumbStage == 2) {\n                    finalAge = 1;\n                } else {\n                    finalAge = 0;\n                }\n                break;\n\n            case \"cocoa\":\n\n                if (getGreenThumbStage(greenTerra) >= 2) {\n                    finalAge = 1;\n                } else {\n                    finalAge = 0;\n                }\n                break;\n\n            default:\n                return false;\n        }\n\n\n        //Start the delayed replant\n        startReplantTask(finalAge, blockBreakEvent, blockState, false);\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    private int getGreenThumbStage(boolean greenTerraActive) {\n        if (greenTerraActive)\n            return Math.min(RankUtils.getHighestRank(SubSkillType.HERBALISM_GREEN_THUMB),\n                    RankUtils.getRank(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.HERBALISM_GREEN_THUMB) + 1);\n\n        return RankUtils.getRank(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.HERBALISM_GREEN_THUMB);\n    }\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "src/main/java/com/gmail/nossr50/skills/mining/MiningManager.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package com.gmail.nossr50.skills.mining;\n\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.api.ItemSpawnReason;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.config.experience.ExperienceConfig;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.experience.XPGainReason;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.interactions.NotificationType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.player.McMMOPlayer;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.PrimarySkillType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.SubSkillType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.SuperAbilityType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.mcMMO;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.runnables.skills.AbilityCooldownTask;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.skills.SkillManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.*;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.player.NotificationManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.random.Probability;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.random.ProbabilityUtil;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.skills.RankUtils;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.skills.SkillUtils;\nimport org.apache.commons.lang3.RandomUtils;\nimport org.bukkit.Material;\nimport org.bukkit.block.Block;\nimport org.bukkit.block.BlockState;\nimport org.bukkit.block.Container;\nimport org.bukkit.enchantments.Enchantment;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.Player;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.TNTPrimed;\nimport org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityExplodeEvent;\nimport org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack;\nimport org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;\n\nimport java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.List;\n\npublic class MiningManager extends SkillManager {\n\n    public static final String BUDDING_AMETHYST = \"budding_amethyst\";\n\n    public MiningManager(@NotNull McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer) {\n        super(mcMMOPlayer, PrimarySkillType.MINING);\n    }\n\n    public boolean canUseDemolitionsExpertise() {\n        if (!RankUtils.hasUnlockedSubskill(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.MINING_DEMOLITIONS_EXPERTISE))\n            return false;\n\n        return getSkillLevel() >= BlastMining.getDemolitionExpertUnlockLevel() && Permissions.demolitionsExpertise(getPlayer());\n    }\n\n    public boolean canDetonate() {\n        Player player = getPlayer();\n\n        return canUseBlastMining() && player.isSneaking()\n                && (ItemUtils.isPickaxe(getPlayer().getInventory().getItemInMainHand()) || player.getInventory().getItemInMainHand().getType() == mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getDetonatorItem())\n                && Permissions.remoteDetonation(player);\n    }\n\n    public boolean canUseBlastMining() {\n        //Not checking permissions?\n        return RankUtils.hasUnlockedSubskill(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.MINING_BLAST_MINING);\n    }\n\n    public boolean canUseBiggerBombs() {\n        if (!RankUtils.hasUnlockedSubskill(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.MINING_BIGGER_BOMBS))\n            return false;\n\n        return getSkillLevel() >= BlastMining.getBiggerBombsUnlockLevel() && Permissions.biggerBombs(getPlayer());\n    }\n\n    public boolean canDoubleDrop() {\n        return RankUtils.hasUnlockedSubskill(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.MINING_DOUBLE_DROPS) && Permissions.isSubSkillEnabled(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.MINING_DOUBLE_DROPS);\n    }\n\n    public boolean canMotherLode() {\n        return Permissions.canUseSubSkill(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.MINING_MOTHER_LODE);\n    }\n\n\n    /**\n     * Process double drops & XP gain for Mining.\n     *\n     * @param blockState The {@link BlockState} to check ability activation for\n     */\n    public void miningBlockCheck(BlockState blockState) {\n        Player player = getPlayer();\n\n        applyXpGain(Mining.getBlockXp(blockState), XPGainReason.PVE);\n\n        if (!Permissions.isSubSkillEnabled(player, SubSkillType.MINING_DOUBLE_DROPS)) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        if (mmoPlayer.getAbilityMode(mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().getSuperAbility(skill))) {\n            SkillUtils.handleDurabilityChange(getPlayer().getInventory().getItemInMainHand(), mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getAbilityToolDamage());\n        }\n\n        if (!mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getDoubleDropsEnabled(PrimarySkillType.MINING, blockState.getType()) || !canDoubleDrop())\n            return;\n\n        boolean silkTouch = player.getInventory().getItemInMainHand().containsEnchantment(Enchantment.SILK_TOUCH);\n\n        if (silkTouch && !mcMMO.p.getAdvancedConfig().getDoubleDropSilkTouchEnabled())\n            return;\n\n        //Mining mastery allows for a chance of triple drops\n        if (canMotherLode()) {\n            //Triple Drops failed so do a normal double drops check\n            if (!processTripleDrops(blockState)) {\n                processDoubleDrops(blockState);\n            }\n        } else {\n            //If the user has no mastery, proceed with normal double drop routine\n            processDoubleDrops(blockState);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private boolean processTripleDrops(@NotNull BlockState blockState) {\n        //TODO: Make this readable\n        if (ProbabilityUtil.isSkillRNGSuccessful(SubSkillType.MINING_MOTHER_LODE, mmoPlayer)) {\n            BlockUtils.markDropsAsBonus(blockState, 2);\n            return true;\n        } else {\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void processDoubleDrops(@NotNull BlockState blockState) {\n        //TODO: Make this readable\n        if (ProbabilityUtil.isSkillRNGSuccessful(SubSkillType.MINING_DOUBLE_DROPS, mmoPlayer)) {\n            boolean useTriple = mmoPlayer.getAbilityMode(SuperAbilityType.SUPER_BREAKER) && mcMMO.p.getAdvancedConfig().getAllowMiningTripleDrops();\n            BlockUtils.markDropsAsBonus(blockState, useTriple);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Detonate TNT for Blast Mining\n     */\n    public void remoteDetonation() {\n        Player player = getPlayer();\n        Block targetBlock = player.getTargetBlock(BlockUtils.getTransparentBlocks(), BlastMining.MAXIMUM_REMOTE_DETONATION_DISTANCE);\n\n        //Blast mining cooldown check needs to be first so the player can be messaged\n        if (!blastMiningCooldownOver() || targetBlock.getType() != Material.TNT || !EventUtils.simulateBlockBreak(targetBlock, player)) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        TNTPrimed tnt = player.getWorld().spawn(targetBlock.getLocation(), TNTPrimed.class);\n\n        //SkillUtils.sendSkillMessage(player, SuperAbilityType.BLAST_MINING.getAbilityPlayer(player));\n        NotificationManager.sendPlayerInformation(player, NotificationType.SUPER_ABILITY, \"Mining.Blast.Boom\");\n        //player.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Mining.Blast.Boom\"));\n\n        tnt.setMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_TRACKED_TNT, mmoPlayer.getPlayerMetadata());\n        tnt.setFuseTicks(0);\n        if (mcMMO.getCompatibilityManager().getMinecraftGameVersion().isAtLeast(1, 16, 4)) {\n            tnt.setSource(player);\n        }\n        targetBlock.setType(Material.AIR);\n\n        mmoPlayer.setAbilityDATS(SuperAbilityType.BLAST_MINING, System.currentTimeMillis());\n        mmoPlayer.setAbilityInformed(SuperAbilityType.BLAST_MINING, false);\n        mcMMO.p.getFoliaLib().getImpl().runAtEntityLater(mmoPlayer.getPlayer(), new AbilityCooldownTask(mmoPlayer, SuperAbilityType.BLAST_MINING), (long) SuperAbilityType.BLAST_MINING.getCooldown() * Misc.TICK_CONVERSION_FACTOR);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Handler for explosion drops and XP gain.\n     *\n     * @param yield The % of blocks to drop\n     * @param event The {@link EntityExplodeEvent}\n     */\n    //TODO: Rewrite this garbage\n    public void blastMiningDropProcessing(float yield, EntityExplodeEvent event) {\n        if (yield == 0)\n            return;\n\n        //Strip out only stuff that gives mining XP\n        List<BlockState> ores = new ArrayList<>();\n\n        List<BlockState> notOres = new ArrayList<>();\n        for (Block targetBlock : event.blockList()) {\n            BlockState blockState = targetBlock.getState();\n            //Containers usually have 0 XP unless someone edited their config in a very strange way\n            if (ExperienceConfig.getInstance().getXp(PrimarySkillType.MINING, targetBlock) != 0\n                    && !(targetBlock instanceof Container)\n                    && !mcMMO.getUserBlockTracker().isIneligible(targetBlock)) {\n                if (BlockUtils.isOre(blockState)) {\n                    ores.add(blockState);\n                } else {\n                    notOres.add(blockState);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        int xp = 0;\n\n        float oreBonus = (float) (getOreBonus() / 100);\n        float debrisReduction = (float) (getDebrisReduction() / 100);\n        int dropMultiplier = getDropMultiplier();\n        float debrisYield = yield - debrisReduction;\n\n        //Drop \"debris\" based on skill modifiers\n        for(BlockState blockState : notOres) {\n            if (isDropIllegal(blockState.getType()))\n                continue;\n\n            if (Probability.ofPercent(50).evaluate()) {\n                Misc.spawnItem(getPlayer(), Misc.getBlockCenter(blockState), new ItemStack(blockState.getType()), ItemSpawnReason.BLAST_MINING_DEBRIS_NON_ORES); // Initial block that would have been dropped\n            }\n        }\n\n        for (BlockState blockState : ores) {\n            if (isDropIllegal(blockState.getType()))\n                continue;\n\n            if (RandomUtils.nextFloat() < (yield + oreBonus)) {\n                xp += Mining.getBlockXp(blockState);\n\n                Misc.spawnItem(getPlayer(), Misc.getBlockCenter(blockState), new ItemStack(blockState.getType()), ItemSpawnReason.BLAST_MINING_ORES); // Initial block that would have been dropped\n\n                if (mcMMO.p.getAdvancedConfig().isBlastMiningBonusDropsEnabled() && !mcMMO.getUserBlockTracker().isIneligible(blockState)) {\n                    for (int i = 1; i < dropMultiplier; i++) {\n//                        Bukkit.broadcastMessage(\"Bonus Drop on Ore: \"+blockState.getType().toString());\n                        Misc.spawnItem(getPlayer(), Misc.getBlockCenter(blockState), new ItemStack(blockState.getType()), ItemSpawnReason.BLAST_MINING_ORES_BONUS_DROP); // Initial block that would have been dropped\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        //Replace the event blocklist with the newYield list\n        event.setYield(0F);\n//        event.blockList().clear();\n//        event.blockList().addAll(notOres);\n\n        applyXpGain(xp, XPGainReason.PVE);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Checks if it would be illegal (in vanilla) to obtain the block\n     * Certain things should never drop ( such as budding_amethyst )\n     *\n     * @param material target material\n     * @return true if it's not legal to obtain the block through normal gameplay\n     */\n    public boolean isDropIllegal(@NotNull Material material) {\n        return material.getKey().getKey().equalsIgnoreCase(BUDDING_AMETHYST);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Increases the blast radius of the explosion.\n     *\n     * @param radius to modify\n     * @return modified radius\n     */\n    public float biggerBombs(float radius) {\n        return (float) (radius + getBlastRadiusModifier());\n    }\n\n    public double processDemolitionsExpertise(double damage) {\n        return damage * ((100.0D - getBlastDamageModifier()) / 100.0D);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Gets the Blast Mining tier\n     *\n     * @return the Blast Mining tier\n     */\n    public int getBlastMiningTier() {\n        return RankUtils.getRank(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.MINING_BLAST_MINING);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Gets the Blast Mining tier\n     *\n     * @return the Blast Mining tier\n     */\n    public double getOreBonus() {\n        return getOreBonus(getBlastMiningTier());\n    }\n\n    public static double getOreBonus(int rank) {\n        return mcMMO.p.getAdvancedConfig().getOreBonus(rank);\n    }\n\n    public static double getDebrisReduction(int rank) {\n        return mcMMO.p.getAdvancedConfig().getDebrisReduction(rank);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Gets the Blast Mining tier\n     *\n     * @return the Blast Mining tier\n     */\n    public double getDebrisReduction() {\n        return getDebrisReduction(getBlastMiningTier());\n    }\n\n    public static int getDropMultiplier(int rank) {\n        return mcMMO.p.getAdvancedConfig().getDropMultiplier(rank);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Gets the Blast Mining tier\n     *\n     * @return the Blast Mining tier\n     */\n    public int getDropMultiplier() {\n        if (!mcMMO.p.getAdvancedConfig().isBlastMiningBonusDropsEnabled()) {\n            return 0;\n        }\n\n        return switch (getBlastMiningTier()) {\n            case 8, 7 -> 3;\n            case 6, 5, 4, 3 -> 2;\n            case 2, 1 -> 1;\n            default -> 0;\n        };\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Gets the Blast Mining tier\n     *\n     * @return the Blast Mining tier\n     */\n    public double getBlastRadiusModifier() {\n        return BlastMining.getBlastRadiusModifier(getBlastMiningTier());\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Gets the Blast Mining tier\n     *\n     * @return the Blast Mining tier\n     */\n    public double getBlastDamageModifier() {\n        return BlastMining.getBlastDamageDecrease(getBlastMiningTier());\n    }\n\n    private boolean blastMiningCooldownOver() {\n        int timeRemaining = mmoPlayer.calculateTimeRemaining(SuperAbilityType.BLAST_MINING);\n\n        if (timeRemaining > 0) {\n            //getPlayer().sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Skills.TooTired\", timeRemaining));\n            NotificationManager.sendPlayerInformation(getPlayer(), NotificationType.ABILITY_COOLDOWN, \"Skills.TooTired\", String.valueOf(timeRemaining));\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        return true;\n    }\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "src/main/java/com/gmail/nossr50/skills/taming/TamingManager.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package com.gmail.nossr50.skills.taming;\n\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.config.experience.ExperienceConfig;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.experience.XPGainReason;\nimport
