com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.interactions.NotificationType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.player.McMMOPlayer;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.PrimarySkillType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.SubSkillType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.subskills.taming.CallOfTheWildType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.subskills.taming.TamingSummon;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.locale.LocaleLoader;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.mcMMO;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.metadata.MobMetaFlagType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.skills.SkillManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.Misc;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.Permissions;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.player.NotificationManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.random.ProbabilityUtil;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.skills.ParticleEffectUtils;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.skills.RankUtils;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.sounds.SoundManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.sounds.SoundType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.text.StringUtils;\nimport org.bukkit.Location;\nimport org.bukkit.Material;\nimport org.bukkit.attribute.Attribute;\nimport org.bukkit.attribute.AttributeInstance;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.*;\nimport org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack;\nimport org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;\n\nimport java.util.HashMap;\n\nimport static com.gmail.nossr50.util.MobMetadataUtils.flagMetadata;\n\npublic class TamingManager extends SkillManager {\n    //TODO: Temporary static cache, will be changed in 2.2\n    private static HashMap<Material, CallOfTheWildType> summoningItems;\n    private static HashMap<CallOfTheWildType, TamingSummon> cotwSummonDataProperties;\n    private long lastSummonTimeStamp;\n\n    public TamingManager(@NotNull McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer) {\n        super(mcMMOPlayer, PrimarySkillType.TAMING);\n        init();\n    }\n\n    //TODO: Hacky stuff for 2.1, will be cleaned up in 2.2\n    private void init() {\n        //prevents accidentally summoning too many things when holding down left click\n        lastSummonTimeStamp = 0L;\n\n        //Init per-player tracking of summoned entities\n        mcMMO.getTransientEntityTracker().initPlayer(mmoPlayer.getPlayer());\n\n        //Hacky stuff used as a band-aid\n        initStaticCaches();\n    }\n\n    private void initStaticCaches() {\n        //TODO: Temporary static cache, will be changed in 2.2\n        //This is shared between instances of TamingManager\n        if (summoningItems == null) {\n            summoningItems = new HashMap<>();\n\n            summoningItems.put(mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getTamingCOTWMaterial(CallOfTheWildType.CAT.getConfigEntityTypeEntry()), CallOfTheWildType.CAT);\n            summoningItems.put(mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getTamingCOTWMaterial(CallOfTheWildType.WOLF.getConfigEntityTypeEntry()), CallOfTheWildType.WOLF);\n            summoningItems.put(mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getTamingCOTWMaterial(CallOfTheWildType.HORSE.getConfigEntityTypeEntry()), CallOfTheWildType.HORSE);\n        }\n\n        //TODO: Temporary static cache, will be changed in 2.2\n        //This is shared between instances of TamingManager\n        if (cotwSummonDataProperties == null) {\n            cotwSummonDataProperties = new HashMap<>();\n\n            for(CallOfTheWildType callOfTheWildType : CallOfTheWildType.values()) {\n                Material itemSummonMaterial = mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getTamingCOTWMaterial(callOfTheWildType.getConfigEntityTypeEntry());\n                int itemAmountRequired = mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getTamingCOTWCost(callOfTheWildType.getConfigEntityTypeEntry());\n                int entitiesSummonedPerCOTW = mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getTamingCOTWAmount(callOfTheWildType.getConfigEntityTypeEntry());\n                int summonLifespanSeconds = mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getTamingCOTWLength(callOfTheWildType.getConfigEntityTypeEntry());\n                int perPlayerMaxAmount = mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getTamingCOTWMaxAmount(callOfTheWildType.getConfigEntityTypeEntry());\n\n                TamingSummon tamingSummon = new TamingSummon(callOfTheWildType, itemSummonMaterial, itemAmountRequired, entitiesSummonedPerCOTW, summonLifespanSeconds, perPlayerMaxAmount);\n                cotwSummonDataProperties.put(callOfTheWildType, tamingSummon);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    public boolean canUseThickFur() {\n        return RankUtils.hasUnlockedSubskill(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.TAMING_THICK_FUR)\n                && Permissions.isSubSkillEnabled(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.TAMING_THICK_FUR);\n    }\n\n    public boolean canUseEnvironmentallyAware() {\n        return RankUtils.hasUnlockedSubskill(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.TAMING_ENVIRONMENTALLY_AWARE)\n                && Permissions.isSubSkillEnabled(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.TAMING_ENVIRONMENTALLY_AWARE);\n    }\n\n    public boolean canUseShockProof() {\n        return RankUtils.hasUnlockedSubskill(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.TAMING_SHOCK_PROOF)\n                && Permissions.isSubSkillEnabled(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.TAMING_SHOCK_PROOF);\n    }\n\n    public boolean canUseHolyHound() {\n        return RankUtils.hasUnlockedSubskill(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.TAMING_ENVIRONMENTALLY_AWARE)\n                && Permissions.isSubSkillEnabled(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.TAMING_HOLY_HOUND);\n    }\n\n    public boolean canUseFastFoodService() {\n        return RankUtils.hasUnlockedSubskill(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.TAMING_FAST_FOOD_SERVICE)\n                && Permissions.isSubSkillEnabled(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.TAMING_FAST_FOOD_SERVICE);\n    }\n\n    public boolean canUseSharpenedClaws() {\n        return RankUtils.hasUnlockedSubskill(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.TAMING_SHARPENED_CLAWS)\n                && Permissions.isSubSkillEnabled(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.TAMING_SHARPENED_CLAWS);\n    }\n\n    public boolean canUseGore() {\n        if (!RankUtils.hasUnlockedSubskill(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.TAMING_GORE))\n            return false;\n\n        return Permissions.isSubSkillEnabled(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.TAMING_GORE);\n    }\n\n    public boolean canUseBeastLore() {\n        if (!RankUtils.hasUnlockedSubskill(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.TAMING_BEAST_LORE))\n            return false;\n\n        return Permissions.isSubSkillEnabled(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.TAMING_BEAST_LORE);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Award XP for taming.\n     *\n     * @param entity The LivingEntity to award XP for\n     */\n    public void awardTamingXP(@NotNull LivingEntity entity) {\n        applyXpGain(ExperienceConfig.getInstance().getTamingXP(entity.getType()), XPGainReason.PVE);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Apply the Fast Food Service ability.\n     *\n     * @param wolf The wolf using the ability\n     * @param damage The damage being absorbed by the wolf\n     */\n    public void fastFoodService(@NotNull Wolf wolf, double damage) {\n        if (!ProbabilityUtil.isSkillRNGSuccessful(SubSkillType.TAMING_FAST_FOOD_SERVICE, mmoPlayer)) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        double health = wolf.getHealth();\n        double maxHealth = wolf.getMaxHealth();\n\n        if (health < maxHealth) {\n            double newHealth = health + damage;\n            wolf.setHealth(Math.min(newHealth, maxHealth));\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Apply the Gore ability.\n     *\n     * @param target The LivingEntity to apply Gore on\n     * @param damage The initial damage\n     */\n    public double gore(@NotNull LivingEntity target, double damage) {\n        damage = (damage * Taming.goreModifier) - damage;\n\n        return damage;\n    }\n\n    public double sharpenedClaws() {\n        return Taming.sharpenedClawsBonusDamage;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Summon an ocelot to your side.\n     */\n    public void summonOcelot() {\n        if (!RankUtils.hasUnlockedSubskill(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.TAMING_CALL_OF_THE_WILD))\n            return;\n\n        if (!Permissions.callOfTheWild(getPlayer(), EntityType.OCELOT)) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        processCallOfTheWild();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Summon a wolf to your side.\n     */\n    public void summonWolf() {\n        if (!RankUtils.hasUnlockedSubskill(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.TAMING_CALL_OF_THE_WILD))\n            return;\n\n        if (!Permissions.callOfTheWild(getPlayer(), EntityType.WOLF)) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        processCallOfTheWild();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Summon a horse to your side.\n     */\n    public void summonHorse() {\n        if (!RankUtils.hasUnlockedSubskill(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.TAMING_CALL_OF_THE_WILD))\n            return;\n\n        if (!Permissions.callOfTheWild(getPlayer(), EntityType.HORSE)) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        processCallOfTheWild();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Handle the Beast Lore ability.\n     *\n     * @param target The entity to examine\n     */\n    public void beastLore(LivingEntity target) {\n        Player player = getPlayer();\n        Tameable beast = (Tameable) target;\n\n        String message = LocaleLoader.getString(\"Combat.BeastLore\") + \" \";\n\n        if (beast.isTamed() && beast.getOwner() != null) {\n            message = message.concat(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Combat.BeastLoreOwner\", beast.getOwner().getName()) + \" \");\n        }\n\n        message = message.concat(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Combat.BeastLoreHealth\", target.getHealth(), target.getMaxHealth()));\n\n        // Bred mules & donkeys can actually have horse-like stats, but llamas cannot.\n        if (beast instanceof AbstractHorse horseLikeCreature && !(beast instanceof Llama)) {\n            AttributeInstance jumpAttribute = horseLikeCreature.getAttribute(mcMMO.p.getAttributeMapper().getHorseJumpStrength());\n\n            if (jumpAttribute != null) {\n                double jumpStrength = jumpAttribute.getValue();\n                // Taken from https://minecraft.wiki/w/Horse#Jump_strength\n                jumpStrength = -0.1817584952 * Math.pow(jumpStrength, 3) + 3.689713992 * Math.pow(jumpStrength, 2) + 2.128599134 * jumpStrength - 0.343930367;\n                message = message.concat(\"\\n\" + LocaleLoader.getString(\"Combat.BeastLoreHorseSpeed\", horseLikeCreature.getAttribute(Attribute.GENERIC_MOVEMENT_SPEED).getValue() * 43))\n                        .concat(\"\\n\" + LocaleLoader.getString(\"Combat.BeastLoreHorseJumpStrength\", jumpStrength));\n            }\n        }\n\n        player.sendMessage(message);\n    }\n\n    public void processEnvironmentallyAware(@NotNull Wolf wolf, double damage) {\n        if (damage > wolf.getHealth()) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        Player owner = getPlayer();\n\n        wolf.teleport(owner);\n        NotificationManager.sendPlayerInformation(owner, NotificationType.SUBSKILL_MESSAGE, \"Taming.Listener.Wolf\");\n    }\n\n    public void pummel(LivingEntity target, Wolf wolf) {\n        if (!RankUtils.hasUnlockedSubskill(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.TAMING_PUMMEL))\n            return;\n\n        if (!ProbabilityUtil.isStaticSkillRNGSuccessful(PrimarySkillType.TAMING, mmoPlayer, mcMMO.p.getAdvancedConfig().getPummelChance()))\n            return;\n\n        ParticleEffectUtils.playGreaterImpactEffect(target);\n        target.setVelocity(wolf.getLocation().getDirection().normalize().multiply(1.5D));\n\n        if (target instanceof Player defender) {\n\n            if (NotificationManager.doesPlayerUseNotifications(defender)) {\n                NotificationManager.sendPlayerInformation(defender, NotificationType.SUBSKILL_MESSAGE, \"Taming.SubSkill.Pummel.TargetMessage\");\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    public void attackTarget(LivingEntity target) {\n        if (target instanceof Tameable tameable) {\n            if (tameable.getOwner() == getPlayer()) {\n                return;\n            }\n        }\n        double range = 5;\n        Player player = getPlayer();\n\n        for (Entity entity : player.getNearbyEntities(range, range, range)) {\n            if (entity.getType() != EntityType.WOLF) {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            Wolf wolf = (Wolf) entity;\n\n            if (!wolf.isTamed() || (wolf.getOwner() != player) || wolf.isSitting()) {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            wolf.setTarget(target);\n        }\n    }\n\n\n    private void processCallOfTheWild() {\n        //Prevent summoning too many things accidentally if a player holds down the button\n        if (lastSummonTimeStamp + 150 > System.currentTimeMillis()) {\n            return;\n        } else {\n            lastSummonTimeStamp = System.currentTimeMillis();\n        }\n\n        Player player = getPlayer();\n        ItemStack itemInMainHand = player.getInventory().getItemInMainHand();\n\n        //Check if the item the player is currently holding is a COTW item\n        if (isCOTWItem(itemInMainHand)) {\n            //Get the summoning type\n            CallOfTheWildType callOfTheWildType = summoningItems.get(itemInMainHand.getType());\n            TamingSummon tamingSummon = cotwSummonDataProperties.get(callOfTheWildType);\n\n            //Players will pay for the cost if at least one thing was summoned\n            int amountSummoned = 0;\n\n            //Check to see if players have the correct amount of the item required to summon\n            if (itemInMainHand.getAmount() >= tamingSummon.getItemAmountRequired()) {\n                //Initial Spawn location\n                Location spawnLocation = Misc.getLocationOffset(player.getLocation(), 1);\n\n                //COTW can summon multiple entities per usage\n                for (int i = 0; i < tamingSummon.getEntitiesSummoned(); i++) {\n\n                    if (getAmountCurrentlySummoned(callOfTheWildType) >= tamingSummon.getSummonCap()) {\n                        NotificationManager.sendPlayerInformationChatOnly(player, \"Taming.Summon.COTW.Limit\",\n                                String.valueOf(tamingSummon.getSummonCap()),\n                                StringUtils.getCapitalized(callOfTheWildType.toString()));\n                        break;\n                    }\n\n                    spawnLocation = Misc.getLocationOffset(spawnLocation, 1);\n                    spawnCOTWEntity(callOfTheWildType, spawnLocation, tamingSummon.getEntityType());\n\n                    //Inform the player about what they have just done\n                    if (tamingSummon.getSummonLifespan() > 0) {\n                        NotificationManager.sendPlayerInformationChatOnly(player, \"Taming.Summon.COTW.Success.WithLifespan\",\n                                StringUtils.getCapitalized(callOfTheWildType.toString()), String.valueOf(tamingSummon.getSummonLifespan()));\n                    } else {\n                        NotificationManager.sendPlayerInformationChatOnly(player, \"Taming.Summon.COTW.Success.WithoutLifespan\", StringUtils.getCapitalized(callOfTheWildType.toString()));\n                    }\n\n                    //Send Sound\n                    SoundManager.sendSound(player, player.getLocation(), SoundType.ABILITY_ACTIVATED_GENERIC);\n\n                    amountSummoned++;\n                }\n\n                //Remove items from the player if they had at least one entity summoned successfully\n                if (amountSummoned >= 1) {\n                    //Remove the items used to summon\n                    int itemAmountAfterPayingCost = itemInMainHand.getAmount() - tamingSummon.getItemAmountRequired();\n                    itemInMainHand.setAmount(itemAmountAfterPayingCost);\n                    player.updateInventory();\n                }\n\n            } else {\n                //Player did not have enough of the item in their main hand\n                int difference = tamingSummon.getItemAmountRequired() - itemInMainHand.getAmount();\n                NotificationManager.sendPlayerInformationChatOnly(player, \"Taming.Summon.COTW.NeedMoreItems\", String.valueOf(difference), StringUtils.getPrettyItemString(itemInMainHand.getType()));\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void spawnCOTWEntity(CallOfTheWildType callOfTheWildType, Location spawnLocation, EntityType entityType) {\n        switch (callOfTheWildType) {\n            case CAT ->\n                //Entity type is needed for cats because in 1.13 and below we spawn ocelots, in 1.14 and above we spawn cats\n                    spawnCat(spawnLocation, entityType);\n            case HORSE -> spawnHorse(spawnLocation);\n            case WOLF -> spawnWolf(spawnLocation);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void spawnWolf(Location spawnLocation) {\n        LivingEntity callOfWildEntity = (LivingEntity) getPlayer().getWorld().spawnEntity(spawnLocation, EntityType.WOLF);\n\n        //This is used to prevent XP gains for damaging this entity\n        applyMetaDataToCOTWEntity(callOfWildEntity);\n\n        setBaseCOTWEntityProperties(callOfWildEntity);\n\n        ((Wolf) callOfWildEntity).setAdult();\n        addToTracker(callOfWildEntity, CallOfTheWildType.WOLF);\n\n        //Setup wolf stats\n        callOfWildEntity.setMaxHealth(20.0);\n        callOfWildEntity.setHealth(callOfWildEntity.getMaxHealth());\n\n        callOfWildEntity.setCustomName(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Taming.Summon.Name.Format\", getPlayer().getName(), StringUtils.getPrettyEntityTypeString(EntityType.WOLF)));\n    }\n\n    private void spawnCat(Location spawnLocation, EntityType entityType) {\n        LivingEntity callOfWildEntity = (LivingEntity) getPlayer().getWorld().spawnEntity(spawnLocation, entityType);\n\n        //This is used to prevent XP gains for damaging this entity\n        applyMetaDataToCOTWEntity(callOfWildEntity);\n\n        setBaseCOTWEntityProperties(callOfWildEntity);\n\n        addToTracker(callOfWildEntity, CallOfTheWildType.CAT);\n\n        //Randomize the cat\n        if (callOfWildEntity instanceof Ocelot) {\n            int numberOfTypes = Ocelot.Type.values().length;\n            ((Ocelot) callOfWildEntity).setCatType(Ocelot.Type.values()[Misc.getRandom().nextInt(numberOfTypes)]);\n            ((Ocelot) callOfWildEntity).setAdult();\n        } else if (callOfWildEntity instanceof Cat) {\n            int numberOfTypes = Cat.Type.values().length;\n            ((Cat) callOfWildEntity).setCatType(Cat.Type.values()[Misc.getRandom().nextInt(numberOfTypes)]);\n            ((Cat) callOfWildEntity).setAdult();\n        }\n\n        callOfWildEntity.setCustomName(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Taming.Summon.Name.Format\", getPlayer().getName(), StringUtils.getPrettyEntityTypeString(entityType)));\n\n        //Particle effect\n        ParticleEffectUtils.playCallOfTheWildEffect(callOfWildEntity);\n    }\n\n    private void spawnHorse(Location spawnLocation) {\n        LivingEntity callOfWildEntity = (LivingEntity) getPlayer().getWorld().spawnEntity(spawnLocation, EntityType.HORSE);\n        applyMetaDataToCOTWEntity(callOfWildEntity);\n\n        setBaseCOTWEntityProperties(callOfWildEntity);\n\n        addToTracker(callOfWildEntity, CallOfTheWildType.HORSE);\n\n        //Randomize Horse\n        Horse horse = (Horse) callOfWildEntity;\n\n        callOfWildEntity.setMaxHealth(15.0 + (Misc.getRandom().nextDouble() * 15));\n        callOfWildEntity.setHealth(callOfWildEntity.getMaxHealth());\n        horse.setColor(Horse.Color.values()[Misc.getRandom().nextInt(Horse.Color.values().length)]);\n        horse.setStyle(Horse.Style.values()[Misc.getRandom().nextInt(Horse.Style.values().length)]);\n        horse.setJumpStrength(Math.max(mcMMO.p.getAdvancedConfig().getMinHorseJumpStrength(), Math.min(Math.min(Misc.getRandom().nextDouble(), Misc.getRandom().nextDouble()) * 2, mcMMO.p.getAdvancedConfig().getMaxHorseJumpStrength())));\n        horse.setAdult();\n\n        //TODO: setSpeed, once available\n\n        callOfWildEntity.setCustomName(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Taming.Summon.Name.Format\", getPlayer().getName(), StringUtils.getPrettyEntityTypeString(EntityType.HORSE)));\n\n        //Particle effect\n        ParticleEffectUtils.playCallOfTheWildEffect(callOfWildEntity);\n    }\n\n    private void setBaseCOTWEntityProperties(LivingEntity callOfWildEntity) {\n        ((Tameable) callOfWildEntity).setOwner(getPlayer());\n        callOfWildEntity.setRemoveWhenFarAway(false);\n    }\n\n    private void applyMetaDataToCOTWEntity(LivingEntity summonedEntity) {\n        //This helps identify the entity as being summoned by COTW\n        flagMetadata(MobMetaFlagType.COTW_SUMMONED_MOB, summonedEntity);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Whether the itemstack is used for COTW\n     * @param itemStack target ItemStack\n     * @return true if it is used for any COTW\n     */\n    public boolean isCOTWItem(@NotNull ItemStack itemStack) {\n        return summoningItems.containsKey(itemStack.getType());\n    }\n\n    private int getAmountCurrentlySummoned(@NotNull CallOfTheWildType callOfTheWildType) {\n        return mcMMO.getTransientEntityTracker().getAmountCurrentlySummoned(getPlayer().getUniqueId(), callOfTheWildType);\n    }\n\n    private void addToTracker(@NotNull LivingEntity livingEntity, @NotNull CallOfTheWildType callOfTheWildType) {\n        mcMMO.getTransientEntityTracker().registerEntity(getPlayer().getUniqueId(), new TrackedTamingEntity(livingEntity, callOfTheWildType, getPlayer()));\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Remove all tracked entities from existence if they currently exist\n     * Clear the tracked entity lists afterwards\n     */\n    //TODO: The way this tracker was written is garbo, I should just rewrite it, I'll save that for a future update\n    public void cleanupAllSummons() {\n        mcMMO.getTransientEntityTracker().cleanupPlayer(getPlayer());\n    }\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "src/main/java/com/gmail/nossr50/skills/unarmed/UnarmedManager.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package com.gmail.nossr50.skills.unarmed;\n\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.api.ItemSpawnReason;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.interactions.NotificationType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.player.McMMOPlayer;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.PrimarySkillType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.SubSkillType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.SuperAbilityType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.ToolType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.mcMMO;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.skills.SkillManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.*;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.player.NotificationManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.player.UserManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.random.ProbabilityUtil;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.skills.RankUtils;\nimport org.bukkit.Material;\nimport org.bukkit.block.BlockState;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.Item;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.LivingEntity;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.Player;\nimport org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack;\nimport org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;\n\npublic class UnarmedManager extends SkillManager {\n\n    public UnarmedManager(McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer) {\n        super(mcMMOPlayer, PrimarySkillType.UNARMED);\n    }\n\n    public boolean canActivateAbility() {\n        return mmoPlayer.getToolPreparationMode(ToolType.FISTS) && Permissions.berserk(getPlayer());\n    }\n\n    public boolean canUseSteelArm() {\n        if (!RankUtils.hasUnlockedSubskill(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.UNARMED_STEEL_ARM_STYLE))\n            return false;\n\n        return Permissions.isSubSkillEnabled(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.UNARMED_STEEL_ARM_STYLE);\n    }\n\n    public boolean canUseBerserk() {\n        return mmoPlayer.getAbilityMode(SuperAbilityType.BERSERK);\n    }\n\n    public boolean canDisarm(LivingEntity target) {\n        if (!RankUtils.hasUnlockedSubskill(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.UNARMED_DISARM))\n            return false;\n\n        return target instanceof Player && ((Player) target).getInventory().getItemInMainHand().getType() != Material.AIR && Permissions.isSubSkillEnabled(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.UNARMED_DISARM);\n    }\n\n    public boolean canDeflect() {\n        if (!RankUtils.hasUnlockedSubskill(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.UNARMED_ARROW_DEFLECT))\n            return false;\n\n        Player player = getPlayer();\n\n        return ItemUtils.isUnarmed(player.getInventory().getItemInMainHand()) && Permissions.isSubSkillEnabled(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.UNARMED_ARROW_DEFLECT);\n    }\n\n    public boolean canUseBlockCracker() {\n        if (!RankUtils.hasUnlockedSubskill(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.UNARMED_BLOCK_CRACKER))\n            return false;\n\n        return Permissions.isSubSkillEnabled(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.UNARMED_BLOCK_CRACKER);\n    }\n\n    public boolean blockCrackerCheck(@NotNull BlockState blockState) {\n        if (!ProbabilityUtil.isNonRNGSkillActivationSuccessful(SubSkillType.UNARMED_BLOCK_CRACKER, mmoPlayer)) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        switch (blockState.getType()) {\n            case STONE_BRICKS:\n                if (!Unarmed.blockCrackerSmoothBrick) {\n                    return false;\n                }\n\n                blockState.getBlock().setType(Material.CRACKED_STONE_BRICKS);\n                return true;\n            case INFESTED_STONE_BRICKS:\n                if (!Unarmed.blockCrackerSmoothBrick) {\n                    return false;\n                }\n\n                blockState.getBlock().setType(Material.INFESTED_CRACKED_STONE_BRICKS);\n                return true;\n\n            default:\n                return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Check for disarm.\n     *\n     * @param defender The defending player\n     */\n    public void disarmCheck(@NotNull Player defender) {\n        if (ProbabilityUtil.isSkillRNGSuccessful(SubSkillType.UNARMED_DISARM, mmoPlayer) && !hasIronGrip(defender)) {\n            if (EventUtils.callDisarmEvent(defender).isCancelled()) {\n                return;\n            }\n\n            if (UserManager.getPlayer(defender) == null)\n                return;\n\n            Item item = Misc.spawnItem(getPlayer(), defender.getLocation(), defender.getInventory().getItemInMainHand(), ItemSpawnReason.UNARMED_DISARMED_ITEM);\n\n            if (item != null && mcMMO.p.getAdvancedConfig().getDisarmProtected()) {\n                item.setMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_DISARMED_ITEM, UserManager.getPlayer(defender).getPlayerMetadata());\n            }\n\n            defender.getInventory().setItemInMainHand(new ItemStack(Material.AIR));\n            NotificationManager.sendPlayerInformation(defender, NotificationType.SUBSKILL_MESSAGE, \"Skills.Disarmed\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Check for arrow deflection.\n     */\n    public boolean deflectCheck() {\n        if (ProbabilityUtil.isSkillRNGSuccessful(SubSkillType.UNARMED_ARROW_DEFLECT, mmoPlayer)) {\n            NotificationManager.sendPlayerInformation(getPlayer(), NotificationType.SUBSKILL_MESSAGE, \"Combat.ArrowDeflect\");\n            return true;\n        }\n\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Handle the effects of the Berserk ability\n     *\n     * @param damage The amount of damage initially dealt by the event\n     */\n    public double berserkDamage(double damage) {\n        damage = ((damage * Unarmed.berserkDamageModifier) * mmoPlayer.getAttackStrength()) - damage;\n\n        return damage;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Handle the effects of the Iron Arm ability\n     */\n    public double calculateSteelArmStyleDamage() {\n        if (ProbabilityUtil.isNonRNGSkillActivationSuccessful(SubSkillType.UNARMED_STEEL_ARM_STYLE, mmoPlayer)) {\n            return getSteelArmStyleDamage();\n        }\n\n        return 0;\n    }\n\n    public double getSteelArmStyleDamage() {\n        double rank = RankUtils.getRank(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.UNARMED_STEEL_ARM_STYLE);\n\n        double bonus = 0;\n\n        if (rank >= 18)\n            bonus = 1 + rank - 18;\n\n        double finalBonus = bonus + 0.5 + (rank / 2);\n\n\n        if (mcMMO.p.getAdvancedConfig().isSteelArmDamageCustom()) {\n            return mcMMO.p.getAdvancedConfig().getSteelArmOverride(RankUtils.getRank(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.UNARMED_STEEL_ARM_STYLE), finalBonus);\n        } else {\n            return finalBonus;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Check Iron Grip ability success\n     *\n     * @param defender The defending player\n     * @return true if the defender was not disarmed, false otherwise\n     */\n    private boolean hasIronGrip(@NotNull Player defender) {\n        if (!Misc.isNPCEntityExcludingVillagers(defender)\n                && Permissions.isSubSkillEnabled(defender, SubSkillType.UNARMED_IRON_GRIP)\n                && ProbabilityUtil.isSkillRNGSuccessful(SubSkillType.UNARMED_IRON_GRIP, UserManager.getPlayer(defender))) {\n            NotificationManager.sendPlayerInformation(defender, NotificationType.SUBSKILL_MESSAGE, \"Unarmed.Ability.IronGrip.Defender\");\n            NotificationManager.sendPlayerInformation(getPlayer(), NotificationType.SUBSKILL_MESSAGE, \"Unarmed.Ability.IronGrip.Attacker\");\n\n            return true;\n        }\n\n        return false;\n    }\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "src/main/java/com/gmail/nossr50/skills/woodcutting/WoodcuttingManager.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package com.gmail.nossr50.skills.woodcutting;\n\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.api.FakeBlockBreakEventType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.api.ItemSpawnReason;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.config.experience.ExperienceConfig;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.experience.XPGainReason;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.experience.XPGainSource;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.interactions.NotificationType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.player.McMMOPlayer;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.PrimarySkillType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.SubSkillType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.SuperAbilityType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.mcMMO;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.skills.SkillManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.*;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.player.NotificationManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.random.ProbabilityUtil;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.skills.CombatUtils;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.skills.RankUtils;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.skills.SkillUtils;\nimport org.bukkit.Bukkit;\nimport org.bukkit.Material;\nimport org.bukkit.block.Block;\nimport org.bukkit.block.BlockFace;\nimport org.bukkit.block.BlockState;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.Player;\nimport org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerItemDamageEvent;\nimport org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack;\nimport org.bukkit.inventory.meta.Damageable;\nimport org.bukkit.inventory.meta.ItemMeta;\nimport org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;\n\nimport java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.HashSet;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.Set;\nimport java.util.concurrent.ThreadLocalRandom;\n\nimport static com.gmail.nossr50.util.Misc.getBlockCenter;\nimport static com.gmail.nossr50.util.Misc.spawnItem;\nimport static com.gmail.nossr50.util.skills.RankUtils.hasUnlockedSubskill;\n\n//TODO: Seems to not be using the item drop event for bonus drops, may want to change that.. or may not be able to be changed?\npublic class WoodcuttingManager extends SkillManager {\n    public static final String SAPLING = \"sapling\";\n    public static final String PROPAGULE = \"propagule\";\n    private boolean treeFellerReachedThreshold = false;\n    private static int treeFellerThreshold; //TODO: Shared setting, will be removed in 2.2\n\n    /**\n     * The x/y differences to the blocks in a flat cylinder around the center\n     * block, which is excluded.\n     */\n    private static final int[][] directions = {\n            new int[] {-2, -1}, new int[] {-2, 0}, new int[] {-2, 1},\n            new int[] {-1, -2}, new int[] {-1, -1}, new int[] {-1, 0}, new int[] {-1, 1}, new int[] {-1, 2},\n            new int[] { 0, -2}, new int[] { 0, -1},                    new int[] { 0, 1}, new int[] { 0, 2},\n            new int[] { 1, -2}, new int[] { 1, -1}, new int[] { 1, 0}, new int[] { 1, 1}, new int[] { 1, 2},\n            new int[] { 2, -1}, new int[] { 2, 0}, new int[] { 2, 1},\n    };\n\n    public WoodcuttingManager(McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer) {\n        super(mcMMOPlayer, PrimarySkillType.WOODCUTTING);\n        treeFellerThreshold = mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getTreeFellerThreshold();\n    }\n\n    public boolean canUseLeafBlower(ItemStack heldItem) {\n        return Permissions.isSubSkillEnabled(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.WOODCUTTING_LEAF_BLOWER)\n                && hasUnlockedSubskill(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.WOODCUTTING_LEAF_BLOWER)\n                && ItemUtils.isAxe(heldItem);\n    }\n\n    public boolean canUseTreeFeller(ItemStack heldItem) {\n        return mmoPlayer.getAbilityMode(SuperAbilityType.TREE_FELLER)\n                && ItemUtils.isAxe(heldItem);\n    }\n\n    private boolean checkHarvestLumberActivation(Material material) {\n        return Permissions.isSubSkillEnabled(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.WOODCUTTING_HARVEST_LUMBER)\n                && RankUtils.hasReachedRank(1, getPlayer(), SubSkillType.WOODCUTTING_HARVEST_LUMBER)\n                && ProbabilityUtil.isSkillRNGSuccessful(SubSkillType.WOODCUTTING_HARVEST_LUMBER, mmoPlayer)\n                && mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getDoubleDropsEnabled(PrimarySkillType.WOODCUTTING, material);\n    }\n\n    private boolean checkCleanCutsActivation(Material material) {\n        return Permissions.isSubSkillEnabled(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.WOODCUTTING_HARVEST_LUMBER)\n                && RankUtils.hasReachedRank(1, getPlayer(), SubSkillType.WOODCUTTING_HARVEST_LUMBER)\n                && ProbabilityUtil.isSkillRNGSuccessful(SubSkillType.WOODCUTTING_CLEAN_CUTS, mmoPlayer)\n                && mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getDoubleDropsEnabled(PrimarySkillType.WOODCUTTING, material);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Processes bonus drops for a block\n     *\n     * @param blockState Block being broken\n     */\n    public void processBonusDropCheck(@NotNull BlockState blockState) {\n        //TODO: Why isn't this using the item drop event? Potentially because of Tree Feller? This should be adjusted either way.\n        if (mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getDoubleDropsEnabled(PrimarySkillType.WOODCUTTING, blockState.getType())) {\n            //Mastery enabled for player\n            if (Permissions.canUseSubSkill(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.WOODCUTTING_CLEAN_CUTS)) {\n                if (checkCleanCutsActivation(blockState.getType())) {\n                    //Triple drops\n                    spawnHarvestLumberBonusDrops(blockState);\n                    spawnHarvestLumberBonusDrops(blockState);\n                } else {\n                    //Harvest Lumber Check\n                    if (checkHarvestLumberActivation(blockState.getType())) {\n                        spawnHarvestLumberBonusDrops(blockState);\n                    }\n                }\n            //No Mastery (no Clean Cuts)\n            } else if (Permissions.canUseSubSkill(getPlayer(), SubSkillType.WOODCUTTING_HARVEST_LUMBER)) {\n                if (checkHarvestLumberActivation(blockState.getType())) {\n                    spawnHarvestLumberBonusDrops(blockState);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    public void processWoodcuttingBlockXP(@NotNull BlockState blockState) {\n        if (mcMMO.getUserBlockTracker().isIneligible(blockState))\n            return;\n\n        int xp = getExperienceFromLog(blockState);\n        applyXpGain(xp, XPGainReason.PVE);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Begins Tree Feller\n     *\n     * @param blockState Block being broken\n     */\n    public void processTreeFeller(BlockState blockState) {\n        Player player = getPlayer();\n        Set<BlockState> treeFellerBlocks = new HashSet<>();\n\n        treeFellerReachedThreshold = false;\n\n        processTree(blockState, treeFellerBlocks);\n\n        // If the tool can't sustain the durability loss\n        if (!handleDurabilityLoss(treeFellerBlocks, player.getInventory().getItemInMainHand(), player)) {\n            NotificationManager.sendPlayerInformation(player, NotificationType.SUBSKILL_MESSAGE_FAILED, \"Woodcutting.Skills.TreeFeller.Splinter\");\n\n            double health = player.getHealth();\n\n            if (health > 1) {\n                CombatUtils.dealDamage(player, Misc.getRandom().nextInt((int) (health - 1)));\n            }\n\n            return;\n        }\n\n        dropTreeFellerLootFromBlocks(treeFellerBlocks);\n        treeFellerReachedThreshold = false; // Reset the value after we're done with Tree Feller each time.\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Processes Tree Feller in a recursive manner\n     *\n     * @param blockState Block being checked\n     * @param treeFellerBlocks List of blocks to be removed\n     */\n    /*\n     * Algorithm: An int[][] of X/Z directions is created on static class\n     * initialization, representing a cylinder with radius of about 2 - the\n     * (0,0) center and all (+-2, +-2) corners are omitted.\n     *\n     * processTreeFellerTargetBlock() returns a boolean, which is used for the sole purpose of\n     * switching between these two behaviors:\n     *\n     * (Call blockState \"this log\" for the below explanation.)\n     *\n     *  [A] There is another log above this log (TRUNK)\n     *    Only the flat cylinder in the directions array is searched.\n     *  [B] There is not another log above this log (BRANCH AND TOP)\n     *    The cylinder in the directions array is extended up and down by 1\n     *    block in the Y-axis, and the block below this log is checked as\n     *    well. Due to the fact that the directions array will catch all\n     *    blocks on a red mushroom, the special method for it is eliminated.\n     *\n     * This algorithm has been shown to achieve a performance of 2-5\n     * milliseconds on regular trees and 10-15 milliseconds on jungle trees\n     * once the JIT has optimized the function (use the ability about 4 times\n     * before taking measurements).\n     */\n    private void processTree(BlockState blockState, Set<BlockState> treeFellerBlocks) {\n        List<BlockState> futureCenterBlocks = new ArrayList<>();\n\n        // Check the block up and take different behavior (smaller search) if it's a log\n        if (processTreeFellerTargetBlock(blockState.getBlock().getRelative(BlockFace.UP).getState(), futureCenterBlocks, treeFellerBlocks)) {\n            for (int[] dir : directions) {\n                processTreeFellerTargetBlock(blockState.getBlock().getRelative(dir[0], 0, dir[1]).getState(), futureCenterBlocks, treeFellerBlocks);\n\n                if (treeFellerReachedThreshold) {\n                    return;\n                }\n            }\n        } else {\n            // Cover DOWN\n            processTreeFellerTargetBlock(blockState.getBlock().getRelative(BlockFace.DOWN).getState(), futureCenterBlocks, treeFellerBlocks);\n            // Search in a cube\n            for (int y = -1; y <= 1; y++) {\n                for (int[] dir : directions) {\n                    processTreeFellerTargetBlock(blockState.getBlock().getRelative(dir[0], y, dir[1]).getState(), futureCenterBlocks, treeFellerBlocks);\n\n                    if (treeFellerReachedThreshold) {\n                        return;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Recursive call for each log found\n        for (BlockState futureCenterBlock : futureCenterBlocks) {\n            if (treeFellerReachedThreshold) {\n                return;\n            }\n\n            processTree(futureCenterBlock, treeFellerBlocks);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Handles the durability loss\n     *\n     * @param treeFellerBlocks List of blocks to be removed\n     * @param inHand tool being used\n     * @param player the player holding the item\n     * @return True if the tool can sustain the durability loss\n     */\n    private static boolean handleDurabilityLoss(@NotNull Set<BlockState> treeFellerBlocks, @NotNull ItemStack inHand, @NotNull Player player) {\n        //Treat the NBT tag for unbreakable and the durability enchant differently\n        ItemMeta meta = inHand.getItemMeta();\n\n        if (meta != null && meta.isUnbreakable()) {\n            return true;\n        }\n\n        int durabilityLoss = 0;\n        Material type = inHand.getType();\n\n        for (BlockState blockState : treeFellerBlocks) {\n            if (BlockUtils.hasWoodcuttingXP(blockState)) {\n                durabilityLoss += mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getAbilityToolDamage();\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Call PlayerItemDamageEvent first to make sure it's not cancelled\n        //TODO: Put this event stuff in handleDurabilityChange\n        final PlayerItemDamageEvent event = new PlayerItemDamageEvent(player, inHand, durabilityLoss);\n        Bukkit.getPluginManager().callEvent(event);\n\n        if (event.isCancelled()) {\n            return true;\n        }\n\n        SkillUtils.handleDurabilityChange(inHand, durabilityLoss);\n        int durability = meta instanceof Damageable ? ((Damageable) meta).getDamage(): 0;\n        return (durability < (mcMMO.getRepairableManager().isRepairable(type) ? mcMMO.getRepairableManager().getRepairable(type).getMaximumDurability() : type.getMaxDurability()));\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Handle a block addition to the list of blocks to be removed and to the\n     * list of blocks used for future recursive calls of\n     * 'processTree()'\n     *\n     * @param blockState Block to be added\n     * @param futureCenterBlocks List of blocks that will be used to call\n     *     'processTree()'\n     * @param treeFellerBlocks List of blocks to be removed\n     * @return true if and only if the given blockState was a Log not already\n     *     in treeFellerBlocks.\n     */\n    private boolean processTreeFellerTargetBlock(@NotNull BlockState blockState, @NotNull List<BlockState> futureCenterBlocks, @NotNull Set<BlockState> treeFellerBlocks) {\n        if (treeFellerBlocks.contains(blockState) || mcMMO.getUserBlockTracker().isIneligible(blockState)) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        // Without this check Tree Feller propagates through leaves until the threshold is hit\n        if (treeFellerBlocks.size() > treeFellerThreshold) {\n            treeFellerReachedThreshold = true;\n        }\n\n        if (BlockUtils.hasWoodcuttingXP(blockState)) {\n            treeFellerBlocks.add(blockState);\n            futureCenterBlocks.add(blockState);\n            return true;\n        } else if (BlockUtils.isNonWoodPartOfTree(blockState)) {\n            treeFellerBlocks.add(blockState);\n            return false;\n        }\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Handles the dropping of blocks\n     *\n     * @param treeFellerBlocks List of blocks to be dropped\n     */\n    private void dropTreeFellerLootFromBlocks(@NotNull Set<BlockState> treeFellerBlocks) {\n        Player player = getPlayer();\n        int xp = 0;\n        int processedLogCount = 0;\n        ItemStack itemStack = player.getInventory().getItemInMainHand();\n\n        for (BlockState blockState : treeFellerBlocks) {\n            int beforeXP = xp;\n            Block block = blockState.getBlock();\n\n            if (!EventUtils.simulateBlockBreak(block, player, FakeBlockBreakEventType.TREE_FELLER)) {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            /*\n             * Handle Drops & XP\n             */\n\n            if (BlockUtils.hasWoodcuttingXP(blockState)) {\n                //Add XP\n                xp += processTreeFellerXPGains(blockState, processedLogCount);\n\n                //Drop displaced block\n                Misc.spawnItemsFromCollection(player, getBlockCenter(blockState), block.getDrops(itemStack), ItemSpawnReason.TREE_FELLER_DISPLACED_BLOCK);\n\n                //Bonus Drops / Harvest lumber checks\n                processBonusDropCheck(blockState);\n            } else if (BlockUtils.isNonWoodPartOfTree(blockState)) {\n                // 75% of the time do not drop leaf blocks\n                if (ThreadLocalRandom.current().nextInt(100) > 75) {\n                    Misc.spawnItemsFromCollection(player,\n                            getBlockCenter(blockState),\n                            block.getDrops(itemStack),\n                            ItemSpawnReason.TREE_FELLER_DISPLACED_BLOCK);\n                } else if (hasUnlockedSubskill(player, SubSkillType.WOODCUTTING_KNOCK_ON_WOOD)) {\n                    // if KnockOnWood is unlocked, then drop any saplings from the remaining blocks\n                    spawnItem(block.getDrops(itemStack),\n                            ItemSpawnReason.TREE_FELLER_DISPLACED_BLOCK,\n                            getBlockCenter(blockState),\n                            // only spawn saplings\n                            p -> p.toLowerCase().contains(SAPLING) || p.toLowerCase().contains(PROPAGULE),\n                            player\n                    );\n                }\n\n                //Drop displaced non-woodcutting XP blocks\n                if (hasUnlockedSubskill(player, SubSkillType.WOODCUTTING_KNOCK_ON_WOOD)) {\n                    if (RankUtils.hasReachedRank(2, player, SubSkillType.WOODCUTTING_KNOCK_ON_WOOD)) {\n                        if (mcMMO.p.getAdvancedConfig().isKnockOnWoodXPOrbEnabled()) {\n                            if (ProbabilityUtil.isStaticSkillRNGSuccessful(PrimarySkillType.WOODCUTTING, mmoPlayer, 10)) {\n                                int randOrbCount = Math.max(1, Misc.getRandom().nextInt(100));\n                                Misc.spawnExperienceOrb(blockState.getLocation(), randOrbCount);\n                            }\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n\n            blockState.setType(Material.AIR);\n            blockState.update(true);\n\n            //Update only when XP changes\n            processedLogCount = updateProcessedLogCount(xp, processedLogCount, beforeXP);\n        }\n\n        applyXpGain(xp, XPGainReason.PVE, XPGainSource.SELF);\n    }\n\n    private int updateProcessedLogCount(int xp, int processedLogCount, int beforeXP) {\n        if (beforeXP != xp)\n            processedLogCount+=1;\n\n        return processedLogCount;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Retrieves the experience reward from logging via Tree Feller\n     * Experience is reduced per log processed so far\n     * Experience is only reduced if the config option to reduce Tree Feller XP is set\n     * Experience per log will not fall below 1 unless the experience for that log is set to 0 in the config\n     *\n     * @param blockState Log being broken\n     * @param woodCount how many logs have given out XP for this tree feller so far\n     * @return Amount of experience\n     */\n    private static int processTreeFellerXPGains(BlockState blockState, int woodCount) {\n        if (mcMMO.getUserBlockTracker().isIneligible(blockState))\n            return 0;\n\n        int rawXP = ExperienceConfig.getInstance().getXp(PrimarySkillType.WOODCUTTING, blockState.getType());\n\n        if (rawXP <= 0)\n            return 0;\n\n        if (ExperienceConfig.getInstance().isTreeFellerXPReduced()) {\n            int reducedXP = rawXP - (woodCount * 5);\n            rawXP = Math.max(1, reducedXP);\n            return rawXP;\n        } else {\n            return ExperienceConfig.getInstance().getXp(PrimarySkillType.WOODCUTTING, blockState.getType());\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Retrieves the experience reward from a log\n     *\n     * @param blockState Log being broken\n     * @return Amount of experience\n     */\n    protected static int getExperienceFromLog(BlockState blockState) {\n        if (mcMMO.getModManager().isCustomLog(blockState)) {\n            return mcMMO.getModManager().getBlock(blockState).getXpGain();\n        }\n\n        return ExperienceConfig.getInstance().getXp(PrimarySkillType.WOODCUTTING, blockState.getType());\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Spawns harvest lumber bonus drops\n     *\n     * @param blockState Block being broken\n     */\n    protected void spawnHarvestLumberBonusDrops(@NotNull BlockState blockState) {\n        Misc.spawnItemsFromCollection(\n                getPlayer(),\n                getBlockCenter(blockState),\n                blockState.getBlock().getDrops(getPlayer().getInventory().getItemInMainHand()),\n                ItemSpawnReason.BONUS_DROPS);\n    }\n}\n"
    },
