      "path": "src/main/java/com/gmail/nossr50/util/ChimaeraWing.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package com.gmail.nossr50.util;\n\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.interactions.NotificationType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.player.McMMOPlayer;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.locale.LocaleLoader;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.mcMMO;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.runnables.items.ChimaeraWingWarmup;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.player.NotificationManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.player.UserManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.skills.CombatUtils;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.skills.SkillUtils;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.sounds.SoundManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.sounds.SoundType;\nimport org.bukkit.ChatColor;\nimport org.bukkit.Location;\nimport org.bukkit.Material;\nimport org.bukkit.NamespacedKey;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.Player;\nimport org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack;\nimport org.bukkit.inventory.ShapelessRecipe;\nimport org.bukkit.inventory.meta.ItemMeta;\nimport org.bukkit.util.Vector;\n\nimport java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.List;\n\npublic final class ChimaeraWing {\n    private static McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer;\n    private static Location location;\n\n    private ChimaeraWing() {}\n\n    /**\n     * Check for item usage.\n     *\n     * @param player Player whose item usage to check\n     */\n    public static void activationCheck(Player player) {\n        if (!mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getChimaeraEnabled()) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        ItemStack inHand = player.getInventory().getItemInMainHand();\n\n        if (!ItemUtils.isChimaeraWing(inHand)) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        if (!Permissions.chimaeraWing(player)) {\n            NotificationManager.sendPlayerInformation(player, NotificationType.NO_PERMISSION, \"mcMMO.NoPermission\");\n            return;\n        }\n\n        mcMMOPlayer = UserManager.getPlayer(player);\n\n        //Not loaded\n        if (mcMMOPlayer == null)\n            return;\n\n        if (mcMMOPlayer.getTeleportCommenceLocation() != null) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        int amount = inHand.getAmount();\n\n        if (amount < mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getChimaeraUseCost()) {\n            NotificationManager.sendPlayerInformation(player, NotificationType.REQUIREMENTS_NOT_MET, \"Item.ChimaeraWing.NotEnough\",String.valueOf(mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getChimaeraUseCost() - amount), \"Item.ChimaeraWing.Name\");\n            return;\n        }\n\n        long lastTeleport = mcMMOPlayer.getChimeraWingLastUse();\n        int cooldown = mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getChimaeraCooldown();\n\n        if (cooldown > 0) {\n            int timeRemaining = SkillUtils.calculateTimeLeft(lastTeleport * Misc.TIME_CONVERSION_FACTOR, cooldown, player);\n\n            if (timeRemaining > 0) {\n                NotificationManager.sendPlayerInformation(player, NotificationType.ABILITY_COOLDOWN, \"Item.Generic.Wait\", String.valueOf(timeRemaining));\n                return;\n            }\n        }\n\n        long recentlyHurt = mcMMOPlayer.getRecentlyHurt();\n        int hurtCooldown = mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getChimaeraRecentlyHurtCooldown();\n\n        if (hurtCooldown > 0) {\n            int timeRemaining = SkillUtils.calculateTimeLeft(recentlyHurt * Misc.TIME_CONVERSION_FACTOR, hurtCooldown, player);\n\n            if (timeRemaining > 0) {\n                NotificationManager.sendPlayerInformation(player, NotificationType.ITEM_MESSAGE, \"Item.Injured.Wait\", String.valueOf(timeRemaining));\n                return;\n            }\n        }\n\n        location = player.getLocation();\n\n        if (mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getChimaeraPreventUseUnderground()) {\n            if (location.getY() < player.getWorld().getHighestBlockYAt(location)) {\n                player.getInventory().setItemInMainHand(new ItemStack(getChimaeraWing(amount - mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getChimaeraUseCost())));\n                NotificationManager.sendPlayerInformation(player, NotificationType.REQUIREMENTS_NOT_MET, \"Item.ChimaeraWing.Fail\");\n                player.updateInventory();\n                player.setVelocity(new Vector(0, 0.5D, 0));\n                CombatUtils.dealDamage(player, Misc.getRandom().nextInt((int) (player.getHealth() - 10)));\n                mcMMOPlayer.actualizeChimeraWingLastUse();\n                return;\n            }\n        }\n\n        mcMMOPlayer.actualizeTeleportCommenceLocation(player);\n\n        long warmup = mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getChimaeraWarmup();\n\n        if (warmup > 0) {\n            NotificationManager.sendPlayerInformation(player, NotificationType.ITEM_MESSAGE, \"Teleport.Commencing\", String.valueOf(warmup));\n            mcMMO.p.getFoliaLib().getImpl().runAtEntityLater(player, new ChimaeraWingWarmup(mcMMOPlayer), 20 * warmup);\n        } else {\n            chimaeraExecuteTeleport();\n        }\n    }\n\n    public static void chimaeraExecuteTeleport() {\n        Player player = mcMMOPlayer.getPlayer();\n\n        if (mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getChimaeraUseBedSpawn() && player.getBedSpawnLocation() != null) {\n//            player.teleport(player.getBedSpawnLocation());\n            mcMMO.p.getFoliaLib().getImpl().teleportAsync(player, player.getBedSpawnLocation());\n        } else {\n            Location spawnLocation = player.getWorld().getSpawnLocation();\n            if (spawnLocation.getBlock().getType() == Material.AIR) {\n//                player.teleport(spawnLocation);\n                mcMMO.p.getFoliaLib().getImpl().teleportAsync(player, spawnLocation);\n            } else {\n//                player.teleport(player.getWorld().getHighestBlockAt(spawnLocation).getLocation());\n                mcMMO.p.getFoliaLib().getImpl().teleportAsync(player, player.getWorld().getHighestBlockAt(spawnLocation).getLocation());\n            }\n        }\n\n        player.getInventory().setItemInMainHand(new ItemStack(getChimaeraWing(player.getInventory().getItemInMainHand().getAmount() - mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getChimaeraUseCost())));\n        player.updateInventory();\n        mcMMOPlayer.actualizeChimeraWingLastUse();\n        mcMMOPlayer.setTeleportCommenceLocation(null);\n\n        if (mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getChimaeraSoundEnabled()) {\n            SoundManager.sendSound(player, location, SoundType.CHIMAERA_WING);\n        }\n\n        NotificationManager.sendPlayerInformation(player, NotificationType.ITEM_MESSAGE, \"Item.ChimaeraWing.Pass\");\n    }\n\n    public static ItemStack getChimaeraWing(int amount) {\n        ItemStack itemStack = new ItemStack(mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getChimaeraItem(), amount);\n\n        ItemMeta itemMeta = itemStack.getItemMeta();\n        //noinspection ConstantConditions\n        itemMeta.setDisplayName(ChatColor.GOLD + LocaleLoader.getString(\"Item.ChimaeraWing.Name\"));\n\n        List<String> itemLore = new ArrayList<>();\n        itemLore.add(\"mcMMO Item\");\n        itemLore.add(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Item.ChimaeraWing.Lore\"));\n        itemMeta.setLore(itemLore);\n\n        itemStack.setItemMeta(itemMeta);\n        return itemStack;\n    }\n\n    public static ShapelessRecipe getChimaeraWingRecipe() {\n        Material ingredient = mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getChimaeraItem();\n        int amount = mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getChimaeraRecipeCost();\n\n        ShapelessRecipe chimeraWing = new ShapelessRecipe(new NamespacedKey(mcMMO.p, \"Chimera\"), getChimaeraWing(1));\n        chimeraWing.addIngredient(amount, ingredient);\n        return chimeraWing;\n    }\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "src/main/java/com/gmail/nossr50/util/EventUtils.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package com.gmail.nossr50.util;\n\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.api.FakeBlockBreakEventType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.api.TreeFellerBlockBreakEvent;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.experience.XPGainReason;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.experience.XPGainSource;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.party.Party;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.player.McMMOPlayer;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.player.PlayerProfile;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.PrimarySkillType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.SubSkillType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.SuperAbilityType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.events.experience.McMMOPlayerLevelChangeEvent;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.events.experience.McMMOPlayerLevelDownEvent;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.events.experience.McMMOPlayerLevelUpEvent;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.events.experience.McMMOPlayerXpGainEvent;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.events.fake.FakeBlockBreakEvent;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.events.fake.FakeBlockDamageEvent;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.events.fake.FakeEvent;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.events.fake.FakePlayerFishEvent;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.events.hardcore.McMMOPlayerPreDeathPenaltyEvent;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.events.hardcore.McMMOPlayerStatLossEvent;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.events.hardcore.McMMOPlayerVampirismEvent;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.events.party.McMMOPartyLevelUpEvent;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.events.party.McMMOPartyTeleportEvent;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.events.party.McMMOPartyXpGainEvent;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.events.players.McMMOPlayerProfileLoadEvent;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.events.skills.abilities.McMMOPlayerAbilityActivateEvent;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.events.skills.abilities.McMMOPlayerAbilityDeactivateEvent;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.events.skills.fishing.McMMOPlayerFishingTreasureEvent;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.events.skills.fishing.McMMOPlayerMagicHunterEvent;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.events.skills.repair.McMMOPlayerRepairCheckEvent;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.events.skills.salvage.McMMOPlayerSalvageCheckEvent;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.events.skills.secondaryabilities.SubSkillBlockEvent;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.events.skills.secondaryabilities.SubSkillEvent;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.events.skills.unarmed.McMMOPlayerDisarmEvent;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.locale.LocaleLoader;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.mcMMO;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.player.NotificationManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.player.UserManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.skills.CombatUtils;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.skills.SkillTools;\nimport org.bukkit.block.Block;\nimport org.bukkit.enchantments.Enchantment;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.Entity;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.FishHook;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.LivingEntity;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.Player;\nimport org.bukkit.event.Event;\nimport org.bukkit.event.block.BlockBreakEvent;\nimport org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityDamageEvent;\nimport org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerFishEvent;\nimport org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack;\nimport org.bukkit.plugin.Plugin;\nimport org.bukkit.plugin.PluginManager;\nimport org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;\n\nimport java.util.HashMap;\nimport java.util.Map;\n\nimport static java.util.Objects.requireNonNull;\n\n/**\n * This class is meant to help make event related code less boilerplate\n */\npublic final class EventUtils {\n\n    /**\n     * This is a static utility class, therefore we don't want any instances of\n     * this class. Making the constructor private prevents accidents like that.\n     */\n    private EventUtils() {}\n\n    /*\n     * Quality of Life methods\n     */\n\n    /**\n     * This is a simple check to see if an {@link Event} is fake or not.\n     * {@link FakeEvent FakeEvents} should not be processed like normally and maybe even \n     * be ignored by other {@link Plugin plugins} completely.\n     * \n     * @param event The {@link Event} in question\n     * @return Whether this {@link Event} has been faked by mcMMO and should not be processed normally.\n     */\n    public static boolean isFakeEvent(@NotNull Event event) {\n        return event instanceof FakeEvent;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * This little method is just to make the code more readable\n     * \n     * @param entity target entity\n     * @return the associated McMMOPlayer for this entity\n     */\n    public static McMMOPlayer getMcMMOPlayer(@NotNull Entity entity) {\n        return UserManager.getPlayer((Player)entity);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Checks to see if a Player was damaged in this EntityDamageEvent\n     *\n     * This method checks for the following things and if they are all true it returns true\n     *\n     * 1) The player is real and not an NPC\n     * 2) The player is not in god mode\n     * 3) The damage dealt is above 0\n     * 4) The player is loaded into our mcMMO user profiles\n     *\n     * @param entityDamageEvent\n     * @return\n     */\n    public static boolean isRealPlayerDamaged(@NotNull EntityDamageEvent entityDamageEvent) {\n        //Make sure the damage is above 0\n        double damage = entityDamageEvent.getFinalDamage();\n\n        if (damage <= 0) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        Entity entity = entityDamageEvent.getEntity();\n\n        //Check to make sure the entity is not an NPC\n        if (Misc.isNPCEntityExcludingVillagers(entity))\n            return false;\n\n        if (!entity.isValid() || !(entity instanceof LivingEntity livingEntity)) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        if (CombatUtils.isInvincible(livingEntity, damage)) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        if (livingEntity instanceof Player) {\n            Player player = (Player) entity;\n\n            if (!UserManager.hasPlayerDataKey(player)) {\n                return true;\n            }\n\n            McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer = UserManager.getPlayer(player);\n\n            if (mcMMOPlayer == null) {\n                return true;\n            }\n\n            /* Check for invincibility */\n            if (mcMMOPlayer.getGodMode()) {\n                entityDamageEvent.setCancelled(true);\n                return false;\n            }\n\n            return true;\n        } else {\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /*\n     * Others\n     */\n\n    @Deprecated(forRemoval = true, since = \"2.2.010\")\n    public static @NotNull McMMOPlayerAbilityActivateEvent callPlayerAbilityActivateEvent(@NotNull Player player,\n                                                                                          @NotNull PrimarySkillType skill) {\n        return callPlayerAbilityActivateEvent(requireNonNull(UserManager.getPlayer(player)), skill);\n    }\n\n    public static @NotNull McMMOPlayerAbilityActivateEvent callPlayerAbilityActivateEvent(@NotNull McMMOPlayer mmoPlayer,\n                                                                                          @NotNull PrimarySkillType skill) {\n        requireNonNull(mmoPlayer, \"mmoPlayer cannot be null\");\n        requireNonNull(skill, \"skill cannot be null\");\n        McMMOPlayerAbilityActivateEvent event = new McMMOPlayerAbilityActivateEvent(mmoPlayer, skill);\n        mcMMO.p.getServer().getPluginManager().callEvent(event);\n\n        return event;\n    }\n\n    public static @NotNull McMMOPlayerProfileLoadEvent callPlayerProfileLoadEvent(@NotNull Player player, @NotNull PlayerProfile profile){\n        McMMOPlayerProfileLoadEvent event = new McMMOPlayerProfileLoadEvent(player, profile);\n        mcMMO.p.getServer().getPluginManager().callEvent(event);\n\n        return event;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Calls a new SubSkillEvent for this SubSkill and then returns it\n     * @param player target player\n     * @param subSkillType target subskill\n     * @return the event after it has been fired\n     */\n    @Deprecated(forRemoval = true, since = \"2.2.010\")\n    public static @NotNull SubSkillEvent callSubSkillEvent(@NotNull Player player, @NotNull SubSkillType subSkillType) {\n        return callSubSkillEvent(requireNonNull(UserManager.getPlayer(player)), subSkillType);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Calls a new SubSkillEvent for this SubSkill and then returns it\n     * @param mmoPlayer target mmoPlayer\n     * @param subSkillType target subskill\n     * @return the event after it has been fired\n     */\n    public static @NotNull SubSkillEvent callSubSkillEvent(@NotNull McMMOPlayer mmoPlayer, @NotNull SubSkillType subSkillType) {\n        requireNonNull(mmoPlayer, \"mmoPlayer cannot be null\");\n        requireNonNull(subSkillType, \"subSkillType cannot be null\");\n        final SubSkillEvent event = new SubSkillEvent(mmoPlayer, subSkillType);\n        mcMMO.p.getServer().getPluginManager().callEvent(event);\n\n        return event;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Calls a new SubSkillBlockEvent for this SubSkill and its related block and then returns it\n     * @param player target player\n     * @param subSkillType target subskill\n     * @param block associated block\n     * @return the event after it has been fired\n     */\n    public static @NotNull SubSkillBlockEvent callSubSkillBlockEvent(@NotNull Player player, @NotNull SubSkillType subSkillType, @NotNull Block block) {\n        SubSkillBlockEvent event = new SubSkillBlockEvent(player, subSkillType, block);\n        mcMMO.p.getServer().getPluginManager().callEvent(event);\n\n        return event;\n    }\n\n    public static boolean tryLevelChangeEvent(Player player, PrimarySkillType skill, int levelsChanged, float xpRemoved, boolean isLevelUp, XPGainReason xpGainReason) {\n        McMMOPlayerLevelChangeEvent event = isLevelUp ? new McMMOPlayerLevelUpEvent(player, skill, levelsChanged, xpGainReason) : new McMMOPlayerLevelDownEvent(player, skill, levelsChanged, xpGainReason);\n        mcMMO.p.getServer().getPluginManager().callEvent(event);\n\n        boolean isCancelled = event.isCancelled();\n\n        if (isCancelled) {\n            PlayerProfile profile = UserManager.getPlayer(player).getProfile();\n\n            profile.modifySkill(skill, profile.getSkillLevel(skill) - (isLevelUp ? levelsChanged : -levelsChanged));\n            profile.addXp(skill, xpRemoved);\n        }\n\n        return isCancelled;\n    }\n\n    public static boolean tryLevelChangeEvent(@NotNull McMMOPlayer mmoPlayer, PrimarySkillType skill, int levelsChanged, float xpRemoved, boolean isLevelUp, XPGainReason xpGainReason) {\n        McMMOPlayerLevelChangeEvent event = isLevelUp ? new McMMOPlayerLevelUpEvent(mmoPlayer.getPlayer(), skill, levelsChanged, xpGainReason) : new McMMOPlayerLevelDownEvent(mmoPlayer.getPlayer(), skill, levelsChanged, xpGainReason);\n        mcMMO.p.getServer().getPluginManager().callEvent(event);\n\n        boolean isCancelled = event.isCancelled();\n\n        if (isCancelled) {\n            mmoPlayer.modifySkill(skill, mmoPlayer.getSkillLevel(skill) - (isLevelUp ? levelsChanged : -levelsChanged));\n            mmoPlayer.addXp(skill, xpRemoved);\n        } else {\n            if (isLevelUp) {\n                NotificationManager.processLevelUpBroadcasting(mmoPlayer, skill, mmoPlayer.getSkillLevel(skill));\n                NotificationManager.processPowerLevelUpBroadcasting(mmoPlayer, mmoPlayer.getPowerLevel());\n\n            }\n        }\n\n        return isCancelled;\n    }\n\n    public static boolean tryLevelEditEvent(Player player, PrimarySkillType skill, int levelsChanged, float xpRemoved, boolean isLevelUp, XPGainReason xpGainReason, int oldLevel) {\n        McMMOPlayerLevelChangeEvent event = isLevelUp ? new McMMOPlayerLevelUpEvent(player, skill, levelsChanged - oldLevel, xpGainReason) : new McMMOPlayerLevelDownEvent(player, skill, levelsChanged, xpGainReason);\n        mcMMO.p.getServer().getPluginManager().callEvent(event);\n\n        boolean isCancelled = event.isCancelled();\n\n        if (isCancelled) {\n            PlayerProfile profile = UserManager.getPlayer(player).getProfile();\n\n            profile.modifySkill(skill, profile.getSkillLevel(skill) - (isLevelUp ? levelsChanged : -levelsChanged));\n            profile.addXp(skill, xpRemoved);\n        }\n\n        return isCancelled;\n    }\n\n    public static boolean tryLevelEditEvent(@NotNull McMMOPlayer mmoPlayer, PrimarySkillType skill, int levelsChanged, float xpRemoved, boolean isLevelUp, XPGainReason xpGainReason, int oldLevel) {\n        McMMOPlayerLevelChangeEvent event = isLevelUp ? new McMMOPlayerLevelUpEvent(mmoPlayer.getPlayer(), skill, levelsChanged - oldLevel, xpGainReason) : new McMMOPlayerLevelDownEvent(mmoPlayer.getPlayer(), skill, levelsChanged, xpGainReason);\n        mcMMO.p.getServer().getPluginManager().callEvent(event);\n\n        boolean isCancelled = event.isCancelled();\n\n        if (isCancelled) {\n            mmoPlayer.modifySkill(skill, mmoPlayer.getSkillLevel(skill) - (isLevelUp ? levelsChanged : -levelsChanged));\n            mmoPlayer.addXp(skill, xpRemoved);\n        } else {\n            if (isLevelUp) {\n                NotificationManager.processLevelUpBroadcasting(mmoPlayer, skill, mmoPlayer.getSkillLevel(skill));\n                NotificationManager.processPowerLevelUpBroadcasting(mmoPlayer, mmoPlayer.getPowerLevel());\n            }\n        }\n\n        return isCancelled;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Simulate a block break event.\n     *\n     * @param block          The block to break\n     * @param player         The player breaking the block\n     * @param shouldArmSwing ignored (here for API compatibility)\n     * @return true if the event wasn't cancelled, false otherwise\n     * {@code @Deprecated} use {@link #simulateBlockBreak(Block, Player, FakeBlockBreakEventType)} instead\n     */\n    public static boolean simulateBlockBreak(Block block, Player player, boolean shouldArmSwing) {\n        return simulateBlockBreak(block, player);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Simulate a block break event.\n     *\n     * @param block The block to break\n     * @param player The player breaking the block\n     * @return true if the event wasn't cancelled, false otherwise\n     * {@code @Deprecated} use {@link #simulateBlockBreak(Block, Player, FakeBlockBreakEventType)} instead\n     */\n    public static boolean simulateBlockBreak(Block block, Player player) {\n        return simulateBlockBreak(block, player, FakeBlockBreakEventType.FAKE);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Simulate a block break event.\n     *\n     * @param block  The block to break\n     * @param player The player breaking the block\n     * @param eventType The type of event to signal to other plugins\n     * @return true if the event wasn't cancelled, false otherwise\n     */\n    public static boolean simulateBlockBreak(Block block, Player player, FakeBlockBreakEventType eventType) {\n        PluginManager pluginManager = mcMMO.p.getServer().getPluginManager();\n\n        FakeBlockDamageEvent damageEvent = new FakeBlockDamageEvent(player, block, player.getInventory().getItemInMainHand(), true);\n        pluginManager.callEvent(damageEvent);\n\n        BlockBreakEvent fakeBlockBreakEvent = null;\n\n        switch (eventType) {\n            case FAKE -> fakeBlockBreakEvent = new FakeBlockBreakEvent(block, player);\n            case TREE_FELLER -> fakeBlockBreakEvent = new TreeFellerBlockBreakEvent(block, player);\n        }\n        pluginManager.callEvent(fakeBlockBreakEvent);\n        return !damageEvent.isCancelled() && !fakeBlockBreakEvent.isCancelled();\n    }\n\n    public static void handlePartyTeleportEvent(Player teleportingPlayer, Player targetPlayer) {\n        McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer = UserManager.getPlayer(teleportingPlayer);\n\n        if (mcMMOPlayer == null)\n            return;\n\n        McMMOPartyTeleportEvent event = new McMMOPartyTeleportEvent(teleportingPlayer, targetPlayer, mcMMOPlayer.getParty().getName());\n        mcMMO.p.getServer().getPluginManager().callEvent(event);\n\n        if (event.isCancelled()) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n//        teleportingPlayer.teleport(targetPlayer);\n        mcMMO.p.getFoliaLib().getImpl().teleportAsync(teleportingPlayer, targetPlayer.getLocation());\n\n        teleportingPlayer.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Party.Teleport.Player\", targetPlayer.getName()));\n        targetPlayer.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Party.Teleport.Target\", teleportingPlayer.getName()));\n\n        mcMMOPlayer.getPartyTeleportRecord().actualizeLastUse();\n    }\n\n    public static boolean handlePartyXpGainEvent(Party party, float xpGained) {\n        McMMOPartyXpGainEvent event = new McMMOPartyXpGainEvent(party, xpGained);\n        mcMMO.p.getServer().getPluginManager().callEvent(event);\n\n        boolean isCancelled = event.isCancelled();\n\n        if (!isCancelled) {\n            party.addXp(event.getRawXpGained());\n        }\n\n        return !isCancelled;\n    }\n\n    public static boolean handlePartyLevelChangeEvent(Party party, int levelsChanged, float xpRemoved) {\n        McMMOPartyLevelUpEvent event = new McMMOPartyLevelUpEvent(party, levelsChanged);\n        mcMMO.p.getServer().getPluginManager().callEvent(event);\n\n        boolean isCancelled = event.isCancelled();\n\n        if (isCancelled) {\n            party.setLevel(party.getLevel() + levelsChanged);\n            party.addXp(xpRemoved);\n        }\n\n        return !isCancelled;\n    }\n\n    public static boolean handleXpGainEvent(Player player, PrimarySkillType skill, float xpGained, XPGainReason xpGainReason) {\n        McMMOPlayer mmoPlayer = UserManager.getPlayer(player);\n        if (mmoPlayer == null)\n            return true;\n\n        McMMOPlayerXpGainEvent event = new McMMOPlayerXpGainEvent(player, skill, xpGained, xpGainReason);\n        mcMMO.p.getServer().getPluginManager().callEvent(event);\n\n        boolean isCancelled = event.isCancelled();\n\n        if (!isCancelled) {\n            mmoPlayer.addXp(skill, event.getRawXpGained());\n            mmoPlayer.getProfile().registerXpGain(skill, event.getRawXpGained());\n        }\n\n        return !isCancelled;\n    }\n\n    public static boolean handleStatsLossEvent(Player player, HashMap<String, Integer> levelChanged, HashMap<String, Float> experienceChanged) {\n        if (UserManager.getPlayer(player) == null)\n            return true;\n\n        McMMOPlayerStatLossEvent event = new McMMOPlayerStatLossEvent(player, levelChanged, experienceChanged);\n        mcMMO.p.getServer().getPluginManager().callEvent(event);\n\n        boolean isCancelled = event.isCancelled();\n\n        if (!isCancelled) {\n            levelChanged = event.getLevelChanged();\n            experienceChanged = event.getExperienceChanged();\n            PlayerProfile playerProfile = UserManager.getPlayer(player).getProfile();\n\n            for (PrimarySkillType primarySkillType : SkillTools.NON_CHILD_SKILLS) {\n                String skillName = primarySkillType.toString();\n                int playerSkillLevel = playerProfile.getSkillLevel(primarySkillType);\n                int threshold = mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getHardcoreDeathStatPenaltyLevelThreshold();\n                if (playerSkillLevel > threshold) {\n                    playerProfile.modifySkill(primarySkillType, Math.max(threshold, playerSkillLevel - levelChanged.get(skillName)));\n                    playerProfile.removeXp(primarySkillType, experienceChanged.get(skillName));\n\n                    if (playerProfile.getSkillXpLevel(primarySkillType) < 0) {\n                        playerProfile.setSkillXpLevel(primarySkillType, 0);\n                    }\n\n                    if (playerProfile.getSkillLevel(primarySkillType) < 0) {\n                        playerProfile.modifySkill(primarySkillType, 0);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        return !isCancelled;\n    }\n\n    public static boolean handleVampirismEvent(Player killer, Player victim, HashMap<String, Integer> levelChanged, HashMap<String, Float> experienceChanged) {\n        McMMOPlayerVampirismEvent eventKiller = new McMMOPlayerVampirismEvent(killer, false, levelChanged, experienceChanged);\n        McMMOPlayerVampirismEvent eventVictim = new McMMOPlayerVampirismEvent(victim, true, levelChanged, experienceChanged);\n        mcMMO.p.getServer().getPluginManager().callEvent(eventKiller);\n        mcMMO.p.getServer().getPluginManager().callEvent(eventVictim);\n\n        boolean isCancelled = eventKiller.isCancelled() || eventVictim.isCancelled();\n\n        if (!isCancelled) {\n            HashMap<String, Integer> levelChangedKiller = eventKiller.getLevelChanged();\n            HashMap<String, Float> experienceChangedKiller = eventKiller.getExperienceChanged();\n\n            HashMap<String, Integer> levelChangedVictim = eventVictim.getLevelChanged();\n            HashMap<String, Float> experienceChangedVictim = eventVictim.getExperienceChanged();\n\n            McMMOPlayer killerPlayer = UserManager.getPlayer(killer);\n\n            //Not loaded\n            if (killerPlayer == null)\n                return true;\n\n            //Not loaded\n            if (UserManager.getPlayer(victim) == null)\n                return true;\n\n            PlayerProfile victimProfile = UserManager.getPlayer(victim).getProfile();\n\n            for (PrimarySkillType primarySkillType : SkillTools.NON_CHILD_SKILLS) {\n                String skillName = primarySkillType.toString();\n                int victimSkillLevel = victimProfile.getSkillLevel(primarySkillType);\n\n                killerPlayer.addLevels(primarySkillType, levelChangedKiller.get(skillName));\n                killerPlayer.beginUnsharedXpGain(primarySkillType, experienceChangedKiller.get(skillName), XPGainReason.VAMPIRISM, XPGainSource.VAMPIRISM);\n\n                victimProfile.modifySkill(primarySkillType, victimSkillLevel - levelChangedVictim.get(skillName));\n                victimProfile.removeXp(primarySkillType, experienceChangedVictim.get(skillName));\n\n                if (victimProfile.getSkillXpLevel(primarySkillType) < 0) {\n                    victimProfile.setSkillXpLevel(primarySkillType, 0);\n                }\n\n                if (victimProfile.getSkillLevel(primarySkillType) < 0) {\n                    victimProfile.modifySkill(primarySkillType, 0);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        return !isCancelled;\n    }\n\n    @Deprecated(forRemoval = true, since = \"2.2.010\")\n    public static McMMOPlayerAbilityDeactivateEvent callAbilityDeactivateEvent(Player player, SuperAbilityType ability) {\n        return callAbilityDeactivateEvent(requireNonNull(UserManager.getPlayer(player)), ability);\n    }\n\n    public static McMMOPlayerAbilityDeactivateEvent callAbilityDeactivateEvent(@NotNull McMMOPlayer mmoPlayer, @NotNull SuperAbilityType ability) {\n        final McMMOPlayerAbilityDeactivateEvent event = new McMMOPlayerAbilityDeactivateEvent(mmoPlayer, mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().getPrimarySkillBySuperAbility(ability));\n        mcMMO.p.getServer().getPluginManager().callEvent(event);\n\n        return event;\n    }\n\n    @Deprecated(forRemoval = true, since = \"2.2.010\")\n    public static McMMOPlayerFishingTreasureEvent callFishingTreasureEvent(Player player, ItemStack treasureDrop, int treasureXp, Map<Enchantment, Integer> enchants) {\n        return callFishingTreasureEvent(requireNonNull(UserManager.getPlayer(player)), treasureDrop, treasureXp, enchants);\n    }\n\n    public static McMMOPlayerFishingTreasureEvent callFishingTreasureEvent(McMMOPlayer mmoPlayer, ItemStack treasureDrop, int treasureXp, Map<Enchantment, Integer> enchants) {\n        final McMMOPlayerFishingTreasureEvent event = enchants.isEmpty() ? new McMMOPlayerFishingTreasureEvent(mmoPlayer, treasureDrop, treasureXp) : new McMMOPlayerMagicHunterEvent(mmoPlayer, treasureDrop, treasureXp, enchants);\n        mcMMO.p.getServer().getPluginManager().callEvent(event);\n\n        return event;\n    }\n\n    public static FakePlayerFishEvent callFakeFishEvent(Player player, FishHook hook) {\n        FakePlayerFishEvent event = new FakePlayerFishEvent(player, null, hook, PlayerFishEvent.State.FISHING);\n        mcMMO.p.getServer().getPluginManager().callEvent(event);\n\n        return event;\n    }\n\n    public static McMMOPlayerRepairCheckEvent callRepairCheckEvent(Player player, short durability, ItemStack repairMaterial, ItemStack repairedObject) {\n        McMMOPlayerRepairCheckEvent event = new McMMOPlayerRepairCheckEvent(player, durability, repairMaterial, repairedObject);\n        mcMMO.p.getServer().getPluginManager().callEvent(event);\n\n        return event;\n    }\n\n    public static McMMOPlayerPreDeathPenaltyEvent callPreDeathPenaltyEvent(Player player) {\n        McMMOPlayerPreDeathPenaltyEvent event = new McMMOPlayerPreDeathPenaltyEvent(player);\n        mcMMO.p.getServer().getPluginManager().callEvent(event);\n\n        return event;\n    }\n\n    public static McMMOPlayerDisarmEvent callDisarmEvent(Player defender) {\n        McMMOPlayerDisarmEvent event = new McMMOPlayerDisarmEvent(defender);\n        mcMMO.p.getServer().getPluginManager().callEvent(event);\n\n        return event;\n    }\n\n    public static McMMOPlayerSalvageCheckEvent callSalvageCheckEvent(Player player, ItemStack salvageMaterial, ItemStack salvageResults, ItemStack enchantedBook) {\n        McMMOPlayerSalvageCheckEvent event = new McMMOPlayerSalvageCheckEvent(player, salvageMaterial, salvageResults, enchantedBook);\n        mcMMO.p.getServer().getPluginManager().callEvent(event);\n\n        return event;\n    }\n\n\n}\n"

      "path": "src/main/java/com/gmail/nossr50/util/ItemUtils.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package com.gmail.nossr50.util;\n\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.config.experience.ExperienceConfig;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.config.party.ItemWeightConfig;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.treasure.EnchantmentWrapper;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.treasure.FishingTreasureBook;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.locale.LocaleLoader;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.mcMMO;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.skills.smelting.Smelting;\nimport org.bukkit.ChatColor;\nimport org.bukkit.Material;\nimport org.bukkit.NamespacedKey;\nimport org.bukkit.enchantments.Enchantment;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.Player;\nimport org.bukkit.inventory.FurnaceRecipe;\nimport org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack;\nimport org.bukkit.inventory.Recipe;\nimport org.bukkit.inventory.meta.EnchantmentStorageMeta;\nimport org.bukkit.inventory.meta.ItemMeta;\nimport org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;\nimport org.jetbrains.annotations.Nullable;\n\nimport java.lang.reflect.InvocationTargetException;\nimport java.lang.reflect.Method;\nimport java.util.Collections;\nimport java.util.List;\n\nimport static java.util.Objects.requireNonNull;\n\npublic final class ItemUtils {\n    /**\n     * This is a static utility class, therefore we don't want any instances of\n     * this class. Making the constructor private prevents accidents like that.\n     */\n    private ItemUtils() {}\n\n    private static final Method setItemName;\n\n    static {\n        setItemName = getSetItemName();\n    }\n\n    private static Method getSetItemName() {\n        try {\n            return ItemMeta.class.getMethod(\"setItemName\", String.class);\n        } catch (NoSuchMethodException e) {\n            return null;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Sets the item name using the new API if available\n     * or falls back to the old API.\n     * @param itemMeta The item meta to set the name on\n     * @param name The name to set\n     */\n    public static void setItemName(ItemMeta itemMeta, String name) {\n        if (setItemName != null) {\n            setItemNameModern(itemMeta, name);\n        } else {\n            itemMeta.setDisplayName(ChatColor.RESET + name);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static void setItemNameModern(ItemMeta itemMeta, String name) {\n        try {\n            setItemName.invoke(itemMeta, name);\n        } catch (IllegalAccessException | InvocationTargetException e) {\n            mcMMO.p.getLogger().severe(\"Failed to set item name: \" + e.getMessage());\n            throw new RuntimeException(e);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Checks if the item is a bow.\n     *\n     * @param item Item to check\n     * @return true if the item is a bow, false otherwise\n     */\n    // TODO: Unit tests\n    public static boolean isBow(@NotNull ItemStack item) {\n        return mcMMO.getMaterialMapStore().isBow(item.getType().getKey().getKey());\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Exhaustive lookup for a Material by name.\n     * <p>\n     *     This method will first try a normal lookup, then a legacy lookup, then a lookup by ENUM name,\n     *      and finally a lookup by ENUM name with legacy name.\n     * @param materialName The name of the material to lookup\n     * @return The Material if found, or null if not found\n     */\n    public static @Nullable Material exhaustiveMaterialLookup(@NotNull String materialName) {\n        requireNonNull(materialName, \"materialName cannot be null\");\n\n        // First try a normal lookup\n        Material material = Material.matchMaterial(materialName);\n\n        // If that fails, try a legacy lookup\n        if (material == null) {\n            material = Material.matchMaterial(materialName, true);\n        }\n\n        // try to match to Material ENUM\n        if (material == null) {\n            material = Material.getMaterial(materialName.toUpperCase());\n        }\n\n        // try to match to Material ENUM with legacy name\n        if (material == null) {\n            material = Material.getMaterial(materialName.toUpperCase(), true);\n        }\n        return material;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Checks if a player has an item in their inventory or offhand.\n     *\n     * @param player Player to check\n     * @param material Material to check for\n     * @return true if the player has the item in their inventory or offhand, false otherwise\n     */\n    public static boolean hasItemIncludingOffHand(Player player, Material material) {\n        // Checks main inventory / item bar\n        boolean containsInMain = player.getInventory().contains(material);\n\n        if (containsInMain) {\n            return true;\n        }\n\n        return player.getInventory().getItemInOffHand().getType() == material;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Removes an item from a player's inventory, including their offhand.\n     *\n     * @param player Player to remove the item from\n     * @param material Material to remove\n     * @param amount Amount of the material to remove\n     */\n    public static void removeItemIncludingOffHand(@NotNull Player player, @NotNull Material material, int amount) {\n        // Checks main inventory / item bar\n        if (player.getInventory().contains(material)) {\n            player.getInventory().removeItem(new ItemStack(material, amount));\n            return;\n        }\n\n        // Check off-hand\n        final ItemStack offHandItem = player.getInventory().getItemInOffHand();\n        if (offHandItem.getType() == material) {\n            int newAmount = offHandItem.getAmount() - amount;\n            if (newAmount > 0) {\n                offHandItem.setAmount(newAmount);\n            } else {\n                player.getInventory().setItemInOffHand(new ItemStack(Material.AIR));\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // TODO: Unit tests\n    public static boolean isCrossbow(@NotNull ItemStack item) {\n        return mcMMO.getMaterialMapStore().isCrossbow(item.getType().getKey().getKey());\n    }\n\n    // TODO: Unit tests\n    public static boolean isBowOrCrossbow(@NotNull ItemStack item) {\n        return isBow(item) || isCrossbow(item);\n    }\n\n    // TODO: Unit tests\n    public static boolean isTrident(@NotNull ItemStack item) {\n        return mcMMO.getMaterialMapStore().isTrident(item.getType().getKey().getKey());\n    }\n\n    public static boolean isMace(@NotNull ItemStack item) {\n        return mcMMO.getMaterialMapStore().isMace(item.getType().getKey().getKey());\n    }\n\n    public static boolean hasItemInEitherHand(@NotNull Player player, Material material) {\n        return player.getInventory().getItemInMainHand().getType() == material || player.getInventory().getItemInOffHand().getType() == material;\n    }\n\n    public static boolean doesPlayerHaveEnchantmentOnArmor(@NotNull Player player, @NotNull String enchantmentByName) {\n        Enchantment enchantment = getEnchantment(enchantmentByName);\n\n        if (enchantment == null)\n            return false;\n\n        return doesPlayerHaveEnchantmentOnArmor(player, enchantment);\n    }\n\n    public static boolean doesPlayerHaveEnchantmentOnArmor(@NotNull Player player, @NotNull Enchantment enchantment) {\n        for(ItemStack itemStack : player.getInventory().getArmorContents()) {\n            if (itemStack != null) {\n                if (hasEnchantment(itemStack, enchantment))\n                    return true;\n            }\n        }\n\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    public static boolean doesPlayerHaveEnchantmentOnArmorOrHands(@NotNull Player player, @NotNull String enchantmentName) {\n        Enchantment enchantment = getEnchantment(enchantmentName);\n\n        if (enchantment == null)\n            return false;\n\n        return doesPlayerHaveEnchantmentOnArmorOrHands(player, enchantment);\n    }\n\n    public static boolean doesPlayerHaveEnchantmentOnArmorOrHands(@NotNull Player player, @NotNull Enchantment enchantment) {\n        if (doesPlayerHaveEnchantmentOnArmor(player, enchantment))\n            return true;\n\n        if (doesPlayerHaveEnchantmentInHands(player, enchantment))\n            return true;\n\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    public static boolean doesPlayerHaveEnchantmentInHands(@NotNull Player player, @NotNull NamespacedKey enchantmentNameKey) {\n        Enchantment enchantment = Enchantment.getByKey(enchantmentNameKey);\n\n        if (enchantment == null)\n            return false;\n\n        return doesPlayerHaveEnchantmentInHands(player, enchantment);\n    }\n\n    public static boolean doesPlayerHaveEnchantmentInHands(@NotNull Player player, @NotNull String enchantmentName) {\n        Enchantment enchantment = getEnchantment(enchantmentName);\n\n        if (enchantment == null)\n            return false;\n\n        return doesPlayerHaveEnchantmentInHands(player, enchantment);\n    }\n\n    public static boolean doesPlayerHaveEnchantmentInHands(@NotNull Player player, @NotNull Enchantment enchantment) {\n        return hasEnchantment(player.getInventory().getItemInMainHand(), enchantment) ||\n            hasEnchantment(player.getInventory().getItemInOffHand(), enchantment);\n    }\n\n    public static boolean hasEnchantment(@NotNull ItemStack itemStack, @NotNull Enchantment enchantment) {\n        if (itemStack.getItemMeta() != null) {\n            return itemStack.getItemMeta().hasEnchant(enchantment);\n        }\n\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    public static @Nullable Enchantment getEnchantment(@NotNull String enchantmentName) {\n        for(Enchantment enchantment : Enchantment.values()) {\n            if (enchantment.getKey().getKey().equalsIgnoreCase(enchantmentName)) {\n                return enchantment;\n            }\n        }\n\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Checks if the item is a sword.\n     *\n     * @param item Item to check\n     * @return true if the item is a sword, false otherwise\n     */\n    public static boolean isSword(@NotNull ItemStack item) {\n        return mcMMO.getMaterialMapStore().isSword(item.getType().getKey().getKey());\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Checks if the item is a hoe.\n     *\n     * @param item Item to check\n     * @return true if the item is a hoe, false otherwise\n     */\n    public static boolean isHoe(@NotNull ItemStack item) {\n        return mcMMO.getMaterialMapStore().isHoe(item.getType().getKey().getKey());\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Checks if the item is a shovel.\n     *\n     * @param item Item to check\n     * @return true if the item is a shovel, false otherwise\n     */\n    public static boolean isShovel(@NotNull ItemStack item) {\n        return mcMMO.getMaterialMapStore().isShovel(item.getType().getKey().getKey());\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Checks if the item is an axe.\n     *\n     * @param item Item to check\n     * @return true if the item is an axe, false otherwise\n     */\n    public static boolean isAxe(@NotNull ItemStack item) {\n        return mcMMO.getMaterialMapStore().isAxe(item.getType().getKey().getKey());\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Checks if the item is a pickaxe.\n     *\n     * @param item Item to check\n     * @return true if the item is a pickaxe, false otherwise\n     */\n    public static boolean isPickaxe(@NotNull ItemStack item) {\n        return mcMMO.getMaterialMapStore().isPickAxe(item.getType().getKey().getKey());\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Checks if the item counts as unarmed.\n     *\n     * @param item Item to check\n     * @return true if the item counts as unarmed, false otherwise\n     */\n    public static boolean isUnarmed(ItemStack item) {\n        if (mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getUnarmedItemsAsUnarmed()) {\n            return !isMinecraftTool(item);\n        }\n\n        return item.getType() == Material.AIR;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Checks to see if an item is a wearable armor piece.\n     *\n     * @param item Item to check\n     * @return true if the item is armor, false otherwise\n     */\n    public static boolean isArmor(ItemStack item) {\n        return mcMMO.getMaterialMapStore().isArmor(item.getType());\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Checks to see if an item is a leather armor piece.\n     *\n     * @param item Item to check\n     * @return true if the item is leather armor, false otherwise\n     */\n    public static boolean isLeatherArmor(ItemStack item) {\n        return mcMMO.getMaterialMapStore().isLeatherArmor(item.getType());\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Checks to see if an item is a gold armor piece.\n     *\n     * @param item Item to check\n     * @return true if the item is gold armor, false otherwise\n     */\n    public static boolean isGoldArmor(ItemStack item) {\n        return mcMMO.getMaterialMapStore().isGoldArmor(item.getType().getKey().getKey());\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Checks to see if an item is an iron armor piece.\n     *\n     * @param item Item to check\n     * @return true if the item is iron armor, false otherwise\n     */\n    public static boolean isIronArmor(ItemStack item) {\n        return mcMMO.getMaterialMapStore().isIronArmor(item.getType().getKey().getKey());\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Checks to see if an item is a diamond armor piece.\n     *\n     * @param item Item to check\n     * @return true if the item is diamond armor, false otherwise\n     */\n    public static boolean isDiamondArmor(ItemStack item) {\n        return mcMMO.getMaterialMapStore().isDiamondArmor(item.getType().getKey().getKey());\n    }\n\n    public static boolean isNetheriteArmor(ItemStack itemStack) {\n        return mcMMO.getMaterialMapStore().isNetheriteArmor(itemStack.getType().getKey().getKey());\n    }\n\n    public static boolean isNetheriteTool(ItemStack itemStack) {\n        return mcMMO.getMaterialMapStore().isNetheriteTool(itemStack.getType().getKey().getKey());\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Checks to see if an item is a chainmail armor piece.\n     *\n     * @param item Item to check\n     * @return true if the item is chainmail armor, false otherwise\n     */\n    public static boolean isChainmailArmor(ItemStack item) {\n        return mcMMO.getMaterialMapStore().isChainmailArmor(item.getType().getKey().getKey());\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Checks to see if an item is a *vanilla* tool.\n     *\n     * @param item Item to check\n     * @return true if the item is a tool, false otherwise\n     */\n    public static boolean isMinecraftTool(ItemStack item) {\n        return mcMMO.getMaterialMapStore().isTool(item.getType().getKey().getKey());\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Checks to see if an item is a stone tool.\n     *\n     * @param item Item to check\n     * @return true if the item is a stone tool, false otherwise\n     */\n    public static boolean isStoneTool(ItemStack item) {\n        return mcMMO.getMaterialMapStore().isStoneTool(item.getType().getKey().getKey());\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Checks to see if an item is a wooden tool.\n     *\n     * @param item Item to check\n     * @return true if the item is a wooden tool, false otherwise\n     */\n    public static boolean isWoodTool(ItemStack item) {\n        return mcMMO.getMaterialMapStore().isWoodTool(item.getType().getKey().getKey());\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Checks to see if an item is a string tool.\n     *\n     * @param item Item to check\n     * @return true if the item is a string tool, false otherwise\n     */\n    public static boolean isStringTool(ItemStack item) {\n        return mcMMO.getMaterialMapStore().isStringTool(item.getType().getKey().getKey());\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Checks to see if an item is a gold tool.\n     *\n     * @param item Item to check\n     * @return true if the item is a stone tool, false otherwise\n     */\n    public static boolean isGoldTool(ItemStack item) {\n        return mcMMO.getMaterialMapStore().isGoldTool(item.getType().getKey().getKey());\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Checks to see if an item is an iron tool.\n     *\n     * @param item Item to check\n     * @return true if the item is an iron tool, false otherwise\n     */\n    public static boolean isIronTool(ItemStack item) {\n        return mcMMO.getMaterialMapStore().isIronTool(item.getType().getKey().getKey());\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Checks to see if an item is a diamond tool.\n     *\n     * @param item Item to check\n     * @return true if the item is a diamond tool, false otherwise\n     */\n    public static boolean isDiamondTool(ItemStack item) {\n        return mcMMO.getMaterialMapStore().isDiamondTool(item.getType().getKey().getKey());\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Checks to see if an item is enchantable.\n     *\n     * @param item Item to check\n     * @return true if the item is enchantable, false otherwise\n     */\n    public static boolean isEnchantable(ItemStack item) {\n        return mcMMO.getMaterialMapStore().isEnchantable(item.getType().getKey().getKey());\n    }\n\n    public static boolean isSmeltable(ItemStack item) {\n        return item != null && Smelting.getSmeltXP(item) >= 1;\n    }\n\n    public static boolean isSmelted(ItemStack item) {\n        if (item == null) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        for (Recipe recipe : mcMMO.p.getServer().getRecipesFor(item)) {\n            if (recipe instanceof FurnaceRecipe\n                    && ((FurnaceRecipe) recipe).getInput().getType().isBlock()\n                    && MaterialUtils.isOre(((FurnaceRecipe) recipe).getInput().getType())) {\n                return true;\n            }\n        }\n\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Check if an item is sharable.\n     *\n     * @param item Item that will get shared\n     * @return True if the item can be shared.\n     */\n    public static boolean isSharable(ItemStack item) {\n        if (item == null || item.getType() == Material.AIR) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        return isMiningDrop(item) || isWoodcuttingDrop(item) || isMobDrop(item) || isHerbalismDrop(item) || isMiscDrop(item);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Checks to see if an item is a mining drop.\n     *\n     * @param item Item to check\n     * @return true if the item is a mining drop, false otherwise\n     */\n    public static boolean isMiningDrop(ItemStack item) {\n        //TODO: 1.14 This needs to be updated\n        switch (item.getType()) {\n            case COAL:\n            case COAL_ORE:\n            case DIAMOND:\n            case DIAMOND_ORE:\n            case EMERALD:\n            case EMERALD_ORE:\n            case GOLD_ORE:\n            case IRON_ORE:\n            case LAPIS_ORE:\n            case REDSTONE_ORE: // Should we also have Glowing Redstone Ore here?\n            case REDSTONE:\n            case GLOWSTONE_DUST: // Should we also have Glowstone here?\n            case QUARTZ:\n            case NETHER_QUARTZ_ORE:\n            case LAPIS_LAZULI:\n                return true;\n\n            default:\n                return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Checks to see if an item is a herbalism drop.\n     *\n     * @param item Item to check\n     * @return true if the item is a herbalism drop, false otherwise\n     */\n    public static boolean isHerbalismDrop(ItemStack item) {\n        //TODO: 1.14 This needs to be updated\n        switch (item.getType().getKey().getKey().toLowerCase()) {\n            case \"wheat\":\n            case \"wheat_seeds\":\n            case \"carrot\":\n            case \"chorus_fruit\":\n            case \"chorus_flower\":\n            case \"potato\":\n            case \"beetroot\":\n            case \"beetroots\":\n            case \"beetroot_seeds\":\n            case \"nether_wart\":\n            case \"brown_mushroom\":\n            case \"red_mushroom\":\n            case \"rose_bush\":\n            case \"dandelion\":\n            case \"cactus\":\n            case \"sugar_cane\":\n            case \"melon\":\n            case \"melon_seeds\":\n            case \"pumpkin\":\n            case \"pumpkin_seeds\":\n            case \"lily_pad\":\n            case \"vine\":\n            case \"tall_grass\":\n            case \"cocoa_beans\":\n                return true;\n\n            default:\n                return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n\n    /**\n     * Checks to see if an item is a mob drop.\n     *\n     * @param item Item to check\n     * @return true if the item is a mob drop, false otherwise\n     */\n    public static boolean isMobDrop(ItemStack item) {\n        //TODO: 1.14 This needs to be updated\n        switch (item.getType()) {\n            case STRING:\n            case FEATHER:\n            case CHICKEN:\n            case COOKED_CHICKEN:\n            case LEATHER:\n            case BEEF:\n            case COOKED_BEEF:\n            case PORKCHOP:\n            case COOKED_PORKCHOP:\n            case WHITE_WOOL:\n            case BLACK_WOOL:\n            case BLUE_WOOL:\n            case BROWN_WOOL:\n            case CYAN_WOOL:\n            case GRAY_WOOL:\n            case GREEN_WOOL:\n            case LIGHT_BLUE_WOOL:\n            case LIGHT_GRAY_WOOL:\n            case LIME_WOOL:\n            case MAGENTA_WOOL:\n            case ORANGE_WOOL:\n            case PINK_WOOL:\n            case PURPLE_WOOL:\n            case RED_WOOL:\n            case YELLOW_WOOL:\n            case IRON_INGOT:\n            case SNOWBALL:\n            case BLAZE_ROD:\n            case SPIDER_EYE:\n            case GUNPOWDER:\n            case ENDER_PEARL:\n            case GHAST_TEAR:\n            case MAGMA_CREAM:\n            case BONE:\n            case ARROW:\n            case SLIME_BALL:\n            case NETHER_STAR:\n            case ROTTEN_FLESH:\n            case GOLD_NUGGET:\n            case EGG:\n            case ROSE_BUSH:\n            case COAL:\n                return true;\n\n            default:\n                return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Checks to see if an item is a woodcutting drop.\n     *\n     * @param item Item to check\n     * @return true if the item is a woodcutting drop, false otherwise\n     */\n    public static boolean isWoodcuttingDrop(ItemStack item) {\n        switch (item.getType().toString()) {\n            case \"ACACIA_LOG\":\n            case \"BIRCH_LOG\":\n            case \"DARK_OAK_LOG\":\n            case \"JUNGLE_LOG\":\n            case \"OAK_LOG\":\n            case \"SPRUCE_LOG\":\n            case \"STRIPPED_ACACIA_LOG\":\n            case \"STRIPPED_BIRCH_LOG\":\n            case \"STRIPPED_DARK_OAK_LOG\":\n            case \"STRIPPED_JUNGLE_LOG\":\n            case \"STRIPPED_OAK_LOG\":\n            case \"STRIPPED_SPRUCE_LOG\":\n            case \"STRIPPED_MANGROVE_LOG\":\n            case \"ACACIA_SAPLING\":\n            case \"SPRUCE_SAPLING\":\n            case \"BIRCH_SAPLING\":\n            case \"DARK_OAK_SAPLING\":\n            case \"JUNGLE_SAPLING\":\n            case \"OAK_SAPLING\":\n            case \"ACACIA_LEAVES\":\n            case \"BIRCH_LEAVES\":\n            case \"DARK_OAK_LEAVES\":\n            case \"JUNGLE_LEAVES\":\n            case \"OAK_LEAVES\":\n            case \"SPRUCE_LEAVES\":\n            case \"BEE_NEST\":\n            case \"APPLE\":\n                return true;\n\n            default:\n                return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Checks to see if an item is a miscellaneous drop. These items are read from the config file\n     *\n     * @param item Item to check\n     * @return true if the item is a miscellaneous drop, false otherwise\n     */\n    public static boolean isMiscDrop(ItemStack item) {\n        return ItemWeightConfig.getInstance().getMiscItems().contains(item.getType());\n    }\n\n    public static boolean isMcMMOItem(ItemStack item) {\n        if (!item.hasItemMeta()) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        ItemMeta itemMeta = item.getItemMeta();\n\n        if (itemMeta == null)\n            return false;\n\n        return itemMeta.getLore() != null\n                && itemMeta.getLore().contains(\"mcMMO Item\");\n    }\n\n    public static boolean isChimaeraWing(ItemStack item) {\n        if (!isMcMMOItem(item)) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        ItemMeta itemMeta = item.getItemMeta();\n\n        if (itemMeta == null)\n            return false;\n\n        return itemMeta.hasDisplayName() && itemMeta.getDisplayName().equals(ChatColor.GOLD + LocaleLoader.getString(\"Item.ChimaeraWing.Name\"));\n    }\n\n//    public static void addAbilityLore(@NotNull ItemStack itemStack) {\n//        ItemMeta itemMeta = itemStack.getItemMeta();\n//        List<String> itemLore = new ArrayList<>();\n//\n//        if (itemMeta == null)\n//            return;\n//\n//        if (itemMeta.hasLore()) {\n//            itemLore = itemMeta.getLore();\n//        }\n//\n//        itemLore.add(\"mcMMO Ability Tool\");\n//\n//        itemMeta.setLore(itemLore);\n//        itemStack.setItemMeta(itemMeta);\n//    }\n\n    public static void removeAbilityLore(@NotNull ItemStack itemStack) {\n        ItemMeta itemMeta = itemStack.getItemMeta();\n\n        if (itemMeta == null)\n            return;\n\n        if (itemMeta.hasLore()) {\n            List<String> itemLore = itemMeta.getLore();\n\n            if (itemLore == null)\n                return;\n\n            if (itemLore.remove(\"mcMMO Ability Tool\")) {\n                itemMeta.setLore(itemLore);\n                itemStack.setItemMeta(itemMeta);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    public static void addDigSpeedToItem(@NotNull ItemStack itemStack, int existingEnchantLevel) {\n        ItemMeta itemMeta = itemStack.getItemMeta();\n\n        if (itemMeta == null)\n            return;\n\n        itemMeta.addEnchant(mcMMO.p.getEnchantmentMapper().getEfficiency(),\n                existingEnchantLevel + mcMMO.p.getAdvancedConfig().getEnchantBuff(), true);\n        itemStack.setItemMeta(itemMeta);\n    }\n\n    public static boolean canBeSuperAbilityDigBoosted(@NotNull ItemStack itemStack) {\n        return isShovel(itemStack) || isPickaxe(itemStack);\n    }\n\n    public static @NotNull ItemStack createEnchantBook(@NotNull FishingTreasureBook fishingTreasureBook) {\n        ItemStack itemStack = fishingTreasureBook.getDrop().clone();\n        EnchantmentWrapper enchantmentWrapper = getRandomEnchantment(fishingTreasureBook.getLegalEnchantments());\n        ItemMeta itemMeta = itemStack.getItemMeta();\n\n        if (itemMeta == null) {\n            return itemStack;\n        }\n\n        EnchantmentStorageMeta enchantmentStorageMeta = (EnchantmentStorageMeta) itemMeta;\n        enchantmentStorageMeta.addStoredEnchant(enchantmentWrapper.getEnchantment(), enchantmentWrapper.getEnchantmentLevel(), ExperienceConfig.getInstance().allowUnsafeEnchantments());\n        itemStack.setItemMeta(enchantmentStorageMeta);\n        return itemStack;\n    }\n\n    public static @NotNull EnchantmentWrapper getRandomEnchantment(@NotNull List<EnchantmentWrapper> enchantmentWrappers) {\n        Collections.shuffle(enchantmentWrappers, Misc.getRandom());\n\n        int randomIndex = Misc.getRandom().nextInt(enchantmentWrappers.size());\n        return enchantmentWrappers.get(randomIndex);\n    }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "src/main/java/com/gmail/nossr50/util/Misc.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package com.gmail.nossr50.util;\n\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.api.ItemSpawnReason;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.mcMMO;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.runnables.player.PlayerProfileLoadingTask;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.player.UserManager;\nimport org.bukkit.Location;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.Item;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.Player;\nimport org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack;\nimport org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;\nimport org.jetbrains.annotations.Nullable;\n\nimport java.util.Collection;\nimport java.util.function.Predicate;\n\npublic final class Misc {\n    public static final int TIME_CONVERSION_FACTOR = 1000;\n    public static final int TICK_CONVERSION_FACTOR = 20;\n    public static final int PLAYER_RESPAWN_COOLDOWN_SECONDS = 5;\n    public static final double SKILL_MESSAGE_MAX_SENDING_DISTANCE = 10.0;\n\n    private Misc() {\n    }\n\n    public static void profileCleanup(@NotNull String playerName) {\n        Player player = mcMMO.p.getServer().getPlayerExact(playerName);\n        if (player != null) {\n            UserManager.remove(player);\n            mcMMO.p.getFoliaLib().getImpl().runLaterAsync(new PlayerProfileLoadingTask(player), 1);\n        }\n    }\n\n    public static void printProgress(int convertedUsers, int progressInterval, long startMillis) {\n        if (convertedUsers % progressInterval == 0) {\n            mcMMO.p.getLogger().info(String.format(\"Conversion progress: %d users at %.2f users/second\", convertedUsers, convertedUsers / (double) ((System.currentTimeMillis() - startMillis) / TIME_CONVERSION_FACTOR)));\n        }\n    }\n\n    public static void spawnItemsFromCollection(@NotNull Player player, @NotNull Location location, @NotNull Collection<ItemStack> drops, @NotNull ItemSpawnReason itemSpawnReason) {\n        ItemSpawnUtil.spawnItemsFromCollection(player, location, drops, itemSpawnReason);\n    }\n\n    public static void spawnItemsFromCollection(@Nullable Player player, @NotNull Location location, @NotNull Collection<ItemStack> drops, @NotNull ItemSpawnReason itemSpawnReason, int sizeLimit) {\n        ItemSpawnUtil.spawnItemsFromCollection(player, location, drops, itemSpawnReason, sizeLimit);\n    }\n\n    public static void spawnItem(@NotNull Collection<ItemStack> potentialItemDrops, @NotNull ItemSpawnReason itemSpawnReason, @NotNull Location spawnLocation, @NotNull Predicate<String> predicate, @NotNull Player player) {\n        ItemSpawnUtil.spawnItem(potentialItemDrops, itemSpawnReason, spawnLocation, predicate, player);\n    }\n\n    public static void spawnItem(@NotNull ItemStack potentialItemSpawn, @NotNull ItemSpawnReason itemSpawnReason, @NotNull Location spawnLocation, @NotNull Predicate<String> predicate, @NotNull Player player) {\n        ItemSpawnUtil.spawnItem(potentialItemSpawn, itemSpawnReason, spawnLocation, predicate, player);\n    }\n\n    public static void spawnItems(@Nullable Player player, @NotNull Location location, @NotNull ItemStack itemStack, int quantity, @NotNull ItemSpawnReason itemSpawnReason) {\n        ItemSpawnUtil.spawnItems(player, location, itemStack, quantity, itemSpawnReason);\n    }\n\n    public static @Nullable Item spawnItem(@Nullable Player player, @NotNull Location location, @NotNull ItemStack itemStack, @NotNull ItemSpawnReason itemSpawnReason) {\n        return ItemSpawnUtil.spawnItem(player, location, itemStack, itemSpawnReason);\n    }\n\n    public static @Nullable Item spawnItemNaturally(@Nullable Player player, @NotNull Location location, @NotNull ItemStack itemStack, @NotNull ItemSpawnReason itemSpawnReason) {\n        return ItemSpawnUtil.spawnItemNaturally(player, location, itemStack, itemSpawnReason);\n    }\n\n    public static void spawnItemsTowardsLocation(@Nullable Player player, @NotNull Location fromLocation, @NotNull Location toLocation, @NotNull ItemStack itemStack, int quantity, double speed, @NotNull ItemSpawnReason itemSpawnReason) {\n        ItemSpawnUtil.spawnItemsTowardsLocation(player, fromLocation, toLocation, itemStack, quantity, speed, itemSpawnReason);\n    }\n\n    public static @Nullable Item spawnItemTowardsLocation(@Nullable Player player, @NotNull Location fromLocation, @NotNull Location toLocation, @NotNull ItemStack itemStack, double speed, @NotNull ItemSpawnReason itemSpawnReason) {\n        return ItemSpawnUtil.spawnItemTowardsLocation(player, fromLocation, toLocation, itemStack, speed, itemSpawnReason);\n    }\n}\n"
    },
