      "path": "src/main/java/com/gmail/nossr50/util/player/NotificationManager.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package com.gmail.nossr50.util.player;\n\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.LevelUpBroadcastPredicate;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.PowerLevelUpBroadcastPredicate;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.interactions.NotificationType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.notifications.SensitiveCommandType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.player.McMMOPlayer;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.PrimarySkillType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.SubSkillType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.events.skills.McMMOPlayerNotificationEvent;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.locale.LocaleLoader;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.mcMMO;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.Permissions;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.sounds.SoundManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.sounds.SoundType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.text.McMMOMessageType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.text.TextComponentFactory;\nimport net.kyori.adventure.audience.Audience;\nimport net.kyori.adventure.identity.Identity;\nimport net.kyori.adventure.text.Component;\nimport net.kyori.adventure.text.event.HoverEvent;\nimport net.kyori.adventure.text.format.TextColor;\nimport net.kyori.adventure.text.serializer.legacy.LegacyComponentSerializer;\nimport org.bukkit.Bukkit;\nimport org.bukkit.ChatColor;\nimport org.bukkit.Server;\nimport org.bukkit.SoundCategory;\nimport org.bukkit.command.CommandSender;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.Player;\nimport org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;\n\nimport java.time.LocalDate;\n\npublic class NotificationManager {\n\n    public static final String HEX_BEIGE_COLOR = \"#c2a66e\";\n    public static final String HEX_LIME_GREEN_COLOR = \"#8ec26e\";\n\n    /**\n     * Sends players notifications from mcMMO\n     * Does so by sending out an event so other plugins can cancel it\n     * @param player target player\n     * @param notificationType notifications defined type\n     * @param key the locale key for the notifications defined message\n     */\n    public static void sendPlayerInformation(Player player, NotificationType notificationType, String key) {\n        if (UserManager.getPlayer(player) == null || !UserManager.getPlayer(player).useChatNotifications())\n            return;\n\n        McMMOMessageType destination\n                = mcMMO.p.getAdvancedConfig().doesNotificationUseActionBar(notificationType)\n                ? McMMOMessageType.ACTION_BAR : McMMOMessageType.SYSTEM;\n\n        Component message = TextComponentFactory.getNotificationTextComponentFromLocale(key);\n        McMMOPlayerNotificationEvent customEvent = checkNotificationEvent(player, notificationType, destination, message);\n\n        sendNotification(player, customEvent);\n    }\n\n\n    public static boolean doesPlayerUseNotifications(Player player) {\n        if (UserManager.getPlayer(player) == null)\n            return false;\n        else\n            return UserManager.getPlayer(player).useChatNotifications();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Sends players notifications from mcMMO\n     * This does this by sending out an event so other plugins can cancel it\n     * This event in particular is provided with a source player, and players near the source player are sent the information\n     * @param targetPlayer the recipient player for this message\n     * @param notificationType type of notification\n     * @param key Locale Key for the string to use with this event\n     * @param values values to be injected into the locale string\n     */\n    public static void sendNearbyPlayersInformation(Player targetPlayer, NotificationType notificationType, String key,\n                                                    String... values) {\n        sendPlayerInformation(targetPlayer, notificationType, key, values);\n    }\n\n    public static void sendPlayerInformationChatOnly(Player player, String key, String... values) {\n        if (UserManager.getPlayer(player) == null || !UserManager.getPlayer(player).useChatNotifications())\n            return;\n\n        String preColoredString = LocaleLoader.getString(key, (Object[]) values);\n        player.sendMessage(preColoredString);\n    }\n\n    public static void sendPlayerInformationChatOnlyPrefixed(Player player, String key, String... values) {\n        if (UserManager.getPlayer(player) == null || !UserManager.getPlayer(player).useChatNotifications())\n            return;\n\n        String preColoredString = LocaleLoader.getString(key, (Object[]) values);\n        String prefixFormattedMessage = LocaleLoader.getString(\"mcMMO.Template.Prefix\", preColoredString);\n        player.sendMessage(prefixFormattedMessage);\n    }\n\n    public static void sendPlayerInformation(Player player, NotificationType notificationType, String key,\n                                             String... values) {\n        if (UserManager.getPlayer(player) == null || !UserManager.getPlayer(player).useChatNotifications())\n            return;\n\n        McMMOMessageType destination = mcMMO.p.getAdvancedConfig().doesNotificationUseActionBar(notificationType) ? McMMOMessageType.ACTION_BAR : McMMOMessageType.SYSTEM;\n\n        Component message = TextComponentFactory.getNotificationMultipleValues(key, values);\n        McMMOPlayerNotificationEvent customEvent = checkNotificationEvent(player, notificationType, destination, message);\n\n        sendNotification(player, customEvent);\n    }\n\n    private static void sendNotification(Player player, McMMOPlayerNotificationEvent customEvent) {\n        if (customEvent.isCancelled())\n            return;\n\n        final Audience audience = mcMMO.getAudiences().player(player);\n\n\n        Component notificationTextComponent = customEvent.getNotificationTextComponent();\n        if (customEvent.getChatMessageType() == McMMOMessageType.ACTION_BAR) {\n            audience.sendActionBar(notificationTextComponent);\n\n            // If the message is being sent to the action bar we need to check if a copy is also sent to the chat system\n            if (customEvent.isMessageAlsoBeingSentToChat()) {\n                //Send copy to chat system\n                audience.sendMessage(notificationTextComponent);\n            }\n        } else {\n            audience.sendMessage(notificationTextComponent);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static McMMOPlayerNotificationEvent checkNotificationEvent(Player player, NotificationType notificationType,\n                                                                       McMMOMessageType destination,\n                                                                       Component message) {\n        //Init event\n        McMMOPlayerNotificationEvent customEvent = new McMMOPlayerNotificationEvent(player,\n                notificationType, message, destination,\n                mcMMO.p.getAdvancedConfig().doesNotificationSendCopyToChat(notificationType));\n\n        //Call event\n        Bukkit.getServer().getPluginManager().callEvent(customEvent);\n        return customEvent;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Handles sending level up notifications to a mcMMOPlayer\n     * @param mcMMOPlayer target mcMMOPlayer\n     * @param skillName skill that leveled up\n     * @param newLevel new level of that skill\n     */\n    public static void sendPlayerLevelUpNotification(McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer, PrimarySkillType skillName,\n                                                     int levelsGained, int newLevel) {\n        if (!mcMMOPlayer.useChatNotifications())\n            return;\n\n        McMMOMessageType destination\n                = mcMMO.p.getAdvancedConfig().doesNotificationUseActionBar(NotificationType.LEVEL_UP_MESSAGE)\n                ? McMMOMessageType.ACTION_BAR : McMMOMessageType.SYSTEM;\n\n        Component levelUpTextComponent = TextComponentFactory.getNotificationLevelUpTextComponent(\n                skillName, levelsGained, newLevel);\n        McMMOPlayerNotificationEvent customEvent = checkNotificationEvent(\n                mcMMOPlayer.getPlayer(),\n                NotificationType.LEVEL_UP_MESSAGE,\n                destination,\n                levelUpTextComponent);\n\n        sendNotification(mcMMOPlayer.getPlayer(), customEvent);\n    }\n\n    public static void broadcastTitle(Server server, String title, String subtitle, int i1, int i2, int i3) {\n        for(Player player : server.getOnlinePlayers()) {\n            player.sendTitle(title, subtitle, i1, i2, i3);\n        }\n    }\n\n    public static void sendPlayerUnlockNotification(McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer, SubSkillType subSkillType) {\n        if (!mcMMOPlayer.useChatNotifications())\n            return;\n\n        //CHAT MESSAGE\n        mcMMO.getAudiences().player(mcMMOPlayer.getPlayer()).sendMessage(Identity.nil(),\n                TextComponentFactory.getSubSkillUnlockedNotificationComponents(mcMMOPlayer.getPlayer(), subSkillType));\n\n        //Unlock Sound Effect\n        SoundManager.sendCategorizedSound(mcMMOPlayer.getPlayer(), mcMMOPlayer.getPlayer().getLocation(),\n                SoundType.SKILL_UNLOCKED, SoundCategory.MASTER);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Sends a message to all admins with the admin notification formatting from the locale\n     * Admins are currently players with either Operator status or Admin Chat permission\n     * @param msg message fetched from locale\n     */\n    private static void sendAdminNotification(String msg) {\n        //If its not enabled exit\n        if (!mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().adminNotifications())\n            return;\n\n        for(Player player : Bukkit.getServer().getOnlinePlayers()) {\n            if (player.isOp() || Permissions.adminChat(player)) {\n                player.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Notifications.Admin.Format.Others\", msg));\n            }\n        }\n\n        //Copy it out to Console too\n        mcMMO.p.getLogger().info(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Notifications.Admin.Format.Others\", msg));\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Sends a confirmation message to the CommandSender who just executed an admin command\n     * @param commandSender target command sender\n     * @param msg message fetched from locale\n     */\n    private static void sendAdminCommandConfirmation(CommandSender commandSender, String msg) {\n        commandSender.sendMessage(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Notifications.Admin.Format.Self\", msg));\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Convenience method to report info about a command sender using a sensitive command\n     * @param commandSender the command user\n     * @param sensitiveCommandType type of command issued\n     */\n    public static void processSensitiveCommandNotification(CommandSender commandSender,\n                                                           SensitiveCommandType sensitiveCommandType, String... args) {\n        /*\n         * Determine the 'identity' of the one who executed the command to pass as a parameters\n         */\n        String senderName = LocaleLoader.getString(\"Server.ConsoleName\");\n\n        if (commandSender instanceof Player) {\n            senderName = ((Player) commandSender).getDisplayName()\n                    + ChatColor.RESET + \"-\" + ((Player) commandSender).getUniqueId();\n        }\n\n        //Send the notification\n        switch(sensitiveCommandType) {\n            case XPRATE_MODIFY:\n                sendAdminNotification(LocaleLoader.getString(\"Notifications.Admin.XPRate.Start.Others\",\n                        addItemToFirstPositionOfArray(senderName, args)));\n                sendAdminCommandConfirmation(\n                        commandSender, LocaleLoader.getString(\"Notifications.Admin.XPRate.Start.Self\", args));\n                break;\n            case XPRATE_END:\n                sendAdminNotification(\n                        LocaleLoader.getString(\n                                \"Notifications.Admin.XPRate.End.Others\",\n                                addItemToFirstPositionOfArray(senderName, args)));\n                sendAdminCommandConfirmation(commandSender,\n                        LocaleLoader.getString(\"Notifications.Admin.XPRate.End.Self\", args));\n                break;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Takes an array and an object, makes a new array with object in the first position of the new array,\n     * and the following elements in this new array being a copy of the existing array retaining their order\n     * @param itemToAdd the string to put at the beginning of the new array\n     * @param existingArray the existing array to be copied to the new array at position [0]+1 relative to their original index\n     * @return the new array combining itemToAdd at the start and existing array elements following while retaining their order\n     */\n    public static String[] addItemToFirstPositionOfArray(String itemToAdd, String... existingArray) {\n        String[] newArray = new String[existingArray.length + 1];\n        newArray[0] = itemToAdd;\n\n        System.arraycopy(existingArray, 0, newArray, 1, existingArray.length);\n\n        return newArray;\n    }\n\n    public static void processLevelUpBroadcasting(@NotNull McMMOPlayer mmoPlayer, @NotNull PrimarySkillType primarySkillType, int level) {\n        if (level <= 0)\n            return;\n\n        //Check if broadcasting is enabled\n        if (mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().shouldLevelUpBroadcasts()) {\n            //Permission check\n            if (!Permissions.levelUpBroadcast(mmoPlayer.getPlayer())) {\n                return;\n            }\n\n            int levelInterval = mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getLevelUpBroadcastInterval();\n            int remainder = level % levelInterval;\n\n            if (remainder == 0) {\n                //Grab appropriate audience\n                Audience audience = mcMMO.getAudiences().filter(getLevelUpBroadcastPredicate(mmoPlayer.getPlayer()));\n                //TODO: Make prettier\n                HoverEvent<Component> levelMilestoneHover = Component.text(mmoPlayer.getPlayer().getName())\n                        .append(Component.newline())\n                        .append(Component.text(LocalDate.now().toString()))\n                        .append(Component.newline())\n                        .append(Component.text(\n                                mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().getLocalizedSkillName(primarySkillType)\n                                        + \" reached level \"+level)).color(TextColor.fromHexString(HEX_BEIGE_COLOR))\n                        .asHoverEvent();\n\n                String localeMessage = LocaleLoader.getString(\n                        \"Broadcasts.LevelUpMilestone\", mmoPlayer.getPlayer().getDisplayName(), level,\n                        mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().getLocalizedSkillName(primarySkillType));\n                Component component = LegacyComponentSerializer\n                        .legacySection()\n                        .deserialize(localeMessage)\n                        .hoverEvent(levelMilestoneHover);\n\n                // TODO: Update system msg API\n                mcMMO.p.getFoliaLib().getImpl().runNextTick(\n                        t -> audience.sendMessage(component));\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    //TODO: Remove the code duplication, am lazy atm\n    //TODO: Fix broadcasts being skipped for situations where a player skips over the milestone like with the addlevels command\n    public static void processPowerLevelUpBroadcasting(@NotNull McMMOPlayer mmoPlayer, int powerLevel) {\n        if (powerLevel <= 0)\n            return;\n\n        //Check if broadcasting is enabled\n        if (mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().shouldPowerLevelUpBroadcasts()) {\n            //Permission check\n            if (!Permissions.levelUpBroadcast(mmoPlayer.getPlayer())) {\n                return;\n            }\n\n            int levelInterval = mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getPowerLevelUpBroadcastInterval();\n            int remainder = powerLevel % levelInterval;\n\n            if (remainder == 0) {\n                //Grab appropriate audience\n                Audience audience = mcMMO.getAudiences().filter(getPowerLevelUpBroadcastPredicate(mmoPlayer.getPlayer()));\n                //TODO: Make prettier\n                HoverEvent<Component> levelMilestoneHover = Component.text(mmoPlayer.getPlayer().getName())\n                        .append(Component.newline())\n                        .append(Component.text(LocalDate.now().toString()))\n                        .append(Component.newline())\n                        .append(Component.text(\"Power level has reached \"+powerLevel)).color(TextColor.fromHexString(HEX_BEIGE_COLOR))\n                        .asHoverEvent();\n\n                String localeMessage = LocaleLoader.getString(\"Broadcasts.PowerLevelUpMilestone\", mmoPlayer.getPlayer().getDisplayName(), powerLevel);\n                Component message = LegacyComponentSerializer.legacySection().deserialize(localeMessage).hoverEvent(levelMilestoneHover);\n\n                mcMMO.p.getFoliaLib().getImpl().runNextTick(t -> audience.sendMessage(message));\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    //TODO: Could cache\n    public static @NotNull LevelUpBroadcastPredicate<CommandSender> getLevelUpBroadcastPredicate(@NotNull CommandSender levelUpPlayer) {\n        return new LevelUpBroadcastPredicate<>(levelUpPlayer);\n    }\n\n    public static @NotNull PowerLevelUpBroadcastPredicate<CommandSender> getPowerLevelUpBroadcastPredicate(@NotNull CommandSender levelUpPlayer) {\n        return new PowerLevelUpBroadcastPredicate<>(levelUpPlayer);\n    }\n\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "src/main/java/com/gmail/nossr50/util/scoreboards/ScoreboardManager.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package com.gmail.nossr50.util.scoreboards;\n\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.database.PlayerStat;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.player.McMMOPlayer;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.player.PlayerProfile;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.PrimarySkillType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.SuperAbilityType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.events.scoreboard.McMMOScoreboardMakeboardEvent;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.events.scoreboard.ScoreboardEventReason;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.locale.LocaleLoader;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.mcMMO;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.LogUtils;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.Misc;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.player.UserManager;\nimport com.google.common.collect.ImmutableList;\nimport com.google.common.collect.ImmutableMap;\nimport com.google.common.collect.Lists;\nimport org.bukkit.Bukkit;\nimport org.bukkit.ChatColor;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.Player;\nimport org.bukkit.scoreboard.DisplaySlot;\nimport org.bukkit.scoreboard.Objective;\nimport org.bukkit.scoreboard.Scoreboard;\nimport org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;\nimport org.jetbrains.annotations.Nullable;\n\nimport java.util.*;\n\n/**\n * Manages the Scoreboards used to display a variety of mcMMO related information to the player\n */\npublic class ScoreboardManager {\n    static final Map<String, ScoreboardWrapper> PLAYER_SCOREBOARDS = new HashMap<>();\n\n    // do not localize; these are internal identifiers\n    static final String SIDEBAR_OBJECTIVE = \"mcmmo_sidebar\";\n    static final String POWER_OBJECTIVE = \"mcmmo_pwrlvl\";\n\n    static final String HEADER_STATS = LocaleLoader.getString(\"Scoreboard.Header.PlayerStats\");\n    static final String HEADER_COOLDOWNS = LocaleLoader.getString(\"Scoreboard.Header.PlayerCooldowns\");\n    static final String HEADER_RANK = LocaleLoader.getString(\"Scoreboard.Header.PlayerRank\");\n    static final String TAG_POWER_LEVEL = LocaleLoader.getString(\"Scoreboard.Header.PowerLevel\");\n\n    static final String POWER_LEVEL = LocaleLoader.getString(\"Scoreboard.Misc.PowerLevel\");\n\n    static final String LABEL_POWER_LEVEL = POWER_LEVEL;\n    static final String LABEL_LEVEL = LocaleLoader.getString(\"Scoreboard.Misc.Level\");\n    static final String LABEL_CURRENT_XP = LocaleLoader.getString(\"Scoreboard.Misc.CurrentXP\");\n    static final String LABEL_REMAINING_XP = LocaleLoader.getString(\"Scoreboard.Misc.RemainingXP\");\n//    static final String LABEL_ABILITY_COOLDOWN = LocaleLoader.getString(\"Scoreboard.Misc.Cooldown\");\n//    static final String LABEL_OVERALL = LocaleLoader.getString(\"Scoreboard.Misc.Overall\");\n\n    static final Map<PrimarySkillType, String>   skillLabels;\n    static final Map<SuperAbilityType, String> abilityLabelsColored;\n    static final Map<SuperAbilityType, String> abilityLabelsSkill;\n\n    public static final String DISPLAY_NAME = \"powerLevel\";\n\n    /*\n     * Initializes the static properties of this class\n     */\n    static {\n        /*\n         * We need immutable objects for our Scoreboard's labels\n         */\n        ImmutableMap.Builder<PrimarySkillType, String> skillLabelBuilder = ImmutableMap.builder();\n        ImmutableMap.Builder<SuperAbilityType, String> abilityLabelBuilder = ImmutableMap.builder();\n        ImmutableMap.Builder<SuperAbilityType, String> abilityLabelSkillBuilder = ImmutableMap.builder();\n\n        /*\n         * Builds the labels for our ScoreBoards\n         * Stylizes the targetBoard in a Rainbow Pattern\n         * This is off by default\n         */\n        if (mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getScoreboardRainbows()) {\n            // Everything but black, gray, gold\n            List<ChatColor> colors = Lists.newArrayList(\n                    ChatColor.WHITE,\n                    ChatColor.YELLOW,\n                    ChatColor.LIGHT_PURPLE,\n                    ChatColor.RED,\n                    ChatColor.AQUA,\n                    ChatColor.GREEN,\n                    ChatColor.DARK_GRAY,\n                    ChatColor.BLUE,\n                    ChatColor.DARK_PURPLE,\n                    ChatColor.DARK_RED,\n                    ChatColor.DARK_AQUA,\n                    ChatColor.DARK_GREEN,\n                    ChatColor.DARK_BLUE);\n\n            Collections.shuffle(colors, Misc.getRandom());\n\n            int i = 0;\n            for (PrimarySkillType primarySkillType : PrimarySkillType.values()) {\n                // Include child skills\n                skillLabelBuilder.put(primarySkillType, getShortenedName(colors.get(i) + mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().getLocalizedSkillName(primarySkillType), false));\n\n                if (mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().getSuperAbility(primarySkillType) != null) {\n                    abilityLabelBuilder.put(mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().getSuperAbility(primarySkillType), getShortenedName(colors.get(i) + mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().getSuperAbility(primarySkillType).getLocalizedName()));\n\n                    if (primarySkillType == PrimarySkillType.MINING) {\n                        abilityLabelBuilder.put(SuperAbilityType.BLAST_MINING, getShortenedName(colors.get(i) + SuperAbilityType.BLAST_MINING.getLocalizedName()));\n                    }\n                }\n\n                if (++i == colors.size()) {\n                    i = 0;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        /*\n         * Builds the labels for our ScoreBoards\n         * Stylizes the targetBoard using our normal color scheme\n         */\n        else {\n            for (PrimarySkillType primarySkillType : PrimarySkillType.values()) {\n                // Include child skills\n                skillLabelBuilder.put(primarySkillType, getShortenedName(ChatColor.GREEN + mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().getLocalizedSkillName(primarySkillType)));\n\n                if (mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().getSuperAbility(primarySkillType) != null) {\n                    abilityLabelBuilder.put(mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().getSuperAbility(primarySkillType), formatAbility(mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().getSuperAbility(primarySkillType).getLocalizedName()));\n\n                    if (primarySkillType == PrimarySkillType.MINING) {\n                        abilityLabelBuilder.put(SuperAbilityType.BLAST_MINING, formatAbility(SuperAbilityType.BLAST_MINING.getLocalizedName()));\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        for (SuperAbilityType type : SuperAbilityType.values()) {\n            abilityLabelSkillBuilder.put(type, formatAbility((type == SuperAbilityType.BLAST_MINING ? ChatColor.BLUE : ChatColor.AQUA), type.getLocalizedName()));\n        }\n\n        skillLabels = skillLabelBuilder.build();\n        abilityLabelsColored = abilityLabelBuilder.build();\n        abilityLabelsSkill = abilityLabelSkillBuilder.build();\n    }\n\n    private static final List<String> dirtyPowerLevels = new ArrayList<>();\n\n    public enum SidebarType {\n        NONE,\n        SKILL_BOARD,\n        STATS_BOARD,\n        COOLDOWNS_BOARD,\n        RANK_BOARD,\n        TOP_BOARD\n    }\n\n    private static String formatAbility(String abilityName) {\n        return formatAbility(ChatColor.AQUA, abilityName);\n    }\n\n    private static String formatAbility(ChatColor color, String abilityName) {\n        if (mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getShowAbilityNames()) {\n            return getShortenedName(color + abilityName);\n        } else {\n            return color + LocaleLoader.getString(\"Scoreboard.Misc.Ability\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static String getShortenedName(String name) {\n        return getShortenedName(name, true);\n    }\n\n    private static String getShortenedName(String name, boolean useDots) {\n        if (name.length() > 16) {\n            name = useDots ? name.substring(0, 14) + \"..\" : name.substring(0, 16);\n        }\n\n        return name;\n    }\n\n    // **** Listener call-ins **** //\n\n    // Called by PlayerJoinEvent listener\n    public static void setupPlayer(Player player) {\n        teardownPlayer(player);\n\n        PLAYER_SCOREBOARDS.put(player.getName(), makeNewScoreboard(player));\n        dirtyPowerLevels.add(player.getName());\n    }\n\n    // Called by PlayerQuitEvent listener and OnPlayerTeleport under certain circumstances\n    public static void teardownPlayer(Player player) {\n        if (player == null)\n            return;\n\n        //Hacky world blacklist fix\n        if (player.isOnline() && player.isValid()) {\n            if (Bukkit.getServer().getScoreboardManager() != null)\n                player.setScoreboard(Bukkit.getServer().getScoreboardManager().getMainScoreboard());\n        }\n\n        if (getWrapper(player) != null) {\n            ScoreboardWrapper wrapper = PLAYER_SCOREBOARDS.remove(player.getName());\n            if (wrapper.revertTask != null) {\n                wrapper.revertTask.cancel();\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Called in onDisable()\n    public static void teardownAll() {\n        ImmutableList<Player> onlinePlayers = ImmutableList.copyOf(mcMMO.p.getServer().getOnlinePlayers());\n        LogUtils.debug(mcMMO.p.getLogger(), \"Tearing down scoreboards... (\" + onlinePlayers.size() + \")\");\n        for (Player player : onlinePlayers) {\n            teardownPlayer(player);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Called by ScoreboardWrapper when its Player logs off and an action tries to be performed\n    public static void cleanup(ScoreboardWrapper wrapper) {\n        PLAYER_SCOREBOARDS.remove(wrapper.playerName);\n\n        if (wrapper.revertTask != null) {\n            wrapper.revertTask.cancel();\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Called by internal level-up event listener\n    public static void handleLevelUp(Player player, PrimarySkillType skill) {\n        // Selfboards\n        ScoreboardWrapper wrapper = getWrapper(player);\n\n        if (wrapper == null) {\n            setupPlayer(player);\n            wrapper = getWrapper(player);\n        }\n\n        if (wrapper != null) {\n            if ((wrapper.isSkillScoreboard() && wrapper.targetSkill == skill) || (wrapper.isStatsScoreboard()) && wrapper.isBoardShown()) {\n                wrapper.doSidebarUpdateSoon();\n            }\n\n            // Otherboards\n            String playerName = player.getName();\n\n            for (ScoreboardWrapper iWrapper : PLAYER_SCOREBOARDS.values()) {\n                if (iWrapper.isStatsScoreboard() && playerName.equals(iWrapper.targetPlayer) && wrapper.isBoardShown()) {\n                    wrapper.doSidebarUpdateSoon();\n                }\n            }\n\n            if (mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getPowerLevelTagsEnabled() && !dirtyPowerLevels.contains(playerName)) {\n                dirtyPowerLevels.add(playerName);\n            }\n\n            if (mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getSkillLevelUpBoard()) {\n                enablePlayerSkillLevelUpScoreboard(player, skill);\n            }\n\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Called by internal xp event listener\n    public static void handleXp(Player player, PrimarySkillType skill) {\n        // Selfboards\n        ScoreboardWrapper wrapper = getWrapper(player);\n\n        if (wrapper != null && wrapper.isSkillScoreboard() && wrapper.targetSkill == skill && wrapper.isBoardShown()) {\n            wrapper.doSidebarUpdateSoon();\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Called by internal ability event listeners\n    public static void cooldownUpdate(Player player, PrimarySkillType skill) {\n        // Selfboards\n        ScoreboardWrapper wrapper = getWrapper(player);\n\n        if (wrapper == null) {\n            setupPlayer(player);\n            wrapper = getWrapper(player);\n        }\n\n        if (wrapper != null) {\n            if ((wrapper.isCooldownScoreboard() || wrapper.isSkillScoreboard() && wrapper.targetSkill == skill) && wrapper.isBoardShown()) {\n                wrapper.doSidebarUpdateSoon();\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // **** Setup methods **** //\n\n    public static void enablePlayerSkillScoreboard(Player player, PrimarySkillType skill) {\n        McMMOPlayer mmoPlayer = UserManager.getPlayer(player);\n        mmoPlayer.setLastSkillShownScoreboard(skill);\n\n        ScoreboardWrapper wrapper = getWrapper(player);\n\n        if (wrapper == null) {\n            setupPlayer(player);\n            wrapper = getWrapper(player);\n        }\n\n        if (wrapper != null) {\n            wrapper.setOldScoreboard();\n            wrapper.setTypeSkill(skill);\n\n            changeScoreboard(wrapper, mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getSkillScoreboardTime());\n        }\n    }\n\n    public static void retryLastSkillBoard(Player player) {\n        McMMOPlayer mmoPlayer = UserManager.getPlayer(player);\n        PrimarySkillType primarySkillType = mmoPlayer.getLastSkillShownScoreboard();\n\n        ScoreboardWrapper wrapper = getWrapper(player);\n\n        if (wrapper == null) {\n            setupPlayer(player);\n            wrapper = getWrapper(player);\n        }\n\n        if (wrapper != null) {\n            wrapper.setOldScoreboard();\n            wrapper.setTypeSkill(primarySkillType);\n\n            changeScoreboard(wrapper, mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getSkillScoreboardTime());\n        }\n    }\n\n    public static void enablePlayerSkillLevelUpScoreboard(Player player, PrimarySkillType skill) {\n        ScoreboardWrapper wrapper = getWrapper(player);\n\n        // Do NOT run if already shown\n        if (wrapper != null && wrapper.isBoardShown()) {\n\n            if (wrapper.isBoardShown())\n                return;\n\n            wrapper.setOldScoreboard();\n            wrapper.setTypeSkill(skill);\n            changeScoreboard(wrapper, mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getSkillLevelUpTime());\n        }\n    }\n\n    public static void enablePlayerStatsScoreboard(Player player) {\n        ScoreboardWrapper wrapper = getWrapper(player);\n\n        if (wrapper == null)\n            return;\n\n\n        wrapper.setOldScoreboard();\n        wrapper.setTypeSelfStats();\n\n        changeScoreboard(wrapper, mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getStatsScoreboardTime());\n    }\n\n    public static void enablePlayerInspectScoreboard(@NotNull Player player, @NotNull PlayerProfile targetProfile) {\n        ScoreboardWrapper wrapper = getWrapper(player);\n\n        if (wrapper == null) {\n            setupPlayer(player);\n            wrapper = getWrapper(player);\n        }\n\n        if (wrapper != null) {\n            wrapper.setOldScoreboard();\n            wrapper.setTypeInspectStats(targetProfile);\n\n            changeScoreboard(wrapper, mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getInspectScoreboardTime());\n        }\n    }\n\n    public static void enablePlayerInspectScoreboard(@NotNull Player player, @NotNull McMMOPlayer targetMcMMOPlayer) {\n        ScoreboardWrapper wrapper = getWrapper(player);\n\n        if (wrapper == null) {\n            setupPlayer(player);\n            wrapper = getWrapper(player);\n        }\n\n        if (wrapper != null) {\n            wrapper.setOldScoreboard();\n            wrapper.setTypeInspectStats(targetMcMMOPlayer);\n\n            changeScoreboard(wrapper, mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getInspectScoreboardTime());\n        }\n    }\n\n    public static void enablePlayerCooldownScoreboard(Player player) {\n        ScoreboardWrapper wrapper = getWrapper(player);\n\n        if (wrapper == null) {\n            setupPlayer(player);\n            wrapper = getWrapper(player);\n        }\n\n        if (wrapper != null) {\n            wrapper.setOldScoreboard();\n            wrapper.setTypeCooldowns();\n\n            changeScoreboard(wrapper, mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getCooldownScoreboardTime());\n        }\n    }\n\n    public static void showPlayerRankScoreboard(Player player, Map<PrimarySkillType, Integer> rank) {\n        ScoreboardWrapper wrapper = getWrapper(player);\n\n        if (wrapper == null) {\n            setupPlayer(player);\n            wrapper = getWrapper(player);\n        }\n\n        if (wrapper != null) {\n            wrapper.setOldScoreboard();\n            wrapper.setTypeSelfRank();\n            wrapper.acceptRankData(rank);\n\n            changeScoreboard(wrapper, mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getRankScoreboardTime());\n        }\n    }\n\n    public static void showPlayerRankScoreboardOthers(Player player, String targetName, Map<PrimarySkillType, Integer> rank) {\n        ScoreboardWrapper wrapper = getWrapper(player);\n\n        if (wrapper == null) {\n            setupPlayer(player);\n            wrapper = getWrapper(player);\n        }\n\n        if (wrapper != null) {\n            wrapper.setOldScoreboard();\n            wrapper.setTypeInspectRank(targetName);\n            wrapper.acceptRankData(rank);\n\n            changeScoreboard(wrapper, mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getRankScoreboardTime());\n        }\n    }\n\n    public static void showTopScoreboard(Player player, PrimarySkillType skill, int pageNumber, List<PlayerStat> stats) {\n\n        ScoreboardWrapper wrapper = getWrapper(player);\n\n        if (wrapper == null) {\n            setupPlayer(player);\n            wrapper = getWrapper(player);\n        }\n\n        if (wrapper != null) {\n            wrapper.setOldScoreboard();\n            wrapper.setTypeTop(skill, pageNumber);\n            wrapper.acceptLeaderboardData(stats);\n\n            changeScoreboard(wrapper, mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getTopScoreboardTime());\n        }\n    }\n\n    public static void showTopPowerScoreboard(Player player, int pageNumber, List<PlayerStat> stats) {\n        ScoreboardWrapper wrapper = getWrapper(player);\n\n        if (wrapper == null) {\n            setupPlayer(player);\n            wrapper = getWrapper(player);\n        }\n\n        if (wrapper != null) {\n            wrapper.setOldScoreboard();\n            wrapper.setTypeTopPower(pageNumber);\n            wrapper.acceptLeaderboardData(stats);\n\n            changeScoreboard(wrapper, mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getTopScoreboardTime());\n        }\n    }\n\n    public static @Nullable ScoreboardWrapper getWrapper(Player player) {\n        if (PLAYER_SCOREBOARDS.get(player.getName()) == null) {\n            makeNewScoreboard(player);\n        }\n\n        return PLAYER_SCOREBOARDS.get(player.getName());\n    }\n\n    // **** Helper methods **** //\n\n    /**\n     * @return false if power levels are disabled\n     */\n    public static boolean powerLevelHeartbeat() {\n        Objective mainObjective = getPowerLevelObjective();\n\n        if (mainObjective == null) {\n            return false; // indicates\n        }\n\n        for (String playerName : dirtyPowerLevels) {\n            McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer = UserManager.getPlayer(playerName);\n\n            if (mcMMOPlayer == null) {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            Player player = mcMMOPlayer.getPlayer();\n            int power = mcMMOPlayer.getPowerLevel();\n\n            mainObjective.getScore(playerName).setScore(power);\n\n            for (ScoreboardWrapper wrapper : PLAYER_SCOREBOARDS.values()) {\n                wrapper.updatePowerLevel(player, power);\n            }\n        }\n\n        dirtyPowerLevels.clear();\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Gets or creates the power level objective on the main targetBoard.\n     * <p/>\n     * If power levels are disabled, the objective is deleted and null is\n     * returned.\n     *\n     * @return the main targetBoard objective, or null if disabled\n     */\n    public static @Nullable Objective getPowerLevelObjective() {\n        if (!mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getPowerLevelTagsEnabled()) {\n            if (getScoreboardManager() == null)\n                return null;\n\n            Objective objective = getScoreboardManager().getMainScoreboard().getObjective(POWER_OBJECTIVE);\n\n            if (objective != null) {\n                objective.unregister();\n                LogUtils.debug(mcMMO.p.getLogger(), \"Removed leftover targetBoard objects from Power Level Tags.\");\n            }\n\n            return null;\n        }\n\n\n        if (getScoreboardManager() ==  null)\n            return null;\n\n        Objective powerObjective = getScoreboardManager().getMainScoreboard().getObjective(POWER_OBJECTIVE);\n\n        if (powerObjective == null) {\n            powerObjective = getScoreboardManager().getMainScoreboard().registerNewObjective(POWER_OBJECTIVE, \"dummy\", DISPLAY_NAME);\n            powerObjective.setDisplayName(TAG_POWER_LEVEL);\n            powerObjective.setDisplaySlot(DisplaySlot.BELOW_NAME);\n        }\n\n        return powerObjective;\n    }\n\n    public @Nullable static org.bukkit.scoreboard.ScoreboardManager getScoreboardManager() {\n        return mcMMO.p.getServer().getScoreboardManager();\n    }\n\n    public static void changeScoreboard(ScoreboardWrapper wrapper, int displayTime) {\n        if (displayTime == -1) {\n            wrapper.showBoardWithNoRevert();\n        } else {\n            wrapper.showBoardAndScheduleRevert(displayTime * Misc.TICK_CONVERSION_FACTOR);\n        }\n    }\n\n    public static boolean isBoardShown(String playerName) {\n        return PLAYER_SCOREBOARDS.get(playerName).isBoardShown();\n    }\n\n    public static void clearBoard(String playerName) {\n        PLAYER_SCOREBOARDS.get(playerName).tryRevertBoard();\n    }\n\n    public static void keepBoard(String playerName) {\n        PLAYER_SCOREBOARDS.get(playerName).cancelRevert();\n    }\n\n    public static void setRevertTimer(String playerName, int seconds) {\n        PLAYER_SCOREBOARDS.get(playerName).showBoardAndScheduleRevert(seconds * Misc.TICK_CONVERSION_FACTOR);\n    }\n\n    public static boolean isPlayerBoardSetup(@NotNull String playerName) {\n        return PLAYER_SCOREBOARDS.get(playerName) != null;\n    }\n\n    public static @Nullable ScoreboardWrapper makeNewScoreboard(Player player) {\n        if (getScoreboardManager() == null)\n            return null;\n\n        //Call our custom event\n        Scoreboard scoreboard = getScoreboardManager().getNewScoreboard();\n        McMMOScoreboardMakeboardEvent event = new McMMOScoreboardMakeboardEvent(scoreboard, player.getScoreboard(), player, ScoreboardEventReason.CREATING_NEW_SCOREBOARD);\n        player.getServer().getPluginManager().callEvent(event);\n        //Use the values from the event\n        return new ScoreboardWrapper(event.getTargetPlayer(), event.getTargetBoard());\n    }\n}\n"
    },

      "content": "package com.gmail.nossr50.util.skills;\n\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.config.experience.ExperienceConfig;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.experience.XPGainReason;\nimport
