com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.PrimarySkillType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.SubSkillType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.mcMMO;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.metadata.MobMetaFlagType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.runnables.skills.AwardCombatXpTask;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.skills.acrobatics.AcrobaticsManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.skills.archery.ArcheryManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.skills.axes.AxesManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.skills.swords.SwordsManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.skills.taming.TamingManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.skills.tridents.TridentsManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.skills.unarmed.UnarmedManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.*;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.player.NotificationManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.player.UserManager;\nimport org.bukkit.GameMode;\nimport org.bukkit.Material;\nimport org.bukkit.attribute.Attribute;\nimport org.bukkit.attribute.AttributeInstance;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.*;\nimport org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityDamageByEntityEvent;\nimport org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityDamageEvent.DamageCause;\nimport org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack;\nimport org.bukkit.metadata.MetadataValue;\nimport org.bukkit.potion.PotionEffectType;\nimport org.bukkit.projectiles.ProjectileSource;\nimport org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;\nimport org.jetbrains.annotations.Nullable;\n\nimport java.util.List;\n\nimport static com.gmail.nossr50.util.MobMetadataUtils.hasMobFlag;\n\npublic final class CombatUtils {\n\n    private CombatUtils() {}\n\n    public static boolean isDamageLikelyFromNormalCombat(@NotNull DamageCause damageCause) {\n        return switch (damageCause) {\n            case ENTITY_ATTACK, ENTITY_SWEEP_ATTACK, PROJECTILE -> true;\n            default -> false;\n        };\n    }\n\n    public static boolean hasWeakenedDamage(@NotNull LivingEntity livingEntity) {\n        return livingEntity.hasPotionEffect(PotionEffectType.WEAKNESS);\n    }\n\n    private static void processSwordCombat(@NotNull LivingEntity target, @NotNull Player player, @NotNull EntityDamageByEntityEvent event) {\n        if (event.getCause() == DamageCause.THORNS) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer = UserManager.getPlayer(player);\n\n        //Make sure the profiles been loaded\n        if (mcMMOPlayer == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        SwordsManager swordsManager = mcMMOPlayer.getSwordsManager();\n        double boostedDamage = event.getDamage();\n\n        if (swordsManager.canActivateAbility()) {\n            mcMMOPlayer.checkAbilityActivation(PrimarySkillType.SWORDS);\n        }\n\n        if (target.getHealth() - event.getFinalDamage() > 0) {\n            swordsManager.processRupture(target);\n        }\n\n        //Add Stab Damage\n        if (swordsManager.canUseStab()) {\n            boostedDamage += (swordsManager.getStabDamage() * mcMMOPlayer.getAttackStrength());\n        }\n\n        if (swordsManager.canUseSerratedStrike()) {\n            swordsManager.serratedStrikes(target, event.getDamage());\n        }\n\n        if (canUseLimitBreak(player, target, SubSkillType.SWORDS_SWORDS_LIMIT_BREAK)) {\n            boostedDamage += (getLimitBreakDamage(player, target, SubSkillType.SWORDS_SWORDS_LIMIT_BREAK) * mcMMOPlayer.getAttackStrength());\n        }\n\n        event.setDamage(boostedDamage);\n        processCombatXP(mcMMOPlayer, target, PrimarySkillType.SWORDS);\n\n        printFinalDamageDebug(player, event, mcMMOPlayer);\n    }\n\n    private static void printFinalDamageDebug(@NotNull Player player, @NotNull EntityDamageByEntityEvent event, @NotNull McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer, @Nullable String @Nullable ... extraInfoLines) {\n        if (mcMMOPlayer.isDebugMode()) {\n            player.sendMessage(\"Final Damage value after mcMMO modifiers: \"+ event.getFinalDamage());\n            if (extraInfoLines != null) {\n                for(String str : extraInfoLines) {\n                    if (str != null)\n                        player.sendMessage(str);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static void processTridentCombatMelee(@NotNull LivingEntity target, @NotNull Player player, @NotNull EntityDamageByEntityEvent event) {\n        if (event.getCause() == DamageCause.THORNS) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        double boostedDamage = event.getDamage();\n\n        McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer = UserManager.getPlayer(player);\n\n        //Make sure the profiles been loaded\n        if (mcMMOPlayer == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        TridentsManager tridentsManager = mcMMOPlayer.getTridentsManager();\n\n//        if (tridentsManager.canActivateAbility()) {\n//            mcMMOPlayer.checkAbilityActivation(PrimarySkillType.TRIDENTS);\n//        }\n\n        if (SkillUtils.canUseSubskill(player, SubSkillType.TRIDENTS_IMPALE)) {\n            boostedDamage += (tridentsManager.impaleDamageBonus() * mcMMOPlayer.getAttackStrength());\n        }\n\n        if (canUseLimitBreak(player, target, SubSkillType.TRIDENTS_TRIDENTS_LIMIT_BREAK)) {\n            boostedDamage += (getLimitBreakDamage(player, target, SubSkillType.TRIDENTS_TRIDENTS_LIMIT_BREAK) * mcMMOPlayer.getAttackStrength());\n        }\n\n        event.setDamage(boostedDamage);\n        processCombatXP(mcMMOPlayer, target, PrimarySkillType.TRIDENTS);\n\n        printFinalDamageDebug(player, event, mcMMOPlayer);\n    }\n\n    private static void processTridentCombatRanged(@NotNull Trident trident, @NotNull LivingEntity target, @NotNull Player player, @NotNull EntityDamageByEntityEvent event) {\n        if (event.getCause() == DamageCause.THORNS) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        double boostedDamage = event.getDamage();\n\n        McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer = UserManager.getPlayer(player);\n\n        //Make sure the profiles been loaded\n        if (mcMMOPlayer == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        TridentsManager tridentsManager = mcMMOPlayer.getTridentsManager();\n\n        if (SkillUtils.canUseSubskill(player, SubSkillType.TRIDENTS_IMPALE)) {\n            boostedDamage += (tridentsManager.impaleDamageBonus() * mcMMOPlayer.getAttackStrength());\n        }\n\n        if (canUseLimitBreak(player, target, SubSkillType.TRIDENTS_TRIDENTS_LIMIT_BREAK)) {\n            boostedDamage += (getLimitBreakDamage(player, target, SubSkillType.TRIDENTS_TRIDENTS_LIMIT_BREAK) * mcMMOPlayer.getAttackStrength());\n        }\n\n        event.setDamage(boostedDamage);\n        processCombatXP(mcMMOPlayer, target, PrimarySkillType.TRIDENTS);\n\n        printFinalDamageDebug(player, event, mcMMOPlayer);\n    }\n\n    private static void processCrossbowsCombat(@NotNull LivingEntity target, @NotNull Player player,\n                                               @NotNull EntityDamageByEntityEvent event, @NotNull Arrow arrow) {\n        double initialDamage = event.getDamage();\n\n        McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer = UserManager.getPlayer(player);\n\n        //Make sure the profiles been loaded\n        if (mcMMOPlayer == null) {\n            delayArrowMetaCleanup(arrow);\n            return;\n        }\n\n        double boostedDamage = event.getDamage();\n\n        if (SkillUtils.canUseSubskill(player, SubSkillType.CROSSBOWS_POWERED_SHOT)) {\n            //Not Additive\n            boostedDamage = mcMMOPlayer.getCrossbowsManager().poweredShot(initialDamage);\n        }\n\n        if (canUseLimitBreak(player, target, SubSkillType.CROSSBOWS_CROSSBOWS_LIMIT_BREAK)) {\n            boostedDamage+=getLimitBreakDamage(player, target, SubSkillType.CROSSBOWS_CROSSBOWS_LIMIT_BREAK);\n        }\n\n        double distanceMultiplier = ArcheryManager.distanceXpBonusMultiplier(target, arrow);\n        double forceMultiplier = 1.0;\n\n        event.setDamage(boostedDamage);\n        processCombatXP(mcMMOPlayer, target, PrimarySkillType.CROSSBOWS, forceMultiplier * distanceMultiplier);\n\n        printFinalDamageDebug(player, event, mcMMOPlayer,\n                \"Distance Multiplier: \"+distanceMultiplier,\n                \"Force Multiplier: \"+forceMultiplier,\n                \"Initial Damage: \"+initialDamage,\n                \"Final Damage: \"+boostedDamage);\n\n        //Clean data\n        delayArrowMetaCleanup(arrow);\n    }\n\n    private static void processMacesCombat(@NotNull LivingEntity target, @NotNull Player player, @NotNull EntityDamageByEntityEvent event) {\n        if (event.getCause() == DamageCause.THORNS) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        double boostedDamage = event.getDamage();\n\n        McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer = UserManager.getPlayer(player);\n\n        //Make sure the profiles been loaded\n        if (mcMMOPlayer == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        // MacesManager macesManager = mcMMOPlayer.getMacesManager();\n\n        if (canUseLimitBreak(player, target, SubSkillType.MACES_MACES_LIMIT_BREAK)) {\n            boostedDamage += (getLimitBreakDamage(player, target, SubSkillType.MACES_MACES_LIMIT_BREAK) * mcMMOPlayer.getAttackStrength());\n        }\n\n        event.setDamage(boostedDamage);\n        processCombatXP(mcMMOPlayer, target, PrimarySkillType.MACES);\n\n        printFinalDamageDebug(player, event, mcMMOPlayer);\n    }\n\n    private static void processAxeCombat(@NotNull LivingEntity target, @NotNull Player player, @NotNull EntityDamageByEntityEvent event) {\n        if (event.getCause() == DamageCause.THORNS) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        double boostedDamage = event.getDamage();\n\n        McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer = UserManager.getPlayer(player);\n\n        //Make sure the profiles been loaded\n        if (mcMMOPlayer == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        AxesManager axesManager = mcMMOPlayer.getAxesManager();\n\n        if (axesManager.canActivateAbility()) {\n            mcMMOPlayer.checkAbilityActivation(PrimarySkillType.AXES);\n        }\n\n        if (axesManager.canUseAxeMastery()) {\n            boostedDamage+=axesManager.axeMastery();\n        }\n\n        if (axesManager.canImpact(target)) {\n            axesManager.impactCheck(target);\n        } else if (axesManager.canGreaterImpact(target)) {\n            boostedDamage+=axesManager.greaterImpact(target);\n        }\n\n        if (axesManager.canUseSkullSplitter(target)) {\n            axesManager.skullSplitterCheck(target, event.getDamage());\n        }\n\n        if (axesManager.canCriticalHit(target)) {\n            boostedDamage+=(axesManager.criticalHit(target, boostedDamage) * mcMMOPlayer.getAttackStrength());\n        }\n\n        if (canUseLimitBreak(player, target, SubSkillType.AXES_AXES_LIMIT_BREAK)) {\n            boostedDamage+=(getLimitBreakDamage(player, target, SubSkillType.AXES_AXES_LIMIT_BREAK) * mcMMOPlayer.getAttackStrength());\n        }\n\n        event.setDamage(boostedDamage);\n        processCombatXP(mcMMOPlayer, target, PrimarySkillType.AXES);\n\n        printFinalDamageDebug(player, event, mcMMOPlayer);\n    }\n\n    private static void processUnarmedCombat(@NotNull LivingEntity target, @NotNull Player player, @NotNull EntityDamageByEntityEvent event) {\n        if (event.getCause() == DamageCause.THORNS) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        double boostedDamage = event.getDamage();\n\n        McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer = UserManager.getPlayer(player);\n\n        //Make sure the profiles been loaded\n        if (mcMMOPlayer == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        UnarmedManager unarmedManager = mcMMOPlayer.getUnarmedManager();\n\n        if (unarmedManager.canActivateAbility()) {\n            mcMMOPlayer.checkAbilityActivation(PrimarySkillType.UNARMED);\n        }\n\n        if (unarmedManager.canUseSteelArm()) {\n            boostedDamage+=(unarmedManager.calculateSteelArmStyleDamage() * mcMMOPlayer.getAttackStrength());\n        }\n\n        if (unarmedManager.canUseBerserk()) {\n            boostedDamage+=(unarmedManager.berserkDamage(boostedDamage) * mcMMOPlayer.getAttackStrength());\n        }\n\n        if (unarmedManager.canDisarm(target)) {\n            unarmedManager.disarmCheck((Player) target);\n        }\n\n        if (canUseLimitBreak(player, target, SubSkillType.UNARMED_UNARMED_LIMIT_BREAK)) {\n            boostedDamage+=(getLimitBreakDamage(player, target, SubSkillType.UNARMED_UNARMED_LIMIT_BREAK) * mcMMOPlayer.getAttackStrength());\n        }\n\n        event.setDamage(boostedDamage);\n        processCombatXP(mcMMOPlayer, target, PrimarySkillType.UNARMED);\n\n        printFinalDamageDebug(player, event, mcMMOPlayer);\n    }\n\n    private static void processTamingCombat(@NotNull LivingEntity target, @Nullable Player master, @NotNull Wolf wolf, @NotNull EntityDamageByEntityEvent event) {\n        double initialDamage = event.getDamage();\n        double boostedDamage = initialDamage;\n\n        if (master != null && master.isOnline() && master.isValid()) {\n            McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer = UserManager.getPlayer(master);\n\n            //Make sure the profiles been loaded\n            if (mcMMOPlayer == null) {\n                return;\n            }\n\n            TamingManager tamingManager = mcMMOPlayer.getTamingManager();\n\n            if (tamingManager.canUseFastFoodService()) {\n                tamingManager.fastFoodService(wolf, event.getDamage());\n            }\n\n            tamingManager.pummel(target, wolf);\n\n            if (tamingManager.canUseSharpenedClaws()) {\n                boostedDamage+=tamingManager.sharpenedClaws();\n            }\n\n            if (tamingManager.canUseGore()) {\n                boostedDamage+=tamingManager.gore(target, initialDamage);\n            }\n\n            event.setDamage(boostedDamage);\n            processCombatXP(mcMMOPlayer, target, PrimarySkillType.TAMING, 3);\n        }\n\n    }\n\n    private static void processArcheryCombat(@NotNull LivingEntity target, @NotNull Player player,\n                                             @NotNull EntityDamageByEntityEvent event, @NotNull Arrow arrow) {\n        double initialDamage = event.getDamage();\n\n        McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer = UserManager.getPlayer(player);\n\n        //Make sure the profiles been loaded\n        if (mcMMOPlayer == null) {\n            delayArrowMetaCleanup(arrow);\n            return;\n        }\n\n        ArcheryManager archeryManager = mcMMOPlayer.getArcheryManager();\n        \n        double boostedDamage = event.getDamage();\n\n        if (archeryManager.canSkillShot()) {\n            //Not Additive\n            boostedDamage = archeryManager.skillShot(initialDamage);\n        }\n\n        if (archeryManager.canDaze(target)) {\n            boostedDamage+=archeryManager.daze((Player) target); //the cast is checked by the if condition\n        }\n\n        if (!arrow.hasMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_INF_ARROW) && archeryManager.canRetrieveArrows()) {\n            archeryManager.retrieveArrows(target, arrow);\n        }\n\n        if (canUseLimitBreak(player, target, SubSkillType.ARCHERY_ARCHERY_LIMIT_BREAK)) {\n            boostedDamage+=getLimitBreakDamage(player, target, SubSkillType.ARCHERY_ARCHERY_LIMIT_BREAK);\n        }\n\n        double distanceMultiplier = ArcheryManager.distanceXpBonusMultiplier(target, arrow);\n        double forceMultiplier = 1.0; //Hacky Fix - some plugins spawn arrows and assign them to players after the ProjectileLaunchEvent fires\n\n        if (arrow.hasMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_BOW_FORCE))\n            forceMultiplier = arrow.getMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_BOW_FORCE).get(0).asDouble();\n\n        event.setDamage(boostedDamage);\n        processCombatXP(mcMMOPlayer, target, PrimarySkillType.ARCHERY, forceMultiplier * distanceMultiplier);\n\n        printFinalDamageDebug(player, event, mcMMOPlayer,\n                \"Distance Multiplier: \"+distanceMultiplier,\n                \"Force Multiplier: \"+forceMultiplier,\n                \"Initial Damage: \"+initialDamage,\n                \"Final Damage: \"+boostedDamage);\n        //Clean data\n        delayArrowMetaCleanup(arrow);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Apply combat modifiers and process and XP gain.\n     *\n     * @param event The event to run the combat checks on.\n     */\n    public static void processCombatAttack(@NotNull EntityDamageByEntityEvent event, @NotNull Entity painSourceRoot, @NotNull LivingEntity target) {\n        Entity painSource = event.getDamager();\n        EntityType entityType = painSource.getType();\n\n        if (target instanceof ArmorStand) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        if (target instanceof Player player) {\n            if (ExperienceConfig.getInstance().isNPCInteractionPrevented()) {\n                if (Misc.isNPCEntityExcludingVillagers(target)) {\n                    return;\n                }\n            }\n\n            if (!UserManager.hasPlayerDataKey(player)) {\n                return;\n            }\n\n            McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer = UserManager.getPlayer(player);\n            AcrobaticsManager acrobaticsManager = mcMMOPlayer.getAcrobaticsManager();\n\n            if (acrobaticsManager.canDodge(target)) {\n                event.setDamage(acrobaticsManager.dodgeCheck(painSourceRoot, event.getDamage()));\n            }\n\n            if (ItemUtils.isSword(player.getInventory().getItemInMainHand())) {\n                if (!mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().canCombatSkillsTrigger(PrimarySkillType.SWORDS, target)) {\n                    return;\n                }\n\n                SwordsManager swordsManager = mcMMOPlayer.getSwordsManager();\n\n                if (swordsManager.canUseCounterAttack(painSource)) {\n                    swordsManager.counterAttackChecks((LivingEntity) painSource, event.getDamage());\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (painSourceRoot instanceof Player player && entityType == EntityType.PLAYER) {\n\n            if (!UserManager.hasPlayerDataKey(player)) {\n                return;\n            }\n\n            ItemStack heldItem = player.getInventory().getItemInMainHand();\n\n            if (target instanceof Tameable) {\n                if (heldItem.getType() == Material.BONE) {\n                    TamingManager tamingManager = UserManager.getPlayer(player).getTamingManager();\n\n                    if (tamingManager.canUseBeastLore()) {\n                        tamingManager.beastLore(target);\n                        event.setCancelled(true);\n                        return;\n                    }\n                }\n\n                if (isFriendlyPet(player, (Tameable) target)) {\n                    return;\n                }\n            }\n\n            if (ItemUtils.isSword(heldItem)) {\n                if (!mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().canCombatSkillsTrigger(PrimarySkillType.SWORDS, target)) {\n                    return;\n                }\n\n                if (mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().doesPlayerHaveSkillPermission(player, PrimarySkillType.SWORDS)) {\n                    processSwordCombat(target, player, event);\n\n                }\n            } else if (ItemUtils.isAxe(heldItem)) {\n                if (!mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().canCombatSkillsTrigger(PrimarySkillType.AXES, target)) {\n                    return;\n                }\n\n                if (mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().doesPlayerHaveSkillPermission(player, PrimarySkillType.AXES)) {\n                    processAxeCombat(target, player, event);\n                }\n            } else if (ItemUtils.isUnarmed(heldItem)) {\n                if (!mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().canCombatSkillsTrigger(PrimarySkillType.UNARMED, target)) {\n                    return;\n                }\n\n                if (mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().doesPlayerHaveSkillPermission(player, PrimarySkillType.UNARMED)) {\n                    processUnarmedCombat(target, player, event);\n                }\n            } else if (ItemUtils.isTrident(heldItem)) {\n                if (!mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().canCombatSkillsTrigger(PrimarySkillType.TRIDENTS, target)) {\n                    return;\n                }\n\n                if (mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().doesPlayerHaveSkillPermission(player, PrimarySkillType.TRIDENTS)) {\n                    processTridentCombatMelee(target, player, event);\n                }\n            } else if (ItemUtils.isMace(heldItem)) {\n                if (!mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().canCombatSkillsTrigger(PrimarySkillType.MACES, target)) {\n                    return;\n                }\n\n                if (mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().doesPlayerHaveSkillPermission(player, PrimarySkillType.MACES)) {\n                    processMacesCombat(target, player, event);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        else if (entityType == EntityType.WOLF) {\n            Wolf wolf = (Wolf) painSource;\n            AnimalTamer tamer = wolf.getOwner();\n\n            if (tamer instanceof Player master && mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().canCombatSkillsTrigger(PrimarySkillType.TAMING, target)) {\n\n                if (!Misc.isNPCEntityExcludingVillagers(master) && mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().doesPlayerHaveSkillPermission(master, PrimarySkillType.TAMING)) {\n                    processTamingCombat(target, master, wolf, event);\n                }\n            }\n        } else if (painSource instanceof Trident trident) {\n            ProjectileSource projectileSource = trident.getShooter();\n\n            if (projectileSource instanceof Player player) {\n                if (!Misc.isNPCEntityExcludingVillagers(player)) {\n                    if (mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().canCombatSkillsTrigger(PrimarySkillType.TRIDENTS, target)) {\n                        processTridentCombatRanged(trident, target, player, event);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        } else if (painSource instanceof Arrow arrow) {\n            ProjectileSource projectileSource = arrow.getShooter();\n            boolean isCrossbow = arrow.isShotFromCrossbow();\n            if (projectileSource instanceof Player player) {\n\n                if (!Misc.isNPCEntityExcludingVillagers(player)) {\n                    if (!isCrossbow && mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().canCombatSkillsTrigger(PrimarySkillType.ARCHERY, target)) {\n                        processArcheryCombat(target, player, event, arrow);\n                    } else if (isCrossbow && mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().canCombatSkillsTrigger(PrimarySkillType.CROSSBOWS, target)) {\n                        processCrossbowsCombat(target, player, event, arrow);\n                    }\n                } else {\n                    //Cleanup Arrow\n                    delayArrowMetaCleanup(arrow);\n                }\n\n                if (target.getType() != EntityType.CREEPER\n                        && !Misc.isNPCEntityExcludingVillagers(player)\n                        && mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().doesPlayerHaveSkillPermission(player, PrimarySkillType.TAMING)) {\n                    McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer = UserManager.getPlayer(player);\n\n                    if (mcMMOPlayer == null)\n                        return;\n\n                    TamingManager tamingManager = mcMMOPlayer.getTamingManager();\n                    tamingManager.attackTarget(target);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * This cleans up names from displaying in chat as hearts\n     * @param entity target entity\n     */\n    public static void fixNames(@NotNull LivingEntity entity) {\n        List<MetadataValue> metadataValue = entity.getMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_OLD_NAME_KEY);\n\n        if (metadataValue.size() <= 0)\n            return;\n\n        OldName oldName = (OldName) metadataValue.get(0);\n        entity.setCustomName(oldName.asString());\n        entity.setCustomNameVisible(false);\n\n        entity.removeMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_OLD_NAME_KEY, mcMMO.p);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Calculate and return the RAW damage bonus from Limit Break before reductions\n     * @param attacker attacking player\n     * @param defender defending living entity\n     * @param subSkillType the specific limit break skill for calculations\n     * @return the RAW damage bonus from Limit Break which is applied before reductions\n     */\n    public static int getLimitBreakDamage(@NotNull Player attacker, @NotNull LivingEntity defender, @NotNull SubSkillType subSkillType) {\n        if (defender instanceof Player playerDefender) {\n            return getLimitBreakDamageAgainstQuality(attacker, subSkillType, getArmorQualityLevel(playerDefender));\n        } else {\n            return getLimitBreakDamageAgainstQuality(attacker, subSkillType, 1000);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Calculate the RAW daamge value of limit break based on the armor quality of the target\n     * PVE mobs are passed in with a value of 1000 for armor quality, hacky... I'll change it later\n     * @param attacker Living entity attacker\n     * @param subSkillType Target limit break\n     * @param armorQualityLevel Armor quality level\n     * @return the RAW damage boost after its been mutated by armor quality\n     */\n    public static int getLimitBreakDamageAgainstQuality(@NotNull Player attacker, @NotNull SubSkillType subSkillType, int armorQualityLevel) {\n        int rawDamageBoost = RankUtils.getRank(attacker, subSkillType);\n\n        if (armorQualityLevel <= 4) {\n            rawDamageBoost *= .25; //75% Nerf\n        } else if (armorQualityLevel <= 8) {\n            rawDamageBoost *= .50; //50% Nerf\n        } else if (armorQualityLevel <= 12) {\n            rawDamageBoost *= .75; //25% Nerf\n        }\n\n        return rawDamageBoost;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Get the quality level of the armor of a player used for Limit Break calculations\n     * @param defender target defending player\n     * @return the armor quality of the defending player\n     */\n    public static int getArmorQualityLevel(@NotNull Player defender) {\n        int armorQualityLevel = 0;\n\n        for(ItemStack itemStack : defender.getInventory().getArmorContents()) {\n            if (itemStack != null) {\n                armorQualityLevel += getArmorQuality(itemStack);\n            }\n        }\n\n        return armorQualityLevel;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Get the armor quality for a specific item used for Limit Break calculations\n     * @param itemStack target item stack\n     * @return the armor quality of a specific Item Stack\n     */\n    private static int getArmorQuality(@NotNull ItemStack itemStack) {\n        return mcMMO.getMaterialMapStore().getTier(itemStack.getType().getKey().getKey());\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Checks if player has access to their weapons limit break\n     * @param player target entity\n     * @return true if the player has access to the limit break\n     */\n    public static boolean canUseLimitBreak(@NotNull Player player, LivingEntity target, @NotNull SubSkillType subSkillType) {\n        if (target instanceof Player || mcMMO.p.getAdvancedConfig().canApplyLimitBreakPVE()) {\n            return RankUtils.hasUnlockedSubskill(player, subSkillType)\n                    && Permissions.isSubSkillEnabled(player, subSkillType);\n        } else {\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Attempt to damage target for value dmg with reason CUSTOM\n     *\n     * @param target LivingEntity which to attempt to damage\n     * @param damage Amount of damage to attempt to do\n     */\n    public static void dealDamage(@NotNull LivingEntity target, double damage) {\n        dealDamage(target, damage, DamageCause.CUSTOM, null);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Attempt to damage target for value dmg with reason ENTITY_ATTACK with damager attacker\n     *\n     * @param target LivingEntity which to attempt to damage\n     * @param damage Amount of damage to attempt to do\n     * @param attacker Player to pass to event as damager\n     */\n    @Deprecated\n    public static void dealDamage(@NotNull LivingEntity target, double damage, @NotNull LivingEntity attacker) {\n        dealDamage(target, damage, DamageCause.CUSTOM, attacker);\n    }\n\n//    /**\n//     * Attempt to damage target for value dmg with reason ENTITY_ATTACK with damager attacker\n//     *\n//     * @param target LivingEntity which to attempt to damage\n//     * @param damage Amount of damage to attempt to do\n//     * @param attacker Player to pass to event as damager\n//     */\n//    public static void dealDamage(LivingEntity target, double damage, Map<DamageModifier, Double> modifiers, LivingEntity attacker) {\n//        if (target.isDead()) {\n//            return;\n//        }\n//\n//        // Aren't we applying the damage twice????\n//        target.damage(getFakeDamageFinalResult(attacker, target, damage, modifiers));\n//    }\n\n    /**\n     * Attempt to damage target for value dmg with reason ENTITY_ATTACK with damager attacker\n     *\n     * @param target LivingEntity which to attempt to damage\n     * @param damage Amount of damage to attempt to do\n     * @param attacker Player to pass to event as damager\n     */\n    @Deprecated\n    public static void dealDamage(@NotNull LivingEntity target, double damage, @NotNull DamageCause cause, @Nullable Entity attacker) {\n        if (target.isDead()) {\n            return;\n        }\n        try {\n            // TODO: Check for Spigot API for DamageSource, if DamageSource is found then send out a damage() with a custom damage source\n            applyIgnoreDamageMetadata(target);\n            if (attacker != null) {\n                target.damage(damage, attacker);\n            } else {\n                target.damage(damage);\n            }\n        } finally {\n            removeIgnoreDamageMetadata(target);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static boolean processingNoInvulnDamage;\n    public static boolean isProcessingNoInvulnDamage() {\n        return processingNoInvulnDamage;\n    }\n\n    public static void dealNoInvulnerabilityTickDamage(@NotNull LivingEntity target, double damage, @Nullable Entity attacker) {\n        if (target.isDead()) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        // TODO: This is horrible, but there is no cleaner way to do this without potentially breaking existing code right now\n        // calling damage here is a double edged sword: On one hand, without a call, plugins won't see this properly when the entity dies,\n        // potentially mis-attributing the death cause; calling a fake event would partially fix this, but this and setting the last damage\n        // cause do have issues around plugin observability. This is not a perfect solution, but it appears to be the best one here\n        // We also set no damage ticks to 0, to ensure that damage is applied for this case, and reset it back to the original value\n        // Snapshot current state so we can pop up properly\n        boolean wasMetaSet = hasIgnoreDamageMetadata(target);\n        boolean wasProcessing = processingNoInvulnDamage;\n        // set markers\n        processingNoInvulnDamage = true;\n        applyIgnoreDamageMetadata(target);\n        int noDamageTicks = target.getNoDamageTicks();\n        target.setNoDamageTicks(0);\n        target.damage(damage, attacker);\n        target.setNoDamageTicks(noDamageTicks);\n        if (!wasMetaSet)\n            removeIgnoreDamageMetadata(target);\n        if (!wasProcessing)\n            processingNoInvulnDamage = false;\n    }\n\n    public static void removeIgnoreDamageMetadata(@NotNull LivingEntity target) {\n        target.removeMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_CUSTOM_DAMAGE, mcMMO.p);\n    }\n\n    public static void applyIgnoreDamageMetadata(@NotNull LivingEntity target) {\n        target.setMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_CUSTOM_DAMAGE, MetadataConstants.MCMMO_METADATA_VALUE);\n    }\n\n    public static boolean hasIgnoreDamageMetadata(@NotNull LivingEntity target) {\n        return target.hasMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_CUSTOM_DAMAGE);\n    }\n\n    public static void dealNoInvulnerabilityTickDamageRupture(@NotNull LivingEntity target, double damage, Entity attacker, int toolTier) {\n        if (target.isDead()) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        dealNoInvulnerabilityTickDamage(target, damage, attacker);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Apply Area-of-Effect ability actions.\n     *  @param attacker The attacking player\n     * @param target The defending entity\n     * @param damage The initial damage amount\n     * @param type The type of skill being used\n     */\n    public static void applyAbilityAoE(@NotNull Player attacker, @NotNull LivingEntity target, double damage, @NotNull PrimarySkillType type) {\n        int numberOfTargets = getTier(attacker.getInventory().getItemInMainHand()); // The higher the weapon tier, the more targets you hit\n        double damageAmount = Math.max(damage, 1);\n\n        for (Entity entity : target.getNearbyEntities(2.5, 2.5, 2.5)) {\n            if (numberOfTargets <= 0) {\n                break;\n            }\n\n            if ((ExperienceConfig.getInstance().isNPCInteractionPrevented() && Misc.isNPCEntityExcludingVillagers(entity))\n                    || !(entity instanceof LivingEntity livingEntity) || !shouldBeAffected(attacker, entity)) {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            //EventUtils.callFakeArmSwingEvent(attacker);\n\n            switch (type) {\n                case SWORDS:\n                    if (entity instanceof Player) {\n                        NotificationManager.sendPlayerInformation((Player)entity, NotificationType.SUBSKILL_MESSAGE, \"Swords.Combat.SS.Struck\");\n                    }\n\n                    McMMOPlayer mmoAttacker = UserManager.getPlayer(attacker);\n\n                    if (mmoAttacker != null) {\n                        mmoAttacker.getSwordsManager().processRupture(livingEntity);\n                    }\n\n                    break;\n\n                case AXES:\n                    if (entity instanceof Player) {\n                        NotificationManager.sendPlayerInformation((Player)entity, NotificationType.SUBSKILL_MESSAGE, \"Axes.Combat.SS.Struck\");\n                    }\n\n                    break;\n\n                default:\n                    break;\n            }\n\n            dealDamage(livingEntity, damageAmount, attacker);\n            numberOfTargets--;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Start the task that gives combat XP.\n     *\n     * @param mcMMOPlayer The attacking player\n     * @param target The defending entity\n     * @param primarySkillType The skill being used\n     */\n    public static void processCombatXP(@NotNull McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer, @NotNull LivingEntity target, @NotNull PrimarySkillType primarySkillType) {\n        processCombatXP(mcMMOPlayer, target, primarySkillType, 1.0);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Start the task that gives combat XP.\n     *\n     * @param mcMMOPlayer The attacking player\n     * @param target The defending entity\n     * @param primarySkillType The skill being used\n     * @param multiplier final XP result will be multiplied by this\n     */\n    public static void processCombatXP(@NotNull McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer,\n                                       @NotNull LivingEntity target,\n                                       @NotNull PrimarySkillType primarySkillType,\n                                       double multiplier) {\n        double baseXP = 0;\n        XPGainReason xpGainReason;\n\n        if (target instanceof Player defender) {\n            if (!ExperienceConfig.getInstance().getExperienceGainsPlayerVersusPlayerEnabled()\n                    ||\n                    (mcMMO.p.getPartyConfig().isPartyEnabled() && mcMMO.p.getPartyManager().inSameParty(mcMMOPlayer.getPlayer(), (Player) target))) {\n                return;\n            }\n\n            xpGainReason = XPGainReason.PVP;\n\n            if (defender.isOnline()\n                    && SkillUtils.cooldownExpired(mcMMOPlayer.getRespawnATS(), Misc.PLAYER_RESPAWN_COOLDOWN_SECONDS)) {\n                baseXP = 20 * ExperienceConfig.getInstance().getPlayerVersusPlayerXP();\n            }\n        } else {\n            if (mcMMO.getModManager().isCustomEntity(target)) {\n                baseXP = mcMMO.getModManager().getEntity(target).getXpMultiplier();\n            } else if (target instanceof Animals) {\n                EntityType type = target.getType();\n                baseXP = ExperienceConfig.getInstance().getAnimalsXP(type);\n            } else if (target instanceof Monster) {\n                EntityType type = target.getType();\n                baseXP = ExperienceConfig.getInstance().getCombatXP(type);\n            } else {\n                EntityType type = target.getType();\n\n                if (ExperienceConfig.getInstance().hasCombatXP(type)) {\n                    if (type == EntityType.IRON_GOLEM) {\n                        if (!((IronGolem) target).isPlayerCreated()) {\n                            baseXP = ExperienceConfig.getInstance().getCombatXP(type);\n                        }\n                    } else {\n                        baseXP = ExperienceConfig.getInstance().getCombatXP(type);\n                    }\n                } else {\n                    baseXP = 1.0;\n                    mcMMO.getModManager().addCustomEntity(target);\n                }\n            }\n\n            if (hasMobFlag(MobMetaFlagType.COTW_SUMMONED_MOB, target)) {\n                baseXP = 0;\n            } else if (hasMobFlag(MobMetaFlagType.MOB_SPAWNER_MOB, target) || target.hasMetadata(\"ES\")) {\n                baseXP *= ExperienceConfig.getInstance().getSpawnedMobXpMultiplier();\n            } else if (hasMobFlag(MobMetaFlagType.NETHER_PORTAL_MOB, target)) {\n                baseXP *= ExperienceConfig.getInstance().getNetherPortalXpMultiplier();\n            } else if (hasMobFlag(MobMetaFlagType.EGG_MOB, target)) {\n                baseXP *= ExperienceConfig.getInstance().getEggXpMultiplier();\n            } else if (hasMobFlag(MobMetaFlagType.PLAYER_BRED_MOB, target)) {\n                baseXP *= ExperienceConfig.getInstance().getBredMobXpMultiplier();\n            } else if (hasMobFlag(MobMetaFlagType.PLAYER_TAMED_MOB, target)) {\n                baseXP *= ExperienceConfig.getInstance().getTamedMobXpMultiplier();\n            }\n\n            baseXP *= 10;\n            xpGainReason = XPGainReason.PVE;\n        }\n\n        baseXP *= multiplier;\n\n        if (baseXP > 0) {\n            mcMMO.p.getFoliaLib().getImpl().runAtEntity(mcMMOPlayer.getPlayer(), new AwardCombatXpTask(mcMMOPlayer, primarySkillType, baseXP, target, xpGainReason));\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Check to see if the given LivingEntity should be affected by a combat ability.\n     *\n     * @param player The attacking Player\n     * @param entity The defending Entity\n     * @return true if the Entity should be damaged, false otherwise.\n     */\n    private static boolean shouldBeAffected(@NotNull Player player, @NotNull Entity entity) {\n        if (entity instanceof Player defender) {\n            //TODO: NPC Interaction?\n            if (UserManager.getPlayer(defender) == null)\n                return true;\n\n            if (!defender.getWorld().getPVP() || defender == player || UserManager.getPlayer(defender).getGodMode()) {\n                return false;\n            }\n\n            if (mcMMO.p.getPartyConfig().isPartyEnabled()) {\n                if ((mcMMO.p.getPartyManager().inSameParty(player, defender) || mcMMO.p.getPartyManager().areAllies(player, defender)) && !(Permissions.friendlyFire(player) && Permissions.friendlyFire(defender))) {\n                    return false;\n                }\n            }\n\n            // Vanished players should not be able to get hit by AoE effects\n            if (!player.canSee(defender)) {\n                return false;\n            }\n            \n            // Spectators should not be affected \n            return defender.getGameMode() != GameMode.SPECTATOR;\n        } else if (entity instanceof Tameable tameableEntity) {\n            if (isFriendlyPet(player, tameableEntity)) {\n                // isFriendlyPet ensures that the Tameable is: Tamed, owned by a player, and the owner is in the same party\n                // So we can make some assumptions here, about our casting and our check\n                Player owner = (Player) tameableEntity.getOwner();\n                return Permissions.friendlyFire(player) && Permissions.friendlyFire(owner);\n            }\n        }\n\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Checks to see if an entity is currently invincible.\n     *\n     * @param entity The {@link LivingEntity} to check\n     * @param eventDamage The damage from the event the entity is involved in\n     * @return true if the entity is invincible, false otherwise\n     */\n    public static boolean isInvincible(@NotNull LivingEntity entity, double eventDamage) {\n        /*\n         * So apparently if you do more damage to a LivingEntity than its last damage int you bypass the invincibility.\n         * So yeah, this is for that.\n         */\n        return (entity.getNoDamageTicks() > entity.getMaximumNoDamageTicks() / 2.0F) && (eventDamage <= entity.getLastDamage());\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Checks to see if an entity is currently friendly toward a given player.\n     *\n     * @param attacker The player to check.\n     * @param pet The entity to check.\n     * @return true if the entity is friendly, false otherwise\n     */\n    public static boolean isFriendlyPet(@NotNull Player attacker, @NotNull Tameable pet) {\n        if (pet.isTamed()) {\n            AnimalTamer tamer = pet.getOwner();\n\n            if (tamer instanceof Player owner) {\n\n                return (owner == attacker || (mcMMO.p.getPartyConfig().isPartyEnabled() && (mcMMO.p.getPartyManager().inSameParty(attacker, owner) || mcMMO.p.getPartyManager().areAllies(attacker, owner))));\n            }\n        }\n\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Get the upgrade tier of the item in hand.\n     *\n     * @param inHand The item to check the tier of\n     * @return the tier of the item\n     */\n    private static int getTier(@NotNull ItemStack inHand) {\n        int tier = 0;\n\n        if (ItemUtils.isWoodTool(inHand)) {\n            tier = 1;\n        } else if (ItemUtils.isStoneTool(inHand)) {\n            tier = 2;\n        } else if (ItemUtils.isIronTool(inHand)) {\n            tier = 3;\n        } else if (ItemUtils.isGoldTool(inHand)) {\n            tier = 1;\n        } else if (ItemUtils.isDiamondTool(inHand)) {\n            tier = 4;\n        } else if (ItemUtils.isNetheriteTool(inHand)) {\n            tier = 5;\n        } else if (mcMMO.getModManager().isCustomTool(inHand)) {\n            tier = mcMMO.getModManager().getTool(inHand).getTier();\n        }\n\n        return tier;\n    }\n\n    public static void handleHealthbars(@NotNull Entity attacker, @NotNull LivingEntity target, double damage, @NotNull mcMMO plugin) {\n        if (!(attacker instanceof Player player)) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        if (Misc.isNPCEntityExcludingVillagers(player) || Misc.isNPCEntityExcludingVillagers(target)) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        if (!player.hasMetadata(MetadataConstants.METADATA_KEY_PLAYER_DATA)) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        MobHealthbarUtils.handleMobHealthbars(target, damage, plugin);\n    }\n\n    public static void modifyMoveSpeed(@NotNull LivingEntity livingEntity, double multiplier) {\n        AttributeInstance attributeInstance = livingEntity.getAttribute(Attribute.GENERIC_MOVEMENT_SPEED);\n\n        if (attributeInstance != null) {\n            double normalSpeed = attributeInstance.getBaseValue();\n            attributeInstance.setBaseValue(normalSpeed * multiplier);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Clean up metadata from a projectile after a minute has passed\n     *\n     * @param arrow the projectile\n     */\n    public static void delayArrowMetaCleanup(@NotNull Arrow arrow) {\n        mcMMO.p.getFoliaLib().getImpl().runLater(() -> ProjectileUtils.cleanupProjectileMetadata(arrow), 20*120);\n    }\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "src/main/java/com/gmail/nossr50/util/skills/SkillUtils.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package com.gmail.nossr50.util.skills;\n\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.config.HiddenConfig;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.experience.XPGainReason;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.experience.XPGainSource;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.interactions.NotificationType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.player.McMMOPlayer;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.PrimarySkillType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.SubSkillType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.SuperAbilityType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.locale.LocaleLoader;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.mcMMO;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.ItemMetadataUtils;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.ItemUtils;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.Misc;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.Permissions;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.player.NotificationManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.player.UserManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.text.StringUtils;\nimport org.bukkit.Bukkit;\nimport org.bukkit.Location;\nimport org.bukkit.Material;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.Player;\nimport org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack;\nimport org.bukkit.inventory.Recipe;\nimport org.bukkit.inventory.ShapedRecipe;\nimport org.bukkit.inventory.ShapelessRecipe;\nimport org.bukkit.inventory.meta.ItemMeta;\nimport org.bukkit.potion.PotionEffect;\nimport org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;\nimport org.jetbrains.annotations.Nullable;\n\nimport java.util.Iterator;\n\nimport static com.gmail.nossr50.util.ItemMetadataUtils.*;\nimport static com.gmail.nossr50.util.PotionEffectUtil.getHastePotionEffectType;\n\npublic final class SkillUtils {\n    /**\n     * This is a static utility class, therefore we don't want any instances of\n     * this class. Making the constructor private prevents accidents like that.\n     */\n    private SkillUtils() {}\n\n    public static void applyXpGain(McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer, PrimarySkillType skill, float xp, XPGainReason xpGainReason) {\n        mcMMOPlayer.beginXpGain(skill, xp, xpGainReason, XPGainSource.SELF);\n    }\n\n    public static void applyXpGain(McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer, PrimarySkillType skill, float xp, XPGainReason xpGainReason, XPGainSource xpGainSource) {\n        mcMMOPlayer.beginXpGain(skill, xp, xpGainReason, xpGainSource);\n    }\n\n    /*\n     * Skill Stat Calculations\n     */\n\n    public static String[] calculateLengthDisplayValues(Player player, float skillValue, PrimarySkillType skill) {\n        int maxLength = mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().getSuperAbilityMaxLength(mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().getSuperAbility(skill));\n        int abilityLengthVar = mcMMO.p.getAdvancedConfig().getAbilityLength();\n        int abilityLengthCap = mcMMO.p.getAdvancedConfig().getAbilityLengthCap();\n\n        int length;\n\n        if (abilityLengthCap > 0) {\n            length =     (int) Math.min(abilityLengthCap, 2 + (skillValue / abilityLengthVar));\n        } else {\n            length = 2 + (int) (skillValue / abilityLengthVar);\n        }\n\n        int enduranceLength = PerksUtils.handleActivationPerks(player, length, maxLength);\n\n        if (maxLength != 0) {\n            length = Math.min(length, maxLength);\n        }\n\n        return new String[] { String.valueOf(length), String.valueOf(enduranceLength) };\n    }\n\n    /*\n     * Others\n     */\n\n    public static int handleFoodSkills(Player player, int eventFoodLevel, SubSkillType subSkillType) {\n        int curRank = RankUtils.getRank(player, subSkillType);\n\n        int currentFoodLevel = player.getFoodLevel();\n        int foodChange = eventFoodLevel - currentFoodLevel;\n\n        foodChange+=curRank;\n\n        return currentFoodLevel + foodChange;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Calculate the time remaining until the cooldown expires.\n     *\n     * @param deactivatedTimeStamp Time of deactivation\n     * @param cooldown The length of the cooldown\n     * @param player The Player to check for cooldown perks\n     *\n     * @return the number of seconds remaining before the cooldown expires\n     */\n    public static int calculateTimeLeft(long deactivatedTimeStamp, int cooldown, Player player) {\n        return (int) (((deactivatedTimeStamp + (PerksUtils.handleCooldownPerks(player, cooldown) * Misc.TIME_CONVERSION_FACTOR)) - System.currentTimeMillis()) / Misc.TIME_CONVERSION_FACTOR);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Check if the cooldown has expired.\n     * This does NOT account for cooldown perks!\n     *\n     * @param deactivatedTimeStamp Time of deactivation in seconds\n     * @param cooldown The length of the cooldown in seconds\n     *\n     * @return true if the cooldown is expired\n     */\n    public static boolean cooldownExpired(long deactivatedTimeStamp, int cooldown) {\n        return System.currentTimeMillis() >= (deactivatedTimeStamp + cooldown) * Misc.TIME_CONVERSION_FACTOR;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Checks if the given string represents a valid skill\n     *\n     * @param skillName The name of the skill to check\n     * @return true if this is a valid skill, false otherwise\n     */\n    public static boolean isSkill(String skillName) {\n        return mcMMO.p.getGeneralConfig().getLocale().equalsIgnoreCase(\"en_US\") ? mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().matchSkill(skillName) != null : isLocalizedSkill(skillName);\n    }\n\n    public static void sendSkillMessage(Player player, NotificationType notificationType, String key) {\n        Location location = player.getLocation();\n\n        for (Player otherPlayer : player.getWorld().getPlayers()) {\n            if (otherPlayer != player && Misc.isNear(location, otherPlayer.getLocation(), Misc.SKILL_MESSAGE_MAX_SENDING_DISTANCE)) {\n                NotificationManager.sendNearbyPlayersInformation(otherPlayer, notificationType, key, player.getName());\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    public static void handleAbilitySpeedIncrease(Player player) {\n        if (HiddenConfig.getInstance().useEnchantmentBuffs()) {\n            ItemStack heldItem = player.getInventory().getItemInMainHand();\n\n            if (heldItem == null)\n                return;\n\n            if (!ItemUtils.canBeSuperAbilityDigBoosted(heldItem)) {\n                return;\n            }\n\n            int originalDigSpeed = heldItem.getEnchantmentLevel(mcMMO.p.getEnchantmentMapper().getEfficiency());\n            ItemUtils.addDigSpeedToItem(heldItem, heldItem.getEnchantmentLevel(mcMMO.p.getEnchantmentMapper().getEfficiency()));\n\n            //1.13.2+ will have persistent metadata for this item\n            ItemMetadataUtils.setSuperAbilityBoostedItem(heldItem, originalDigSpeed);\n        } else {\n            int duration = 0;\n            int amplifier = 0;\n\n            if (player.hasPotionEffect(getHastePotionEffectType())) {\n                for (PotionEffect effect : player.getActivePotionEffects()) {\n                    if (effect.getType() == getHastePotionEffectType()) {\n                        duration = effect.getDuration();\n                        amplifier = effect.getAmplifier();\n                        break;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n\n            McMMOPlayer mcMMOPlayer = UserManager.getPlayer(player);\n\n            //Not Loaded\n            if (mcMMOPlayer == null)\n                return;\n\n            PrimarySkillType skill = mcMMOPlayer.getAbilityMode(SuperAbilityType.SUPER_BREAKER) ? PrimarySkillType.MINING : PrimarySkillType.EXCAVATION;\n\n            int abilityLengthVar = mcMMO.p.getAdvancedConfig().getAbilityLength();\n            int abilityLengthCap = mcMMO.p.getAdvancedConfig().getAbilityLengthCap();\n\n            int ticks;\n\n            if (abilityLengthCap > 0) {\n                ticks = PerksUtils.handleActivationPerks(player,  Math.min(abilityLengthCap, 2 + (mcMMOPlayer.getSkillLevel(skill) / abilityLengthVar)),\n                        mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().getSuperAbilityMaxLength(mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().getSuperAbility(skill))) * Misc.TICK_CONVERSION_FACTOR;\n            } else {\n                ticks = PerksUtils.handleActivationPerks(player, 2 + ((mcMMOPlayer.getSkillLevel(skill)) / abilityLengthVar),\n                        mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().getSuperAbilityMaxLength(mcMMO.p.getSkillTools().getSuperAbility(skill))) * Misc.TICK_CONVERSION_FACTOR;\n            }\n\n            PotionEffect abilityBuff = new PotionEffect(getHastePotionEffectType(), duration + ticks, amplifier + 10);\n            player.addPotionEffect(abilityBuff, true);\n        }\n    }\n\n    public static void removeAbilityBoostsFromInventory(@NotNull Player player) {\n        for (ItemStack itemStack : player.getInventory().getContents()) {\n            removeAbilityBuff(itemStack);\n        }\n    }\n\n    public static void removeAbilityBuff(@Nullable ItemStack itemStack) {\n        if (itemStack == null)\n            return;\n\n        if (!ItemUtils.canBeSuperAbilityDigBoosted(itemStack))\n            return;\n\n\n        //1.13.2+ will have persistent metadata for this itemStack\n        if (isLegacyAbilityTool(itemStack)) {\n            ItemMeta itemMeta = itemStack.getItemMeta();\n\n            if (itemMeta != null) {\n                // This is safe to call without prior checks.\n                itemMeta.removeEnchant(mcMMO.p.getEnchantmentMapper().getEfficiency());\n\n                itemStack.setItemMeta(itemMeta);\n                ItemUtils.removeAbilityLore(itemStack);\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (isSuperAbilityBoosted(itemStack)) {\n            removeBonusDigSpeedOnSuperAbilityTool(itemStack);\n        }\n    }\n\n    public static void handleDurabilityChange(ItemStack itemStack, int durabilityModifier) {\n        handleDurabilityChange(itemStack, durabilityModifier, 1.0);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Modify the durability of an ItemStack, using Tools specific formula for unbreaking enchant damage reduction\n     *\n     * @param itemStack The ItemStack which durability should be modified\n     * @param durabilityModifier the amount to modify the durability by\n     * @param maxDamageModifier the amount to adjust the max damage by\n     */\n    public static void handleDurabilityChange(ItemStack itemStack, double durabilityModifier, double maxDamageModifier) {\n        if (itemStack.hasItemMeta() && itemStack.getItemMeta().isUnbreakable()) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        Material type = itemStack.getType();\n        short maxDurability = mcMMO.getRepairableManager().isRepairable(type) ? mcMMO.getRepairableManager().getRepairable(type).getMaximumDurability() : type.getMaxDurability();\n        durabilityModifier = (int) Math.min(durabilityModifier / (itemStack.getEnchantmentLevel(mcMMO.p.getEnchantmentMapper().getUnbreaking()) + 1), maxDurability * maxDamageModifier);\n\n        itemStack.setDurability((short) Math.min(itemStack.getDurability() + durabilityModifier, maxDurability));\n    }\n\n    private static boolean isLocalizedSkill(String skillName) {\n        for (PrimarySkillType skill : PrimarySkillType.values()) {\n            if (skillName.equalsIgnoreCase(LocaleLoader.getString(StringUtils.getCapitalized(skill.toString()) + \".SkillName\"))) {\n                return true;\n            }\n        }\n\n        return false;\n    }\n\n\n    /**\n     * Modify the durability of an ItemStack, using Armor specific formula for unbreaking enchant damage reduction\n     *\n     * @param itemStack The ItemStack which durability should be modified\n     * @param durabilityModifier the amount to modify the durability by\n     * @param maxDamageModifier the amount to adjust the max damage by\n     */\n    public static void handleArmorDurabilityChange(ItemStack itemStack, double durabilityModifier, double maxDamageModifier) {\n        if (itemStack.hasItemMeta() && itemStack.getItemMeta().isUnbreakable()) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        Material type = itemStack.getType();\n        short maxDurability = mcMMO.getRepairableManager().isRepairable(type) ? mcMMO.getRepairableManager().getRepairable(type).getMaximumDurability() : type.getMaxDurability();\n        durabilityModifier = (int) Math.min(durabilityModifier * (0.6 + 0.4/ (itemStack.getEnchantmentLevel(mcMMO.p.getEnchantmentMapper().getUnbreaking()) + 1)), maxDurability * maxDamageModifier);\n\n        itemStack.setDurability((short) Math.min(itemStack.getDurability() + durabilityModifier, maxDurability));\n    }\n\n    @Nullable\n    public static Material getRepairAndSalvageItem(@NotNull ItemStack inHand) {\n        if (ItemUtils.isDiamondTool(inHand) || ItemUtils.isDiamondArmor(inHand)) {\n            return Material.DIAMOND;\n        } else if (ItemUtils.isGoldTool(inHand) || ItemUtils.isGoldArmor(inHand)) {\n            return Material.GOLD_INGOT;\n        } else if (ItemUtils.isIronTool(inHand) || ItemUtils.isIronArmor(inHand)) {\n            return Material.IRON_INGOT;\n        } else if (ItemUtils.isStoneTool(inHand)) {\n            return Material.COBBLESTONE;\n        } else if (ItemUtils.isWoodTool(inHand)) {\n            return Material.OAK_WOOD;\n        } else if (ItemUtils.isLeatherArmor(inHand)) {\n            return Material.LEATHER;\n        } else if (ItemUtils.isStringTool(inHand)) {\n            return Material.STRING;\n        } else {\n            return null;\n        }\n    }\n\n    public static int getRepairAndSalvageQuantities(ItemStack item) {\n        return getRepairAndSalvageQuantities(item.getType(), getRepairAndSalvageItem(item));\n    }\n\n    public static int getRepairAndSalvageQuantities(Material itemMaterial, Material recipeMaterial) {\n        int quantity = 0;\n\n        if (mcMMO.getMaterialMapStore().isNetheriteTool(itemMaterial) || mcMMO.getMaterialMapStore().isNetheriteArmor(itemMaterial)) {\n            //One netherite bar requires 4 netherite scraps\n            return 4;\n        }\n\n        for(Iterator<? extends Recipe> recipeIterator = Bukkit.getServer().recipeIterator(); recipeIterator.hasNext();) {\n            Recipe bukkitRecipe = recipeIterator.next();\n\n            if (bukkitRecipe.getResult().getType() != itemMaterial)\n                continue;\n\n            if (bukkitRecipe instanceof ShapelessRecipe) {\n                for (ItemStack ingredient : ((ShapelessRecipe) bukkitRecipe).getIngredientList()) {\n                    if (ingredient != null\n                            && (recipeMaterial == null || ingredient.getType() == recipeMaterial)\n                            && (ingredient.getType() == recipeMaterial)) {\n                        quantity += ingredient.getAmount();\n                    }\n                }\n            } else if (bukkitRecipe instanceof ShapedRecipe) {\n                for (ItemStack ingredient : ((ShapedRecipe) bukkitRecipe).getIngredientMap().values()) {\n                    if (ingredient != null\n                            && (recipeMaterial == null || ingredient.getType() == recipeMaterial)\n                            && (ingredient.getType() == recipeMaterial)) {\n                        quantity += ingredient.getAmount();\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        return quantity;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Checks if a player can use a skill\n     * @param player target player\n     * @param subSkillType target subskill\n     * @return true if the player has permission and has the skill unlocked\n     */\n    public static boolean canUseSubskill(Player player, @NotNull SubSkillType subSkillType) {\n        return Permissions.isSubSkillEnabled(player, subSkillType) && RankUtils.hasUnlockedSubskill(player, subSkillType);\n    }\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "src/main/java/com/gmail/nossr50/util/sounds/SoundManager.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package com.gmail.nossr50.util.sounds;\n\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.config.SoundConfig;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.Misc;\nimport org.bukkit.Location;\nimport org.bukkit.Sound;\nimport org.bukkit.SoundCategory;\nimport org.bukkit.World;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.Player;\n\npublic class SoundManager {\n    /**\n     * Sends a sound to the player\n     * @param soundType the type of sound\n     */\n    public static void sendSound(Player player, Location location, SoundType soundType) {\n        if (SoundConfig.getInstance().getIsEnabled(soundType))\n            player.playSound(location, getSound(soundType), SoundCategory.MASTER, getVolume(soundType), getPitch(soundType));\n    }\n\n    public static void sendCategorizedSound(Player player, Location location, SoundType soundType, SoundCategory soundCategory) {\n        if (SoundConfig.getInstance().getIsEnabled(soundType))\n            player.playSound(location, getSound(soundType), soundCategory, getVolume(soundType), getPitch(soundType));\n    }\n\n    public static void sendCategorizedSound(Player player, Location location, SoundType soundType, SoundCategory soundCategory, float pitchModifier) {\n        float totalPitch = Math.min(2.0F, (getPitch(soundType) + pitchModifier));\n\n        if (SoundConfig.getInstance().getIsEnabled(soundType))\n            player.playSound(location, getSound(soundType), soundCategory, getVolume(soundType), totalPitch);\n    }\n\n    public static void worldSendSound(World world, Location location, SoundType soundType) {\n        if (SoundConfig.getInstance().getIsEnabled(soundType))\n            world.playSound(location, getSound(soundType), getVolume(soundType), getPitch(soundType));\n    }\n\n    public static void worldSendSoundMaxPitch(World world, Location location, SoundType soundType) {\n        if (SoundConfig.getInstance().getIsEnabled(soundType))\n            world.playSound(location, getSound(soundType), getVolume(soundType), 2.0F);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * All volume is multiplied by the master volume to get its final value\n     * @param soundType target soundtype\n     * @return the volume for this soundtype\n     */\n    private static float getVolume(SoundType soundType) {\n        return SoundConfig.getInstance().getVolume(soundType) * SoundConfig.getInstance().getMasterVolume();\n    }\n\n    private static float getPitch(SoundType soundType) {\n        if (soundType == SoundType.FIZZ)\n            return getFizzPitch();\n        else if (soundType == SoundType.POP)\n            return getPopPitch();\n        else\n            return SoundConfig.getInstance().getPitch(soundType);\n    }\n\n    private static Sound getSound(SoundType soundType) {\n        return switch (soundType) {\n            case ANVIL -> Sound.BLOCK_ANVIL_PLACE;\n            case ITEM_BREAK -> Sound.ENTITY_ITEM_BREAK;\n            case POP -> Sound.ENTITY_ITEM_PICKUP;\n            case CHIMAERA_WING -> Sound.ENTITY_BAT_TAKEOFF;\n            case LEVEL_UP -> Sound.ENTITY_PLAYER_LEVELUP;\n            case FIZZ -> Sound.BLOCK_FIRE_EXTINGUISH;\n            case TOOL_READY -> Sound.ITEM_ARMOR_EQUIP_GOLD;\n            case ROLL_ACTIVATED -> Sound.ENTITY_LLAMA_SWAG;\n            case SKILL_UNLOCKED -> Sound.UI_TOAST_CHALLENGE_COMPLETE;\n            case ABILITY_ACTIVATED_BERSERK, TIRED -> Sound.BLOCK_CONDUIT_AMBIENT;\n            case ABILITY_ACTIVATED_GENERIC -> Sound.ITEM_TRIDENT_RIPTIDE_3;\n            case DEFLECT_ARROWS, BLEED -> Sound.ENTITY_ENDER_EYE_DEATH;\n            case GLASS -> Sound.BLOCK_GLASS_BREAK;\n            case ITEM_CONSUMED -> Sound.ITEM_BOTTLE_EMPTY;\n        };\n    }\n\n    public static float getFizzPitch() {\n        return 2.6F + (Misc.getRandom().nextFloat() - Misc.getRandom().nextFloat()) * 0.8F;\n    }\n\n    public static float getPopPitch() {\n        return ((Misc.getRandom().nextFloat() - Misc.getRandom().nextFloat()) * 0.7F + 1.0F) * 2.0F;\n    }\n\n    public static float getKrakenPitch() {\n        return (Misc.getRandom().nextFloat() - Misc.getRandom().nextFloat()) * 0.2F + 1.0F;\n    }\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "src/test/java/com/gmail/nossr50/MMOTestEnvironment.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package com.gmail.nossr50;\n\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.api.exceptions.InvalidSkillException;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.config.AdvancedConfig;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.config.ChatConfig;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.config.GeneralConfig;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.config.RankConfig;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.config.experience.ExperienceConfig;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.config.party.PartyConfig;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.player.McMMOPlayer;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.player.PlayerProfile;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.PrimarySkillType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.datatypes.skills.SubSkillType;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.*;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.blockmeta.ChunkManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.player.UserManager;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.skills.RankUtils;\nimport com.gmail.nossr50.util.skills.SkillTools;\nimport org.bukkit.*;\nimport org.bukkit.entity.Player;\nimport org.bukkit.inventory.ItemFactory;\nimport org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack;\nimport org.bukkit.inventory.PlayerInventory;\nimport org.bukkit.plugin.PluginManager;\nimport org.mockito.MockedStatic;\nimport org.mockito.Mockito;\n\nimport java.util.UUID;\nimport java.util.logging.Logger;\n\nimport static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.any;\nimport static org.mockito.Mockito.*;\n\npublic abstract class MMOTestEnvironment {\n    protected MockedStatic<Bukkit> mockedBukkit;\n    protected MockedStatic<mcMMO> mockedMcMMO;\n    protected MockedStatic<ChatConfig> mockedChatConfig;\n    protected MockedStatic<ExperienceConfig> experienceConfig;\n    protected MockedStatic<Permissions> mockedPermissions;\n    protected MockedStatic<RankUtils> mockedRankUtils;\n    protected MockedStatic<UserManager> mockedUserManager;\n    protected MockedStatic<MiscGeneralUtil> mockedMiscGeneralUtil;\n    protected MockedStatic<SkillTools> mockedSkillTools;\n    protected MockedStatic<EventUtils> mockedEventUtils;\n    protected TransientEntityTracker transientEntityTracker;\n    protected AdvancedConfig advancedConfig;\n    protected PartyConfig partyConfig;\n    protected GeneralConfig generalConfig;\n    protected RankConfig rankConfig;\n    protected SkillTools skillTools;\n    protected Server server;\n    protected PluginManager pluginManager;\n    protected World world;\n\n    /* Mocks */\n    protected Player player;\n\n    protected UUID playerUUID = UUID.randomUUID();\n    protected ItemStack itemInMainHand;\n\n    protected PlayerInventory playerInventory;\n    protected PlayerProfile
